In this paper, we present P2Echo, a unified framework capable of segmenting diverse cardiac structures across multiple standard echocardiographic views using a single model controlled by structured view-aware text prompts. Unlike recent foundation model adaptations that rely on implicit latent priors, P2Echo explicitly conditions the segmentation process via a novel Dynamic Image-Text Alignment (DyITA) module. DyITA integrates a differentiable routing mechanism and token differential operators to dynamically modulate decoder features according to the requested view and target structure. This allows the network to adaptively switch its processing pathways for different views without architectural changes. Extensive experiments on a large-scale internal dataset (seen during training) and held-out evaluation on three external datasets demonstrate that P2Echo matches or exceeds both view-specific specialists and state-of-the-art universal models at a fraction of their computational cost, offering an efficient, controllable solution for multi-plane cardiac analysis. Code is available at: https://github.com/moeinheidari7829/P2Echo.

</details>


### [33] [Camera3R: Feed-Forward 3D Reconstruction from Heterogeneous Camera Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13453)
*Ruxiao Duan,Yunwen Zhou,Erin Hong,Dongxu Zhao,Eric Turner,Federico Tombari,Alex Wong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Camera3R，一种低秩适配（LoRA 式）框架，能以极少参数与训练成本将透视预训练的前馈三维重建网络迁移到任意非透视相机模型，同时完全保留原有透视输入性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈 3R 模型虽在大型透视数据集上训练，却难以泛化到非透视图像，限制了其在未知、异构相机模型场景下的部署；而从零重训练成本高昂。

Method: Camera3R：对已预训练的透视 3R 基础网络引入低秩适配模块，以参数高效微调方式将网络扩展到其他相机模型，无需大幅重训练。

Result: 仅增加 0.5% 参数、训练时间不足一天，即可使基础 3R 模型适配非透视相机，且在透视输入上性能与原始模型保持一致。

Conclusion: 低秩适配是小成本、可复用地将透视三维重建基础模型推广到异构相机模型的可行途径，代码已开源。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D reconstruction (3R) from unconstrained multi-view images has recently emerged as a prominent research direction in 3D computer vision. Various foundational reconstruction models have been proposed with different architectures and reconstruction paradigms. Despite being trained on large-scale perspective datasets with substantial computational resources, these models generally struggle to generalize to non-perspective images. This limitation hinders their deployment in applications involving arbitrary, unknown, and potentially heterogeneous camera models. To build on the capabilities of existing reconstruction models with minimal retraining, we propose Camera3R, a low-rank adaptation framework that efficiently extends perspective-pretrained 3R networks to any other camera models. Extensive experiments demonstrate that, with only 0.5% additional parameters and less than a day of training, Camera3R adapts a foundational 3D reconstruction model to non-perspective cameras while fully preserving its original performance on perspective inputs. Code is available at https://github.com/android-xr/camera3r.

</details>


### [34] [Hand-Object Contact Transition Detection in Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13486)
*Huy Anh Nguyen,Chang Dong,Feras Dayoub,Minh Hoai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一的端到端框架检测第一人称视频中手-物接触起始（touch）与释放（release）两类时序边界，并构建ContactTransition数据集（4,021段视频、8,498个touch与8,077个release标注）验证其优于两阶段与端到端基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触转移时刻是手-物交互的细粒度时序边界，仅靠手部线索难以判定，需要联合建模手与物体；且现有方法依赖外部物体或交互检测器，推理成本与误差传递高。

Method: 统一框架：在以手为中心的时空表征上融合直接从视频学习到的物体感知特征；提出面向接触转移的transition-aware事件定位（event spotting）建模方式，推理阶段不依赖外部物体检测器或手-物交互检测器；同时发布含感兴趣物体监督的ContactTransition数据集。

Result: 在touch与release两类转移上均稳定优于强两阶段基线和端到端事件定位基线；检测到的接触转移可作为下游第一人称手-物推理的有效时序锚点。

Conclusion: 联合手-物建模的单阶段事件定位是检测接触转移的有效范式，配套数据集为该任务提供基准，且检测结果对下游时序推理具有实用价值。

Abstract: This paper tackles the task of hand-object contact transition detection in egocentric video, which aims to localize the two temporal boundaries of a contact episode: the moment a hand first establishes contact with an object (touch) and the moment contact is released (release). We propose a unified framework that jointly models the interacting hand and object to accurately localize both transition types. The proposed framework augments hand-centric spatiotemporal representations with object-aware features learned directly from the video and introduces a transition-aware event spotting formulation tailored to contact transitions, without relying on external object or hand-object interaction detectors during inference. To support this task, we present ContactTransition, a dataset comprising 4,021 egocentric videos with 8,498 touch and 8,077 release annotations, together with object-of-interest supervision. Experiments demonstrate that the proposed method consistently outperforms strong two-stage and end-to-end event-spotting baselines on both transition types. Finally, we show that detected contact transitions provide effective temporal anchors for downstream egocentric hand-object reasoning.

</details>


### [35] [When Reconstruction Helps Event-Camera Person Triggers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13488)
*Yifan Shuai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文比较了将事件相机数据转换为通用视觉模型可读表示的五种方案（灰度累积、两种时间编码、两种强度重建器），在DSEC真实配对事件数据上以"是否触发录制"为统一任务评估检测收益与误触发代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机输出的是异步亮度变化，本身不是通用视觉模型可直接处理的图像形式。若想用现成视觉模型充当录制触发器，必须先决定如何把事件流表示为模型可解释的输入，而这一表示选择对检测效果与触发成本的影响尚缺乏受控比较。

Method: 在4段未见过的DSEC录制、共192个窗口的配对真实事件数据上，对比直接灰度累积、两种时间编码、两种强度重建器（E2VID、FireNet+）。阈值仅在开发数据上校准；以人物检测数为收益指标、背景正例数为误触发代价，并在统一的10%背景正例上限下做回溯比较。另用Qwen3-VL-4B对同一批窗口测试下游可用性，并进行96秒探索性回放考察触发阈值对片段捕获与录制起点的影响。

Result: E2VID在开发集校准阈值下取得52个行人中40个检测、140个背景中12个误正；最强的直接时间编码在可接受固定截断下仅14检测、7误正。在10%误正上限下，E2VID仍为40检测，任何直接编码最多19。FireNet+准备开销更低，在其冻结截断下21检测、0误正。Qwen3-VL-4B在E2VID下为33/52、2/140，在最高召回直接渲染（三通道图像）下为9/52、9/140。

Conclusion: 在受控输入历史条件下，强度重建能带来明确的检测收益，且该收益可与连续触发带来的准备成本和误启动代价区分开来；方案选择取决于把检测率还是触发开销置于优先位置。

Abstract: Event cameras record asynchronous changes in brightness. To use a general visual model as a recording trigger, a system must represent these measurements in a form the model can interpret. We compare direct grayscale accumulation, two temporal encodings, and two intensity reconstructors on paired real event data. On 192 windows from four held-out DSEC recordings, thresholds calibrated only on development data yielded 40 of 52 person detections and 12 of 140 background positives with E2VID. The strongest direct temporal encoding at an admissible fixed cutoff yielded 14 detections and seven background positives. A retrospective comparison at a common 10% background-positive cap yielded 40 detections for E2VID and at most 19 for any direct encoding. FireNet+ had lower preparation cost and produced 21 detections with zero observed background positives at its frozen cutoff. On the same 192 windows, Qwen3-VL-4B yielded 33/52, 2/140 with E2VID and 9/52, 9/140 with the highest-recall direct rendering, the three-bin image (person detections, background positives). A 96-second exploratory replay illustrates the sensitivity of episode capture and recording starts to cutoff selection. The experiments identify a detection benefit from reconstruction under controlled input history and distinguish it from the cost and false starts of continuous triggering.

</details>


### [36] [Evidential Deep Learning with conditional entropy for glaucoma detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13513)
*Hai Siong Tan,Kuancheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出以条件熵函数作为不确定性度量基础的证据深度学习（EDL）框架，用于眼底图像青光眼检测，在校准误差与可解释性一致性上优于Deep Ensembles、MC Dropout、事后校准及传统基于Dempster不确定性的EDL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 青光眼筛查模型需可靠地量化预测不确定性，而传统EDL以Dempster组合规则导出的不确定性（如 vacuity）作为正则与度量目标，其校准质量与可解释一致性仍不理想，需要更合适的条件熵形式来刻画预测不确定性。

Method: 提出新的条件熵函数作为EDL的预测不确定性度量与正则项；在EyePACS-AIROGS-light-V2与RIM-ONE-DL数据集上与Deep Ensembles、MC Dropout、事后校准（post-hoc calibration）及基于Dempster不确定性的常规EDL进行对比；并通过Grad-CAM热图的"聚焦指数"（focus index，表征空间集中度）分析可解释性，比较正确/误分类样本的指数分布分离度。

Result: 所提条件熵正则化的EDL模型不确定度估计的校准误差显著低于各对比方法；EDL模型在正确与误分类眼底图像间的聚焦指数分布分离度更大；假阴性样本的Grad-CAM激活呈局部化且偏离视盘区域，与视盘在青光眼评估中的临床重要性相符。

Conclusion: 面向青光眼检测的所提EDL框架不仅提升不确定性校准，还具备更符合生理/临床一致性的可解释性，说明不确定性度量函数的设计可同时改善校准与解释质量。

Abstract: We propose a novel conditional entropy function as a basis for quantifying predictive uncertainty in Evidential Deep Learning (EDL) for glaucoma detection from retinal fundus images. Using the EyePACS-AIROGS-light-V2 and RIM-ONE-DL datasets for validation, we found that our proposed EDL model regularized by conditional entropy function produced uncertainty estimates with substantially lower calibration errors than Deep Ensembles, MC Dropout, post-hoc calibration and the conventional EDL framework based on Dempster uncertainty. To further probe our models' interpretability, we analyzed Grad-CAM heatmaps using a focus index representing spatial concentration. Compared with the other uncertainty quantification methods, EDL models exhibited greater separation between the index distributions of correctly and incorrectly classified fundus images. In particular, false negatives were associated with localized Grad-CAM activations that were displaced from the optic disc, consistent with its established clinical importance in glaucoma assessment. Overall, our findings suggest that our proposed EDL framework for glaucoma detection provides not only enhanced uncertainty calibration but also admits a more physiologically consistent interpretability.

</details>


### [37] [Seeing the Small: Deformable Duality Mamba for Retinal Lesion Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13514)
*Chang Yu,Siqing Li,Yuanchen Liu,Zhenyu Bu,Luming Yang,Alper Yilmaz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出D²Mamba，将非因果状态空间对偶与可变形Mamba扫描结合，用于糖尿病视网膜病变（DR）病灶分割，在DDR数据集上取得33.87% mIoU，微小动脉瘤Dice提升10.06个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DR病灶形状差异极大，且微小动脉瘤（MAs）仅占几个像素，现有分割方法难以建模此类小而不规则病灶的空间组织关系。作者假设引入全局上下文与形状自适应扫描的病灶区域空间组织建模，可改善微小不规则病灶的分割效果。

Method: 提出D²Mamba，将非因果状态空间对偶（non-causal state space duality）与可变形Mamba扫描（deformable Mamba scanning）相耦合。在DDR数据集上采用五折交叉验证，与代表性分割基线在同一训练协议下对比，并对各组件进行消融实验。

Result: D²Mamba取得最佳整体性能，mIoU为33.87%，较最强基线提升2.99个百分点；在最小、最易漏检的MAs上增益最大，Dice提升10.06个百分点。消融实验验证了各组件的有效性。

Conclusion: 结果证实了作者假设：空间自适应的状态空间建模有利于微小病灶分割。代码与预训练权重已开源。

Abstract: Diabetic retinopathy (DR) lesions vary widely in shape, and microaneurysms (MAs) span only a few pixels. We hypothesize that modeling the spatial organization of lesion regions, with global context and shape-adaptive scanning, improves the segmentation of such small and irregular lesions. To test this, we propose D$^2$Mamba, which couples non-causal state space duality with deformable Mamba scanning. We evaluate it with five-fold cross-validation on the DDR dataset, comparing against representative segmentation baselines under an identical training protocol, and conduct ablations on each component. D$^2$Mamba achieved the best overall performance, with 33.87% mIoU, 2.99 percentage points above the strongest baseline. The largest gain appeared on MAs, the smallest and most easily missed lesions, where Dice improved by 10.06 percentage points. These results confirm our hypothesis that spatially adaptive state space modeling benefits tiny-lesion segmentation. Code and pretrained weights are available at https://github.com/PUREPANEL1/D2Mamba.

</details>


### [38] [UniWeaver: Harnessing Unified Multimodal Models for Interleaved Text-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13530)
*Hongxu Chen,Zixuan Zhong,Donghao Zhou,Haodong Li,Xiaomin Yu,Tianhao Li,Shiyao Yu,Zhongwei Wan,Pheng-Ann Heng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 UniWeaver，一个结构化、模型无关的"harness"框架，将多模态规划、生成与视觉反馈整合，用于生成连贯的交错图文内容；同时构建 UniWeaver-10K 训练数据集与 UniWeaver-Bench 评测基准，实验显示其在多个统一多模态模型（UMM）骨干上取得 SOTA 表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一多模态模型（UMMs）虽能生成丰富的交错图文内容，但普遍缺乏对文本与图像两种模态的原生协调能力，且将粗粒度用户请求转化为连贯的多模态组合仍需显式规划。现有方法缺少规划—生成—反馈的闭环机制及配套的训练数据与系统评测。

Method: 1）提出 UniWeaver：结构化、模型无关的 harness 框架，串联多模态规划、生成与视觉反馈三个环节，形成闭环以产出连贯交错输出；2）构建 UniWeaver-10K 数据集，覆盖 20 个任务族、共 10,000 个任务，并配有由 UniWeaver 引导的执行轨迹用于训练 UMM；3）提出 UniWeaver-Bench，面向任务的基准，从文本覆盖度、写作质量、视觉覆盖度、图文对齐四个维度进行系统评估。

Result: 实验表明 UniWeaver 达到 SOTA 性能，并在多个 UMM 骨干模型上均提升了交错图文生成质量。

Conclusion: 通过将显式多模态规划、生成与视觉反馈闭环整合，并以配套数据集与基准支撑，UniWeaver 有效弥补了 UMM 在模态协调上的欠缺；其模型无关特性使其具备良好的可迁移性，可跨骨干复用。

Abstract: Unified multimodal models (UMMs) have opened up new possibilities for generating rich interleaved text-image content. However, they generally lack native support for coordinating the two modalities, and translating coarse user requests into coherent multimodal compositions also requires explicit planning. We propose UniWeaver, a structured, model-agnostic harness framework that integrates multimodal planning, generation, and visual feedback to produce coherent interleaved outputs. To train UMMs for this task, we construct UniWeaver-10K, a dataset of 10,000 tasks across 20 task families, paired with execution trajectories guided by UniWeaver. We also introduce UniWeaver-Bench, a task-oriented benchmark systematically assessing text coverage, writing quality, visual coverage, and image-text alignment. Experiments show that UniWeaver achieves state-of-the-art performance and improves interleaved text-image generation across multiple UMM backbones.

</details>


### [39] [AgenticConflictLens: From Long Traffic Videos to Verifiable Conflict Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13540)
*Boshra Khalili,Sonia Moshfeghi,Jinwoo Jang,Andrew W Smyth*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AgenticConflictLens：一种查询条件化的智能体框架，从长时固定摄像头交通视频中高效、可审计地抽取冲突事件，仅检查约9%–12%画面即取得最优冲突检测F1与时间IoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通摄像头持续记录道路使用者行为，可提供事故报告之外的主动安全证据，但冲突、近失、不安全转弯、急刹、人行横道交互等安全关键事件稀疏且短暂，淹没在数小时常规画面中。现有交通视频流水线虽可提取检测、轨迹与风险分数，却对全部视频均匀处理，无法适配分析者的具体查询；视觉语言方法虽提升场景理解，但缺乏交通领域针对参与者级冲突的验证机制。因此需要按查询自适应定位并核验冲突的框架。

Method: 1）以自然语言安全查询为输入，由Traffic-Aware Controller 结合结构化交通记忆与专门的冲突分析工具，决定搜索位置、检查哪些道路使用者、以及验证或拒绝哪些证据；2）采用由粗到细的搜索策略，先检索候选时间区间，再对候选片段有选择地施加精细验证；3）输出含精化时间区间、参与者对、语义区域、轨迹、运动证据、不确定性、拒绝理由与支撑片段的冲突记录，保证可审计性。

Result: 在Bellevue与NYC DOT共96小时标注数据上，冲突存在性F1与时间IoU均为最高：Bellevue达0.84 F1 / 0.77 tIoU，NYC DOT达0.80 F1 / 0.73 tIoU，且仅分别检查8.7%与11.9%的视频内容。

Conclusion: AgenticConflictLens通过查询条件化、由粗到细的智能体搜索与选择性验证，在显著降低视频检查量的同时提升冲突抽取的准确性与可追溯性，支持可扩展、证据可依的城市交通安全分析。

Abstract: Traffic cameras continuously record urban road-user behavior, providing proactive safety evidence beyond crash reports. Yet safety-critical events - conflicts, near misses, unsafe turns, sudden braking, and crosswalk interactions - are rare and brief, buried in hours of routine footage. Existing traffic-video pipelines extract detections, trajectories, and risk scores but process footage uniformly, without adapting to an analyst's query. Vision-language methods improve scene understanding but often lack traffic-specific verification of participant-level conflicts. We introduce AgenticConflictLens, an agentic, query-conditioned framework for efficient, auditable conflict extraction from long fixed-camera traffic videos. Given a natural-language safety query, a Traffic-Aware Controller combines structured traffic memory with specialized conflict-analysis tools to choose where to search, which road users to examine, and which evidence to verify or reject. A coarse-to-fine search retrieves candidate intervals and applies detailed verification selectively. The framework produces conflict records with refined time intervals, participant pairs, semantic regions, trajectories, motion evidence, uncertainty, rejection reasons, and supporting clips. On 96 annotated hours from Bellevue and NYC DOT, AgenticConflictLens achieves the highest conflict-existence F1 and temporal IoU on both datasets: 0.84 F1/0.77 tIoU on Bellevue and 0.80 F1/0.73 tIoU on NYC DOT, while inspecting only 8.7% and 11.9% of video, respectively. These results support scalable, evidence-grounded urban traffic-safety analysis.

</details>


### [40] [PhyRubric: Rubric-Grounded Diagnostic Evaluation of Physical Commonsense in Text-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13576)
*Huiyi Chen,Weitai Kang,Shicheng Fan,Xinshuo Wang,Zhixiang Shen,Yan Yan,Philip S. Yu,Baoxin Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于评分量规（rubric）的T2V物理常识诊断框架PhyRubric，并构建PhyRubric-Bench与PhyRubric-Train，微调出开源评审模型PhyRubric-Judge，其在物理常识评判上与人类判断的相关性优于现有开源评审、媲美前沿闭源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: T2V模型日益被当作"世界模拟器"，需评估其输出是否符合物理常识。但现有基准依赖标注者对每个视频凭直觉打单一总分，评价主观，且无法指出违反了哪条物理原理，缺乏可解释的诊断信息。

Method: 1) PhyRubric：在看到视频之前，先为每条caption构建caption-specific评分量规，将caption分解为语义项与物理项；评分由基于带时间戳证据的item-level判定（verdict）推导得出。2) 实例化为PhyRubric-Bench，覆盖五个物理域共34个类别；3) 扩展为含3,788条视频的PhyRubric-Train，用于微调开源评审模型PhyRubric-Judge。

Result: 在十个T2V模型上的实验表明物理常识仍未解决；PhyRubric-Judge与人类判断的相关性高于现有开源评审模型，并与前沿闭源模型Gemini 4 Argon持平。进一步分析为T2V模型给出了可操作的改进启示。

Conclusion: 以rubric+时间戳证据驱动的细粒度诊断式评估，比单一整体打分更客观、可解释，且可通过训练获得与人类和强闭源模型相当的自动化评审；当前T2V模型的物理常识能力仍有显著差距。

Abstract: As text-to-video (T2V) models are increasingly positioned as world simulators, it is essential to evaluate whether their videos conform to physical commonsense. However, existing benchmarks rely on annotators who rate each video with a single score based on intuition, yielding ratings that are subjective and do not tell which physical principle is violated. We introduce PhyRubric, a rubric-grounded diagnostic framework for evaluating physical commonsense in T2V generation. For each caption, PhyRubric constructs a caption-specific rubric before any video is seen, decomposing the caption into semantic and physical items, and derives scores from item-level verdicts grounded in timestamped evidence. We instantiate this framework as PhyRubric-Bench, which spans 34 categories across five physical domains, and scale it up to PhyRubric-Train with 3,788 videos, on which we fine-tune the open-source PhyRubric-Judge. Empirical results across ten T2V models show that physical commonsense remains far from solved, and that PhyRubric-Judge correlates more closely with human judgments than existing open-source judges while matching the frontier proprietary Gemini 4 Argon. Further analysis provides actionable insights for T2V models.

</details>


### [41] [Lightweight Deep Learning Framework for Astrocyte-Centric 3D Reconstruction across Normal and Stress-associated Ultrastructural States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13581)
*Bowen Deng,Gwang-Jin Jeong,Seulgi Noh,Ji Young Mun,Na Young Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AstroSeg-vEM：一种轻量级、以星形胶质细胞为中心的分割—三维重建—形态定量分析框架，可直接在高分辨率 vEM 切片上运行，在四套小鼠海马数据集上取得 Dice 0.92、加权 Dice 0.95，并可在消费级硬件上高效重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星形胶质细胞形态与其支持、调控神经环路功能的能力密切相关，但基于体电子显微镜（vEM）数据重建其形态仍高度依赖人工且计算资源消耗大；尽管 vEM 具备高空间分辨率，这一瓶颈严重限制了其推广与规模化应用。因此需要一个轻量、可直接处理各向异性 vEM 数据、并兼容下游校对流程的重建方案。

Method: 提出 AstroSeg-vEM 框架：将基于 Transformer 的二维分割与专用后处理方法相结合，直接作用于高分辨率 EM 切片，保留原始 EM 数据的完整面内分辨率，从而适配各向异性 vEM 数据与下游人工校对工作流，并支持三维重建与定量形态学分析。

Result: 在四套代表正常及形态学定义的压力相关星形胶质细胞超微结构状态的小鼠海马数据集上，框架表现出稳健性能与泛化能力；在留出测试集上平均 Dice 为 0.92、加权 Dice 为 0.95。其轻量架构可在消费级硬件上实现高效、高保真重建。

Conclusion: AstroSeg-vEM 为大规模星形胶质细胞超微结构分析及未来星形胶质细胞—神经元相互作用研究提供了可扩展、易获取的平台，降低了 vEM 形态重建在人工与算力上的门槛。

Abstract: The morphology of astrocytes is closely associated with their ability to support and modulate neural circuit functions; however, its reconstruction from volume electron microscopy (vEM) data remains labor-intensive and computationally resource-intensive, placing substantial barriers on its broader adoption and scalability despite the high spatial resolution of vEM. We present AstroSeg-vEM, a lightweight, astrocyte-centric framework for morphology-preserving astrocyte segmentation, 3D reconstruction, and quantitative morphological analysis in vEM images. AstroSeg-vEM combines transformer-based 2D segmentation with dedicated post-processing methods and operates directly on high-resolution EM sections, making it well suited to anisotropic vEM data and downstream proofreading workflows while preserving the full in-plane resolution of the original EM data. Across four mouse hippocampal datasets representing normal and morphologically defined stress-associated astrocyte ultrastructural states, AstroSeg-vEM demonstrates robust performance and generalization, achieving average Dice and weighted Dice values of 0.92 and 0.95, respectively, on a held-out test dataset. Its lightweight architecture enables efficient, high-fidelity reconstruction on consumer-grade hardware, providing a scalable and accessible platform for large-scale astrocyte ultrastructure analysis and future astrocyte-neuron interaction studies.

</details>


### [42] [When to Start Denoising? Adaptive Denoising Entry for Generative Video Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13620)
*Zifu Zhang,Yuqiao Li,Ding Ding,Shengxi Li,Mai Xu,Daowen Li,Kai Li,Ying Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ADE-GVC，首个感知时间步的生成式视频压缩框架，通过轻量预测器自适应选择扩散去噪起始时间步，并结合时序适配器注入压缩感知条件，在低码率下兼顾保真度与感知真实感，取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散式生成视频压缩性能依赖“压缩潜变量何时注入扩散过程”，该问题直接决定重建保真度与感知真实感之间的权衡，而现有方法缺少对去噪起始时间步的显式建模与选择。

Method: 将去噪起始时间步选择建模为几何优化问题；设计轻量预测器从压缩潜变量估计自适应去噪起点；引入时序适配器提取压缩感知条件并注入预训练视频扩散模型。

Result: 观察到预测时间步与原始/压缩潜变量间失真存在强定量关系，为优化建模提供实证支撑；实验中感知指标达SOTA，相对最新开源基线GLC-Video平均节省码率40.99%（DISTS）和95.24%（PSNR）。

Conclusion: 自适应扩散入口是提升生成式视频压缩的关键机制，可在极低码率下显著提升重建真实感并大幅降低码率。

Abstract: Generative video compression offers a promising solution for compressing and reconstructing videos under extremely low bitrate constraints, among which the diffusion-based solutions still achieve the best performance by providing the powerful generative latent prior. However, their effectiveness critically depends on when the compressed latent is introduced into the diffusion process, which directly governs the trade-off between reconstruction fidelity and perceptual realism. To address this limitation, we identify denoising start-timestep selection as a key factor governing this trade-off and formulate it as a geometric optimization problem. Based on this formulation, we propose a novel adaptive diffusion entry method for generative video compression (ADE-GVC), the first timestep-aware generative video compression framework, featuring a lightweight predictor that estimates an adaptive denoising start point from the compressed latent. A temporal adapter further extracts compression-aware conditions and injects them into a pretrained video diffusion model, enabling the reconstruction of videos with both high fidelity and realism. More importantly, we observe a strong quantitative relationship between the predicted timesteps and the distortion between the original and compressed latents, providing empirical support for our optimization formulation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ADE-GVC achieves the state-of-the-art performance on perceptual metrics and substantially improves reconstruction realism over existing generative video compression methods. Notably, our ADE-GVC achieves an average bitrate savings of 40.99\% (on DISTS) and 95.24\% (on PSNR) compared to the latest open-source baseline GLC-Video.

</details>


### [43] [PUMA: Probability-Constrained Sequential Interaction for Forensic Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13621)
*Zhouli Yu,Mengyuan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PUMA框架，将空间约束的非平衡最优传输与逐位置凸选择相结合，用于图像篡改定位中的语义-取证证据融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义-取证融合方法虽提供互补表征，但未在空间对应过程中显式保留质量失配信息，也未自适应选择跨分支信息传播的顺序；空间对应、匹配质量与交互顺序的作用常被混为一谈。

Method: PUMA（Probability-constrained Unbalanced Matching and Aggregation）：(1) 采用空间约束的非平衡最优传输；(2) 归一化前保留带符号的传输质量残差；(3) 构建行随机的跨分支算子与噪声分支内算子；(4) 通过共享位置接口在两个顺序算子间做逐位置凸选择；(5) 以门控残差路径更新噪声特征。

Result: 在四个测试集上取得较强定位性能，COVERAGE与NIST16上的分数超过对比中代表性方法的已发表结果。

Conclusion: 实验支持局部匹配状态与自适应顺序交互对跨源定位的有效性，并在多种后处理条件下表现稳健。

Abstract: Image manipulation localization must jointly exploit semantic structure and noise-sensitive forensic evidence. However, the effects of spatial correspondence, matching quality, and interaction order are often conflated. Existing semantic-forensic fusion methods provide complementary representations, but do not explicitly preserve quality-mismatch information during spatial correspondence or adaptively select the order in which information is propagated across branches. We propose PUMA (Probability-constrained Unbalanced Matching and Aggregation), which combines spatially constrained unbalanced optimal transport with position-wise convex selection between two sequential operators through a shared positional interface. Before normalization, PUMA retains signed transport-mass residuals, constructs row-stochastic cross-branch and within-noise-branch operators, and updates noise features through a gated residual path. PUMA achieves strong localization results on four test sets, with scores on COVERAGE and NIST16 exceeding the published results of the representative methods listed in our comparison. The experiments support the utility of local matching states and adaptive sequential interaction for cross-source localization and several post-processing conditions.

</details>


### [44] [FlashMotion: Ultra-Fast Long-Term 3D Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13626)
*Kaixing Yang,Xulong Tang,Ronghui Li,Xiangyue Zhang,Ziqiao Peng,Mingshuang Luo,Chenxi Zhao,Haoyu Wu,Ju Fan,Hongyan Liu,Puwei Wang,Zijiao Zeng,Jun He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlashMotion：面向严格推理预算的高质量长时程3D人体运动生成协同设计框架，通过3D运动空间MeanFlow少步生成与BiMamba轻量非自回归架构，在文本到运动、音乐到舞蹈、语音到手势三类任务上同时取得质量与效率的SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D人体运动生成广泛用于游戏开发、XR、数字人与具身仿真，实际流水线含绑定、重定向与渲染，因此实时交互应用需要不牺牲质量超高速长时程运动生成。现有方法存在两个耦合瓶颈：生成策略需大量采样步数；模型架构在长序列上每步计算与内存开销大，导致严格推理预算下的高质量长时程生成仍困难。

Method: 提出协同设计框架FlashMotion。轨迹级效率：引入3D运动空间MeanFlow公式，配以运动学与动力学一致性约束，实现少步生成；并提出自适应软掩码（Adaptive Soft masking）机制用于时间连贯的运动编辑。单步效率：设计轻量非自回归BiMamba架构，采用条件引导的状态选择与基于AdaIN的条件融合机制，提升跨模态对齐与对更长序列的泛化能力。统一覆盖文本到运动、音乐到舞蹈、语音到手势任务。

Result: 大量实验表明，FlashMotion在文本到运动、语音到手势、音乐到舞蹈生成上，于质量与效率两方面均取得整体最先进（SOTA）性能。项目主页：https://sun-happy-ykx.github.io/FlashMotion-Project/

Conclusion: 通过生成策略（少步MeanFlow）与模型架构（轻量BiMamba）的协同设计，可在严格推理预算下实现高质量长时程3D运动生成，为实时交互式应用提供可行方案。

Abstract: 3D human motion generation plays a crucial role in game development, extended reality (XR), digital humans, and embodied simulation. Beyond motion generation, practical pipelines include rigging, retargeting, and rendering, making ultra-fast long-term 3D motion generation without compromising quality valuable for real-time interactive applications. Despite promising progress, high-quality long-horizon motion generation under tight inference budgets remains challenging, owing to 2 coupled bottlenecks: (1) generative strategies that require many sampling steps for high-quality 3D motion generation and (2) model architectures with substantial per-step computational and memory costs for long sequences. We present FlashMotion, a co-designed framework for high-quality long-horizon 3D motion generation under a strict inference budget, covering text-to-motion, music-to-dance, and speech-to-gesture tasks. To enhance trajectory-level efficiency, we introduce a 3D motion-space MeanFlow formulation with kinematic and dynamic consistency constraints for few-step generation, together with an Adaptive Soft masking mechanism for temporally coherent motion editing. To improve per-step efficiency, we design a lightweight non-autoregressive BiMamba architecture with condition-guided state selection and AdaIN-based condition fusion mechanism, enhancing cross-modal alignment and generalization to longer motion sequences. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FlashMotion achieves overall state-of-the-art performance in both quality and efficiency across text-to-motion, speech-to-gesture, and music-to-dance generation. The project page is available at https://sun-happy-ykx.github.io/FlashMotion-Project/

</details>


### [45] [DepthGS: Depth-Guided Gaussian Splatting with Adaptive Regularization for High-Quality Surface Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13628)
*Xiao Ren,Yu Liu,Ning An,Jian Cheng,Xin Qiao,You-Fu Li,He Kong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DepthGS：通过推导深度差异与重建误差间的理论关系与误差上界，设计单视图法向自适应正则与多视图跨视深度一致性正则，在DTU、Mip-NeRF360、Tanks and Temples上取得竞争性表面重建与新视图合成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS已扩展至表面重建，但深度信息与重建误差之间的关系缺乏系统探讨，导致几何优化策略的设计缺少理论依据。

Method: 分别在单视图与多视图设定下推导深度差异与重建误差的理论关系，并严格建立重建误差上界；据此设计自适应正则：单视图用由局部深度差计算的几何可靠性权重调节法向约束强度；多视图引入跨视深度一致性正则，约束相邻视图深度图与当前视图深度预测经变换投影所得深度之间的一致性。

Result: 在DTU、Mip-NeRF360、Tanks and Temples数据集上，DepthGS在表面重建与新视图合成两方面均达到有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 理论分析与自适应正则设计有效桥接了深度信息与几何优化，为深度引导的3DGS表面重建提供了可解释的优化框架。

Abstract: Recently, 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has emerged as an efficient representation for novel view synthesis, offering fast differentiable rendering and high visual fidelity, and has increasingly been extended to surface reconstruction. However, the relationship between depth information and reconstruction error remains insufficiently explored, limiting the principled design of geometric optimization strategies. To fill the above gap, we propose DepthGS, a depth-guided Gaussian splatting framework. We derive theoretical relations between depth discrepancies and reconstruction error under single-view and multi-view settings, and rigorously establish corresponding upper bounds on reconstruction error. The above analysis informs the design of adaptive regularization strategies in both single-view and multi-view optimization. More specifically, for single-view optimization, we propose a depth-difference-guided adaptive normal regularization strategy that modulates the normal constraint strength using a geometric reliability weight computed from local depth differences. For multi-view optimization, we introduce a cross-view depth consistency regularization strategy that promotes consistency between neighboring-view depth maps and the corresponding depth predictions obtained by transforming the current view's depth predictions into those neighboring views. Extensive experiments on DTU, Mip-NeRF360, and Tanks and Temples demonstrate that DepthGS achieves competitive performance in both surface reconstruction and novel view synthesis.

</details>


### [46] [picoEmergencyNet : Attention-Enhanced Ultra-Small Deep Learning Model for Aerial Image Classification in Emergency Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13638)
*Christos Kyrkou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出超轻量卷积网络 picoEmergencyNet（23k 参数、7M FLOPs、0.1MB 存储），在 AIDER/AIDERv2 上达 96.1% 准确率，Jetson AGX Orin 上 GPU 258 FPS、CPU 46 FPS，激活内存降低 7 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机航拍灾害识别需在严格的内存、算力与延迟约束下运行，现有高精度模型计算开销大，难以部署于资源受限的 UAV 平台。

Method: 构建超轻量 CNN：以深度可分离空洞卷积实现多尺度特征提取，结合高效通道注意力和通道混洗（channel shuffling），在极低计算成本下完成特征表达。

Result: 模型仅 23k 参数、7M FLOPs、0.1MB 存储；AIDER 与 AIDERv2 上准确率达 96.1%，与体量大数个数量级的 SOTA 轻量模型持平或更优；未做硬件特定优化时，Jetson AGX Orin 上 GPU 258 FPS、CPU 46 FPS，激活内存占用较对比模型低 7 倍。

Conclusion: 精细的架构专门化设计（而非模型缩放）即可实现高效准确的 TinyML 级灾害识别，满足 UAV 实时应急监测需求。

Abstract: Real-time disaster recognition from aerial imagery requires deep learning models that operate under strict memory, compute, and latency constraints. While recent approaches achieve high accuracy, their computational cost limits deployment on resource-constrained UAV platforms. This work introduces picoEmergencyNet, an ultra-lightweight convolutional neural network designed for aerial image disaster classification. The proposed architecture combines depthwise dilated convolutions with efficient channel attention and channel shuffling to enable multi-scale feature extraction at minimal computational cost. picoEmergencyNet has 23k parameters and 7M FLOPs, requiring only 0.1 MB of model storage. Evaluated on the AIDER and AIDERv2 disaster datasets, it achieves up to 96.1% accuracy, matching or exceeding state-of-the-art lightweight models that are orders of magnitude larger. Deployment experiments on the NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin show that, without hardware-specific optimizations, picoEmergencyNet achieves 258 FPS on GPU and 46 FPS on CPU with a 7x lower activation memory footprint compared to competing models. These results demonstrate that careful architectural specialization, rather than model scaling, enables highly efficient and accurate TinyML-ready disaster recognition for real-time UAV-based emergency monitoring.

</details>


### [47] [Effects of Laser Beam Width and Measurement Noise on Tree Diameter Estimation in Laser Scanning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13739)
*Jesse Muhojoki,Nuutti Ervasti,Eric Hyyppä,Aapo Erkkilä,Eppu Honkanen,Tuomas Yrttimaa,Mikko Vastaranta,Juha Hyyppä*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究移动激光扫描(MLS)中测距与扫描角噪声的异方差性及光束宽度导致的胸径估计偏差，并提出基于传感器技术参数的偏差补偿方法，可将胸径估计的RMSE降至亚厘米级，但直径<10 cm的小树仍受限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激光扫描虽广泛用于树木结构与几何测量，但扫描仪特性与测量条件带来的不确定性仍是重要误差源，尤其激光光束直径会引入系统性和随机误差；此前对MLS中这些误差的主要来源及其对树干曲线（直径）估计的定量影响缺乏系统研究。

Method: 通过仿真与实测实验，分析MLS测量噪声中的异方差性（测距与扫描角方差差异）与激光雷达光束宽度的影响；建立可预测的直径偏差模型，并利用传感器技术规格（无需额外标定）进行偏差补偿，在北方森林试验样地进行验证。

Result: 直径估计偏差不仅依赖距离，还依赖树木直径；所提补偿方法可可靠预测并抵消该偏差，使胸径>10 cm树木的胸径估计RMSE降至亚厘米级；而直径<10 cm的小树，直径偏差对树径这一未知因素及树皮差异、树干不圆度等敏感，限制了精度与方法适用性。

Conclusion: 测量噪声的异方差性与光束宽度是MLS树干直径估计偏差的关键因素；基于传感器参数的偏差补偿对中大型树木有效且无需标定，但小径木测量仍受树径未知性与形态因素制约，需进一步研究。

Abstract: Laser scanning has been utilized for decades to measure tree structure and geometry. Despite continuous advances in sensor technology, uncertainties arising from scanner characteristics and measurement conditions has remained relevant sources of error. In particular, factors such as laser beam diameter can introduce both systematic and random errors in the reconstructed tree geometry. This study investigates the main sources of these errors in mobile laser scanning (MLS) and quantifies their effects on stem curve (stem diameter) estimation. We discovered through simulations and experiments that the heteroscedasticity (difference in variance in ranging and scan angle) in the measurement noise is a key factor along with the beam width of the lidar, which cause the bias of the diameter estimates to depend not only on distance, but also on the tree diameter. We demonstrated that this bias can be reliably predicted and negated for trees with a diameter at the breast height greater than 10 cm using technical specifications of the sensor without any additional calibration. In experiments, the proposed bias compensation method reduced the root-mean-square error of stem diameter estimates to sub-centimeter level on boreal forest test sites. However, for small trees with diameter below 10 cm, the diameter bias becomes sensitive to the diameter of the tree$\unicode{x2013}$an unknown factor$\unicode{x2013}$and other factors, such as different bark and out-of-roundness of the stem, limiting the precision of lidar for measuring geometry of trees and applicability of the presented method.

</details>


### [48] [Exploring Synchrony for Communication in Modular Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13759)
*Nikolas Willems,Qiyang Sun,Björn W. Schuller*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出SyncNet：一种将模块内容与动态相位状态分离的模块化架构，通过可学习的Kuramoto动力学演化相位，并由相位对齐决定共享信道中的通信；在SQOOP上系统性泛化最优，但消融显示收益主要来自模块分解而非同步机制本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模块化网络意在将计算分配到学习不同可复用功能的组件中，从而支持组合泛化；但实践中模块结构本身并不能保证这些角色形成，且模块间无限制通信会削弱已出现的专门化。因此核心问题是：模块间通信应如何组织。作者将这一问题类比为知觉绑定问题，其中同步性被视为协调分布式表征的机制。

Method: SyncNet将模块内容与动态相位状态分离：由可学习的Kuramoto动力学演化各模块相位，相位对齐程度决定通过共享信道的通信（即一种基于相位寻址的路由）。在Sort-of-CLEVR（关系推理）与SQOOP（系统性泛化，配对多样性越低则测试期需更多重组）上评测；并进行测试时干预（固定相位、置换模块-区域对应）与内容寻址变体的从头训练对照。

Result: 在SQOOP三个最低配对多样性设置上取得所比较模型中最佳留出准确率，最难设置上高出5.1个百分点；在Sort-of-CLEVR上有竞争力，但低于最强的注意力基线。测试时覆盖实验表明性能依赖于演化中的相位：固定相位造成的损失相当于完全不做寻址，而置换模块所承载区域同样接近有害。然而，从头训练的内容寻址变体达到同等性能。

Conclusion: 相位同步式通信在低配对多样性、需强重组的情境下有效，但消融表明收益主要源于模块化分解本身，而非同步性；这提示模块间通信的组织方式（以及模块化本身）比动态相位机制更关键，也提示需设计能区分同步贡献的对照实验。

Abstract: One motivation for modular neural networks is to divide computation across components that learn distinct, reusable functions, which could then be recombined to support compositional generalisation. In practice, modular structure alone does not guarantee that such roles develop, and unrestricted communication between modules can undermine any specialisation that does emerge. This raises the question of how modular communication should be organised. The problem can be viewed as an analogue of the perceptual binding problem, where synchrony has been proposed as a mechanism for coordinating distributed representations. We introduce SyncNet, a modular architecture that separates module content from a dynamical phase state: learnt Kuramoto dynamics evolve the phases, while phase alignment determines communication through a shared channel. We evaluate SyncNet on Sort-of-CLEVR for relational reasoning and on SQOOP for systematic generalisation, where lower pair variety demands more recombination at test time. SyncNet gives the best held-out accuracy of the models compared at the three lowest pair varieties, by 5.1 percentage points at the hardest, and is competitive on Sort-of-CLEVR though below the strongest attention baselines. Test-time overrides show that performance depends on phases that evolve: fixed phases cost as much as no addressing at all, and permuting which module holds which region is nearly as damaging. A content-addressed variant trained from scratch nonetheless matches it, so the benefit appears to come from the modular decomposition rather than synchrony itself.

</details>


### [49] [RF3DGS: Reflection-Free 3D Gaussian Splatting with Self-Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13772)
*Qiang Wen,Guotao Meng,Junhong Ruan,Hongyu Liu,Chenyang Lei,Qifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 RF3DGS，一种基于 3D 高斯泼溅的无反射（去除反射）重建方法，仅依赖"同一点多视角最小像素强度≈无反射信息"这一光度先验，实现合成与真实场景下的 SOTA 去反射效果，并构建首个基于 Unity 的 3D 去反射基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D 反射去除对博物馆展陈等高保真可视化应用至关重要。现有方法多依赖强假设或额外监督，缺乏将常用光度先验有效扩展到多视角 3D 高斯表示中的手段，同时缺少统一的定量评测基准。

Method: 1) 先用几何先验优化原始 3DGS，为每个视角估计粗略的无反射深度图；2) 结合深度图与光度先验，通过 3D warping 从辅助视角聚合无反射信息，为参考视角构建干净的透射伪标签，作为自监督信号；3) 提出自适应孪生渲染（ATR）损失，在利用伪标签的同时保留原始含反射图像的高频细节；4) 将内部 3D 自先验与 ATR 损失融合。

Result: 在合成与真实场景中均取得 SOTA 的去反射性能；并基于 Unity 渲染引擎建立了首个 3D 反射去除基准，用于严格的定量评测。

Conclusion: RF3DGS 证明了单一光度先验经合理的深度先验与多视角 warping 设计后，可有效驱动 3DGS 完成无反射重建；ATR 损失与内部自先验的融合兼顾了伪标签鲁棒性与细节保真；所提基准填补了 3D 去反射定量评测的空白。

Abstract: 3D reflection removal is crucial in applications demanding high-fidelity visualization, such as museum exhibitions. In this work, we propose Reflection-Free 3D Gaussian Splatting (RF3DGS), a novel method for superior reflection removal. RF3DGS exclusively leverages a widely-adopted photometric prior for reflection removal: that the minimum pixel intensity across multi-view images corresponding to the same 3D point strongly correlates with reflection-free information. However, generalizing this principle to a 3D Gaussian Splatting within a multi-view context is non-trivial. To address this, RF3DGS first optimizes a vanilla 3DGS model using a geometric prior to estimate a coarse, reflection-free depth map for each view. Leveraging these depth maps and photometric prior, a 3D warping process aggregates reflection-free information from auxiliary views to construct a clean transmission pseudo-label for a given reference view. This pseudo-label provides a robust self-supervised signal for optimization. We further introduce an Adaptive Twin Rendering (ATR) loss to synergistically utilize this pseudo-label while preserving high-frequency details from the original, reflection-degraded images. By adeptly fusing these internal 3D self-priors with the ATR loss, RF3DGS demonstrates state-of-the-art reflection removal performance across both synthetic and real-world scenarios. Furthermore, we establish the first 3D reflection removal benchmark, based on the Unity rendering engine, for rigorous quantitative evaluation.

</details>


### [50] [DisasterAgent: An Evidence-Driven Agent for Verifiable Disaster Report Generation From Remote Sensing Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13776)
*Zhijian Li,Jiawei Song,Chao Ren,Yidan Zhang,Peijin Wang,Xian Sun,Kun Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 DisasterAgent，一个基于“感知-增强-生成”流程的证据驱动智能体，从双时相光学影像生成可验证的灾害报告，并构建时空感知图与内联引用形成闭环证据链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感灾害评估正从简单损伤分类转向报告级综合分析，应急响应者不仅需要损伤描述，还需要可核验的证据。现有视觉语言模型与工具增强智能体虽具备多模态推理与感知能力，但在高风险场景下难以提供可验证证据。

Method: 采用 Perception-Augmentation-Generation 工作流：感知阶段调用专用视觉专家工具集构建时空感知图（STPG）作为结构化记忆，组织灾害引起的时空变化；随后通过证据增强（EA）从 STPG 中抽取证据，并为每条陈述附加内联引用，形成从陈述到视觉证据的闭环。

Result: 在 DisasterM3 与 RSCC 基准上，DisasterAgent 稳定优于通用视觉语言模型、遥感专用模型及现有遥感智能体；DisasterM3 灾害报告生成平均分提升 13.5%，RSCC 的 ST5-SCS 提升 15.34%。

Conclusion: 证据驱动的感知-增强-生成智能体能够同时提升灾害报告生成的准确性与可验证性，为灾害应急响应提供更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Remote sensing disaster assessment is moving from simple damage classification toward comprehensive report-level analysis. In this setting, responders need not only damage descriptions but also verifiable evidence to guide response and recovery. Recent vision-language models (VLMs) and tool-augmented agents offer strong multimodal reasoning and perception, but they struggle to provide the evidence required for high-stakes verification. To address these limitations, we propose DisasterAgent, an evidence-driven agent that uses a Perception-Augmentation-Generation workflow to generate verifiable disaster reports from bitemporal optical imagery. DisasterAgent first invokes a specialized vision expert toolkit in the perception phase to build a Spatiotemporal Perception Graph (STPG), serving as a structured memory to store and organize disaster-induced changes across space and time. Evidence-based Augmentation (EA) then extracts evidence from the STPG, attaching inline citations that form a closed evidence loop from each statement to the supporting visual evidence. Extensive experiments on the DisasterM3 and RSCC benchmarks demonstrate that DisasterAgent consistently outperforms general-domain VLMs, remote sensing-specialized models, and existing remote sensing agents. Notably, compared with the original model, it improves the average score for disaster report generation on DisasterM3 by 13.5\% and ST5-SCS on RSCC by 15.34\%, providing a more accurate and verifiable solution for disaster emergency response. The code will be available at https://github.com/ZhijianLi2003/DisasterAgent.

</details>


### [51] [Persistent Metric-Semantic Maritime Scene Memory from Open-Vocabulary 3D Scene Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13903)
*Kim Alexander Christensen,Dina Zimmermann,Martin Steinert,Eleni Kelasidi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究室外开放词汇三维场景图（3DSG）方法向海洋环境迁移的可行性与所需适配，通过对地图证据进行度量-语义合并与多视角VLM推理、并构建海洋宏观区域，获得抗碎片化、可跨重复航次对应的持久海洋场景记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DSG 可为机器人提供持久度量-语义表示以支持空间推理，但现有方法主要面向室内与陆地环境。作者希望考察代表性室外开放词汇方法在海洋环境中的迁移表现及其适配需求。

Method: 保留原有建图方法但对其累积证据做后处理：先用度量-语义合并（geometry-semantic consolidation）整合碎片，再以几何约束的多视角视觉-语言模型（VLM）推理将残余碎片调和为物理实体，并将实体按观测归并为局部场所；同时利用地图先验与船载水面/结构观测构建海洋宏观区域，并与局部场所关联。

Result: 零样本室外3DSG描述在多数由几何支撑的物体上与标注真值一致，但地图存在明显物理实体碎片化，且陆地式高层假设无法刻画海洋空间组织；所提表示在降低实体碎片化方面优于基线及仅用几何-语义合并的版本，支持固定局部场所在重复航次间的对应，并在经审阅的查询上提升上下文检索效果。

Conclusion: 室外开放词汇地图可借助本文的物体/场所身份认定与空间抽象方法，被适配为持久的海洋场景记忆。

Abstract: Three-dimensional scene graphs (3DSGs) can provide robots with persistent metric-semantic representations for spatial reasoning, but the existing methods have primarily been developed for indoor and terrestrial environments. In this paper we investigate how a representative outdoor open-vocabulary method transfers to maritime environments and which adaptations are needed. Our experiments show that zero-shot representative outdoor 3DSG descriptions agree with our labeled ground truth for a majority of geometry-supported objects on our data, but the resulting map exhibits considerable physical-entity fragmentation, and its terrestrial higher-level assumptions don't capture maritime spatial organization needs. We therefore keep the underlying mapping method and post-process its accumulated evidence. We show a metric-semantic consolidation method followed by geometry-grounded multi-view vision-language model (VLM) reasoning that reconciles remaining fragments into physical entities and groups entities through observations into local places. In parallel, we construct maritime macro regions using map priors and onboard water and structure observations, and associate them with the local places. The resulting representation reduces physical-entity fragmentation beyond both the baseline and our geometry-semantic consolidation component alone, supports correspondence of fixed local places across repeated maritime traversals, and improves contextual retrieval on our reviewed queries over tested baselines. These results show how an outdoor open-vocabulary map can be adapted into persistent maritime scene memory with our methods for object/place identity and spatial abstractions.

</details>


### [52] [Marrying Capacity and Precision for Efficient 2D Gaussian Image Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13784)
*Lin Dong,Qi Ming,Liuqian Wang,Wentao Chao,Yufei Gao,Chengming Liu,Lei Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CP-GS 提出容量—精度耦合的2D高斯泼溅框架，在统一码率约束下联合优化高斯数量（容量）分配与属性量化精度，以提升有限预算下的率失真性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2D高斯表示在有限预算下的率失真性能高度依赖高斯容量与参数精度这两类资源的分配，但现有方法通常将二者分开优化，忽略了内容相关的高斯容量需求以及不同属性间异质的量化敏感性。

Method: 1）内容自适应高斯表示：将固定高斯预算从冗余区域动态重分配到表示需求更高的区域；2）层次化失真感知量化：通过高效的基于渲染的校准，在属性间分配精度，并在异质属性组内进一步细化；3）联合优化：构建不同高斯数量与精度配置的候选表示，依据解码失真与实际序列化大小选择工作点。

Result: 在 DIV2K 与 Kodak 数据集上，CP-GS 在多种预算下相较代表性 2D 高斯方法与隐式神经表示取得更优的率失真性能，同时保持固定总表示预算与较低的优化开销。

Conclusion: 联合优化空间容量与参数精度对紧凑高斯图像表示具有明显收益，验证了容量—精度耦合建模的有效性。

Abstract: 2D Gaussian representations provide an explicit and efficient way to represent images, but their rate-distortion performance under limited budgets depends critically on how Gaussian capacity and parameter precision are allocated. Existing methods typically optimize these two resources separately, overlooking content-dependent capacity demands and heterogeneous quantization sensitivity. We present CP-GS, a capacity-precision coupled Gaussian splatting framework for budget-aware 2D Gaussian image representation that jointly optimizes capacity allocation and attribute precision under a shared rate constraint. CP-GS first constructs a content-adaptive Gaussian representation by dynamically reallocating a fixed Gaussian budget from redundant regions to areas with higher representation demand. It then introduces hierarchical distortion-aware representation quantization, using efficient rendering-based calibration to allocate precision across Gaussian attributes and further refine it within heterogeneous attribute groups. Finally, it jointly optimizes Gaussian capacity and attribute precision by constructing candidate representations with different Gaussian counts and precision configurations and selecting operating points according to decoded distortion and actual serialized size. Experiments on DIV2K and Kodak show that CP-GS achieves improved rate-distortion performance over representative 2D Gaussian and implicit neural representations across a range of budgets, while maintaining a fixed overall representation budget and low optimization cost. These results demonstrate the benefit of jointly optimizing spatial capacity and parameter precision for compact Gaussian image representation.

</details>


### [53] [HiPFuse: Real-Time LiDAR Semantic Segmentation for Robotic Perception on Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14025)
*Haiyang Wu,George Vosselman,Ville Lehtola*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HiPFuse点-BEV融合框架，在有限资源下实现实时准确的LiDAR语义感知，在Jetson AGX Orin上超过10Hz扫描率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人实时感知需要在有限算力下获得准确的LiDAR语义。BEV聚合虽高效，但同一柱体内的不同标注表面会被混合，导致垂直方向的语义混淆。

Method: 单帧LiDAR扫描下，在多个水平尺度间交换点特征与高度分区的BEV特征，融合逐点细节与2D空间上下文；采用固定高度划分保持相互独立的垂直聚合组，并用同一3D体素单元的目标对初始BEV特征施加辅助监督；引入双尺度BEV上下文模块，从更粗网格补充更大范围上下文。

Result: SemanticKITTI和MCD上分别达63.7%和62.5% mIoU，在Jetson AGX Orin上为14.2和22.4 FPS，均超过10Hz扫描率；独立训练与评估显示室内可用性；仅在固定机器人系统中替换基线语义前端，两个室外序列上里程计绝对轨迹误差RMSE平均降低10.6%，SemanticKITTI上接受回环召回率提升8.7个百分点；定性语义地图显示道路标注更连续。

Conclusion: 高度分区+点-BEV多尺度融合可在资源受限平台上兼顾精度与实时性，并作为语义前端直接提升下游里程计与回环检测性能。

Abstract: Real-time robotic perception requires accurate LiDAR semantics under limited resources. Bird's-eye-view (BEV) aggregation is efficient but can mix differently labeled surfaces in columns. We present HiPFuse, a point-BEV fusion framework that exchanges point and height-partitioned BEV features across horizontal scales, combining point-wise detail with 2D spatial context from one LiDAR scan. Fixed height assignments preserve separate vertical aggregation groups, with auxiliary supervision of initial BEV features using targets from the same 3D cells. A dual-scale BEV context module adds broader context from a coarser grid. On SemanticKITTI and MCD, HiPFuse achieves 63.7% and 62.5% mIoU at 14.2 and 22.4 FPS on Jetson AGX Orin, respectively, exceeding the 10 Hz scan rate. Separate training and evaluation show indoor applicability. Replacing only the baseline semantic front end with HiPFuse in fixed robotic systems reduces odometry absolute trajectory error RMSE by 10.6% on average across two outdoor sequences and increases accepted-loop recall by 8.7 percentage points on SemanticKITTI. Qualitative semantic maps show more continuous road labeling. Our code is available at https://github.com/haiyang2022/HiPFuse.

</details>


### [54] [The Target Is a Predicate: Predicate-State Grounding for Semantic-Level Video Reasoning Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13785)
*Yuhuan Hong,Yiming Ren,Zhiqiang Lin,Feize Wu,Xingyun Liu,Yitong Xie,Lei Zhou,Lisheng Wu,Junjie Wang,Yujiu Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出谓词—状态视频推理分割（PS-VRS）新任务，构建 PS-Bench 基准，并提出 Predicate-State Grounding（PSG）框架，通过“状态—绑定—发射”流水线显式建模谓词激活/空缺状态，仅在激活帧输出掩码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频推理分割将目标视为一旦识别便永久存在，导致目标空缺区间过度输出掩码（假阳性），且无法处理目标切换；查询本质是对物体与时间的动态谓词 P(o,t)，而非固定物体身份。

Method: 构建 PS-Bench：2,077 个视频、16,250 条查询，标注显式激活/空缺谓词状态、时间区间与像素掩码。提出 PSG 三阶段 State-Bind-Emit 流水线：(1) 预测 [ACTIVE]/[VACANT] 状态 token；(2) 通过稠密跨模态相似度将状态对齐到视频帧；(3) 仅对激活状态用 SAM2 发射掩码。

Result: 在 PS-Bench 上，PSG 相比现有基线提升 +9.32 J&F，并显著降低假阳性掩码发射。

Conclusion: 显式谓词状态建模对具有动态视觉见证的视频推理分割是有效的，状态感知的发射机制可同时提升精度并抑制空洞区间的错误输出。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce Predicate-State Video Reasoning Segmentation (PS-VRS), where a query defines a dynamic predicate $P(o,t)$ over objects and time rather than a fixed object identity. Existing methods typically treat the target as permanently active once identified, leading to over-emission of masks during vacant intervals and failure to handle target switching. To address this, we construct PS-Bench, a large-scale benchmark with 2,077 videos and 16,250 queries annotated with explicit active/vacant predicate states, temporal intervals, and pixel masks. We further propose Predicate-State Grounding (PSG), a State-Bind-Emit pipeline that: (1) predicts [ACTIVE]/[VACANT] state tokens; (2) aligns them to video frames via dense cross-modal similarity; and (3) emits masks via SAM2 only for active states. Experiments show that PSG outperforms existing baselines by +9.32 J&F on PS-Bench and significantly reduces false-positive mask emissions. Our results demonstrate the effectiveness of explicit predicate-state modeling for video reasoning segmentation with dynamic visual witnesses.

</details>


### [55] [Relaxing Cross-Modal Correspondence for Multimodal Remote Sensing Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13807)
*Xianping Ma,Yu Zhang,Mingzhe Jiang,Wanxin Xie,Qing Zhu,Nan Xu,Xiaokang Zhang,Yuanxin Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文挑战"多模态预训练必须依赖严格实例级配对"的假设，将跨模态对应关系从实例级放宽到语义级，提出SemPair（类别一致的跨实例配对）与MixPair（语义/精确配对的连续插值），实验表明SemPair可达到与精确配对相当的可迁移性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态遥感预训练通常默认严格要求模态间实例级精确对应，把严格配对观测视为学习可迁移表示的前提。这一假设成本高昂（获取实例对齐数据困难），且其必要性缺乏检验，因此作者希望重新审视该前提。

Method: 1）提出SemPair：用类别一致的跨实例配对替代精确实例配对，实现配对关系从实例级到语义级的放宽；2）构建隐因子模型，将跨模态依赖分解为共享语义结构与实例特有相关性，理论上证明SemPair在期望意义下保留前者、边缘化后者；3）提出MixPair，在语义配对与精确配对之间做受控插值，以连续谱方式研究对应粒度的影响；4）在多组遥感下游任务上开展迁移实验。

Result: SemPair在迁移性能上与精确配对（ExaPair）相当；MixPair揭示下游迁移性能与实例配对比例之间呈非单调关系，说明对应粒度是关键设计维度。

Conclusion: 精确实例对应并非有效多模态遥感预训练的必要前提；跨模态对应粒度应作为可迁移多模态表示学习的重要设计维度。更广泛地，该发现提供了一条降低对昂贵实例对齐数据依赖、扩展多模态预训练的可行路径。

Abstract: Multimodal remote sensing pretraining commonly relies on exact instance-level correspondence across modalities, implicitly treating strictly paired observations as a prerequisite for learning transferable representations. In this work, we revisit this assumption by relaxing cross-modal correspondence from the instance level to the semantic level. We introduce Semantic Pairing (SemPair), a pairing-relaxed pretraining strategy that replaces exact instance pairs with class-consistent cross-instance pairs. To characterize the effect of correspondence relaxation, we formulate a latent-factor model that separates cross-modal dependence into shared semantic structure and instance-specific correlation, showing that SemPair preserves the former while marginalizing instance-specific cross-modal correlations in expectation. We further introduce Mixed Pairing (MixPair) as a controlled interpolation between semantic and exact pairing to study correspondence granularity along a continuous spectrum. Extensive experiments consistently show that SemPair achieves transfer performance comparable to exact pairing (ExaPair), while MixPair reveals a non-monotonic relationship between downstream transfer and the instance-pairing ratio. These results demonstrate that exact instance correspondence is not a prerequisite for effective multimodal remote sensing pretraining and establish cross-modal correspondence granularity as an important design dimension for transferable multimodal representation learning. More broadly, this finding suggests a practical route toward scaling multimodal pretraining with reduced dependence on costly instance-aligned data.

</details>


### [56] [From Video Frames to Robot Meta-Frames: A Unified Latent Interface for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14365)
*Guoqiang Liang,Yiqi Lin,Yanzhe Chen,Kevin Yuchen Ma,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MetaWAM：将机器人信号（本体感知、动作、价值）通过频域打包折叠进单一“元帧”，与预训练视频扩散模型先验对齐，无需新增模块即可统一支持策略、世界模型与价值预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视频扩散模型（VDM）可作为World Action Models（WAM）的强大基础，但需将低维机器人信号注入由自然视频塑造的潜空间。现有方案把注入接口当作格式细节：每个信号单独占用一帧潜变量，或额外加装专用编解码器；作者认为这本质是分布对齐问题——即表示在去噪过程中与预训练VDM先验的匹配程度。

Method: 以冻结VDM去噪器的相对重建误差作为探针进行诊断，发现常规空间编码严重偏离先验，而频域打包得到的潜变量被去噪器修正的幅度显著更小。据此提出MetaWAM：把每个机器人信号分配到互不重叠的频域通道带，经逆DCT提升到空间潜变量，合并为单一统一元帧，形成可解码且对齐先验的接口，不增加模块或改变架构；固定频带布局配合slot-aware routing，使同一元帧在共享机器人变量上同时支持策略、世界模型与价值预测。

Result: 在RoboCasa上达到68.9%成功率，将每信号一帧压缩为一帧，推理时延降低39%；在三个真实Franka任务上达到85.0%成功率。

Conclusion: 信号注入应被视为分布对齐问题而非格式细节；频域打包提供了一种零额外模块、与预训练VDM先验高度一致的统一注入接口，兼具精度、效率与多任务复用性，为WAM设计提供了新的原则性视角。

Abstract: Pretrained video diffusion models (VDMs) offer a powerful foundation for World Action Models (WAMs), which jointly predict robot actions and future observations. Building such models requires injecting low-dimensional robot signals (e.g. proprioception, actions, and values) into a latent space shaped by natural videos, and a key design choice is the interface through which these signals enter: existing schemes treat it as a formatting detail, spending a separate latent frame on each signal or bolting on dedicated encoders and decoders. We argue that signal injection is instead a distribution-alignment problem: how well a representation matches the prior of the pretrained VDMs during denoising. Using the relative reconstruction error of a frozen VDM denoiser as a probe, we find that conventional spatial encodings sit far off this prior, whereas frequency-domain packing yields latents that the denoiser corrects far less. Motivated by this diagnostic result, we propose MetaWAM, which folds all robot signals into a single unified meta-frame: each signal is assigned to a disjoint frequency-domain channel band and lifted to the spatial latent by an inverse DCT, yielding a decodable interface aligned with the pretrained VDM prior without any added module or architectural change. A fixed band layout together with slot-aware routing lets the same meta-frame support policy, world-model, and value prediction over a shared set of robot variables. On RoboCasa, MetaWAM reaches 68.9% success while collapsing the per-signal frames into one and cutting inference latency by 39%, and attains 85.0% success on three real-world Franka tasks. Code and models are available at https://github.com/showlab/MetaWAM

</details>


### [57] [FastWild-GS: Minutes-Scale In-the-Wild Gaussian Splatting via Compact Per-Image Appearance Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13808)
*Donghun Ryu,Seokhyun Kim,Minhyeok Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FastWild-GS 用“照片外观 Token + 逐通道仿射变换”建模在野外照片的外观差异，替代球谐颜色，实现单场景约 9 分钟训练并在 PhotoTourism、NeRF-OSR 上取得最优或领先的 LPIPS/PSNR/SSIM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在野外（in-the-wild）场景中，同一场景的网络照片在曝光、光照和瞬态物体（如行人）上差异显著，单一组高斯颜色无法解释所有照片；而现有在野外高斯泼溅方法通常需要每场景数小时优化，效率低。

Method: 为每张照片引入 128 维 Photo Appearance Token 及逐通道仿射（scale、offset 共 6 个参数）作用于渲染图像；用小型 MLP 由该 token 与逐高斯特征预测每个高斯颜色，取代球谐颜色；并将同一变换应用于用于致密化与剪枝评分的高斯渲染，使照片间外观差异不被误计为重建误差。

Result: PhotoTourism 上每场景训练 8.8 分钟，LPIPS 为对比方法中最低，比 LPIPS 次优的 AsymGS 快 23 倍；NeRF-OSR 上每场景 9.7 分钟，平均 PSNR、SSIM、LPIPS 均为对比方法中最佳。

Conclusion: 将照片级外观建模（外观 token + 仿射变换）与颜色预测解耦，并把该变换一致地用于致密化/剪枝评分，可在显著提升训练效率的同时获得更优的重建与感知质量，为在野外高斯泼溅提供了快速且有效的方案。

Abstract: Internet photos of a scene differ in exposure, illumination and transient objects such as passers-by, so one set of Gaussian colours cannot explain all photos (the in-the-wild setting). Recent in-the-wild Gaussian Splatting methods mostly report hours of per-scene optimization. We present FastWild-GS, which describes each photo by a 128-dimensional Photo Appearance Token and a per-channel transform, a scale and an offset applied to the rendered image (six parameters); a small MLP predicts the colour of each Gaussian from this token and a per-Gaussian feature, replacing the spherical-harmonics colour. The same transform is applied to the renders that score Gaussians for densification and pruning, so appearance differences between photos are not counted as reconstruction error. On PhotoTourism, FastWild-GS trains in 8.8 minutes per scene with the lowest LPIPS among the compared methods, 23x faster than AsymGS, the next best in LPIPS. On NeRF-OSR, it has the best average PSNR, SSIM and LPIPS among the compared methods in 9.7 minutes per scene.

</details>


### [58] [UGG: Unified Generative Geometry Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14440)
*Amani Kiruga,Zhiyi Li,Ruojin Cai,Hansen Lillemark,Xiaoshan Wu,Ayush Tewari,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 UGG 统一框架，将 RGB 视频生成与几何预测（深度、相机位姿）统一为单一生成模型中的条件补全任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D 场景理解需同时从部分观测中捕捉几何、外观与动态，但现有方法割裂为前馈几何预测模型与生成式视觉合成模型两类，难以共享信息，也难以利用标注异构的数据集。

Method: 按模态与时间分配不同噪声水平，把视频生成与几何预测统一表述为单一生成模型内的条件补全；该形式支持从仅含部分几何/视觉标注的异构数据中学习；推理时利用观测 RGB 帧提取的几何证据进行多假设搜索，无需真值几何即可改进预测。

Result: 在相机可控视频生成、深度估计、相机位姿预测任务上优于非统一基线，并在稀疏观测等困难设定下仍有效。

Conclusion: 统一生成式建模范式可有效耦合外观与几何，并借助推理时搜索提升几何预测，为 4D 场景理解提供通用框架。

Abstract: Understanding 4D scenes is a core challenge in visual intelligence, requiring models to jointly capture scene geometry, appearance, and dynamics from partial observations. However, existing approaches are subdivided into feedforward models for direct geometric prediction, and generative models for visual synthesis. We introduce UGG, a unified framework for 4D scene understanding that jointly models RGB video and geometric representations such as depth and camera pose. By assigning different noise levels across modalities and time, UGG formulates visual generation and geometric prediction as conditional completion within a single generative model. This flexible formulation enables the model to learn from diverse, heterogeneous datasets with different subsets of geometric and visual annotations. Furthermore, our generative formulation enables inference-time search over multiple plausible scene hypotheses, using geometric evidence extracted from observed RGB frames to improve geometric prediction without ground truth geometry. We demonstrate UGG's effectiveness over non-unified baselines across camera-controlled video generation, depth estimation, and camera pose prediction, including in challenging sparse observation settings. Code and visualizations are available at https://ugg-model.github.io/

</details>


### [59] [CASCADE: Constraint-Aware SCAffolded DEcomposition for Logical Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13820)
*Jinchan Kim,Jinhee Im,Hyunsouk Cho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文指出基于LVLM的逻辑异常检测(checklist验证)存在"文本-图像不匹配"导致约束特定感知缺口，提出免训练的CASCADE/CASCADE++，通过约束特定的视觉脚手架(物体级自适应)提升验证精度，MVTec-LOCO/VID-AD上以3个参考样本超越全样本SOTA 3.9 AUROC，VID-AD基线提升11.4，且可跨LVLM骨干迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LAD需要仅凭少量正常参考，验证查询图像是否满足类别特定约束(目标计数、配对关系、测量范围等)。现有LVLM方法将其化为checklist验证，分解为独立的文本检查项，但每个检查项仍读取同一张未修改的查询图像，迫使LVLM从单一原始图像中提取不同形式的视觉证据，即"约束特定感知缺口"，进而引发两类失败：绑定约束的实例混淆(instance conflation)与校准约束的尺度漂移(scale drift)。作者需要诊断失败源于推理不足还是感知不足。

Method: 在免训练设置下对比"文本推理引导"与"视觉感知引导"两种干预以定位问题来源。基于"适配视觉输入显著有效、重复推理无益"的结论，提出CASCADE：在checklist分解基础上，为每个约束引入约束特定的视觉脚手架(visual scaffolds)。进一步提出CASCADE++，根据约束结构与参考分布推导脚手架配置，将其适配到物体级别(object-level)。

Result: 在MVTec-LOCO与VID-AD上，CASCADE++仅用3个参考样本即超越全样本(full-shot)最先进方法3.9个AUROC点，并较最强VID-AD基线提升11.4；可在多种LVLM骨干上零训练迁移。

Conclusion: LAD中的主要瓶颈是感知而非推理：将视觉输入按约束进行适配优于对同一图像反复推理。约束特定的、物体级的视觉脚手架可在免训练条件下显著提升逻辑异常检测性能，并具备良好的骨干泛化性。

Abstract: Logical Anomaly Detection (LAD) requires verifying whether all category-specific constraints, such as object counts, pairing relations, and measurement ranges, are satisfied by a query image given only a few normal references. Recent LAD methods based on Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) formulate this as checklist verification, decomposing the decision into independent textual checks. This decomposition, however, is text-only. Every item still reads the same unmodified query image, so the LVLM must extract different forms of visual evidence from one raw image. We identify this text-image mismatch as a constraint-specific perception gap, the source of two failure modes: instance conflation for binding constraints and scale drift for calibration constraints. To diagnose whether these failures stem from insufficient reasoning or insufficient perception, we compare textual reasoning guides and visual perception guides in a training-free setting. Our analysis shows that reasoning over the same image provides little benefit, whereas adapting the visual input to each constraint substantially improves verification accuracy. Based on this finding, we propose CASCADE, a training-free framework that extends checklist decomposition using constraint-specific visual scaffolds. We further introduce CASCADE++, which adapts these scaffolds at the object level by deriving their configurations from the constraint structure and reference distribution. Across MVTec-LOCO and VID-AD, CASCADE++ surpasses the full-shot state of the art by 3.9 AUROC points with only three references, and improves over the strongest VID-AD baseline by 11.4, transferring across multiple LVLM backbones without any training.

</details>


### [60] [Satellite Pretraining Is Not One Thing: A Matched-Pair Study of Generic and Earth-Observation Foundation Models for Scene Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13849)
*Sandarbh Agrawal,Rakshith Sathish,Ashwathi Nambiar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文系统比较了5对"目标函数与结构匹配、仅预训练数据不同"的EO编码器与通用编码器（共14个冻结编码器），发现地球观测预训练并非普遍有益，且冻结嵌入的silhouette分数可近乎完美预测下游k-NN准确率与检索mAP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地理空间基础模型通常在大规模卫星/航拍影像上预训练，但既有对比很少控制预训练数据这一变量，因此"EO预训练是否优于强通用编码器"在少标签土地利用/土地覆盖制图任务中仍无定论。作者希望通过匹配对设计来隔离预训练数据的影响。

Method: 构建14个冻结编码器的基准，按目标函数与架构匹配成5对：DINO/SatDINO、DINOv3/DINOv3-SAT、MAE/SatMAE、MAE/Scale-MAE、CLIP/RemoteCLIP；在5个RGB场景分类数据集（0.06–30 m GSD）上使用原生预处理，评估k-NN、线性探针、注意力探针与图像检索，覆盖6种低标签划分，并采用逐图像配对显著性检验。

Result: EO预训练并非一致有效：在多数数据集与指标上，SatDINO、Scale-MAE、RemoteCLIP三对显著提升（RemoteCLIP仅在被测数据集之外评估），而DINOv3-SAT（线性探针最大下降22.5点）与SatMAE（最大下降8.8点）显著变差；即便两个MAE在同一fMoW-RGB影像上预训练，结果也相反。效应在patch-token池化与注意力探针下依然稳健，集中于小型人造目标，并在1–5%标签预算下最强。跨编码器层面，冻结嵌入的silhouette分数几乎完美预测k-NN准确率与检索mAP（5个数据集中4个的Spearman ρ≥0.96）。

Conclusion: "在卫星影像上预训练"不能作为可靠的模型选择准则；候选检查点必须在目标数据上做实际验证，而冻结嵌入的聚类可分性（silhouette）可作为低成本、高预测力的筛选代理指标。

Abstract: Geospatial foundation models are pretrained on satellite and aerial imagery, but whether such an encoder beats a strong generic one for few-label land-use/land-cover mapping remains unclear, as prior comparisons rarely isolate the pretraining data. We study five matched pairs of Earth-observation (EO) and generic encoders sharing objective and architecture (DINO/SatDINO, DINOv3/DINOv3-SAT, MAE/SatMAE, MAE/Scale-MAE, CLIP/RemoteCLIP) within a benchmark of 14 frozen encoders on five RGB scene-classification datasets (0.06-30 m GSD). With native preprocessing, we evaluate k-NN, linear and attentive probes and image retrieval over six low-label splits, using paired per-image significance tests. EO pretraining is not uniformly helpful: on most datasets and metrics, it significantly improves three pairs (SatDINO, Scale-MAE, RemoteCLIP, the latter evaluated only on unseen datasets) and significantly hurts two (linear probe: DINOv3-SAT by up to 22.5 points, SatMAE by up to 8.8), with opposite outcomes even for two MAEs pretrained on the same fMoW-RGB imagery. These effects are robust to patch-token pooling and attentive probing, concentrate on small man-made objects, and are largest with 1-5% labels. Across encoders, the silhouette score of frozen embeddings predicts k-NN accuracy and retrieval mAP almost perfectly (Spearman $ρ\geq 0.96$ on four of five datasets). Pretraining on satellite imagery is thus not a reliable selection criterion; candidate checkpoints must be validated on target data.

</details>


### [61] [Towards Text-Only Remote Sensing Scene Classification via Semantic Proxy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13851)
*Xianping Ma,Kunlin Wu,Xiaofeng Liu,Xiaokang Zhang,Yuanxin Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"纯文本遥感场景分类"（text-only RSSC）新问题设定，并构建 SeProRS 基准：先用视觉语言模型把遥感图像转成文本描述（语义代理），再由文本嵌入模型完成场景分类，系统评估该范式的可行性、边界与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统遥感场景分类（RSSC）被视为以视觉为中心的任务，依赖图像中丰富的视觉表征进行推理。作者希望检验一个被默认接受的假设：场景识别是否必须依赖视觉信息，即能否仅凭文本语义表征完成遥感场景识别。同时，现有研究缺乏统一、可复现的评测平台来量化"模态缩减"式场景理解的能力边界。

Method: 提出 SeProRS 框架，将 RSSC 重构为纯文本分类任务：(1) 使用视觉语言模型（VLM）把遥感图像转换为文本描述，称为"语义代理"（semantic proxies）；(2) 用文本嵌入模型对语义代理编码；(3) 在嵌入特征上进行场景分类。该流程为解耦式流水线，形成统一、可复现的基准。在此基础上系统考察代表性提示策略与代理生成器，并分析其对分类性能、文本嵌入行为、跨生成器可迁移性及效率的影响。

Result: 在三个公开遥感场景分类数据集上的实验表明：语义代理能够保留相当可观的可判别信息，验证了纯文本场景识别的可行性；同时也暴露出纯文本场景理解固有的局限性。分析还给出了不同提示策略与代理生成器对性能、嵌入特性和效率的具体影响，以及跨生成器迁移的表现。

Conclusion: 本文并不旨在取代基于图像的 RSSC，而是通过量化"语义代理"式场景识别的可行性与边界，开辟一个新的研究方向，并为高效、模态缩减的遥感场景理解提供基准与分析基础。源码与数据集将公开。

Abstract: Remote sensing scene classification (RSSC) is traditionally formulated as a vision-centric problem, where inference relies on rich visual representations extracted from images. In this work, we revisit this assumption by introducing text-only RSSC, a new problem setting that investigates whether remote sensing scenes can be recognized using only textual semantic representations. To enable this study, we propose SeProRS, which reformulates RSSC as a text-only classification task through semantic proxy generation. Specifically, a vision-language model (VLM) is first employed to convert remote sensing images into textual descriptions, termed semantic proxies, which are then encoded by a text embedding model for scene classification. This decoupled pipeline provides a unified and reproducible benchmark for evaluating text-only scene understanding. Furthermore, we investigate representative prompting strategies and proxy generators, and analyze their impact on classification performance, text embedding behavior, cross-generator transferability, and efficiency. Extensive experiments on three well-known RSSC datasets show that semantic proxies can preserve substantial discriminative information, while also revealing inherent limitations of purely text-based scene understanding. This paper does not aim to replace image-based RSSC, but instead establishes a new research direction by quantifying the feasibility and boundaries of semantic proxy-based scene recognition. The proposed benchmark and analysis provide a foundation for future research on efficient and modality-reduced remote sensing scene understanding. The source code and datasets will be accessible at https://github.com/sstary/SSRS.

</details>


### [62] [Spatial-Spectral Coupling as Attention: Scaling Shape Correspondence to High-Resolution Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13870)
*Bence Demeter,Kilian Ehrenreich,Amelie Kleber,Ioana Oprisiu,Philipp Spohn,Christian Wachinger,Fabian Bongratz,Lennart Bastian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将功能映射中的空间-谱耦合重新解释为缩放点积注意力，提出Geo-FlashAttention（局部精确softmax），在保持对应精度的同时显著降低大网格训练内存与耗时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率3D形状间建立稠密对应仍很困难：功能映射方法核心的空间-谱耦合随网格分辨率二次增长，成为计算与内存瓶颈；而亚二次近似方法在非等距形状对上会引入误差，恰恰在这些场景中最需要精确耦合。

Method: 观察到空间-谱耦合等价于缩放点积注意力：学习到的描述子作为query/key，谱基函数作为value。据此直接将高效注意力实现迁移到形状匹配中；实验表明优化后的二次注意力可扩展深度功能映射流水线，而亚二次近似会引起误差。进一步提出Geo-FlashAttention，将精确softmax限制在几何选取的表面patch上，从而实现近线性经验扩展并保留精确实现的对应精度。

Result: 在六个标准基准与三条功能映射流水线上验证：高效耦合保持对应精度。在60k顶点时，峰值训练内存由106.08 GiB降至5.67 GiB，Geo-FlashAttention每步训练比FlashAttention快1.5倍。该方案使大网格（如皮层表面等在保持疾病相关几何细节上需高分辨率的形状）上的端到端对应学习成为可能。

Conclusion: 空间-谱耦合与注意力机制的结构等价性是扩展深度功能映射的关键洞察，几何局部的精确softmax可在接近线性的复杂度下维持精确方法的精度，为大尺度高分辨率形状对应学习提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Establishing dense correspondence between high-resolution 3D shapes remains challenging due to the spatial--spectral coupling at the core of many functional-map methods, which grows quadratically with mesh resolution. We observe that this coupling is equivalent to a scaled dot-product attention formulation, with learned descriptors serving as queries and keys and spectral basis functions as values. This connection makes efficient attention implementations directly applicable to shape matching and an effective mechanism for scaling spatial-spectral coupling. We show that optimized quadratic attention implementations can scale modern deep functional map pipelines to large mesh sizes, improving both runtime and memory consumption, while sub-quadratic methods introduce errors, particularly for non-isometric shape pairs, where an exact coupling proves most necessary. These insights motivate \textit{Geo-FlashAttention}, which restricts the exact softmax to geometry-selected surface patches and achieves near-linear empirical scaling while preserving correspondence accuracy of exact implementations. Across six standard benchmarks and three functional-map pipelines, rigorous analysis shows that efficient couplings preserve correspondence accuracy. At $60k$ vertices, these insights reduce peak training memory from 106.08 to 5.67\,GiB, with Geo-FlashAttention being $1.5\times$ faster per training step than FlashAttention. This enables end-to-end learning for correspondence problems on large meshes such as cortical surfaces, where high resolution is required to preserve disease-relevant intricacies of the surface geometry. Project page available https://spatial-spectral-attention.github.io/

</details>


### [63] [The BRIDGE dataset: paired brightfield whole-slide images of prostate core-needle biopsies in paraffin, after deparaffinization and H&E staining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13883)
*Julia A. Mielcarz,Justyna Durślewicz,Marek Zdrenka,Ahmad Abbadi,Martin Eklund,Mateusz Matusiak,Łukasz Szylberg,Andrea Camilloni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BRIDGE数据集：波兰Bydgoszcz肿瘤中心采集的前列腺穿刺活检数据，169例患者、869张玻片，切片后经脱蜡与H&E染色顺序数字化，共获得2607张全切片图像，支持免标记虚拟染色、虚拟去染色、图像配准与分割方法的开发与外部验证，并附Gleason评分供分级一致性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算病理尤其是免标记虚拟染色方法日益受关注，但模型开发与验证受限于稀缺的、配对且带临床注释的全切片图像数据集。现有数据难以支持外部验证与跨扫描仪鲁棒性评估。

Method: 构建BRIDGE数据集：收集前列腺穿刺活检玻片（869张，来自169例患者），对同一组织切片在切片、脱蜡及H&E染色后依次数字化，形成配对的全切片图像（共2607张）；每张玻片附带Gleason评分；数据去标识化，以扫描仪原生格式发布，并通过BioImage Archive开放获取。

Result: 获得869张玻片、169例患者、2607张配对全切片图像；数据可支撑免标记虚拟染色与虚拟去染色、图像配准、分割等任务，并用于癌症分类与分级一致性等下游分析；染色图像亦可用于颜色归一化与跨扫描仪鲁棒性研究。

Conclusion: BRIDGE为免标记虚拟染色及相关计算病理任务提供了一个配对、带Gleason注释且可外部验证的公开全切片数据集，有助于缓解该领域配对数据稀缺的瓶颈。

Abstract: Computational approaches are increasingly being explored to streamline histopathology workflows, including label-free methods that aim to reduce reliance on chemical staining. Among these, deep learning-based label-free virtual staining uses images of unstained tissue to computationally generate stained images. Despite growing interest, model development and validation remain constrained by the scarcity of paired, clinically annotated whole-slide imaging datasets. We present BRIDGE, a dataset of prostate core-needle biopsies collected at the Prof. Franciszek Lukaszczyk Oncology Centre in Bydgoszcz, Poland. The dataset comprises 869 glass slides from 169 patients, each digitized sequentially after sectioning, deparaffinization, and hematoxylin and eosin staining, yielding in total 2607 whole-slide images. The dataset supports the development and external validation of label-free virtual staining, virtual unstaining, image-registration and segmentation methods. Each slide is accompanied by a Gleason score, enabling downstream analyses of cancer classification and grading concordance. The stained images can be used independently for color normalization and cross-scanner robustness studies. All images are de-identified, distributed in their native scanner format and will be available through the BioImage Archive.

</details>


### [64] [Region-Wise Logit Fusion and Utility-Gated Refinement for Brain Metastasis Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13884)
*Zhenye Lou,Qing Xu,Wenting Duan,Zhen Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出COMPASS-METS，将三个五折nnU-Net配置集成为对齐的区域概率集成，结合结构化概率锚点与受门控的ET候选补充，以兼顾脑转移瘤小病灶检测与先验层级几何一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BraTS-METS 2026 Task 1需在异质多参数MRI上分割脑转移瘤与术后切除腔，难点在于既要检出小强化灶，又要保持ET/TC/WT/RC的几何与层级关系；常规单模型难以同时满足检测灵敏度与结构一致性。

Method: 方法由两部分组成：(1) 将三个五折nnU-Net配置输出集成为对齐的区域概率集成；(2) 结构化概率锚点执行区域级组件判定、TC边界补全、RC过滤及保层级转换；对被遗漏的ET候选，利用学习到的组件置信度与确定性的多训练器支持进行加性恢复，并以最终效用门控仅接纳具备足够病灶存在概率与几何安全概率的离散候选。

Result: 在复用的179例挑战开发集上，Small F1 0.354、病灶级DSC 0.712、NSD 0.714、All F1 0.779、HD95 56.6 mm。相较ResEncXL，DSC、NSD与All F1提升、HD95下降，Small F1由0.367变为0.354；分阶段分析表明主要几何增益来自结构化概率构建，受门控的ET精修可挽回大部分相关的小病灶检测损失。

Conclusion: 结构化概率集成与受门控的ET补充可在显著改善几何与层级一致性的同时，将小病灶检测损失控制在较小范围；但179例结果仍属受选择偏差影响的开发估计，需在独立测试集上进一步验证。

Abstract: BraTS-METS 2026 Task 1 evaluates segmentation of brain metastases and postoperative resection cavities on heterogeneous multiparametric MRI, requiring detection of small enhancing foci while preserving the geometry and hierarchy of enhancing tumor (ET), tumor core (TC), whole tumor (WT), and resection cavity (RC). We present COMPASS-METS (Component-Aware Multi-Trainer Probability Assembly with Structured Segmentation), which combines three five-fold nnU-Net configurations into aligned regional probability ensembles. A structured-probability anchor applies region-specific component decisions, TC boundary completion, RC filtering, and hierarchy-preserving conversion. Omitted ET candidates are recovered additively using learned component confidence and deterministic multi-trainer support for ET and its TC/WT parents; a final utility gate admits only disconnected candidates with sufficient lesion-existence and geometry-safety probabilities. On the reused 179-case challenge development cohort, COMPASS-METS (Team: MicroBT) achieved Small F1 0.354, lesion-wise DSC 0.712, NSD 0.714, All F1 0.779, and HD95 56.6 mm. Relative to ResEncXL, it improved DSC, NSD, and All F1 and reduced HD95, while Small F1 changed from 0.367 to 0.354. Stagewise analysis attributes the principal geometric gains to structured probability construction and shows that guarded ET refinement recovers most of the associated small-lesion detection loss. These 179-case results remain selection-aware development estimates. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/leonlou921/COMPASS-METS.

</details>


### [65] [DroneSegEdit: Adapting Pretrained Generative Editors for UAV Segmentation with Language-Grounded Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13890)
*Dehua Chen,Long Xin,Jiangtao Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 DroneSegEdit，通过将 RGB 掩码渲染与语言约束的候选验证相结合，弥合语言引导无人机分割中的"Grounding-Rendering Gap"，在 RIS-LAD 上达到 52.78 mIoU 的新 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练图像生成器虽可支持多样视觉任务，但直接用于语言引导的无人机分割存在关键缺陷：渲染出的合理掩码可能对应小而重复目标中的错误实例，即掩码渲染与表达式级目标选择之间的不匹配（Grounding-Rendering Gap）。

Method: DroneSegEdit 将 RGB 候选渲染与语言引导、候选约束的验证结合，在保留渲染几何的前提下选择有支撑的前景。方法以开放权重 LongCat-Image-Edit 为基础，采用任务特定 LoRA 适配与固定颜色到标签的解码；对于指代分割，引入 Language-Grounded Spatial Verifier（LGSV），从冻结的多模态大语言模型骨干中读取分层状态，导出以表达式为条件的空间证据，并映射回渲染候选内部而不引入额外前景。另有免验证器实验，用任务适配器从单一任务级指令联合渲染所有类别，实现全场景解析。

Result: 在 RIS-LAD 上取得 52.78 mIoU 的新 SOTA，比此前最佳高 7.16 分；在 UAVid-RIS 与 VDD-RIS 上总体保持竞争力，在紧凑且应用相关类别（尤其是车辆和行人）上获得最明显的类别级提升。

Conclusion: 通过在生成式渲染框架内显式加入语言引导的空间验证，可有效缓解 Grounding-Rendering Gap；该 RGB 接口亦可扩展至无需枚举类名或调色板的全场景解析。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that pretrained image generators can support diverse visual tasks. Yet directly applying them to language-guided unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) segmentation exposes a key limitation: a plausible mask may correspond to the wrong instance among small and repeated objects. We call this mismatch between mask rendering and expression-level target selection the Grounding-Rendering Gap. We present DroneSegEdit, which combines RGB proposal rendering with language-grounded, proposal-constrained verification to select supported foreground while retaining rendered geometry. DroneSegEdit adapts the open-weight LongCat-Image-Edit model using task-specific LoRA and fixed color-to-label decoding. For referring segmentation, a Language-Grounded Spatial Verifier (LGSV) reads hierarchical states from the frozen multimodal large language model backbone to derive expression-conditioned spatial evidence, which is mapped back to the rendered proposal without introducing foreground outside it. On RIS-LAD, DroneSegEdit reaches a new state of the art at 52.78 mIoU, 7.16 points above the previous best. It remains competitive overall on UAVid-RIS and VDD-RIS, with its clearest class-wise gains on compact, application-relevant categories, notably vehicles and humans. A separate verifier-free experiment extends the RGB interface to full-scene parsing: a task-specific adapter jointly renders all classes from one task-level instruction without enumerating class names or palette colors.

</details>


### [66] [CAppleNet: A Coordinate Attention-Based YOLO11 for Real-Time Apple Stem Cavity and Calyx Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13920)
*Alican Güneş,Robert Selim Hill,Gökhan Hasanoğlu,Murat Kurt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文基于自建的1506张苹果图像数据集，以默认超参YOLO11s为基线，通过单因素消融考察输入分辨率、数据增强、优化器与轻量结构替换对果体、果梗洼、萼洼三类细粒度关键点检测的影响。结果显示仅将C2PSA替换为坐标注意力(CA)可在F1基本持平下减少10.2%参数并取得最佳mAP@0.5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动分选与定向系统需实时、准确地检测果实级形态关键点（果梗洼/萼洼），而非仅依赖果体边界轮廓；同时不同品种间颜色与纹理差异大，细粒度关键点检测困难，而针对该任务的系统性超参/结构消融证据缺乏。

Method: 自采集、自标注1506张图像（训练1054/验证301/测试151），固定相机条件，标注果体、果梗洼、萼洼三类；以默认超参YOLO11s为基线，进行单因素逐项消融：输入分辨率、6种数据增强策略、优化器配置、同槽位轻量结构替换（C2PSA→Coordinate Attention）；并做置信度阈值扫描与结构+增强的验证性组合实验。

Result: 未调优基线F1=0.9047；提高分辨率、全部6种增强变体、手动优化器调参均未超越基线；CA替换取得F1=0.9038（与基线持平）、参数减少10.2%、最佳mAP@0.5=0.935、最佳果梗洼F1=0.8217；部署阈值为0.15；CA替换与最佳增强组合后非加性叠加，反而抵消结构增益。

Conclusion: 推荐方案为仅采用CA替换架构并在0.15置信度阈值下部署，用于面向定向的工业分选与对齐的实时苹果关键点检测；同时说明"结构改进+增强"并非天然互补，需做交互验证。

Abstract: Automated fruit grading, sorting, and orientation systems require real-time, accurate detection of fruit-level morphological landmarks rather than the fruit boundary alone.
