This paper presents a custom, fully self-collected and self-annotated dataset of 1506 apple images (1054 training, 301 validation, 151 test) captured under controlled, fixed-camera conditions and annotated for three target classes: fruit body, stem cavity, and calyx. A YOLO11s detector trained with default hyperparameters is adopted as the baseline, and a systematic, one-factor-at-a-time ablation study is conducted across input resolution, data augmentation policy, optimizer configuration, and lightweight architectural enhancement to identify the factors that most improve detection of these fine-grained landmarks under high inter-cultivar color and texture variability. Neither increased input resolution, nor any of six tested augmentation variants, nor manual optimizer tuning improved on the untuned baseline (F1 = 0.9047); only a same-slot architectural substitution of the backbone's C2PSA block with Coordinate Attention (CA) matched the baseline's overall F1 (0.9038) while reducing parameter count by 10.2% and achieving the study's best mAP@0.5 (0.935) and best stem-cavity F1 (0.8217). A confidence-threshold sweep identified 0.15 as the deployment operating point for this model, and a confirmatory experiment combining the CA-swap architecture with the best-performing augmentation variant showed the two effects do not combine additively, reversing the architectural gain rather than compounding it. The resulting design recommendations --- the CA-swap architecture alone, at a 0.15 confidence threshold --- target real-time apple landmark detection for orientation-aware industrial sorting and alignment systems.

</details>


### [67] [The Case for AI-Enabled Gate Monitoring as Farm Security in the UK: A PRISMA-Guided Review of the Evidence on Rural Crime, Livestock Predation and Farm Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13933)
*Steve Symes,Paul Fergus,Mark Carr,Omar McIver,Arwyn Jones*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文通过PRISMA综述揭示英国农场安全证据薄弱、成本估计依赖保险理赔数据，并提出（作为推断而非实证结论）Fieldmark AI式的门级连续AI监控可填补数据空白，同时给出验证其减损效果的评估方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 英国农场安全投入高昂，但支撑其决策的证据基础远薄于成本所暗示的水平；现有官方数据无法单独识别农业犯罪，独立评估缺失，因此有必要系统盘点证据缺口并明确未来应如何评估新技术。

Method: PRISMA指导的综述：2026年11月2–4日在Web of Science与Scopus进行12次检索，获1,183条记录（去重后761条），灰色文献与手工检索补充35条；最终对96条数据库记录与24条灰色文献做叙述性综合。

Result: 英国同行评审证据仅16条，主要犯罪与农户心理健康调查仅80份回应；成本依赖保险理赔（NFU Mutual：2025年农村犯罪4,150万英镑，牲畜盗窃增近30%至约450万英镑，狗袭牲畜损失195万英镑）；Home Office称中央数据无法单独识别农业犯罪。未发现对摄像头、传感器、追踪器或标记的独立评估；光声驱避在1–5个月内失效；无研究考察农场门开启频率及后果；相机陷阱AI仅在生态数据集上报告精度，机器检测野生动物准确率41%而人工为81%。

Conclusion: 作者以推断（非实证效应）方式主张：Fieldmark AI式的门级连续AI监测可生成官方缺失的农场级时间戳记录、核实周期性人工巡检遗漏的事件、判定肇事主体（狐狸、狗或人），并为其他安全措施提供检验基准；但目前尚无研究证明其降低损失，故提出了相应的评估设计。

Abstract: Farm security in the UK rests on thinner evidence than its cost suggests. We ran a PRISMA guided review: twelve searches of Web of Science and Scopus on 2nd -4th November 2026 returned 1,183 records (761 unique), and grey-literature and hand-searches added 35 assessed sources. We synthesised 96 database records and 24 grey sources narratively. UK peer-reviewed evidence is small (16 records), and the main UK survey of crime and farmer mental health has 80 responses. Cost estimates rest on insurer claims: NFU Mutual put UK rural crime at £41.5 million in 2025, with livestock theft up almost 30% to about £4.5 million and dog attacks on livestock costing £1.95 million. The Home Office states that agricultural crime cannot be separately identified in its central data. We found no independent evaluation of cameras, sensors, trackers or marking; light and sound deterrents lost effect within one to five months in a systematic review of anti-predator interventions; and no study examined how often farm gates are left open or what follows. Camera-trap AI papers report accuracy on ecological datasets, none at a farm gate in the records we found, and in one comparison machine learning detected 41% of wildlife against 81% for analysts. We argue, as an inference from these gaps and not as a demonstrated effect, that continuous gate-level AI-mediated monitoring, as implemented in Fieldmark AI, would be highly beneficial for UK farm security. It would create the farm-level, time-stamped record that no official source holds, verify events that periodic human checks miss, resolve whether foxes, dogs or people were responsible, and give every other security measure something to be tested against. No study has yet shown that it reduces loss, and we set out the evaluation needed to show it.

</details>


### [68] [MEOW: Medical Open-World Interactive Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13935)
*Philip Matthias Winter,Maria Wimmer,Bernhard Schäfl,David Major,Dimitrios Lenis,Astrid Berg,Theresa Neubauer,Ariharasudhan Muthusami,Johannes Novotny,Georg Zotti,Katja Bühler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MEOW是首个将非交互式分割主干网络转变为交互式开放世界学习器的框架，无需更新可学习参数，通过无参数直推推理与记忆巩固累积知识，从而在训练未见类别上持续自我改进并零样本迁移到陌生域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放世界识别要求模型不仅能对未知结构做预测（广义开集），还应随时间从预测中持续学习改进。交互式分割虽可利用用户反馈，但此前仍局限于开集设定，未能实现跨时间的持续学习。

Method: 提出MEOW框架：将非交互式分割主干转为交互式开放世界学习器，且不更新任何可学习模型参数；核心由两部分组成——无参数的直推式推理（parameter-free transductive reasoning）与记忆巩固（memory consolidation），用以累积知识。

Result: 在医学数据上，MEOW对训练中从未见过的类别能持续提升自身预测质量，并可零样本迁移至不熟悉域，包括micro-CT数据及其他成像模态。

Conclusion: 无需参数更新即可实现交互式开放世界持续学习是可行的；结合直推推理与记忆巩固可有效累积知识并改善未来性能，且具备跨域零样本泛化能力。

Abstract: In open-world recognition, machine learning models not only make predictions on unknown structures ("generalized open-set"), but also continually learn and improve from those predictions over time. Interactive segmentation, despite its access to user feedback, has so far remained confined to the open-set setting. In this work we introduce MEOW, the first framework that turns non-interactive segmentation backbones into interactive open-world learners without updating learnable model parameters. MEOW combines parameter-free transductive reasoning with memory consolidation to accumulate knowledge and thereby improve future performance. Focusing on medical data, we show that MEOW consistently improves its own predictions on classes never seen during training, and transfers zero-shot to unfamiliar domains, including micro-CT data and other imaging modalities.

</details>


### [69] [Pulse or Provenance? What rPPG-Based Deepfake Detectors Learn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13992)
*Othmane Harraq,Tamer Aldwairi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: rPPG式深度伪造检测器在说话人脸伪造上达到0.998 AUC，但该信号并非生理来源，而是来自高频伪影与视频源，检测器主要编码数据来源而非操纵痕迹；且Celeb-DF/Celeb-DF++划分存在身份与源视频泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: rPPG检测深伪的前提是合成人脸缺乏真实心搏信号。作者要检验这一前提是否成立：这类检测器到底学到了什么——生理线索，还是其他与伪造无关的伪影或数据偏差。

Method: 在Celeb-DF++（22种伪造方法）与FF++上，每段真/假视频取一个同分布采样窗口。对比简单色度轨迹与专用rPPG提取器；开展受控实验：背景区域、无face detection的全帧、频带分析、帧序打乱、分辨率/帧率/编码归一化、高低频内容增删、注入合成脉搏、独立复现；用两种生成器做自重现（self-reenactment）实验；跨数据集迁移测试（Celeb-DF++训练→FF++测试）；CLIP冻结骨干及与rPPG/频率特征的融合对比；审计官方划分的身份与源视频重叠，并量化自定义协议泄漏带来的AUC膨胀。

Result: 说话人脸伪造：简单色度轨迹AUC 0.998，优于专用rPPG提取器（0.87）。完全合成伪造：背景区域或无face detection全帧同样具判别力，信号集中于0.7–4 Hz心率带以上；打乱帧序基本消除该信号，归一化分辨率、帧率与编码后仍存在，独立复现成功。去除高频使多数合成视频被判为真，添加高频使真视频被判为假，注入合成脉搏影响甚微。单帧驱动的自重现生成引入相同高频信号，逐帧源重建则不引入。Celeb-DF++训练的色度检测器将>99%真实FF++视频判为假，CLIP检测器表现相同；冻结CLIP骨干达到或超过CLIP+rPPG+频率特征融合。官方Celeb-DF/Celeb-DF++划分在训练与测试间共享身份与源视频，复用测试视频与身份的自定义协议可使较弱检测器AUC虚高至0.30。

Conclusion: rPPG式深伪检测的高AUC并非源于生理信号缺失，而是高频生成伪影与视频源特征，检测器学到的是"来源"而非"操纵"；现有基准划分存在严重泄漏，跨数据集与跨方法泛化结论需重新审视。

Abstract: Remote photoplethysmography (rPPG) has been proposed as a physiological cue for deepfake detection, based on the premise that synthesized faces lack a genuine cardiac signal. We examine what rPPG-style detectors learn on Celeb-DF++ (22 forgery methods) and FaceForensics++ (FF++), using one identically sampled window per real and fake video. On talking-face forgeries, simple chrominance traces reach an AUC of 0.998, exceeding a dedicated rPPG extractor (0.87). Controlled experiments indicate that this signal is not physiological in origin. On fully synthesized forgeries, it is equally discriminative when extracted from background regions or from full frames without face detection, and it is concentrated above the 0.7 to 4 Hz heart-rate band. It is largely removed by shuffling frame order but persists after normalizing resolution, frame rate and encoding, and an independent reimplementation reproduces these findings. Removing high-frequency content makes most synthesized videos appear real, adding it makes real videos appear fake, and injecting a synthetic pulse has little effect. In controlled self-reenactment experiments with two generators, animating a face from a single frame introduces the same high-frequency signal, whereas reconstructing each frame from its own source does not. Face-based chrominance detectors trained on Celeb-DF++ classify over 99% of real FF++ videos as fake, indicating that they largely encode video source rather than manipulation, and a CLIP-based detector exhibits the same behavior. A frozen CLIP backbone matches or exceeds fusions of CLIP with rPPG and frequency features. Finally, we show that the official Celeb-DF and Celeb-DF++ splits share identities and source-video content between training and test, and that custom protocols reusing test videos and identities in training inflate weaker detectors by up to 0.30 AUC.

</details>


### [70] [Lipschitz-Controlled Flow Matching for Robust Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13995)
*Sedjro Salomon Hotegni,Konstantin Sonntag,Paul-Christian Bürkner,Sebastian Peitz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LipsFlower 通过 Lipschitz 感知的流匹配降低生成式图像复原中的扰动放大，提升重建稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配模型能生成高质量复原图像，但其学习到的动力学可能放大输入的微小扰动，导致重建不稳定。

Method: 推导 Flow-from-Y（从噪声扰动观测初始化 ODE）与观测条件后验采样两种形式的稳定性界；据此提出 LipHDiT——Lipschitz 感知的沙漏型扩散 Transformer 作为速度场结构，包含稳定化近似谱归一化、有界调制、凸残差混合；并在采样插值状态上使用对抗性有限半径增益正则，惩罚对状态及观测的过度敏感。

Result: 在盲人脸复原任务上评测两种形式，测量对抗鲁棒性与重建感知质量；LipsFlower 降低扰动放大，同时复原质量与所评估基线具竞争力。

Conclusion: Lipschitz 感知的流匹配可改善生成式图像复原的稳定性。

Abstract: Flow-matching models can produce high-quality image restorations, but their learned dynamics may amplify small input perturbations and make reconstructions unstable. We introduce LipsFlower to reduce this amplification. We derive stability bounds for Flow-from-$Y$, which initializes the ordinary differential equation from a noise-perturbed observation, as well as for observation-conditioned posterior sampling, which conditions the velocity field on the observation throughout integration. Based on this analysis, we develop LipHDiT, a Lipschitz-aware hourglass diffusion transformer that serves as the vector-field architecture of LipsFlower. LipHDiT incorporates stabilized approximate spectral normalization, bounded modulation, and convex residual mixing. We also use adversarial finite-radius gain regularization at sampled interpolation states to penalize excessive sensitivity to the state and, for conditional sampling, the observation. We test both formulations on blind face restoration, measuring adversarial robustness and the perceptual quality of the reconstructions. LipsFlower reduces perturbation amplification while keeping restoration quality competitive with the evaluated baselines. These results suggest that Lipschitz-aware flow matching can improve the stability of generative image restoration.

</details>


### [71] [Specify Privacy Yourself: Assessing Inference-Time Personalized Privacy Preservation Ability of Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13997)
*Xingqi Wang,Xiaoyuan Yi,Xing Xie,Jia Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出推理时个性化隐私保护（ITP³）新任务，构建首个系统评测基准SPY-Bench，评估21个先进LVLMs后发现其个性化隐私遵循能力普遍很差（最优仅约24%），并提出包括新型噪声对比对齐变体在内的初始对齐方法，达96.88%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LVLMs虽在通用隐私（如信用卡信息）上对齐较好，但隐私本质上是个人化且情境依赖的；用户需要能通过语言动态指定隐私边界，而当前模型缺乏这一推理时个性化隐私保护能力，且尚无系统评测手段。

Method: 提出ITP³任务：允许用户在推理时用语言规范动态界定隐私边界。构建SPY-Bench基准，含32,700个图像-问题对样本、个性化隐私指令，覆盖67个类别与24个真实场景，并设计基于用户规范与情境感知的新指标。对21个SOTA LVLMs进行评测，并实现初始ITP³对齐方法，包括一种新的噪声对比对齐（Noise Contrastive Alignment）变体。

Result: 多数模型表现很差，即使最优的o4-mini也仅约24%合规准确率；模型的情境化隐私理解能力相当有限。所提初始对齐方法（含新噪声对比对齐变体）达96.88%准确率，同时保持合理的通用性能。

Conclusion: ITP³是迈向更可控、更伦理部署LVLMs的初步一步；SPY-Bench为该方向提供了系统评测基础，所提对齐方法在保持通用能力的同时显著提升个性化隐私遵循水平。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities but raise significant privacy concerns due to their abilities to infer sensitive personal information from images with high precision. While current LVLMs are relatively well aligned to protect universal privacy, e.g., credit card data, we argue that privacy is inherently personalized and context-dependent. This work pivots towards a novel task: can LVLMs achieve Inference-Time Personalized Privacy Protection (ITP$^3$), allowing users to dynamically specify privacy boundaries through language specifications? To this end, we present SPY-Bench, the first systematic assessment of ITP$^3$ ability, which comprises (1) 32,700 unique samples with image-question pairs and personalized privacy instructions across 67 categories and 24 real-world scenarios, and (2) novel metrics grounded in user specifications and context awareness. Benchmarking the ITP$^3$ ability of 21 SOTA LVLMs, we reveal that: (i) most models, even the top-performing o4-mini, perform poorly, with only $\sim$24% compliance accuracy; (ii) they show quite limited contextual privacy understanding capability. Therefore, we implemented initial ITP$^3$ alignment methods, including a novel Noise Contrastive Alignment variant which achieves 96.88% accuracy while maintaining reasonable general performance. These results mark an initial step towards the ethical deployment of more controllable LVLMs. Code and data are available at https://github.com/achernarwang/specify-privacy-yourself

</details>


### [72] [A Stage-Wise Explainable Pipeline for Arteriolar-to-Venular Ratio Estimation and Retinopathy Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14013)
*Sharan Sharmeela Karuppannan,Yoga Sri Varshan,Subin Sahayam,Umarani Jayaraman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个五阶段可解释的视网膜AVR估计与筛查流水线，在30张HRF图像上取得96.7%准确率、F1 0.968、ROC-AUC 0.978，并附消融与VascX对比，但样本量小、标签为代理标签、缺乏独立参考管径测量，结论需更大规模临床分级队列验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AVR是临床上具有意义的视网膜血管健康标志物；现有端到端方法"黑箱"性质强，错误难以归因到具体处理环节，缺乏可检查的中间输出与透明的筛查决策规则。

Method: 五阶段流水线：血管与动静脉掩膜分割 → 视盘定位 → 基于最小弦边界游走（minimal-chord boundary walk）的血管管径估计 → 由优势动脉/静脉分支计算AVR → 显式阈值规则给出筛查决策；每一阶段均可输出可检查的中间结果，实现错误的阶段性归因。另开展消融实验，考察血管配对、管径测量与聚合方式的影响，并与VascX做探索性对比。

Result: 在30张HRF图像的探索性队列上：准确率96.7%，F1-score 0.968，ROC-AUC 0.978。消融实验揭示血管配对、管径测量与聚合策略对性能的影响，与VascX的探索性比较提供了参照。

Conclusion: 该流水线为研究视网膜血管生物标志物提供了透明、可追溯的框架，中间输出可检查、决策规则可解释；但受限于小样本队列、代理疾病标签及缺乏独立参考管径测量，解释力有限，需在更大规模、临床分级队列上进一步验证。

Abstract: The arteriolar-to-venular diameter ratio (AVR) is a clinically motivated marker of retinal vascular health. We present a five-stage pipeline for AVR estimation and exploratory retinopathy screening from fundus images, with inspectable intermediate outputs at each stage. The pipeline combines vessel and artery-vein masks, optic-disc localization, vessel-calibre estimation using a minimal-chord boundary walk, and AVR computation from dominant arterial and venular branches. An explicit threshold-based rule produces the screening decision, enabling errors to be traced to individual processing stages. On an exploratory cohort of 30 HRF images, we report 96.7% accuracy, an F1-score of 0.968 and ROC-AUC of 0.978. Ablation studies and an exploratory comparison with VascX examine the effects of vessel pairing, calibre measurement and aggregation. Interpretation is limited by the small cohort, proxy disease labels and absence of independent reference calibre measurements. The pipeline provides a transparent framework for investigating retinal vascular biomarkers, with further validation needed on larger, clinically graded cohorts.

</details>


### [73] [Interleaving anatomical inspection and diagnostic reasoning for explainable radiology report generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14018)
*Chentao Huang,Runlong Lin,Zhijun Wang,Yunxiang Li,Yuanyuan Sun,Jian Wang,Hongfei Lin,Ling Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出可解释的放射报告生成框架ARIVE，通过"解剖检查+诊断推理"交错的思维链轨迹显式化视觉证据到结论的推理路径，并在MIMIC-CXR上取得优于强基线的临床效能，同时提升报告可追溯性与可复核性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动放射报告生成模型虽能产出临床合理的报告，但从视觉证据到报告发现的推理路径不透明，导致报告诊断依据无法解释、难以审阅与修改。作者希望将推理过程显式化以增强可解释性与可核查性。

Method: 构建ARIVE（Anatomy-guided Reasoning with Interleaved Visual Evidence）框架：在最终报告撰写前，交替进行解剖区域检查与诊断推理。基于MIMIC-CXR构建159,416条多模态思维链（MCoT）轨迹，使所检查解剖区域的视觉证据驱动后续临床解释；在此轨迹上训练，并采用解剖层面与临床内容两类奖励进行进一步优化。

Result: 在MIMIC-CXR及以异常为中心的MIMIC-ABN上，临床效能指标优于强基线；盲评专家打分中报告质量与领先模型相当；MCoT轨迹在诊断可追溯性上获得高评分；配对审阅评估显示，提供MCoT有助于审阅者发现生成报告中的错误。

Conclusion: 将解剖引导的交错视觉证据推理显式嵌入报告生成流程，可在保持临床效能竞争力的同时显著提升报告的可解释性与可审阅性，MCoT可作为辅助人工核查错误的有效中间产物。

Abstract: Automated radiology report generation can draft clinically plausible reports, yet the pathway from visual evidence to reported findings remains opaque, leaving the diagnostic basis of these reports unexplained and the generated reports difficult to review or revise. We introduce ARIVE (Anatomy-guided Reasoning with Interleaved Visual Evidence), an explainable report generation framework that interleaves anatomical inspection with diagnostic reasoning prior to final report composition. To support this process, we constructed 159,416 multimodal chain-of-thought (MCoT) trajectories from MIMIC-CXR, wherein visual evidence from inspected anatomical regions informs subsequent clinical interpretation. Trained on these trajectories and further optimized using anatomical and clinical-content rewards, ARIVE achieved superior clinical-efficacy performance compared with strong baselines on MIMIC-CXR and the abnormality-centered MIMIC-ABN. ARIVE's reports were competitive with those of leading models in blinded expert grading, and its MCoT trajectories earned high ratings for diagnostic traceability. Furthermore, a paired review evaluation suggests that access to MCoT can aid in identifying errors within generated reports.

</details>


### [74] [Few-Step Streaming Video Generation via Condition Shifting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14033)
*Deyuan Liu,Jungang Li,Kaiwen Zheng,Kai Jiang,Jintao Chen,Yaole Wang,Ming Lin,Tao Lin,Jianfei Chen,Jun Zhu,Jintao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: APEX 将条件偏移（condition shifting）引入流式视频生成，在生成器内部估计生成分布的速度场，从而去掉 DMD 中独立的 fake-score 网络，在保持 S2-Avatar 各组件的同时降低显存与训练时间，并提升指令遵循与视觉美感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少步采样可降低流式视频生成成本，但分布匹配蒸馏（DMD）需额外的 fake-score 网络来监督少步生成器，带来训练显存与计算开销，且该网络必须持续跟踪不断更新的生成器，增加训练复杂度。

Method: 将 APEX 扩展到流式视频：采用条件偏移，用原始文本条件选择生成、用偏移条件选择 fake 估计，二者共享同一套参数，从而在生成器内部估计生成分布的速度场，替代独立 fake 网络；同时保留 S2-Avatar 的冻结参考教师、Self-Replay Forcing 与感知正则化。评估设置包括 180 秒视频、每 block 三次骨干评估、一步空间 Refiner，并开展 70 例人工研究和 320 例 StreamAV-Bench 对比。

Result: 相较 S2-Avatar，APEX 在 Progressive track 的视频指令遵循度更高（3.856 vs 3.831），两条 track 的视觉美感均更高（0.641898 vs 0.632606）。人工评测中 APEX 在指令遵循上优势最强（净偏好 +30.0%），一致性与视频质量各 +4.3%；运动质量偏向 S2-Avatar，音视频同步多为持平，可用性无变化。在匹配训练设置下，相比 DMD 峰值显存降低 4.4%（每 GPU），平均迭代时间缩短 9.2%。

Conclusion: 以条件偏移在生成器内部自估速度场，可有效替代 DMD 的独立 fake-score 网络，在不增加结构的前提下减少训练显存与时间，并带来指令遵循和视觉质量方面的增益；但运动质量略有下降，总体收益以效率与语义对齐为主。

Abstract: Few-step sampling reduces the cost of streaming video generation. Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD) supervises few-step generators with a separate fake-score network, which adds training memory and computation and must track the evolving generator. We extend APEX to streaming video by using condition shifting to estimate the velocity field of the generated distribution within the generator. The original text condition selects generation, while a shifted condition selects fake estimation using the same parameters. This removes the independent fake network while retaining S2-Avatar's frozen reference teacher, Self-Replay Forcing, and perceptual regularization. We evaluate APEX on 180-second videos using three backbone evaluations per block and a one-step spatial Refiner, with a 70-case human study and a 320-case StreamAV-Bench comparison. Compared with S2-Avatar, APEX achieves higher video instruction fulfillment on the Progressive track (3.856 versus 3.831) and higher visual aesthetics across both tracks (0.641898 versus 0.632606). Human raters favor APEX most strongly on instruction following, with +30.0% net preference, followed by consistency and video quality at +4.3% each. Motion quality favors S2-Avatar, audiovisual synchronization is predominantly tied, and usability is unchanged. Separate measurements under matched training settings show 4.4% lower peak allocated GPU memory per GPU and 9.2% shorter mean iteration time than DMD.

</details>


### [75] [CURRENT: A Recurrent Neural Network for Concurrent Spatial and Temporal Super-Resolution of 4D Flow MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14058)
*Luuk Jacobs,Pietro Dirix,Sebastian Kozerke*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出名为CURRENT的循环神经网络统一框架，对重建后的主动脉4D flow MRI速度矢量场进行时空超分辨率重建，在空间分辨率上优于4DFlowNet、时间分辨率上相当，同时时空联合SR对相对压估计更准确，并展现出从合成数据向在体数据泛化的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D flow MRI的空间与时间分辨率有限，制约了血流动力学精细分析与基于物理的定量计算（如相对压估计）；现有方法通常分别处理空间或时间超分，缺乏统一且能提升下游物理分析精度的手段。

Method: 构建单一循环神经网络CURRENT，同时完成空间与时间SR；训练采用仿真—实测融合策略：将计算流体动力学模拟的主动脉血流嵌入在体胸部MRI背景生成成对高分辨率数据，再经受控的空间与时间下采样得到低分辨率输入进行监督训练。评估包括 twofold空间/时间SR对比4DFlowNet、二倍时空SR对比基于4DFlowNet的串行时空SR管线，采用图像域与k空间定量指标，并以传统插值为基线；同时考察时空SR对物理信息相对压估计的影响及从合成数据到在体采集的泛化能力。

Result: CURRENT在空间SR上优于专用4DFlowNet模型、时间SR性能相当，且以统一架构同时覆盖两类任务；时空联合SR的相对压估计精度高于单独空间或时间SR，并优于基于4DFlowNet的串行时空SR方案；尽管仅在合成数据上训练，模型对在体4D flow MRI数据表现出良好的泛化性。

Conclusion: CURRENT为4D flow MRI的空间与时间SR提供了统一框架，有望增强基于物理的血流分析并拓展加速4D flow MRI的适用范围。

Abstract: Objective: To improve the spatial and temporal resolution of aortic 4D flow MRI through a unified deep learning framework for post-acquisition super-resolution (SR) of reconstructed velocity vector fields.
  Methods: We propose CURRENT, a recurrent neural network that performs spatial and temporal SR with a single network architecture. Supervised training was conducted using paired low- and high-resolution datasets generated by embedding computational fluid dynamics simulations of aortic flow into in vivo thoracic MRI backgrounds, followed by controlled spatial and temporal downsampling. CURRENT was evaluated for two-fold spatial and temporal SR against 4DFlowNet and for two-fold spatiotemporal SR against sequential spatial and temporal SR pipelines based on 4DFlowNet. Image-based and k-space-based quantitative metrics were considered and conventional interpolation were used as baselines. In addition, the impact of CURRENT's spatiotemporal SR on physics-informed relative pressure estimation was assessed. Finally, the ability of CURRENT to generalize from synthetic training data to in vivo acquisitions was investigated.
  Results: CURRENT achieved superior spatial SR performance and comparable temporal SR performance to dedicated 4DFlowNet models while providing a unified solution for both tasks. Simultaneous spatiotemporal SR using CURRENT yielded more accurate relative pressure estimates than either spatial or temporal SR alone and outperformed sequential 4DFlowNet-based spatial and temporal SR approaches. Furthermore, the proposed model demonstrated promising generalization to in vivo 4D flow MRI data despite being trained exclusively on synthetic datasets.
  Conclusion: CURRENT provides a unified framework for spatial and temporal SR of 4D flow MRI. The proposed approach has the potential to enhance physics-based analysis and broaden the applicability of accelerated 4D flow MRI.

</details>


### [76] [Where Do Vision-Language Models Get Left and Right? Position-Code Counterfactuals in Qwen2.5-VL, Qwen3-VL and InternVL3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14060)
*Enhao Cui,Junzhi Yang,Yaoshun Lin,Yuhan Feng,Zijian Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 视觉语言模型的左右方向判断主要由ViT位置编码决定：仅镜像ViT宽度位置即可在84.3–93.2%的CV-Bench左右题上复现翻转图像的答案，而LLM侧的id/顺序镜像影响很小；该效应集中在全局注意力ViT块（尤以最后一层为主）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM判断左右方向可能依赖三条线索：图像块内内容朝向、ViT位置编码、LLM中的token位置与顺序。作者要厘清三者中哪一条是必要且充分的，以定位左右方向能力的机制来源。

Method: 消融实验证明ViT编码的必要性；随后进行"充分性"检验——只镜像位置编码而不动图像内容。在Qwen2.5-VL-7B、Qwen3-VL-4B、InternVL3-2B（及Qwen2.5-VL-3B）上镜像ViT宽度维度位置，并与半网格平移、LLM侧id镜像、token顺序镜像对照；定位到全局注意力ViT块（主要为最后一层）；在What'sUp上对比内容+token顺序翻转与位置编码翻转；辅以随机置换的事后分析（对象顺序反转、置换命中镜像关系的比例、按"回到原答案"计分、剩余错误的关系一致性）。

Result: ViT宽度位置镜像在84.3–93.2%的CV-Bench左右题上复现翻转答案（Qwen2.5-VL-3B为87.5%，部分属左向回退）；半网格平移也能翻转多数答案（双选项下亦计为扰动）；ViT编码完整时LLM侧镜像只改变少量左右答案（Qwen2.5-VL的id检验较弱）。What'sUp上翻转内容与token顺序（而非位置编码）更能保持上下/叠放关系答案。事后分析：有定位对象时随机置换在反转对象顺序下多翻转18.9–31.0个百分点；Qwen2.5-VL/Qwen3-VL中随机置换带来的左右改变落在镜像关系上的比例多低于随机水平；以"回到原答案"计分时，仅在InternVL3-2B中上下支持与左右关系的对比清晰；多数残余错误在询问不同对象时给出同一关系。

Conclusion: 左右方向判断对ViT位置编码（尤其末层全局注意力块）高度依赖且近乎充分，LLM侧位置信息作用有限；上下/叠放关系则更依赖内容与token顺序。残余错误提示改进方向可能在关系绑定、对象定位或参考对象的使用上。事后分析对上述结论构成限定。

Abstract: A vision-language model may take left-right direction from the orientation of image content within patches, its vision transformer's (ViT) position code, or token positions and order in its language model (LLM). Ablations show the ViT code is necessary; we test sufficiency by mirroring only a position code. In Qwen2.5-VL-7B, Qwen3-VL-4B and InternVL3-2B, mirroring ViT width positions reproduces the flipped-image answer on 84.3-93.2% of CV-Bench left-right questions (Qwen2.5-VL-3B: 87.5%, partly a left fallback). With two options, this also counts disruption; a half-grid shift flips most answers too. With the ViT code intact, LLM-side id and token-order mirrors change few left-right answers in all three series (a weak id test in Qwen2.5-VL). In Qwen2.5-VL-7B the effect sits in the global-attention ViT blocks, mostly the last. On What'sUp, flipping content and token order, not the position code, keeps more on/under than left-right answers at the flipped-image answer. Post hoc analyses qualify these results. On CV-Bench items with located objects, random permutations flip 18.9-31.0 points more answers when reversing the objects' order than when keeping it. On What'sUp, in Qwen2.5-VL and Qwen3-VL, the share of the left-right changes made by random permutations that land on the mirror relation is mostly below chance. Counted as returns to the original answer, the What'sUp contrast of support with left-right relations is clear only in InternVL3-2B. Most remaining errors give the same relation whichever object is asked about, suggesting that repair may target relation binding, object localization or use of the reference object.

</details>


### [77] [Obscured Wildfire Flame Detection by Spatio-temporal Analysis of Smoke Patterns Using Frame-wise Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14074)
*Uma Meleti,Abolfazl Razi,Fatemeh Afghah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于逐帧Transformer的RGB视频时空联合分析范式，利用烟雾运动时序模式检测可见与遮挡火情，在FLAME2数据集上分割前景Dice达92.61%、检测Precision/Recall为93.21%/91.73%、片段级分类准确率98.02%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统野火检测方法对图像或单帧视频进行独立分析，割裂了时空信息，难以检测被烟雾遮挡的火焰。作者希望利用连续视频帧中烟雾运动的时序模式来推断被遮挡火焰的存在，从而突破仅检测可见火焰的局限。同时，方法需适用于仅配备普通相机的低成本商用无人机，因此模型只使用RGB输入。

Method: 提出逐帧Transformer架构：在相邻帧之间施加注意力机制，同时保留帧内空间信息，区别于non-local block等将时空信息压缩为单一向量的常规注意力机制。并在不同空间分辨率下优化时序模式长度，以在降低计算成本的同时获得最高性能。实验在FLAME2数据集（RGB/IR双模态视频）的整理版本上进行：IR视频用于提取真值标注，模型仅以RGB视频训练。

Result: 分割前景Dice得分92.61%；目标检测Precision 93.21%、Recall 91.73%；片段级分类准确率98.02%。并通过消融实验验证了方法的有效性与泛化性。

Conclusion: 逐帧时空注意力可在仅用RGB输入条件下有效检测可见及遮挡火焰，说明联合空间与时序分析优于独立帧分析范式；方法具备在低成本无人机航拍场景与纯RGB数据集上的适用性和可推广性。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a new paradigm in wildfire management for detecting both visible and obscured fire using Deep Learning (DL). The essence of our method is the joint spatial and temporal analysis of RGB videos, embracing the fact that temporal patterns of smoke motions in consecutive video frames can indicate obscured fire flames. This perspective contrasts the traditional approach of independently analyzing images and individual video frames. To this end, we propose a frame-wise transformer architecture that applies an attention mechanism between the consecutive frames while preserving spatial information within frames as opposed to regular attention mechanisms like the non-local block, which combines spatial and temporal information into a single vector. We also optimize the temporal pattern lengths under different spatial resolutions to achieve the highest performance while reducing the computational cost. To investigate the applicability of our method in aerial image processing, we applied it to a curated version of the FLAME2 dataset that includes dual-mode RGB/IR videos. The IR video feed is used to extract ground truth, while the model is trained only on RGB videos to maintain applicability to lowcost commercial drones (which are equipped only with regular cameras) and facilitate processing existing RGB-only datasets. Our proposed method achieves a Segmentation Foreground Dice score of 92.61%, an Object Detection Precision and Recall rate of 93.21% and 91.73%, and clip-level classification accuracy of 98.02%, respectively. We also investigated the efficacy and generalizability of our method through ablation study

</details>


### [78] [ZoomGain: Mining Global-to-Zoom Perception Gaps for Fine-Grained Visual Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14076)
*Haozhan Shen,Liangbo He,Jun Lan,Huijia Zhu,Weiqiang Wang,Tiancheng Zhao,Jianwei Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 利用"全局-放大感知差距"自动构建细粒度视觉监督数据，提出ZoomGain数据流水线及1,000题双语基准ZoomGainBench，并证明9K样本训练即可显著提升VLM细粒度感知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度视觉感知要求VLM在复杂场景中定位并解释细微证据，但此类监督数据构建困难：问题需有正确、无歧义且真正依赖细粒度视觉细节（而非粗粒度线索或语言先验）的答案。人工标注验证代价高昂，而自动生成又受限于模型自身的细粒度感知能力不足。

Method: 观察到模型在区域放大后常能识别出原本难以察觉的细节，作者利用这一"全局→放大"感知差距，设计ZoomGain数据构建流水线：基于仅从放大视图中获得的视觉信息，生成针对全图的问题；用多模型在放大视图上验证答案；确保问题在全图上保持无歧义且无法靠语言先验作答；并专门为多项选择题生成高质量干扰项。仅需人工筛选（无人工QA编辑）即构建出ZoomGainBench。

Result: ZoomGainBench为1,000题中英双语基准，28个VLM中最佳仅达61.08分。失败分析显示模型错答多选题时偏好某类特定干扰项。仅在9K ZoomGain样本上训练，即显著提升4B/9B模型视觉感知能力，超越多个数千亿参数模型；训练的9B模型在多个基准上取得SoTA，如HR-Bench 4K上达90.25。

Conclusion: "全局→放大"感知差距可作为可扩展细粒度视觉监督的实用基础，同时服务于评测与训练，且数据效率极高。

Abstract: Fine-grained visual perception requires Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to locate and interpret subtle evidence within complex scenes. Constructing supervision for this ability is challenging: questions must have correct, unambiguous answers that depend on actual fine-grained visual details rather than coarse cues or language priors. Verifying these properties makes manual annotation labor-intensive and automatic generation difficult for models that themselves struggle with fine-grained perception. Yet the same models can often recognize such details once the region is zoomed in. We exploit this global-to-zoom perception gap and introduce ZoomGain, a data construction pipeline that constructs questions about the full image from visual information gained only by zooming in. It verifies each answer on the zoomed view with multiple models, ensures that each question remains unambiguous in the full image and cannot be answered from language priors, and specifically generates high-quality distractors for multiple-choice questions. With this pipeline and human-only screening (no manual QA editing), we build ZoomGainBench, a 1,000-question bilingual benchmark on which the best of 28 VLMs achieves only 61.08. Failure analysis reveals that models favor a particular type of distractor when answering multiple-choice questions incorrectly. Beyond evaluation, training on just 9K ZoomGain samples substantially improves the visual perception capabilities of our 4B/9B models, enabling them to outperform several hundred-billion-parameter models across various perception benchmarks. The trained 9B model achieves SoTA performance on several benchmarks, such as 90.25 on HR-Bench 4K. These results establish global-to-zoom perception gaps as a practical basis for scaling fine-grained visual supervision for both evaluation and training. Our code is available at https://github.com/inclusionAI/ZoomGain.

</details>


### [79] [Stylization as Geodesic Navigation on Diffusion Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14097)
*Yan Li,Zehao Li,Xinlong Sun,Fuchen Long,Xi Chen,Wenhan Luo,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Stylus：免训练的人脸风格迁移框架，将风格迁移视为在预训练扩散模型的弯曲潜流形上的测地线导航，通过内容-风格潜插值与自注意力查询优化的双控制机制实现强度可控的风格化，同时保持身份。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人脸风格化要求"看起来明显艺术化、但人物仍明显是同一人"的精细平衡。现有免训练与指令驱动方法缺乏这种控制能力：要么身份漂移，要么风格化过弱。

Method: 将人脸风格迁移建模为扩散模型潜流形上的导航，而非平坦欧氏空间插值。对插值得到的内容-风格点，在pullback度量下最小化比例加权离散测地线能量进行精化，并用Jacobian的秩一幂迭代近似使其可计算。该原理衍生两种互补控制：(1) 内容-风格潜空间插值，实现平滑语义融合；(2) 自注意力query优化，实现空间自适应风格注入。

Result: 在具有竞争力的风格化强度下，Stylus取得最强的身份与内容保持效果；双控制机制提供对风格化强度的连续、细粒度调节，且无需额外训练。

Conclusion: 以流形测地线能量最小化替代欧氏插值，是兼顾风格化强度与身份保持的有效途径；免训练框架即可实现连续可控的人脸风格迁移。

Abstract: Facial stylization asks for a delicate transformation: the image should look unmistakably artistic, yet the person should remain unmistakably the same. Existing training-free and instruction-driven methods often fail to expose such control, either drifting away from identity or producing only weak stylization. We introduce Stylus, a training-free framework that treats facial style transfer as navigation on the curved latent manifold of a pretrained diffusion model. Rather than interpolating in flat Euclidean space, \method refines the interpolated content--style point by lowering a ratio-weighted discrete geodesic energy under the pullback metric, made tractable by a rank-one power-iteration approximation of the Jacobian. This principle drives two complementary controls: content--style latent interpolation for smooth semantic fusion, and self-attention query optimization for spatially adaptive style injection. The resulting dual-control mechanism provides continuous, fine-grained control over stylization strength while preserving identity-critical facial structure, all without additional training. Experiments show that Stylus achieves the strongest identity and content preservation at competitive stylization strength.

</details>


### [80] [Looking One Step Ahead: An Anticipation-guided Agent for Object State Readout in Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14113)
*Yuanhe Tian,Yan Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 LASER，一种由"预期引导"的智能体式物体状态读出方法，用下一步可能的变化来决定当前应检查哪些属性，从而生成更精确的物体状态描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解活动进展需要跟踪其中物体随时间的状态变化，物体状态读出（object state readout）正是为此提供跨帧的状态与转移描述。然而多模态大模型（MLLM）生成的视觉描述虽详细，但用于区分相邻状态所需的属性往往刻画不足，导致状态难以被唯一辨识。

Method: LASER 接收按时间排序的帧与目标物体：1）将"预期的下一步变化"转化为有针对性的视觉问题，检查当前帧以确定属性取值；2）用后续帧的独立观测来校验状态读出与预测，迭代精炼状态记录并生成最终描述；3）由可复用的文本观测技能引导该流程，并通过将生成描述与参考描述的差异回溯到责任指令来精炼技能；4）在独立视频上依据"描述质量提升且与参考事实矛盾不增加"筛选候选修订。整个适配与推理过程模型参数冻结，评估时技能固定。

Result: 在 OSCaR 与 PLM RCap 数据集上的实验结果及进一步分析显示，LASER 相对强基线及现有方法均取得一致性提升。

Conclusion: 以"预期变化驱动当前属性检查"并结合后续帧校验与技能自精炼的无参数更新框架，能有效提升物体状态读出的准确性与可区分性，验证了该思路的有效性。

Abstract: Understanding the progress of an activity requires tracking how the objects involved change over time. Object state readout provides this information by describing a target object's states and transitions across video frames. Although multimodal large language models (MLLMs) generate detailed visual descriptions, these descriptions may leave the attributes needed to distinguish successive states insufficiently specified. In this paper, we propose look-ahead state readout (LASER), an anticipation-guided agent for object state readout that uses possible next-step changes to determine which attributes to inspect in the present. Given temporally ordered frames and a target object, LASER converts anticipated changes into targeted visual questions and inspects the current frame to establish attribute values. It then checks state readouts and forecasts against independent observations of subsequent frames, refining a state record used to generate the final description. Reusable textual observation skills guide this process and are refined by tracing discrepancies with reference captions to the responsible instructions. Candidate revisions are selected on separate videos based on improved caption quality without increased contradictions with reference facts. Model parameters remain frozen throughout adaptation and inference, and the revised skills remain fixed during evaluation. Experiment results and further analysis on OSCaR and PLM RCap show consistent improvements of LASER over strong baselines and existing approaches, which demonstrates the effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [81] [TAMEing the Open-World Personalization: Towards Open-Set Personalized MLLM Assistant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14118)
*Rongpei Hong,Jian Lang,Ting Zhong,Fan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个长上下文开集个性化多模态大模型助手TAME-O，通过免训练、即插即用的概念协调技能（CR Skill）在持续交互中自动解锁新概念并避免与已解锁概念混淆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化MLLM研究局限于静态、预定义的概念集合，需用户手动频繁扩充新概念，无法适应动态世界场景。

Method: 将传统MLLM个性化重构为开集（Open-Set）问题，设计训练无关、即插即用的Concept Reconciliation Skill（CR Skill），使模型在长时人机交互中自动解锁新概念、与已有概念区分，并持续精化对已知概念的建模。

Result: 在开集个性化设置下的大量实验验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: TAME-O为MLLM个性化提供了无需训练、可扩展至新概念的开集解决方案，支持概念在交互中持续累积与建模精化。

Abstract: Personalizing Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) as intelligent assistants has recently emerged as an active research topic, where an MLLM is expected to go beyond generic, one-size-fits-all replies, and instead generate responses grounded in user-specific objects, entities, and concepts encountered during interactions. Nevertheless, existing studies mainly focus on personalizing MLLMs on a static, predefined set of personalized concepts and require users to manually and frequently extend this set to accommodate newly emerging concepts, which is infeasible in dynamic-world scenarios. To address this limitation, we recast conventional MLLM personalization as an Open-Set problem, and propose TAME-O, the first long-context open-set personalized MLLM assistant. Specifically, TAME-O introduces a training-free, plug-and-play Concept Reconciliation Skill (CR Skill). It enables the assistant to automatically unlock novel concepts during prolonged human-MLLM interactions while preventing confusion with previously unlocked ones, allowing the MLLM to continually grasp more and more concepts and progressively refine the modeling of known ones. Extensive experiments under the open-set personalization setting underscore the efficacy of our method.

</details>


### [82] [CroCoMAE: Cross-modal self-supervised collaborative representation learning for SAR-optical remote sensing data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14124)
*Vito Recchia,Giuseppe Guarino,Cássio Fraga Dantas,Giuseppina Andresini,Annalisa Appice,Dino Ienco*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CroCoMAE，一个在ViT架构中统一对比学习与掩码图像建模的SAR-光学跨模态自监督框架，在图像级与patch级两个互补层次运作，在场景分类和语义分割基准上优于单模态SSL与现有跨模态SOTA框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAR与光学遥感互为补充但地面真值标注稀缺，自监督学习可缓解标签缺失，跨模态SSL能利用同址传感器的互补性；然而现有跨模态框架多以全局图像级表示对齐，或将两模态视为高度冗余，未充分挖掘细粒度的互补结构。

Method: CroCoMAE在ViT架构中统一对比学习与掩码图像建模，分两个层次：(1) 图像级——对class token施加模态内对比损失，获得全局判别且增强不变的表征；(2) patch级——用掩码图像建模驱动各模态细粒度表示，同时以跨模态对比目标将SAR与光学patch token对齐到共享子空间，并保持互补子空间不受约束以保留模态特有信息。

Result: 在覆盖场景分类与语义分割的两个SAR-光学基准上，相较单模态SSL基线有提升，并在多数配置下优于现有跨模态SOTA框架；在单模态与多模态评估、不同标签规模下均有增益，且语义分割上的提升最大。

Conclusion: 通过图像级与patch级双层设计显式区分共享与模态特有子空间，可有效利用SAR-光学细粒度互补性，为标注稀缺条件下的多模态遥感表征学习提供有效方案，并显示出对稠密预测任务的更强收益。

Abstract: Remote sensing increasingly captures the Earth's surface through complementary sensors such as SAR and optical, yet ground-truth annotations remain scarce, limiting supervised approaches. Self-supervised learning (SSL) addresses the label scarcity, and cross-modal SSL further exploits the complementarity between co-located sensors, but existing cross-modal frameworks align modalities mostly through global image-level representations or treat them as largely redundant, leaving their fine-grained complementary structure underexploited. We propose CroCoMAE, a cross-modal SSL framework for SAR-optical remote sensing that unifies contrastive learning and masked image modeling within a ViT-based architecture, operating at two complementary levels. At the image level, an intra-modality contrastive loss on the class token yields globally discriminative, augmentation-invariant representations. At the patch level, masked image modeling drives fine-grained per-modality representations, while a cross-modal contrastive objective aligns a shared subspace across SAR and optical patch tokens, leaving the complementary subspace unconstrained to retain modality-specific information. On two SAR-optical benchmarks covering scene classification and semantic segmentation, CroCoMAE improves over uni-modal SSL baselines and outperforms state-of-the-art cross-modal frameworks in most configurations, with gains in both single-modality and multi-modal evaluation across varying label regimes and the largest improvements on semantic segmentation.

</details>


### [83] [Continuous 3D Spatial Transcriptomics Reconstruction via Histology-Informed Implicit Neural Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14129)
*Kaito Shiku,Ryoma Bise,Ahtisham Fazeel Abbasi,Yuichiro Iwashita,Shumpei Takezaki,Andreas Dengel,Muhammad Nabeel Asim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HIM-INR：一种组织学信息引导的多任务隐式神经表示，从稀疏2D切片连续重建3D基因表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D空间转录组测量来自离散2D切片，因采集成本高而采样稀疏，亟需从稀疏观测重建连续3D表达图谱。

Method: 采用共享骨干的隐式神经表示（INR）联合重建基因表达谱与组织学表征，将密集采样的组织学图像作为辅助空间监督进行多任务学习；训练完成后无需组织学图像即可在任意3D坐标预测基因表达。

Result: 在两个3D ST数据集上，重建效果优于现有方法，且在缺失切片数量增多时优势更明显。

Conclusion: 组织学信息的多任务隐式表示可有效缓解3D ST稀疏采样问题，实现连续、可任意查询的3D基因表达重建。

Abstract: Three-dimensional spatial transcriptomics (3D ST) enables the analysis of gene-expression patterns across tissue depths. However, 3D ST measurements are acquired from discrete 2D tissue sections, which are often sparsely sampled due to high acquisition costs. To address this limitation, we propose a histology-informed multi-task implicit neural representation (HIM-INR) for continuous 3D gene-expression reconstruction from sparse ST observations. HIM-INR jointly reconstructs gene-expression profiles and histological representations using an INR with a shared backbone, leveraging densely sampled histological images as auxiliary spatial supervision. Once trained, HIM-INR predicts gene expression at arbitrary 3D coordinates without requiring histological images. Experiments on two 3D ST datasets demonstrate improved reconstruction over existing methods, particularly as the number of missing ST sections increases.

</details>


### [84] [Sim(3)-TurboReg: Robust and Efficient Scale-Aware Registration via Tri-Scale Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14135)
*Zijun Zhou,Shaocheng Yan,Zhenjun Zhao,Yutong Hu,Ke Yang,Yingdong Gu,Pengcheng Shi,Jiayuan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Sim(3)-TurboReg，用 Tri-Scale Consistency (TSC) 判定替代 TurboReg 兼容图中的绝对距离约束，在 90% 外点下实现高精度、低延迟的 Sim(3) 配准，并嵌入流式前馈 3D 重建系统，实现公里级高保真重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈 3D 基础模型虽几何保真度高，但受推理显存限制无法原生处理长序列；现有流式系统用滑窗分块规避，却引入严重的单目尺度歧义，需依赖 Sim(3) 配准解决，而该配准必须在外点污染下同时保证精度与效率。

Method: 重构 TurboReg 的兼容图构建方式：以严格的 Tri-Scale Consistency（三尺度一致性）准则取代绝对距离约束来判定兼容边；理论分析表明 TSC 可同时抑制错配外点与坐标畸变外点；将所得求解器集成进现有流式系统。

Result: 在 90% 外点的受控未知尺度配准任务上，精度与 RANSAC 相当，中位时延降低 92.5%；在 KITTI 上的 DA3-Streaming 中，平均 ATE RMSE 改善 22.2%，平均端到端耗时减少 16.6%；支持前馈模型的公里级高保真重建。

Conclusion: 基于 TSC 的兼容图构建能有效替代绝对距离判据，使 Sim(3) 配准在高外点率下兼顾鲁棒性与效率，并可直接赋能现有流式前馈 3D 重建流水线。代码已开源。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D foundation models recover geometry at high fidelity, yet inference memory constraints keep them from processing long sequences natively. Current streaming systems work around this limit with sliding-window chunking, which introduces severe monocular scale ambiguity. Resolving this ambiguity requires Sim(3) registration that stays accurate and efficient under outlier contamination. We introduce Sim(3)-TurboReg, which reformulates the compatibility graph construction of TurboReg by replacing absolute distance constraints with a strict Tri-Scale Consistency (TSC) criterion. Our analysis shows that TSC suppresses both mismatching and coordinate-distortion outliers. We integrate the resulting solver into existing streaming systems and obtain high-fidelity, kilometer-scale reconstruction for feed-forward models. On controlled unknown-scale registration at 90% outliers, ours matches RANSAC accuracy with 92.5% lower median latency. In DA3-Streaming on KITTI, ours improves mean ATE RMSE by 22.2% and reduces mean end-to-end time by 16.6%. Code: https://github.com/ivan-zzj/Sim3-TurboReg

</details>


### [85] [SpecValley: Aggregation as Spectral Valley in Dense Heterophilic Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14176)
*Christoph Jahn,Delvin Bacho,Urs Waldmann,Bastian Goldluecke*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将异配图节点分类的瓶颈定位在传播前的"度归一化聚合"上，指其响应 1−λ 在 λ=1 处形成与数据无关、恰落在稠密图谱聚集区的"谱谷"；据此提出可学习聚合 SpecValley 加普通谱滤波器，并通过能量预算与消融证明增益来自聚合而非滤波器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异配图节点分类中，邻域聚合会削弱承载标签的信号。现有工作一味提升传播算子的表达能力，却把聚合保留为度归一化。作者指出度归一化的响应 1−λ 在 λ=1 处归零，构成一个位置由归一化而非数据决定的固定中频谷，而稠密图的特征值恰好在此聚集，故应当先改进表示（聚合）而非传播。

Method: 两阶段架构：先可学习聚合，再普通谱滤波。SpecValley 对每个图学习谱谷的位置、锐度与两侧增益。理论部分形式化聚合算子与下游滤波器的交互；证明度归一化响应在特征值聚集点处为零；给出谱能量预算，证明聚合移除的频带能量无法被任何线性谱滤波器恢复；并提出以"标签能量损失"度量该谷的实际代价，逐图量化。

Result: 在异配基准上取得强性能；通过覆盖多种聚合算子与下游滤波器的受控消融，将增益归因于聚合环节而非滤波器。研究亦表明"谱拥挤"本身不构成收益证据，收益大小取决于谷所移除的标签能量。

Conclusion: 聚合不是可忽略的预处理，而是异配图表示学习的核心自由度；应在设计传播前显式参数化并学习聚合的谱响应，且用标签能量而非谱拥挤来评估其价值。

Abstract: Node classification in heterophilic graphs remains an open problem: aggregating over neighbourhoods can damp precisely the part of the signal that carries the labels. The field has answered with ever more expressive propagation operators, leaving the aggregation step that precedes them fixed by degree normalisation. However, degree normalisation acts as a spectral valley in its own right, attenuating a fixed middle band whose position at lambda equal 1 is set by the normalisation rather than by the data, exactly where the eigenvalues of dense graphs accumulate. We therefore begin with the representations rather than the propagation, in two stages: a learnable aggregation followed by an ordinary spectral filter. The first, SpecValley learns the position, sharpness and flank gains of the valley for every graph. We show formally how the aggregation operator and downstream filter interact. We establish that the response 1 minus lambda of degree normalisation vanishes exactly at that accumulation point. A spectral energy budget shows that what aggregation removes from a band, no linear spectral filter can recover. Crowding alone says nothing about the benefit. That is decided by how much label energy the valley removes, which we measure for every graph. SpecValley achieves strong results across heterophilic benchmarks, and a controlled ablation over aggregation operators and downstream filters attributes the gain to aggregation rather than the filter.

</details>


### [86] [DailyWildfireCAN: A Dataset and Benchmark for Next-Day Wildfire Spread Prediction in Canada](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14207)
*Mahacine Ettahri,Hager Radi Abdelwahed,Toluwaleke Olutayo,Daniel Aloise,Gaétan Marceau Caron*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DailyWildfireCAN，一个面向加拿大全域次日野火蔓延预测的多模态、多时相数据集与基准，空间分辨率90米、时间分辨率24小时，覆盖2017–2024年8个火季的2440起野火事件，整合33个跨地形、气象、土地覆盖、植被与森林结构、人类影响指数及卫星影像的特征，并建立单时相/多时相模型对比基准与多维评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加拿大广袤北方森林与暖化气候使极端火季的频率和强度上升，野火成为严峻的生态与社会经济挑战；次日蔓延预测有助于火情管理，但需刻画气象、可燃物与地形的复杂交互。深度学习虽有潜力，却高度依赖代表性数据集，而现有研究主要基于美国数据，加拿大火环境严重缺乏代表性。

Method: 构建DailyWildfireCAN数据集：以90米空间分辨率、24小时时间分辨率连续追踪多日火事件蔓延过程，保留火线几何、局地可燃物差异与地形效应等细尺度信息；覆盖8个火季共2440起事件，融合33个多模态特征（地形、气象、土地覆盖、植被与森林结构、人类影响指数、卫星影像）；并搭建单时相与多时相预测模型的综合基准及多维评估框架。

Result: 建立了包含单时相与多时相模型的综合基准；评估框架从野火特征、地理区域、时间条件三个维度的鲁棒性，空间预测质量，以及对参考标签变化的敏感性等方面进行多维评测。

Conclusion: DailyWildfireCAN填补了加拿大野火环境下深度学习蔓延预测数据与基准的空白，其高分辨率、多模态、多时相设计支撑细尺度每日蔓延预报研究，并为模型鲁棒性与评估标准化提供可复用的基准平台。

Abstract: Wildfires represent a critical ecological and socio-economic challenge in Canada, where vast boreal forests and a warming climate have amplified the frequency and severity of extreme fire seasons. Predicting next-day wildfire spread can support fire management, but requires capturing complex interactions among weather, fuels, and terrain. Deep learning has emerged as a promising approach to learning these relationships from heterogeneous environmental data, yet its development and evaluation depend on representative datasets. Existing deep learning research on wildfire spread relies predominantly on US-focused datasets, leaving Canadian fire environments underrepresented. To address this gap, we present DailyWildfireCAN, a multi-modal, multi-temporal dataset and benchmark for predicting next-day wildfire spread across Canada. The dataset tracks continuous fire progression over multi-day events at a high spatial and temporal resolution of 90-m and 24-hour, respectively. This resolution preserves fine-scale information relevant to fire-edge geometry, local fuel variations, and terrain effects, supporting research toward improved daily wildfire spread forecasting. DailyWildfireCAN covers 2,440 wildfire events across eight fire seasons 2017-2024 and integrates 33 features across diverse modalities, including topography, meteorology, land cover, vegetation and forest structure, human influence indices, and satellite imagery. We establish a comprehensive benchmark comparing mono-temporal and multi-temporal prediction models, accompanied by a multi-dimensional evaluation framework assessing robustness across wildfire characteristics, geographic regions, and temporal conditions, spatial prediction quality, and sensitivity to reference label variations.

</details>


### [87] [ViGA-FLAME: Aligning Latent Visual and Geometric Representations for Robust 3D Head Expression Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14218)
*Wen Hao He,Jiacheng Wu,Jie Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ViGA-FLAME两阶段框架：先用PG-VAE构建规范化头部表面与FLAME参数的统一几何隐空间，再用非对称注意力的ViGA将预训练语义视觉特征适配映射到该隐空间，实现鲁棒的3D头部表情估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图3D头部表情估计需将视觉表观映射到面部几何。现有前馈方法在严重面部形变和大视角下鲁棒性不足；同时几何细节重建与可解释参数回归往往割裂，缺乏与语义视觉特征对齐的结构化隐先验。

Method: 两阶段：1）Parametric-Geometric VAE（PG-VAE）联合学习规范化头部表面与FLAME参数的统一表示，通过几何与参数监督及双解码分支间一致性约束，连接表面细节与参数结构，支持细节重建和可解释参数恢复；2）Visual-Geometric Adapter（ViGA）以非对称注意力将预训练语义视觉编码器特征映射进该隐空间，不改变原视觉流，从而将图像证据锚定于几何隐先验。

Result: 在严重面部形变与大视角条件下实现稳健的3D头部表情估计，相比竞争性前馈方法以更高效率提升鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 以结构化几何隐空间为桥梁对齐语义视觉特征，是一种有效且高效的鲁棒3D头部表情估计范式。

Abstract: Estimating 3D head expression from a single image requires mapping visual appearance to underlying facial geometry. We propose ViGA-FLAME, a two-stage framework that aligns semantic visual features with a structured geometric latent space. First, a Parametric-Geometric Variational Autoencoder (PG-VAE) learns a unified representation of canonical head surfaces and FLAME parameters, supporting both detailed surface reconstruction and interpretable parameter recovery. Supervision on geometry and parameters, together with consistency between the two decoding branches, connects surface detail with parametric structure. Second, a trainable Visual-Geometric Adapter (ViGA) maps features from a pretrained semantic visual encoder into this latent space. Asymmetric attention enables ViGA to align semantic image features from the visual encoder with the structured geometric information represented in the PG-VAE latent space, without altering the original visual stream. By grounding image evidence in this latent prior, ViGA-FLAME enables robust 3D head expression estimation across severe facial deformations and large viewing angles, efficiently improving robustness over competing feed-forward methods.

</details>


### [88] [Location Matters: Sparse Latent-Variance Attacks for Face Privacy against Diffusion Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14254)
*Shuting Yang,Shuxin Li,Linyan Dai,Xinwei Zhang,Dong Huang,Renyang Liu,See-Kiong Ng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SEAL：一种仅修改人脸图像5%空间位置的稀疏对抗保护框架，通过可微top-k掩码联合优化扰动位置与数值，并以VAE潜变量方差为目标、辅以显著性引导的分散惩罚，在保持高质量图像的同时有效阻断扩散模型的个体身份习得。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像扩散模型仅需少量参考照片即可学习并复现个人身份，带来未经授权与误导性内容生成的风险。现有主动防御多依赖稠密 L∞ 有界扰动，累积失真会在人脸结构附近显现；而近期提升保护图像质量的方法往往牺牲了保护效果。作者希望同时兼顾高质量与强保护。

Method: 提出 SEAL（Sparse Encoder Attack on Latent Variance）稀疏对抗保护框架：1）可微 top-k 掩码机制，联合优化扰动的空间位置与数值，仅作用于5%的空间位置；2）以冻结 VAE 编码器潜变量分布上的方差为导向的目标函数，破坏身份一致的表征；3）显著性引导的分散惩罚，抑制扰动在感知显著的人脸区域聚集。

Result: 在 CelebA-HQ 与 VGGFace2 上，SEAL 在对比方法中取得最佳保护图像质量：PSNR 超过41 dB、SSIM 达0.97、LPIPS 至多0.07；同时经扩散个性化后身份相似度降至至多0.15。

Conclusion: 稀疏位置优化能够在保持高视觉质量的前提下实现强身份保护，表明保护质量与保护效果之间的权衡可被有效缓解。代码与数据已开源于 https://github.com/Lvxan/SEAL。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models can learn and reproduce an individual's identity from only a few reference photos, creating risks of unauthorized and misleading content generation. Proactive defenses address this risk by adding adversarial perturbations to facial images before release, thereby disrupting identity acquisition during downstream personalization. However, existing methods typically rely on dense $L_\infty$-bounded perturbations, whose accumulated distortions can become visible around facial structures. Recent methods improve protected-image quality, but often at the cost of reduced protection effectiveness. In this paper, we propose Sparse Encoder Attack on Latent Variance (SEAL), a sparse adversarial protection framework that modifies only 5% of the spatial locations in a facial image. SEAL combines a differentiable top-k masking scheme that jointly optimizes perturbation locations and values with a variance-oriented objective over the frozen VAE encoder's latent distribution to disrupt identity-consistent representations. A saliency-guided dispersion penalty further discourages perturbation concentration in perceptually salient facial regions. Experiments on CelebA-HQ and VGGFace2 show that SEAL achieves the best protected-image quality among the compared methods, exceeding 41 dB PSNR with 0.97 SSIM and at most 0.07 LPIPS, while reducing identity similarity to at most 0.15 after diffusion personalization. These results demonstrate that sparse location optimization enables strong identity protection while preserving high visual quality. Code and data are available at https://github.com/Lvxan/SEAL

</details>


### [89] [AutoThreshold: Joint Confusion-Profile Learning for Adaptive Mask Binarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14279)
*Shaokai Wu,Xunchu Zhou,Pinzhi Wang,Bo Dong,Xiao Cui,Qi Kong,Yue Ding,Hongtao Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AutoThreshold 提出一种后验阈值策略：直接预测正负像素在各分数区间上的联合质量分布，从而参数化阈值索引的混淆计数，并可解码得到单调的混淆轮廓，无需重训即可为任意 F_β 目标选择阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一在验证集上调优的阈值在密集分数分布发生漂移时容易失效（brittle）。同时，直接对阈值效用建模或分别估计患病率与类条件率再相乘，会引入估计误差与不一致性。作者希望获得一种无需重训分割头、能随 F_β 目标与分布漂移自适应、且保持混淆计数自洽的阈值选择方法。

Method: 提出 AutoThreshold 后验策略：预测正、负像素在分数分箱上的联合质量（joint mass），直接参数化阈值索引的混淆计数（TP/FP/FN/TN），避免患病率与类条件率的独立乘积；经 softmax 归一化与累积解码得到单调、一致的混淆轮廓；再由 plug-in 解码器对任意 F_β 目标选阈值。实验：(1) 三个独立训练的 Kvasir 分割管线迁移到四个息肉数据集做外部评估，与 continuous Otsu 对比；(2) 参考管线上三个 head 种子复现；(3) 直接效用 Ridge 作为对照；(4) F_0.5/Dice/F_2 多目标检验；(5) KITTI Road 上 leave-one-corruption-family-out 的分布偏移评估，与标量回归对比。

Result: 外部迁移：Joint 相比 continuous Otsu 提升 0.0080 ± 0.0046 macro Dice，3/3 次运行均为正增益。参考管线三个种子达 0.6324 ± 0.0027，高于 Otsu 0.0085；直接效用 Ridge 为 0.6342，配对差值区间跨零（即与 Joint 无显著差异）。同一批 Joint checkpoint 在 F_0.5、Dice、F_2 上均优于 Otsu。KITTI Road 分布偏移下，Joint 比标量回归高 0.0082 [0.0064, 0.0101]，而直接效用再高 0.0022。

Conclusion: 联合混淆预测是一种有用且可按指标重定向（metric-retargetable）的归纳偏置，在分布漂移与多 F_β 目标下稳定优于 Otsu 与标量回归；但作者明确不主张其给出 Bayes 最优的 F_β 决策。直接效用建模仍具竞争力，说明收益主要来自混淆轮廓的结构化约束而非效用回归本身。

Abstract: A single validation-tuned threshold can become brittle when dense-score distributions shift. We introduce AutoThreshold, a post-hoc policy that predicts the joint mass of positive and negative pixels over score bins. This target directly parameterizes threshold-indexed confusion counts, avoiding products of independently estimated prevalence and class-conditional rates. Softmax normalization and cumulative decoding yield coherent, monotone confusion profiles; a plug-in decoder then selects a threshold for any $F_β$ objective without retraining the head. Across three independently trained Kvasir pipelines transferred to four polyp datasets, Joint exceeds continuous Otsu by $0.0080 \pm 0.0046$ external macro Dice, with positive gains in 3/3 runs. On the reference pipeline, three head seeds achieve $0.6324 \pm 0.0027$, 0.0085 above Otsu; direct-utility Ridge achieves 0.6342, with the paired difference interval spanning zero. The same Joint checkpoints outperform Otsu for $F_{0.5}$, Dice, and $F_2$. On developmental KITTI Road leave-one-corruption-family-out evaluation, Joint improves over scalar regression by 0.0082 [0.0064, 0.0101], while direct utility is 0.0022 higher. These results establish joint confusion prediction as a useful, metric-retargetable inductive bias, without claiming Bayes-optimal $F_β$ decisions.

</details>


### [90] [Textual Response-Aware Guidance for Zero-Shot Adversarial Robustness of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40309)
*Yubo Cui,Xianchao Guan,Zheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文分析视觉语言模型对抗微调中视觉表征漂移问题，指出现有特征匹配与预测分布匹配两类预训练引导的互补局限，提出文本响应感知引导（TRAG），利用预训练文本编码器对输入嵌入局部变化的响应构建锚点级约束，并按图文相关性聚合为图像自适应约束，实现SOTA的干净与鲁棒性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM零样本泛化强但易受对抗攻击。现有对抗微调依赖预训练模型引导以保持图文对齐，但两类主流引导各有缺陷：匹配视觉特征对所有方向等权约束，覆盖广但缺乏语义侧重；匹配预测分布侧重语义相关方向，却受候选文本集合与预测概率限制。二者均可能损害预训练图文关系与零样本性能，因此需要一种兼顾方向语义性与覆盖性的引导方式。

Method: 提出TRAG（Textual Response-Aware Guidance）。以预训练文本编码器对输入嵌入局部扰动的响应为依据刻画视觉表征漂移约束：先在语义锚点周围依据响应方向与幅度构建锚点级约束，再利用预训练图文相关性（image–anchor relevance）对这些锚点约束加权聚合，得到图像自适应的约束项并用于对抗微调。

Result: 跨多种架构与任务的广泛实验表明，方法在干净精度与对抗鲁棒性上均达到state-of-the-art；进一步分析显示TRAG有助于保持预训练图文关系。

Conclusion: TRAG通过文本响应引导实现语义感知且覆盖充分的表征漂移约束，弥补特征匹配与分布匹配的不足，在提升鲁棒性的同时更好地保持预训练图文对齐与零样本泛化能力。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) exhibit strong zero-shot generalization but remain vulnerable to adversarial attacks. Existing adversarial fine-tuning methods often use pre-trained model guidance to preserve image--text alignment while improving robustness. Our analysis of visual representation drift reveals complementary limitations of two representative forms of guidance. Matching visual features constrains all directions equally, providing broad coverage without semantic emphasis. Matching prediction distributions prioritizes semantically relevant directions, but remains limited by the candidate texts and prediction probabilities. These limitations can hinder preservation of pre-trained image--text relationships and degrade zero-shot performance. We therefore propose Textual Response-Aware Guidance (TRAG), which constrains visual representation drift using the pre-trained text encoder's responses to local variations in input embeddings. TRAG constructs anchor-level constraints from the response directions and magnitudes around semantic anchors, then aggregates them using pre-trained image--anchor relevance, yielding an image-adaptive constraint. Extensive experiments across architectures and tasks demonstrate state-of-the-art clean and robust performance. Further analysis shows that TRAG helps preserve pre-trained image--text relationships.

</details>


### [91] [UniFlow-RS: Unified Generative Prediction for Heterogeneous Remote-Sensing Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14281)
*Jianlong He,Liang Liao,Jing Xiao,Chaofeng Chen,Gui-Song Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniFlow-RS 提出用单一共享的潜在流匹配预测器替代任务特定的解码器与损失，统一遥感异构任务的预测过程，用单一模型在六个任务、十三个数据集上取得领先或持平性能，并具备强零样本迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感基础模型虽在表征层面统一了多模态与多任务，但预测机制仍是任务特定的：各自的解码器、预测头与目标函数导致预测过程割裂，无法共享。因此作者追问：异构遥感任务能否共享同一预测过程。

Method: 核心是单一潜在流匹配（latent flow matching）预测器。针对两大挑战：(1) 预测空间统一——将异构任务输出映射为公共潜在空间中的结构化视觉目标，由同一预训练流预测器建模，并用任务相关条件（task-dependent conditioning）适配不同输入配置；(2) 联合优化协调——所有任务共用同一 Flow Matching 目标，去除任务特定预测损失；提出 Task-Modality Adaptive Timestep Sampling（TMAS），依据各任务-模态组的在线学习状态动态分配训练时间步，缓解跨任务与跨模态的优化失配。

Result: 单一共享模型覆盖 6 个任务、13 个数据集，在多个基准上超过近期任务特定 SOTA，其余基准保持竞争力。同一 checkpoint 在未见数据集、新任务设定、遮挡条件以及近红外假彩色影像上展现出强零样本迁移，且无需目标域适应。

Conclusion: 异构遥感任务可以在统一生成式流匹配框架下共享预测过程，任务特定头与损失并非必需；时间步采样的自适应调度是平衡跨任务/模态优化的关键。该工作为遥感基础模型从“统一表征”走向“统一预测”提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Remote-sensing foundation models increasingly unify representations across sensing modalities and downstream tasks, yet their prediction mechanisms remain largely task-specific. Separate decoders, prediction heads, and objectives leave prediction fragmented across tasks. This naturally raises a question: can heterogeneous remote-sensing tasks share the prediction process? UniFlow-RS answers this question with a single latent flow-matching predictor shared across tasks. This requires addressing two key challenges: unifying heterogeneous prediction spaces and harmonizing joint optimization across tasks. First, we cast heterogeneous task outputs into structured visual targets in a common latent space, allowing them to be modeled by the same pretrained flow predictor. Task-dependent conditioning further accommodates differences in input configurations. Second, we optimize all tasks with a common Flow Matching objective, eliminating task-specific prediction losses. To mitigate optimization mismatch across tasks and modalities, we introduce Task-Modality Adaptive Timestep Sampling (TMAS), which dynamically allocates training timesteps according to the online learning state of each task-modality group. Using a single shared model, UniFlow-RS handles six tasks and thirteen datasets, surpassing recent task-specific state-of-the-art methods on several benchmarks while remaining competitive on the others. Beyond these benchmarks, the same checkpoint demonstrates strong zero-shot transfer across unseen datasets, task settings, occlusion conditions, and near-infrared false-color imagery, without target-specific adaptation. Codes are available at https://github.com/MindVision-AI/UniFlow-RS.

</details>


### [92] [TaoMate-R1: Temporal Role Specialization for Streaming Multi-Reference Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14307)
*Qijun Gan,Chenwei Zhang,Xinyang Li,Meiguang Jin,Junfeng Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 TaoMate-R1，一种流式多参考视频生成器，按"时间角色专门化"（场景建立 vs. 延续）组织低秩适配器参数，并配合 Self Gradient Forcing 联合训练，在边界一致性与时序相似度上优于按操作专门化的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式多参考视频生成需要先根据指定的人物与物体建立场景，再在指令变化下持续延续。现有参数分配多按"操作"划分（去噪与上下文写入分离），未必与场景建立/延续这一时间角色结构相匹配，因此需要探明更合理的适配参数共享方案。

Method: 在冻结主干上为场景建立与延续分别配置独立的低秩适配器，每个适配器同时承担其角色下的去噪与上下文写入；采用 Self Gradient Forcing，通过各角色自身构建的历史对两个角色进行联合训练。并用固定回放的梯度分析刻画两种共享方案所允许的更新方向。

Result: 在 32 个多参考案例、相同双适配器预算下比较两种方案；在十段约两分钟、带变化指令与共享教师音频条件的流上，TaoMate-R1 将平均边界亮度跳变从 1.96 降至 0.613，并具有更高的边界特征相似度；在短交互、长流与公开参考组合上取得更高的平均 CLIP 与 DINO 时序相似度。

Conclusion: 结果支持在流式多参考生成中按场景建立与延续对适配参数进行分组；代码与模型已开源。

Abstract: Streaming multi-reference video generation establishes a scene from specified people and objects, then continues it under changing instructions. We introduce TaoMate-R1, a streaming multi-reference generator that organizes adaptation parameters through temporal role specialization. It assigns separate low-rank adapters to scene establishment and continuation on a frozen backbone. Each adapter performs both denoising and context writing for its role; Self Gradient Forcing jointly trains the roles through the history they construct. This allocation contrasts with operation-based specialization, which separates denoising from context writing across all chunks. A fixed-replay gradient analysis characterizes the update directions admitted by the two sharing schemes. On 32 multi-reference cases, we compare the schemes at the same two-adapter budget. Across ten approximately two-minute streams with changing instructions and shared teacher-audio conditioning, TaoMate-R1 reduces the mean boundary luminance jump from 1.96 to 0.613, with higher boundary feature similarity. It also achieves higher mean CLIP and DINO temporal similarity across short interactions, long streams, and public reference compositions. These results support grouping adaptation parameters by scene establishment and continuation for streaming multi-reference generation. Our code and model are released at https://github.com/TaoLiveAIGC/TaoMate-H3.

</details>


### [93] [What Factors Matter for Collection-Level Counting with Human-AI Collaboration?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14380)
*Seoyun Jeong,Logan Lawrence,Owen L. Martin,Benjamin Koger,Daniel Sheldon,Subhransu Maji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文在大规模图像集合的"人在回环"计数框架下，系统比较了模型、估计器与自适应策略的选择问题，覆盖12个跨领域任务；发现多模态模型是强免训练起点，微调检测器在大标注预算与重复部署下更优，模型引导的优先标注显著提升效率，且小规模试点即可预估模型排序质量；在叉角羚航拍实测中仅审查中位数3.2%的图像即达到±5%容差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学与管理决策常需估计大图像集合中目标对象总数。穷尽式人工标注不可行，直接使用模型预测又存在偏差（尤其在域偏移下）。已有的人类-AI混合估计方法虽能结合不完美预测与有限人工核验得到可靠的集合级估计，但缺乏面向新部署任务的实践指导：从业者应如何在模型、估计器与自适应策略之间做选择，尚无系统性依据。

Method: 构建统一的human-in-the-loop计数框架，在12个任务上开展受控比较，任务涵盖生物医学显微图像、地面与航拍野生动物调查、城市构筑物俯视影像；对比不同模型类型（免训练多模态模型 vs. 微调检测器）、估计器与自适应标注策略（模型引导优先采样 vs. 均匀采样），并考察标注预算与重复部署的影响；进一步用小型试点集估计各模型的排序质量（ranking quality），以形成策略选择准则。

Result: 现代多模态模型在多数任务上提供强且免训练的起点；随标注预算增大及重复部署，微调检测器优势显现。以模型预测优先决定人工标注单元（而非均匀抽样）可进一步提升估计效率。模型排序质量可由小规模试点集有效估计，从而为新计数任务的策略选择提供实用指导。在航拍叉角羚调查的实地部署中，任务特化后的方法在仅审查中位数3.2%采集图像的情况下满足调查±5%的容差要求。

Conclusion: 研究为human-AI集合级计数提供了可操作的选择指南：按标注预算与部署频次选择模型，采用模型引导的优先标注以提升效率，并通过小规模试点预估排序质量来指导新任务策略配置；方法在真实航拍调查中达到实用精度与极高人工成本节省。

Abstract: Many scientific and policy decisions require estimating the total number of target objects across a large image collection. Exhaustive human labeling is often infeasible, while raw model predictions can be biased, especially under domain shift. Recent human-AI estimation methods combine imperfect model predictions with limited human verification to obtain reliable collection-level estimates. However, existing work provides little guidance on how practitioners should choose among models, estimators, and adaptation strategies for a new deployment. We study these choices within a common human-in-the-loop counting framework across 12 tasks spanning biomedical microscopy, ground-level and aerial wildlife surveys, and overhead imagery of urban structures. Our results show that modern multimodal models provide a strong, training-free starting point for many tasks, while fine-tuned detectors become more advantageous at larger labeling budgets and across repeated deployments. Estimation efficiency improves further when model predictions are used to prioritize which units humans label, rather than sampling uniformly. We also find that each model's ranking quality can be estimated with a small pilot set, yielding practical guidance for selecting strategies on new counting tasks. In a field deployment on aerial pronghorn surveys, a task-specific adaptation of these methods meets the survey's $\pm 5\%$ tolerance after reviewing a median of only $3.2\%$ of collected images.

</details>


### [94] [OnlyAct: Extracting Action Representations from Foundation Models for World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14420)
*Runjia Li,Minghao Chen,Junyu Xie,Philip Torr,Andrea Vedaldi,Tomas Jakab*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需标注的对比式适配框架，以预训练基础模型（V-JEPA 2）为起点，通过对比学习重塑其嵌入空间几何，从中提取动作信息并丢弃场景信息，从而获得更具表现力且场景不变的动作表示，并构建覆盖三维度的统一评测协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界模型受限于动作监督：动作标签稀缺、规格不统一且无法随无标注视频语料扩展。现有"从零学习潜在动作+重构瓶颈"的联合训练方案存在两点局限：(1) 从头学习动作空间泛化性差；(2) 重构瓶颈在表达力与分离性之间存在权衡，过紧丢失细节、过松导致场景泄漏。

Method: 不从零学习动作，而以预训练基础模型 V-JEPA 2 为起点，利用其帧对嵌入已蕴含丰富动作信息的特点，提出无标签对比适配框架：将不同场景但相同运动的帧对在嵌入空间拉近，将相同场景但不同运动的帧对推开，以此重塑预训练空间几何，且不施加重构瓶颈。

Result: 提出覆盖三个维度的统一评测协议（动作表达力、场景不变性、动作条件视频生成质量），涵盖机器人操作、相机导航、游戏与自动驾驶基准，其中多数在适配阶段未见。与无标注潜在动作方法相比，本文表示表达力显著更强且场景不变性相当，所得世界模型能更忠实地将演示动作迁移到新场景。

Conclusion: 以预训练基础模型的嵌入空间为起点、用对比目标实现"提取动作、丢弃场景"，是替代从零学习潜在动作与重构瓶颈的有效路径，可在无标注条件下获得更泛化、更可迁移的动作表示与世界模型。

Abstract: Video world models promise general-purpose interactive simulators, but their training is limited by action supervision: action labels are scarce, fragmented across incompatible specifications, and do not scale with the unlabeled video pretraining corpus. A common workaround jointly trains an action encoder to compress consecutive frames into a latent action and a decoder to reconstruct the next frame from the previous frame and that latent, forcing the latent to capture only new information. This has two limitations: learning the action space from scratch can limit generalization to unseen domains, and the bottleneck trades expressiveness for separation, with tighter settings losing nuance and looser ones risking scene leakage under domain shifts. We argue for a different starting point: rather than learning actions from scratch, we start from a pretrained foundation model (V-JEPA 2), whose embeddings of a frame pair already carry substantial action information after large-scale, diverse video pretraining. The remaining challenge is to extract the action while discarding the scene. We propose a label-free contrastive adaptation framework that pulls together frame pairs sharing the same motion under a different scene and pushes apart pairs from the same scene with different motion, reshaping the geometry of the pretrained space without imposing a reconstruction bottleneck. We introduce a unified evaluation protocol along three axes (action expressiveness, scene invariance, and action-conditioned video generation quality) across robot manipulation, camera navigation, game, and driving benchmarks, most of which are unseen during adaptation. Compared with annotation-free latent-action methods, our representations are substantially more expressive while matching their scene invariance, and they yield world models that transfer demonstrated actions to new scenes more faithfully.

</details>


### [95] [Metric-Aligned Group Relative Policy Optimization for Explainable Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14436)
*Luka Dragar,Peter Peer,Vitomir Štruc,Borut Batagelj*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"先预测后解释"的解耦式深度伪造检测与解释流水线：视觉基础模型集成做真伪分类，Qwen3-VL-8B生成决策条件下的解释，并对伪造图像用第二个Qwen3-VL-8B在无图像条件下压缩为非专家摘要；再用与评测指标对齐的GRPO优化解释器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度伪造检测器多以真/假二分类评估，而实际应用还要求给出预测所依据的视觉证据解释；同时联合视觉-语言模型在真实性判断上表现不佳，会对被篡改图像生成"真实"的叙述，因此需要将"判断"与"解释"解耦。

Method: 1) 视觉基础模型集成先对图像进行真伪分类；2) Qwen3-VL-8B以分类结果为条件生成视觉证据解释；3) 对伪造图像，第二个Qwen3-VL-8B在不输入图像的情况下将解释文本压缩为简短的非专业摘要；4) 采用与挑战赛指标对齐的Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)对两个解释器进行优化。实验基于XPlainVerse基准训练与评测。

Result: 在同等条件下，GRPO将复杂BERTScore-F1从0.686提升至0.712，并同时改善了grounding（实体F1与证据F1）；投稿在ACMMM 2026可解释深度伪造检测挑战赛中，复杂BERTScore-F1单项排名第一，总排名第七。

Conclusion: 将分类与解释解耦、并以决策为条件生成解释，可有效缓解联合视觉-语言模型的真实性误判与解释偏差；指标对齐的GRPO强化学习能显著提升解释质量与证据定位能力，验证了该流水线在可解释深度伪造检测任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Deepfake detectors are commonly evaluated on real/fake classification alone, but real-world applications require explanations of the visual evidence behind each prediction. Joint vision-language models struggle with authenticity assessment, producing "real" narratives for manipulated images. To address this, we propose a decoupled predict-then-explain pipeline as our submission to the ACM Multimedia 2026 Explainable Deepfake Detection Challenge, with methods trained and evaluated on the XPlainVerse benchmark. A vision-foundation-model ensemble first classifies the image, then a Qwen3-VL-8B model generates a decision-conditioned explanation of the visual evidence. For fake images, a second Qwen3-VL-8B compresses that text into a short non-expert summary without using the image. We further apply metric-aligned Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) to optimize both explainers against the challenge criteria. Under matched conditions, GRPO raises complex BERTScore-F1 from 0.686 to 0.712 and also improves grounding (entity and evidence F1); our submission ranked first on complex BERTScore-F1 and seventh overall.

</details>


### [96] [Reasoning-Aware Relational Representation Learning for Open-Vocabulary Scene Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13496)
*Jiawei Xiao,Boya Wang,Longtian Qiu,Shan Ning,Xuming He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RaRe框架，将MLLM生成的思维链（CoT）理由转化为结构化关系表示，直接优化理由派生表示空间以提升开放词汇场景图生成（OV-SGG）的关系判别性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OV-SGG方法依赖数据集特定的一目标—谓词共现模式，在长尾分布与噪声物体定位下导致关系表示有偏且纠缠，限制稀有关系识别与未见谓词泛化。

Method: Ree：①用自监督对比学习将CoT理由嵌入与文本化关系描述对齐；②用面向特异性的强化学习目标精炼理由生成，鼓励语义上对象对特定的关系证据；CoT不再仅作预测中间轨迹，而用于直接优化理由派生表示空间。

Result: 在VG150与PSG上相较基线实现更优的类别均衡识别；但学习带来的额外增益因数据集与候选框设置而异。

Conclusion: 以理由表示学习替代共现先验可缓解偏差与纠缠，但收益具数据集与提案设置依赖性，泛化稳定性仍待进一步验证。

Abstract: Open-Vocabulary Scene Graph Generation (OV-SGG) requires models to recognize visual relationships beyond the training vocabulary, yet existing methods often rely on dataset-specific object--predicate co-occurrence patterns. Under long-tailed distributions and noisy object localization, such reliance leads to biased, entangled relation representations, limiting both rare-relation recognition and generalization to unseen predicates. To address these challenges, we propose RaRe, a reasoning-aware relational representation learning framework that transforms MLLM-generated chain-of-thought (CoT) rationales into structured relation embeddings. Rather than using CoT only as an intermediate reasoning trace for final prediction, RaRe directly optimizes the rationale-derived representation space to improve inter-relation discriminability. Specifically, RaRe first aligns rationale embeddings with textualized relation descriptions through self-supervised contrastive learning, and then refines rationale generation with a specificity-oriented reinforcement learning objective that encourages semantically object-pair-specific relational evidence. Experiments on VG150 and PSG demonstrate improved category-balanced recognition over existing baselines, while the additional gains from learning vary across datasets and proposal settings. Code available at: https://artanic30.github.io/project_pages/RaRe.

</details>


### [97] [Dark Matters: VAE-Aware HDR Encoding for SDR-to-HDR Video Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13504)
*Donghoon Cha,Bomseok Park,Hansl von Kwon,Ku DoHyung,Hanwool Sul*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EquivFlow：一种紧凑、可逆的曝光等变编码，将曝光变化映射为加性偏移并保留尺度无关的色度分量，在不修改预训练VAE的前提下缓解SDR→HDR重建中暗部色度塌陷问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SDR-to-HDR重建是病态问题，需在保留原始内容的同时恢复过曝高光、压缩阴影与量化伪影。潜在扩散模型虽提供生成先验，但其效果受限于HDR信号通过预训练VAE的方式：常见对数类编码会在生成前抑制低亮度色度差异，导致冻结VAE潜空间中的"暗侧塌陷"。

Method: 提出EquivFlow——紧凑、可逆的曝光等变编码，把曝光变化映射为潜空间中的加性平移，同时保留尺度无关的色度分量，且无需改动VAE权重。在预训练图像与视频VAE上进行编码层测试，并将其集成到SDR-to-HDR视频扩散框架中。

Result: 编码层测试表明其曝光结构与深暗部色度传输特性在测试的多个宿主VAE上均得以保持；集成到SDR-to-HDR视频扩散后，在测试设置下取得一致的色度保真度提升，同时保持有竞争力的重建保真度。

Conclusion: 问题根源在于HDR信号编码方式而非VAE本身容量；通过曝光等变的可逆编码设计，可在冻结VAE下改善暗部色度恢复，为扩散式HDR重建提供即插即用的编码改进方案。

Abstract: SDR-to-HDR reconstruction is an ill-posed problem in which clipped highlights, crushed shadows, and quantization artifacts must be recovered while preserving the original content. Latent diffusion models provide powerful generative priors for restoring missing detail, but their effectiveness is constrained by how HDR signals pass through the pretrained VAE. We find that common log-like encodings suppress low-luminance chromatic differences before generation, producing a dark-side collapse in the frozen-VAE latent. We introduce EquivFlow, a compact, invertible exposure-equivariant encoding that maps exposure changes to additive shifts while retaining a scale-independent chromatic component, without modifying the VAE. Encoding-level tests across pretrained image and video VAEs show that its exposure structure and deep-dark chroma transport persist across the tested hosts. Integrated into SDR-to-HDR video diffusion, EquivFlow delivers consistent chromatic-fidelity gains across the tested settings while retaining competitive reconstruction fidelity.

</details>


### [98] [DUO: Dual Readouts of Diffusion Models for OOD Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13304)
*Alireza Javid,Shirin Shoushtari,Nuria González Prelcic*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无监督、免训练的扩散模型OOD检测方法DUO：将重建误差与中间去噪特征的"主敏感谱"信号双重读出融合，AUROC平均提升约6个点且推理成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型部署需检测训练分布外输入。现有基于重建误差的OOD检测隐含假设"ID样本重建更准确"，但OOD输入可能被同样好甚至更好地重建，导致该分数不可靠，需要互补的OOD信号。

Method: 提出以中间去噪特征的主敏感谱（leading sensitivity spectrum）作为互补OOD信号，并设计DUO——完全无监督、无需训练的检测器，对扩散模型进行双读出（重建误差+特征敏感度）融合打分。

Result: 相较最强基线，平均AUROC提升约6个百分点，同时显著降低推理成本；在near-OOD与高分辨率基准上达到SOTA。

Conclusion: 重建误差并非可靠的OOD判据，特征敏感度可作为有效补充；两者结合可在无需训练与标签的条件下实现更准、更省的高质量OOD检测。

Abstract: Diffusion models are powerful generative methods for learning and sampling from complex data distributions. Safely deploying these models requires detecting inputs that fall outside their training distribution. Because diffusion models recover clean samples from noisy inputs, reconstruction error is often used for out-of-distribution (OOD) detection. This approach assumes that in-distribution (ID) inputs are reconstructed more accurately than OOD inputs. However, OOD inputs may be reconstructed equally well or even better, making this score unreliable. We therefore propose the leading sensitivity spectrum of intermediate denoising features as a complementary OOD signal and demonstrate that it remains informative when reconstruction error is misleading. We introduce DUal-readout of diffusion models for OOD detection (DUO), a fully unsupervised, training-free OOD detector that combines reconstruction error and feature sensitivity. DUO improves average AUROC by approximately 6% points over the strongest baselines while substantially reducing inference cost, and achieves SOTA on near-OOD and high-resolution benchmarks.

</details>


### [99] [Distribution Shift Detection in Remote Sensing Imagery using Machine Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13510)
*Abdullah Almansour,Ozan K. Tonguz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对三种开源分布漂移检测工具（Alibi-Detect、NannyML、PyTorch-OOD）在无人机遥感跨数据集漂移场景下进行了实证评估，比较其检测有效性与计算延迟，揭示检测能力与响应时延之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UAV遥感的ML部署面临资源受限平台与训练-部署分布不一致（地理、传感器、季节等视觉变化）的双重挑战；除预测精度外，还需模型具备漂移检测与响应能力，而现有工具在检测效果与计算开销上的实际表现缺乏系统对比。

Method: 采用实证案例研究方法，基于跨数据集遥感数据构造分布漂移场景，对三种开源检测工具（Alibi-Detect、NannyML、PyTorch-OOD）统一评估两类指标：检测有效性（漂移识别表现）与处理延迟（计算开销）。

Result: 不同方法与实现之间在检测行为和处理成本上存在明显差异，各工具在检测能力与响应时间上呈现不同的权衡关系，未出现单一工具在全维度占优的情形。

Conclusion: 面向资源受限航拍平台，需要进一步研究轻量化、时延感知的漂移监控机制，以在检测能力与实时响应之间取得平衡。

Abstract: Remote sensing imagery acquired by Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) supports wide range of applications; however, deploying machine learning (ML) models on resource-constrained aerial platforms introduces challenges beyond predictive accuracy, particularly when deployment data differ from the distributions observed during training. Such shifts are common in remote sensing due to geographic, sensor, seasonal, and other visual variations. A practical solution is to equip deployed models with mechanisms that detect emerging distribution shifts and support subsequent responsive actions. This paper presents an empirical case study of three open-source detection tools: (i) Alibi-Detect; (ii) NannyML; and (iii) PyTorch-OOD. Using cross-dataset remote-sensing shifts, we evaluate both detection effectiveness and computational latency of these tools. Our results reveal differences in detection behavior and processing cost across methods and implementations, highlighting trade-offs between detection capability and response-time. These findings motivate further work toward lightweight, and latency-aware monitoring mechanisms for resource-constrained systems.

</details>


### [100] [MicroBT: Centroid-Guided Synergistic Learning for Multi-Modal Micro Brain Tumor Segmentation and Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13912)
*Zhenye Lou,Qing Xu,Stefanie Thust,Dorothee P. Auer,Zhen Chen,Wenting Duan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MicroBT统一多模态框架，利用尺度不变的质心属性联合建模微脑肿瘤分割与计数，并用VC-Loss与SACO缓解尺寸不平衡与多任务训练不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脑肿瘤分割依赖基于重叠度（如Dice）的目标函数，天然偏向大区域，使≤3mm微病灶在训练中被系统性抑制；同时像素级分割框架缺乏显式实例级建模，病灶计数不可靠，影响临床分期与治疗决策。
