We vary the universality of a leak, or the fraction of prompts that accept the same wrong response, and the verifier's static false-positive rate. At its KL-regularized optimum, RLVR acts as a filter that reweights the accepted set without altering its composition, so the hack rate is inherited exactly. Taking that as a null model, a known decomposition writes the drift from it as a sum over prompts of covariances between reward and the empirical tangent kernel. The terms from other prompts vanish without parameter sharing. With sharing, every prompt that accepts a wrong answer pushes it up and every prompt that rejects it pushes it down through the baseline, in proportion to the model's probability of producing it there. A shared-parameter policy thus drives a leak that few prompts accept below the base rate and amplifies one that most prompts accept. At every convergent learning rate under Adam, SGD, and sampled GRPO, hacking ends higher at high universality than at low, but whether either end crosses the base rate depends on the step size. Independent per-prompt copies remove the dependence. The direction of this push depends on neither the verifier's error rate nor universality alone, but on how much of the model's kernel-weighted probability of a wrong answer falls on prompts that accept it, which frozen base model controls support. Verifier fixes should target the mistakes the model repeats across prompts: two patches that remove equally many errors differ by up to 38 points of true accuracy. We propose this alignment as a hypothesis for why earlier studies disagree.

</details>


### [133] [An Analytical Model of Compute-limited Multistage Training Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13276)
*Nishil Patel,Jin Hwa Lee,Basile Confavreux,Andrew Saxe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个解析可处理的高维模型，统一刻画无监督预训练（线性自编码器）、带噪逐步标签的监督微调（SFT）与稀疏回合奖励的强化学习（RL）三阶段训练，用随机矩阵理论与算子投影闭合导出低维学习动力学，揭示了预训练数据量、表示质量、反馈结构与算力最优分配之间的机制联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代基础模型需经多阶段训练（预训练、SFT、RL），但各阶段如何相互作用、算力应如何在阶段间分配仍缺乏理论理解。作者希望建立一个可解析的理论框架，定量解释表示质量、监督/奖励信号结构与算力分配三者的耦合关系。

Method: 提出一个高维可解析模型：无监督预训练采用线性自编码器，SFT 使用带噪声的逐步（stepwise）标签，RL 使用稀疏回合（episodic）奖励。借助随机矩阵理论与算子投影闭合（operator-projection closure）方法，推导出近似的低维学习动力学，并用数值模拟验证其准确性。此外在 OLMo-2 1B 于 GSM8K 上做实证对照，并辅以一个组合式（compositional）模型分析 RL 对技能的强化/保留效应。

Result: （1）有限预训练数据造成依赖表示的泛化上限，贝叶斯分类误差随预训练样本比例的平方根反比缩放，且在渐近 SFT 与 RL 误差中观察到相同指数；（2）预训练质量越高，对噪声监督的敏感性越低；（3）SFT 初始化可缩短稀疏奖励导致的 RL 初始平台期，该效应在 OLMo-2 1B/GSM8K 上得到验证；（4）更长的 SFT warm start 还能提升 RL 的渐近性能；（5）三阶段联合算力优化显示最优分配依赖监督质量与总预算，且 RL 算力占比随预算呈非单调依赖；（6）组合模型显示 RL 会选择性地强化任务相关的预训练技能，而基本保留未使用技能。

Conclusion: 研究刻画了多阶段训练中表示质量、反馈结构（监督噪声与奖励稀疏性）与算力分配之间的定量机制：预训练数据决定表示上限并对齐各阶段误差指数，SFT 为 RL 提供有效初始化并改善渐近性能，阶段间算力分配需依监督质量与预算灵活调整。该框架为多阶段训练设计提供了可分析的理论指引。

Abstract: Modern foundation models are trained through multiple stages, yet how these stages interact and how compute should be allocated across them remain poorly understood. We introduce an analytically tractable high-dimensional model combining unsupervised pretraining with a linear autoencoder, supervised fine-tuning (SFT) with noisy stepwise labels, and reinforcement learning (RL) with sparse episodic rewards. Using random matrix theory and an operator-projection closure, we derive approximate low-dimensional learning dynamics that closely match simulations. We show that finite pretraining data imposes a representation-dependent performance limit, with Bayes classification error scaling as the inverse square root of the pretraining sample ratio, and observe the same exponent in asymptotic SFT and RL errors. Better pretraining also reduces sensitivity to noisy supervision. SFT initialization shortens the initial RL plateau induced by sparse rewards, an effect we also observe in OLMo-2 1B on GSM8K. Longer SFT warm starts additionally improve asymptotic RL performance. Optimizing compute across all three stages reveals that the optimal allocation depends on supervision quality and total budget, with a non-monotonic dependence of the RL compute share on budget. Finally, a complementary compositional model shows how RL selectively strengthens task-relevant pretrained skills while largely preserving unused skills. Together, these results identify mechanisms linking representation quality, feedback structure, and compute allocation in multistage training.

</details>


### [134] [Neural Network-Assisted CLEAN for Efficient Multipath Parameter Estimation in MIMO Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13298)
*Chaofan Deng,Linyu Sun,Jaeho Lee,Zishen Wan,Arijit Raychowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出神经网络辅助的 CLEAN 算法（NN-CLEAN），用多头分类器替代每步提取中的多维角度/时延搜索，在保持 LS 增益估计、参数重构与残差抵消的同时，实现约 27 倍加速与 52 倍 GPU 显存下降，并在 Sionna 射线追踪信道中优于插值 LS 估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宽带 MIMO 信道高分辨多径估计需在到达角、离开角与传播距离上反复多维搜索，计算与显存开销大，难以满足实际链路需求。

Method: 将 CLEAN 每次提取步骤的多维搜索替换为多头分类器进行参数预测，同时保留复杂路径增益的最小二乘估计、参数化重构和残差抵消；同一分类器迭代作用于后续残差，抽取深度可随参考 MPC 数变化而无需改变输出结构；在离格合成数据上训练后，零重训直接用于 Sionna 视距/非视距射线追踪信道的编码 MIMO 链路。

Result: 在迭代次数等于参考 MPC 数的设定下，单条与双条 MPC 时路径精度略优于网格搜索 CLEAN（GS-CLEAN），但多径数增大时精度下降更快；三条 MPC、批大小 8 的完整提取较 GS-CLEAN 提速约 27 倍、峰值 GPU 显存降低 52 倍；在两条 MPC 的 LOS/NLOS Sionna 信道中显著降低信道重构 MSE 与 BER，优于插值 LS 估计，但精度仍逊于 GS-CLEAN。

Conclusion: NN-CLEAN 以可接受的精度损失换取显著的算力与显存节省，验证了学习式替代搜索在多径参数估计中的可行性；但多径数增大时的精度退化、以及相对 GS-CLEAN 的系统性精度差距，表明该计算节省仍带有明确估计代价，后续需提升可扩展性与精度完备性。

Abstract: High-resolution multipath estimation in wideband multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) channels requires repeated searches over angle of arrival, angle of departure, and propagation distance. This paper presents neural network-assisted CLEAN (NN-CLEAN), which replaces the multidimensional search at each extraction step with a multi-head classifier while retaining least-squares (LS) estimation of the complex path gain, parametric reconstruction, and residual cancellation. The same classifier is applied to successive residuals, allowing the extraction depth to vary without changing its output structure. With the number of iterations prescribed by the reference multipath-component (MPC) count, off-grid synthetic tests show that NN-CLEAN slightly exceeds grid-search CLEAN (GS-CLEAN) in path accuracy for one and two MPCs, although its accuracy declines more rapidly at larger path counts. For complete three-MPC extraction at a batch size of eight, NN-CLEAN achieves an approximately $27\times$ speedup and a $52$-fold reduction in peak GPU memory relative to GS-CLEAN. Applied without retraining to two-MPC line-of-sight (LOS) and non-line-of-sight (NLOS) Sionna ray-tracing channels within a coded MIMO link, NN-CLEAN substantially reduces channel-reconstruction mean-squared error (MSE) and bit error rate (BER) relative to interpolated LS channel estimation. GS-CLEAN retains an accuracy advantage on these channels, indicating the estimation cost associated with the computational savings.

</details>


### [135] [Train High, Ship Low: Efficient LoRA Synchronization for Distributed On-Policy Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13306)
*Shenghui Li,Li Ju,Thiemo Voigt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HiLo框架用压缩梯度广播+本地优化器重放替代LoRA参数传输，配合预算感知压缩器Spar实现低带宽下的高效on-policy后训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式on-policy后训练中，向rollout worker传输LoRA适配器的通信在带宽受限网络上成为瓶颈；降低训练秩会损害任务容量，而自适应优化器状态又难以压缩。

Method: 提出HiLo：worker维护复制的优化器状态，通过接收压缩梯度并在本地重放优化器更新来重构参数，替代直接传输参数；在可复现执行下learner与worker用相同解码梯度保持版本一致的策略状态。并提出Spar压缩器：结合谱截断、自适应秩分配与误差反馈，使训练秩与通信成本解耦。

Result: 在on-policy蒸馏与奖励引导后训练实验中，HiLo+Spar在仅传输约1.6%适配器字节的情况下，学习质量接近全同步，带宽受限下端到端更新加速最高达2.2倍。

Conclusion: 以压缩梯度传播与本地优化器重放替代参数同步，可在不牺牲训练质量的前提下显著降低通信开销并加速分布式后训练。

Abstract: Transmitting LoRA adapters to rollout workers can bottleneck distributed on-policy post-training over bandwidth-constrained networks. Lowering the training rank may limit the capacity a workload needs, while adaptive optimizer updates are difficult to compress. Rollout workers can instead reconstruct updates from gradients by maintaining replicated optimizer states. We introduce HiLo, a compressor-agnostic framework that replaces parameter transmission with compressed gradient dissemination and local optimizer replay. By applying the same decoded gradients, the learner and workers maintain consistent policy states at the same version under reproducible execution. We further develop Spar, a budget-aware gradient compressor that combines spectral truncation, adaptive rank allocation and error feedback to decouple training rank from communication cost. Experiments across on-policy distillation and reward-guided post-training show that HiLo with Spar maintains learning quality close to full synchronization while transmitting as little as 1.6% of the adapter bytes, and accelerates end-to-end updates by up to $2.2\times$ under bandwidth constraints.

</details>


### [136] [One Trajectory, Two Requirements: Accuracy and Local Stability in Euler Sampling for Flow Matching Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13311)
*Mahindra Rautela*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文追问流匹配机器人策略中固定的欧拉步数是否足够：在给定输入与初始噪声时，轨迹精度与局部欧拉稳定性可能提出不同的步数要求；在冻结的π_{0.5}-LIBERO检查点上实测显示，所采样模态均满足欧拉非扩张条件，五个配对案例表现为精度受限而非稳定性受限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配策略通常沿用离线精度扫描选定的固定欧拉步数，但每个推理输入（视觉观测、机器人状态、语言指令）连同源噪声采样都会定义不同的ODE轨迹与局部雅可比序列。作者由此质疑：能准确跟随名义噪声到动作轨迹的步数，是否仍可能因轨迹附近强收缩方向而显得过粗。

Method: 构建精确的二维坐标系算例，并对六个已训练流模型进行受控增广实验，在保持原学习传输不变的前提下比较轨迹精度与局部欧拉稳定性对步数的要求；随后在不改动权重与动作动力学的情况下研究冻结的π_{0.5}-LIBERO检查点，对来自10个LIBERO-10任务的30个策略输入做雅可比分析与步数扫描。

Result: 30个策略输入的雅可比在动作终点附近存在强收缩方向，但无采样模态违反欧拉非扩张条件；其中五个输入在5%终端误差下需4--9步、在1%下需87--112步，这些配对案例在局部判据下与精度受限而非稳定性受限的采样一致。

Conclusion: 受控实验表明轨迹精度与局部稳定性可施加不同的步数要求，因此仅凭终点精度无法排除独立的稳定性需求；但在实测的π_{0.5}-LIBERO设置中，采样结果表现为精度受限，稳定性在这一局部判据下未构成额外约束。

Abstract: Flow matching robot policies commonly use a fixed Euler step count selected from offline accuracy sweeps. At inference time, however, each policy input (visual observation, robot state, and language instruction) together with a source-noise draw defines a different ODE trajectory and sequence of local Jacobians. We ask whether a step count that accurately follows the nominal noise-to-action trajectory can still be too coarse for strongly contracting directions around it. An exact two-coordinate system and a controlled augmentation of six trained flow models show that trajectory accuracy and local Euler stability can impose different step requirements even when the original learned transport is unchanged. We then study a frozen $π_{0.5}$-LIBERO checkpoint without modifying its weights or action dynamics. Across 30 policy inputs from 10 LIBERO-10 tasks, the Jacobian exhibits strong contracting directions near the action endpoint, yet no sampled mode violates the Euler non-expansion condition. On five of those inputs, accuracy sweeps require 4--9 Euler steps at 5% terminal error and 87--112 at 1%. These five paired cases are therefore consistent with accuracy-limited rather than stability-limited sampling under this local criterion, while the controlled experiments show why endpoint accuracy alone cannot rule out a separate stability requirement.

</details>


### [137] [MicroLens: Read, Trace, and Rewire the Residual Stream with Two Lenses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13322)
*Nika Zahedi,Fangyi Zhu,Anna Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个交互式可视化工具，在残差流的"层×token"网格上同时支持解码（logit lens 与 J-lens）、溯源和编辑，并在 Gemma-3-4B-it 上通过三种使用场景揭示了两种透镜的编辑方向差异、两跳问答中实体信息的可读性与可控性，以及推理提示下答案在写入推理步骤前已存在于残差流中的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的"透镜"方法（将隐藏状态解码为词）只能展示某个向量"读起来像什么"，其热力图无法区分模型是否以及多大程度上真正使用了该信息。作者希望提供一个能同时"读、追、改"的工具，从而超越单纯的解码可视化，直接检验信息的因果作用。

Method: 构建交互式查看器：在残差流的层×token 网格上，每个单元格可用两种透镜（logit lens 与 Jacobian lens）解码，并可溯源到其信息来源 token，同时支持就地编辑并即时重跑模型剩余部分。基于 Gemma-3-4B-it，在数百条提示上做扫描实验，设计三个使用场景进行演示。

Result: （1）两种透镜给出不同的写入方向：logit lens 的词方向决定 token 的输出，J-lens 的方向改变后续 token 的读取；（2）在两跳常识题中，州/国家信息最早在第 7 层即可在"城市"token 处读出，在此处替换可使答案改变方向的比例达 92–98%，但在答案位置该信息并未被使用；（3）在逐步推理提示下，正确答案往往在写出任何推理之前已是残差流中的首个词——检索类问题 89–92%，多跳与串行类 39–63%，远高于干扰项（至多 5%）；排序类谜题是例外，模型只有在写出最后推理步骤时才解出；此外，对残差流中可见的答案，J-lens 比 logit lens 早一到两层读出。

Conclusion: 将解码、溯源与因果编辑统一在同一网格上，能够区分"可读"与"被使用"，为机制可解释性提供更可操作的实验范式；J-lens 在读出时序上优于 logit lens，而不同题型下模型对信息的实际依赖程度差异显著。

Abstract: Lenses that decode hidden states into words are a popular way to look inside language models, but a heatmap of decoded words only shows what a vector reads as and cannot distinguish whether, or how much, the model uses the information. We present an interactive viewer that puts reading, tracing and editing on the same layers-by-tokens grid of the residual stream. In the viewer, every cell can be decoded with two lenses, the logit lens and the Jacobian lens (J-lens), traced back to the tokens it drew from, and edited, with the rest of the model rerun on the spot. We demonstrate three usage scenarios on Gemma-3-4B-it, backed by sweeps over a few hundred prompts, to show what this tool can discover. First, the two lenses give different directions to write with, each suiting a different edit: the logit lens's word direction sets what a token outputs, while the J-lens's direction changes what later tokens read. Second, in two-hop trivia questions (the capital of the state or country containing a given city), the state or country is readable at the city token from as early as layer 7, and swapping it there can redirect the answer 92-98% of the time; however, at the answer position, the state or country information is not used. Third, when a chat model answers questions with a step-by-step reasoning prompt, the correct answer is often already the top word in the residual stream before any reasoning is written: for 89-92% of retrieval questions and 39-63% of multi-hop and serial ones, far above their respective distractors (at most 5%). However, ordering puzzles are an exception, as the model works them out only once it writes its final reasoning steps. Furthermore, for answers that are visible in the residual stream, the J-lens reads them a layer or two earlier than the logit lens across the different types of question.

</details>


### [138] [Robust Flow Matching under Target Corruption and Label Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13324)
*Mert Can Kurucu,Erik Englesson,Hossein Azizpour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将Flow Matching训练重构为条件向量场上的时间相关回归问题，从理论上分析目标污染与标签噪声两种损坏对损失的影响，并据此提出基于残差分数的样本加权方法TRS与CCS以提升FM的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Flow Matching隐含假设训练数据与标签是干净的，但实际场景中常存在目标污染（数据损坏）与标签噪声，这一假设被违背时性能下降；缺乏对两种损坏如何影响FM目标的机制性理解，也缺少针对性的鲁棒化手段。

Method: 将FM训练表述为条件向量场上的时间相关回归问题，分别刻画两类损坏的影响：目标污染引入随时间增长的时间相关残差项；标签噪声导致更新指向错误的类条件向量场。基于该分析，将损坏特异的残差分数映射为样本权重：针对目标污染提出Temporal Residual Score (TRS)，强调FM中后段的残差幅度（此时干净样本与损坏样本更易区分）；针对标签噪声提出Comparative Conditional Score (CCS)，对同一轨迹与速度目标，比较观测类条件向量场残差与替代类条件向量场残差。

Result: 在受控损坏设置与真实噪声标签场景下的实验表明，所提方法在鲁棒性上优于标准FM及现有鲁棒训练基线。

Conclusion: 损坏特异的残差结构可作为实现鲁棒Flow Matching的实用基础。

Abstract: Flow Matching (FM) is a strong framework for generative modeling due to its stable training and efficient sampling. However, FM assumes clean target data and labels, an assumption often violated in practice. We formulate FM training as a time-dependent regression problem over conditional vector fields and analyze how the two corruption types affect the objective: i) target corruption adds a time-dependent residual term that grows with time, ii) label noise causes updates to the wrong class-conditional vector field. Based on this analysis, we propose to improve FM robustness by mapping corruption-specific residual scores to sample weights. For target corruption, we propose the Temporal Residual Score (TRS), which emphasizes residual magnitudes at intermediate-to-late FM times, where clean and corrupted samples are more separable. For label noise, we propose the Comparative Conditional Score (CCS), which compares the residual of the observed class-conditional vector field against alternative class-conditional vector fields for the same trajectory and velocity target. Experiments across controlled corruption settings and real-world noisy labels show that the proposed methods improve robustness over standard FM and robust-training baselines, supporting corruption-specific residual structure as a practical basis for robust FM.

</details>


### [139] [Stochastic Projections and Rank-Aware Learning Rates for Federated LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13331)
*Saber Malekmohammadi,Kevin Kuo,Virginia Smith,Golnoosh Farnadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SPRA-LoRA：在联邦学习中冻结并按周期重采样LoRA的A矩阵，使LoRA微调近似为带秩相关扰动的全参数微调的随机近似；并推导出信噪比随秩r线性增长，从而设计秩相关学习率，实现客户端开销减半且性能优于SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限的分布式客户端上微调大规模基础模型存在计算与通信瓶颈。LoRA与联邦学习结合虽提供秩上的灵活性，但A、B矩阵相互耦合使优化复杂；同时客户端上行通信与算力有限，难以支撑高秩自适应。

Method: 1）理论分析：证明在高斯初始化且A冻结时，LoRA微调是全参数微调的随机近似，批梯度带有与秩相关的随机扰动。2）策略：客户端级冻结A并周期性重采样，解耦A、B梯度、削弱低秩约束，并减少50%计算与上行通信开销，可将资源转向更高秩。3）理论推导：在冻结A情形下，适应秩r直接控制扰动梯度的信噪比，SNR随r线性增长，故需使客户端学习率随秩动态缩放。4）据此提出SPRA-LoRA联邦秩感知微调策略，并证明其在客户端资源异构下的收敛性。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，SPRA-LoRA在客户端开销仅为一半的情况下，持续优于现有最先进基线方法。

Conclusion: 冻结并周期重采样A矩阵是一种有效解耦LoRA优化的手段，可将节省的通信/计算预算用于提升适应秩；秩与梯度信噪比的线性关系为秩相关学习率设计提供了理论依据，使异构资源下的联邦LoRA微调更高效且收敛可控。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large-scale foundation models across resource-constrained, distributed clients presents significant computational and communication challenges. While merging Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) with Federated Learning (FL) enables rank-based flexibility, the interdependence of the adaptation matrices ($\mathbf{A}$ and $\mathbf{B}$) often complicates optimization. In this work, we theoretically demonstrate that LoRA fine-tuning with Gaussian initialization and a frozen $\mathbf{A}$ matrix acts as a stochastic approximation of full fine-tuning, where batch gradients are subject to a random, rank-dependent perturbation. Motivated by this insight, we propose freezing and periodically resampling $\mathbf{A}$ matrices at the client level. This strategy decouples the $\mathbf{A}$ and $\mathbf{B}$ gradients, diminishes LoRA low-rank constraints and reduces both computational and uplink communication overheads by 50\%, allowing for the reallocation of resources toward higher adaptation ranks. Furthermore, we show that in this frozen-$\mathbf{A}$ regime, the adaptation rank $r$ directly governs the signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) of the perturbed gradients. Specifically, we find that the SNR increases linearly with $r$, implying that clients' learning rates must be dynamically scaled with their adaptation ranks for higher utility. Building on these foundations, we introduce SPRA-LoRA (Stochastic Projection Rank-Aware LoRA), a federated rank-aware fine-tuning strategy that utilizes frozen, resampled $\mathbf{A}$ matrices and rank-dependent learning rates for clients. We also prove its convergence with heterogeneous client resources. Our experiments across benchmark datasets demonstrate that SPRA-LoRA consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines with half the overhead on clients.

</details>


### [140] [AdjointFlow: Accelerating Scientific Inverse Problems with Physics-Based Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13389)
*Ethan Hanold,Nicholas M. Boffi,Qin Li,Leonardo Zepeda-Núñez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 AdjointFlow 框架，基于随机插值（stochastic interpolants）把已知物理模型注入生成式传输过程，用伴随（adjoint）初始化与正则化速度场实现高质量、低开销的逆问题重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆问题（地震成像、天体物理、无损检测等）中目标分布常高度集中，导致条件扩散模型传输映射病态且几乎不利用已知物理；PnP 方法虽利用物理模型提升数据一致性，却需重新引入昂贵的迭代计算与反复的正演评估。

Method: 在 stochastic interpolants 框架上：(1) 设计数据依赖、基于伴随的初始化，缩短到目标的传输距离；(2) 修改传输速度场，使端点分布趋于集中时仍保持正则性；(3) 通过特定网络参数化把初始化直接内嵌进学习到的速度场；另提供可选的推理时细化，仅需少量物理模型查询。

Result: 在逆散射、地震成像与黑洞成像实验中，重建精度一致优于条件扩散模型，推理速度比 PnP 方法快至多五个数量级；可选细化可匹配甚至超过 PnP 精度且无需重训练。

Conclusion: 将物理先验前移至生成传输路径本身，可同时兼顾精度与效率，为逆问题提供了一个优于条件扩散与 PnP 的方案，并可灵活扩展以进一步提升数据一致性。

Abstract: Inverse problems are ubiquitous across scientific applications ranging from seismic imaging to astrophysics to nondestructive testing, yet classical solvers often struggle with nonconvexity and repeated evaluations of expensive physical models. Diffusion models can amortize this computation offline, but largely data-driven conditional models underutilize known physics and suffer from ill-conditioned transport maps when the target distribution is concentrated. Plug-and-play (PnP) methods explicitly leverage the physical model to guide a learned prior, improving data consistency but reintroducing costly iterative computations. We instead propose AdjointFlow, a framework built on stochastic interpolants that injects the known physics into the generative transport itself. We design a data-dependent, adjoint-based initialization to reduce transport distance to the target, modify the transport velocity to remain regular as the endpoint distributions concentrate, and parameterize the network to build the initialization directly into the learned velocity. Together, these enable accurate reconstruction at substantially lower cost than generative models starting from Gaussian noise. Experiments on inverse scattering, seismic imaging, and black hole imaging show that AdjointFlow consistently produces more accurate reconstructions than conditional diffusion models while being up to five orders of magnitude faster at inference than PnP methods. Optional inference-time refinement that queries the physical model can further improve data consistency, matching or exceeding the accuracy of PnP methods without retraining.

</details>


### [141] [Free Lunch Uncertainty: Sequence-Level Rényi Entropy from Particle Decoding for Adaptive LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13414)
*Erfan Baghaei Potraghloo,Milad Heidari Sorkhehdizaj,Shahab Rahimirad,Aliasghar Pourghani,Seyedarmin Azizi,Souvik Kundu,Abolfazl Hashemi,Joseph Campbell,Vijay Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种零边际成本的序列级不确定性信号：利用SMC幂采样解码过程中已估计的幂分布归一化常数，直接读出序列级Rényi熵（SRE），并通过Rényi链式法则扩展到任意阶的逐步熵族，其中1/2阶信号在13个设置中的10个为最佳零成本选择性预测信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对整个LLM生成结果量化不确定性对选择性预测（selective prediction）和自适应计算（adaptive compute）至关重要。现有零成本信号（mean token entropy、log-probability）是局部性的——仅涵盖单个token或单条completion；而self-consistency需要额外大量completions，成本高。

Method: 指出SMC解码用于power sampling时已在估计幂分布的归一化常数，因此可零额外开销地得到completion分布的序列级Rényi熵（SRE），且对任意particle预算都有保证。再用Rényi链式法则将该读出扩展为任意阶的逐步熵族。进一步证明：阶数大于1的成员与每步最可能token绑定（解释了其为何追踪log-probability），而1/2阶成员累积的是每步搜索努力的对数。

Result: 1/2阶成员在13个设置中的10个上是最佳零成本选择性预测信号。在MATH500上，其AUROC较log-probability高0.022–0.044（覆盖全部四个模型及所有长度四分位），且在各设置留一验证时仍选中同一阶数。8-particle探针可识别出受益于更多particles的问题；两遍策略在三个随机种子上以少34.2%的particles达到与64-particle相当的MATH500准确率。

Conclusion: 序列级Rényi熵可作为零成本、有理论保证的LLM不确定性度量；1/2阶SRE在选择性预测上稳定优于log-probability，并可用于自适应粒子预算以节省计算。

Abstract: Quantifying uncertainty over an entire LLM completion is critical for selective prediction and adaptive compute. Zero-cost signals such as mean token entropy and log-probability are local to tokens or to one completion, and self-consistency needs many extra completions. We show that Sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) decoding for power sampling already estimates the normalizing constant of the power distribution, which gives the sequence-level R'enyi entropy (SRE) of the model's completion distribution at zero marginal cost, with a guarantee at every particle budget. A R'enyi chain rule extends this readout to a family of stepwise entropies of any order. We prove that orders above one are tied to the most likely token of each step, which explains why they track log-probability, while the order-1/2 member accumulates the log of per-step search effort. This member is the best zero-cost selective-prediction signal in 10 of 13 settings. On MATH500 its AUROC is 0.022 to 0.044 above log-probability with all four models and within length quartiles, and the same order is selected when each setting is held out. An 8-particle probe identifies the problems that benefit from more particles, and a two-pass policy matches 64-particle accuracy on MATH500 with 34.2% fewer particles over three seeds.

</details>


### [142] [You Don't Need Feature Learning to Do In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13431)
*Joseph Turnbull,Jamie Simon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过核机器的谱分解分析ICL线性回归任务，指出"泛化解"实为"记忆解"的低阶截断：随样本量增加，模型先泛化后退化为记忆，且特征学习对ICL并非必需，Transformer实验亦印证该高阶统计记忆机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICL中记忆与泛化的界限及发生条件尚不清晰。作者希望用一个可解析的理论模型（旋转不变核机器）刻画ICL学习过程的内部机制，厘清何时泛化、何时记忆，并检验特征学习是否必要。

Method: 以Raventos等（2023）提出的ICL线性回归任务为对象，研究旋转不变核机器的学习行为；在核特征基（kernel eigenbasis）下分解任务，得到ICL学习的谱图景，并推导记忆解各阶分量与训练任务集高阶统计量之间的对应关系，再与经验训练的Transformer进行对照。

Result: 谱图景显示：随样本量增长，核机器依次学习记忆解中越来越高阶的分量，这些分量依赖训练任务集越来越高阶的统计量；泛化解近似为记忆解的低阶截断，因此训练过程中模型先泛化、随后退化为记忆。Transformer实验中也观察到核理论所预测的、通过训练任务高阶统计量进行任务记忆的现象。

Conclusion: 三点结论：①ICL任务上的"泛化解"本质上只是对记忆解欠拟合；②核机器无需特征学习即可在该任务上泛化，说明特征学习对ICL并非必要；③该核理论预测的高阶统计记忆机制在具备特征学习能力的Transformer上同样成立，提示理论具有更广适用性。

Abstract: How should we understand memorization vs. generalization in in-context learning (ICL) tasks, and when does one happen vs. the other? To investigate, we study how rotation-invariant kernel machines learn the ICL linear regression task proposed by Raventos et al. (2023). By decomposing the task in the kernel eigenbasis, we obtain a spectral picture of learning in this ICL setting. This picture reveals that as sample size grows, kernel machines learn successively-higher-degree components of the memorizing solution, which depend upon successively-higher-degree statistics of the training task set. The generalizing solution is approximately a low-degree truncation of the memorizing solution, and so the model first generalizes, then degrades into memorization as training proceeds.
  The takeaways are threefold. First, if this case is indicative, learning a "generalizing" solution on an ICL task is really just underfitting the memorizing solution! Second, the fact that kernel machines can generalize on this task means that feature learning is inessential to ICL. Third, transformers empirically trained to do ICL are shown to memorize tasks through the higher-order statistics of the training tasks that the kernel-based theory predicts, suggesting it applies even to models capable of feature learning.

</details>


### [143] [Fermat ProbCover: Coverage-Based Active Learning on Fermat Distance Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13448)
*Gokul Bhusal,Delanna Do,Kevin Miller,Ekaterina Merkurjev,Stephan Wojtowytsch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PC-Fermat：用Fermat距离替代欧氏距离定义覆盖邻域的低预算主动学习方法，通过加权欧氏邻域图+截断最短路计算Fermat覆盖图，并给出精确保持完整图上Fermat覆盖的充分条件；在8个分类数据集上多数优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低预算场景下基于不确定性的主动学习方法常失效，而ProbCover等基于覆盖的方法虽有效，却依赖欧氏距离，难以充分刻画数据的潜在几何结构（流形/分布结构）。

Method: 用数据自适应的Fermat距离替换欧氏距离来定义覆盖邻域；为便于计算，构建加权欧氏邻域图并用截断最短路算法计算Fermat覆盖图；理论上给出该稀疏图上构造能精确保持完整图Fermat覆盖的充分条件；在8个分类数据集、不同标注预算（重点关注极低预算）下评测。

Result: 在8个分类数据集、不同标注预算（侧重极低预算）下，PC-Fermat在多数数据集上优于多种现有主动学习方法。

Conclusion: 采用反映数据分布的Fermat距离定义覆盖邻域，可在低预算主动学习中更有效地选择样本；配合邻域图+截断最短路可实现高效计算并具备理论保证，是一种有效的覆盖式主动学习改进方案。

Abstract: Active learning aims to reduce annotation cost by selecting the most informative examples for labeling. In the low-budget regime, where uncertainty-based methods often fail, coverage-based active learning methods such as ProbCover have been shown to be effective. However, these methods typically rely on Euclidean distance, which may not adequately capture the underlying geometry of data. In this article, we propose PC-Fermat, a coverage-based active learning method that replaces Euclidean coverage neighborhoods with neighborhoods defined by a Fermat distance, i.e., a data-adaptive distance that reflects the underlying distribution of the data points. To facilitate computation, we construct a weighted Euclidean neighborhood graph and use a truncated shortest-path algorithm to compute the Fermat coverage graph. We also establish sufficient conditions under which this construction exactly preserves the Fermat coverage computed on the complete graph. Finally, we evaluate PC-Fermat on eight classification datasets across varying labeling budgets, with particular emphasis on the very low-budget regime. Our experimental results demonstrate that PC-Fermat outperforms several existing active learning methods on the majority of the datasets.

</details>


### [144] [SLIC: Reinforcement Fine-Tuning Small LMs for Multi-Turn Analog Circuit Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13452)
*Yan Xu,Zhengqi Gao,Ching-Yun Ko,Zhang-Wei Hong,Wenjie Lu,Tao Yu,Xin Zhang,Duane S. Boning,Ruonan Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 SLIC：用强化微调训练 4B 级可本地部署小模型完成闭环模拟电路优化，配结构化电路表示、离散相对更新动作、多目标门控奖励与 RGRS 引导 rollout；仅用开源 65nm PTM 训练，在 41 个放大器拓扑基准上同分布 Pass@1 达 87.1%（超 763B 模型 48.5 个百分点），并跨五个未见解工艺节点及商用 TSMC 65nm PDK 迁移成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟 IC 设计是多轮、依赖仿真器的耦合权衡迭代任务；由于专有 PDK 保密限制，托管式大模型工作流难以在实际设计中部署，因此需要本地可运行的小模型方案。同时该任务奖励稀疏、约束强，直接强化学习困难。

Method: 1) 结构化电路表示 + 离散化"相对更新"动作空间；2) 领域与成功门控的多目标奖励；3) Reward-Guided Rollout Steering (RGRS)，将逐步仿真器反馈同时用于策略学习与在线 rollout 构造；4) 以 4B 规模模型做强化微调；5) 发布 41 个放大器拓扑的目标条件基准，含同分布验证、留出拓扑评测与跨工艺节点迁移协议。

Result: 仅用开源 65nm CMOS PTM 训练：SLIC-Random 在 8 轮内同分布四指标 Pass@1 达 87.1%，比 DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash Think（763B）高 48.5 个百分点；SLIC-Softmax 在留出拓扑上 Pass@1 为 72.6%；策略无需再微调即可跨 5 个未见工艺节点迁移，并迁移至商用 TSMC 65nm PDK，留出拓扑 Pass@1 为 49.9%；测试时仿真交互扩展至 32 轮时留出拓扑 Pass@1 提升至 83.2%。

Conclusion: 小规模本地模型经强化微调可在模拟电路闭环优化中显著超越超大托管模型，并具备跨节点/跨 PDK 泛化与测试时算力扩展性，从而在不上传专有数据的前提下实现实际可部署的设计流程。

Abstract: Analog integrated circuit (IC) design is a multi-turn, simulator-grounded engineering task that demands iterative reasoning over coupled performance tradeoffs, yet proprietary process design kits (PDKs) make hosted large language model workflows difficult to deploy in practical design settings. We present SLIC (Small LM Iterating on Circuits), a reinforcement fine-tuning framework that trains a 4B-scale, locally deployable language model for closed-loop analog circuit optimization. SLIC pairs structured circuit representations with discretized relative-update actions, domain- and success-gated multiobjective rewards, and Reward-Guided Rollout Steering (RGRS), which turns per-step simulator feedback into signals for both policy learning and online rollout construction in this sparse, highly constrained domain. We further release an open, goal-conditioned benchmark of 41 amplifier topologies with standardized protocols for in-distribution validation, held-out topology evaluation, and cross-technology-node transfer. Trained solely with open source 65 nm CMOS predictive technology models (PTM), SLIC-Random achieves 87.1% in-distribution four-specification Pass@1 within eight turns, surpassing the 763B DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash Think by 48.5 percentage points. Beyond the training distribution, SLIC-Softmax reaches 72.6% Pass@1 on held-out topologies, and the trained policies consistently transfer across five unseen technology nodes without additional fine-tuning. This transfer extends to a commercial TSMC 65 nm CMOS PDK, where SLIC-Random achieves 49.9% held-out-topology Pass@1, demonstrating practical deployment through local inference without transmitting proprietary data to external LLM services. Performance further scales with test-time simulator interactions, reaching up to 83.2% held-out-topology Pass@1 at a 32-turn budget.

</details>


### [145] [Deep Personalization: Continuous Context Observation for Local Proactive Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13457)
*Yijia Shao,Song Bian,Junxian Guo,Jindong Jiang,Zhen Dong,Huizi Mao,Diyi Yang,Song Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"深度个性化"(deep personalization)闭环框架，通过独立Observer Agent持续观察用户桌面活动、选择性保留长期上下文并利用隐式反馈不断调整干预时机，在设备端实现主动式桌面助手Coco；在合成基准OSWorld-Persona上macro F1由0.12–0.19提升至0.35–0.48（2.4–3.0倍），一周用户研究中Coco由0.199提升至0.515。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体能力增强，但用户往往不清楚何时以及如何求助，错失大量可获帮助的机会。主动式智能体可弥补这一缺口，但有效主动性依赖对用户实时活动的理解以及对干预时机的个性化，否则会造成打扰。现有方法缺乏持续上下文观察与持续自适应之间的闭环，也缺乏在不外传原始活动数据前提下的可扩展评估手段。

Method: 1) 提出deep personalization闭环过程：连续上下文观察—选择性长期记忆—依据隐式反馈持续调整干预决策。2) 在主动式桌面智能体Coco中实现，采用独立Observer Agent监控桌面活动、维护紧凑的长期用户建模上下文，从参与、忽略/关闭、显式请求与重复工作模式等隐式信号中学习"何时提供帮助"。3) 设计高效端侧(on-device)策略，使Observer Agent可在本地AI计算平台运行，原始活动数据不出设备。4) 构建数据合成流水线，将现有计算机使用轨迹转化为带人格条件干预标签的基准OSWorld-Persona，用于规模化评估且不采集真实敏感用户活动。

Result: 在OSWorld-Persona上，深度个性化将三个模型骨干的macro F1从直接提示下的0.12–0.19提升至自适应后的0.35–0.48，相对提升2.4–3.0倍。为期一周的用户研究中，Coco的宏平均F1从0.199提升至0.515。

Conclusion: 结果表明，端侧深度个性化可使主动式智能体的干预更及时，并逐步贴合每位用户的日常工作习惯；同时验证了以合成基准替代真实敏感数据开展规模化评估的可行性。

Abstract: As AI agents become increasingly capable, users must still recognize when and how to ask for help, leaving many opportunities for useful support unrealized. Proactive agents hold the promise to close this gap, but effective proactivity requires understanding users' real-time activities and personalizing intervention timing so that assistance is offered when it is likely to be helpful rather than disruptive. We introduce deep personalization, a closed-loop process that connects continuous context observation with continual agent adaptation: an agent observes user activity, selectively retains long-term context, and adapts intervention decisions from implicit feedback. We initiate deep personalization in Coco, a proactive desktop agent, by having a separate Observer Agent that monitors desktop activity, maintains compact long-term user modeling context, and learns when assistance is desirable from implicit signals such as engagement, dismissal, explicit requests, and repeated work patterns. We further develop efficient on-device strategies to enable the Observer Agent to run on local AI compute platforms without streaming raw activity data off-device. For evaluation at scale without collecting sensitive real-user activity, we introduce a data-synthesis pipeline that transforms existing computer-use trajectories into OSWorld-Persona, a benchmark with persona-conditioned intervention labels. On OSWorld-Persona, deep personalization improves macro F1 across three model backbones from 0.12-0.19 under direct prompting to 0.35-0.48 after adaptation, a 2.4-3.0x relative gain. In a week-long user study, we observe that Coco improves its macro-averaged F1 score from 0.199 to 0.515. These results suggest that on-device deep personalization can make proactive agents more timely and increasingly aligned with each user's everyday work.

</details>


### [146] [Leveraging Psychophysical Attentional Distribution for Gaze-Augmented Reward Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13477)
*Chaeho Lee,Junyup Kim,Wansoo Kim,Young Min Jung,Sang Ho Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将认知心理学中关于字母识别的研究结论引入基于眼动数据的奖励模型训练，用"以注视点为中心的空间分级分布"替代固定注视点表示，从而提升偏好预测准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF 依赖人类偏好对齐模型输出，近期工作把眼动（ET）数据作为额外监督信号训练奖励模型，使奖励学习扎根于人类阅读行为。但已有方法多以固定注视点为中心，忽略了人类视觉注意在空间上是渐变分布的这一认知心理学发现。

Method: 在已有注视点（fixation-centric）模型基础上，将视觉注意建模为以当前注视位置为中心的空间分级分布（spatially graded distribution），并将该分布表示纳入奖励模型训练。

Result: 采用所提出的注视分布训练的奖励模型，其偏好预测准确率高于基线模型；部分实验结果在定性上与阅读过程中人类注意的既有认知发现一致，提示模型可能捕捉到了人类注意分配的认知有意义成分。

Conclusion: 以空间分级注视分布替代单点注视表示可有效改进基于眼动数据的奖励模型，且模型行为与人类阅读注意的心理学结论具有一致性，表明认知启发的注意建模是提升偏好对齐质量的有前景方向。

Abstract: Integrating human feedback into language models has gained increasing attention as a means to align model outputs with human preferences, with Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) serving as a prominent framework for preference-based alignment. Recent studies have incorporated eye-tracking (ET) data as an additional supervisory signal for reward model training, grounding reward learning in human reading behavior. Building on this approach, we extend prior fixation-centric models by leveraging established findings from cognitive psychology on human letter recognition. Specifically, we model visual attention as a spatially graded distribution centered on the current gaze location and incorporate this representation into reward model training. Our results show that the reward model trained with the proposed gaze distribution achieves higher preference prediction accuracy than a baseline model. Moreover, some results from our experiments are qualitatively consistent with established findings on human attention during reading, suggesting that they potentially capture cognitively meaningful aspects of human attention allocation.

</details>


### [147] [Sharp Sample Complexity of KL-Regularized Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13480)
*Andrew Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究KL正则化强化学习（最大熵RL与RLHF的目标）在生成模型下的后验学习样本复杂度，给出plug-in估计器的上界与匹配下界，并刻画其渐近精确常数与最优采样分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KL正则化RL等价于"控制即推断"图模型中的变分推断，正则化最优策略是轨迹后验，策略次优度为β倍路径测度KL散度。但即使有生成模型，学习该后验所需样本数未知：早期1/ε保证在有效时域H上高达H^5，且MDP的下界均不依赖温度β。

Method: 针对含K个状态-动作对的折扣MDP，分析plug-in估计器（对估计的soft Q函数取软最大），证明其从每个初始状态ε最优的样本复杂度；构造下界（允许自适应采样设计）；推导plug-in估计器的渐近精确常数（估计soft Q函数的Fisher加权方差），并证明在等量采样各状态-动作对的方法中局部渐近极小极大；给出自适应设计下的最优样本分配。

Result: 上界：plug-in估计器达到ε最优需Õ(KH^3/(ε max{ε,β}) + KH)个样本；下界：当ε≲min{H, H^2/β}时，任何算法（含可选择采样位置者）都无法更少。当ε<β时为1/ε速率，且时域依赖与无正则化RL相同。给出精确渐近常数与最优/自适应采样分配，小规模MDP仿真验证速率与常数。

Conclusion: KL正则化RL的后验学习样本复杂度被紧确刻画：上界与下界在给定ε区间内匹配，正则化在ε<β时不额外增加H的代价，plug-in估计器在等量采样类中局部渐近极小极大。

Abstract: KL-regularized reinforcement learning, the objective behind maximum-entropy RL and RL from human feedback, is variational inference in the graphical model of "control as inference": the regularized optimal policy is a posterior over trajectories, and the suboptimality of a policy is $β$ times a KL divergence between path measures. However, it is not known how many samples it takes to learn this posterior, even with a generative model. Earlier $1/ε$ guarantees scale with $H^5$ in the effective horizon $H$, and no lower bound for MDPs depends on the temperature $β$. In this work, we show that for a discounted MDP with $K$ state-action pairs, the plug-in estimator finds a policy that is $ε$-optimal from every initial state with $\widetilde{O}(KH^3/(ε\max\{ε,β\}) + KH)$ samples, and that no algorithm, even one that chooses where to sample, can do with fewer when $ε\lesssim \min\{H, H^2/β\}$. For $ε< β$ this is a $1/ε$ rate with the same horizon dependence as unregularized RL. We also find the exact asymptotic constant of the plug-in estimator, a Fisher-weighted variance of the estimated soft Q-function. The estimator is locally asymptotically minimax among methods that sample every pair equally often, and we give the allocation of samples that is optimal among adaptive designs. Simulations on small MDPs match the rates and the constants.

</details>


### [148] [Evaluating Compositional Generalization in Transformers: The Role of Composition Equivalence and Module Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13498)
*Purva Pruthi,Andrew Yuan,Alexander D'Amour,David Jensen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估Transformer的组合泛化能力，识别出训练-测试分布中两个关键因素——“组合等价性”与“模块覆盖”，并揭示直接模型的高性能主要源于利用组合等价性产生的捷径，而非真正的组合泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于Transformer组合泛化的实证研究结论不一致，原因在于评估往往只在有限的、临时设定的训练-测试划分上进行，缺乏系统化的评测设计，难以判断模型是否真正具备组合泛化能力。

Method: 对组合泛化任务进行系统性评测，构造受控的训练-测试划分，分别操纵两个维度：(1)组合等价性——不同模块序列归约为相同端到端函数；(2)模块覆盖——模块在训练与测试中出现于不同上下文。对比只学习最终输出的“直接模型”与学习中间及最终输出的“逐步模型”。并以Llama3-8B在LiveCodeBench代码输出预测任务上作为案例研究。

Result: 直接模型的表面性能主要来自利用组合等价性；当基准消除等价性后，性能显著下降，表明其无法泛化到已知模块的新组合所产生的新端到端函数。随着模块覆盖偏移增大，性能单调下降，且同时影响直接模型与逐步模型。基准若同时包含模块覆盖违反与组合等价性，会加剧捷径学习。

Conclusion: 含组合等价性的基准会显著高估直接模型的泛化能力，因此基准设计应避免组合等价性并控制模块覆盖偏移；案例研究进一步证实组合等价性在预训练模型Llama3-8B的代码预测性能中起关键作用。

Abstract: Compositional generalization is an important form of out-of-distribution generalization, defined as the ability to train on some combinations of modules (e.g., functions such as sort, filter) and then generalize to unseen combinations. Despite substantial empirical work evaluating compositional generalization in transformers, findings have been inconsistent because evaluations were often conducted on a limited set of train-test splits, defined in an ad hoc manner. Through a systematic evaluation, we identify two key aspects of the train-test distribution that strongly affect performance: composition equivalence and module coverage. We show that the apparent performance of direct models (trained only on final outputs) results primarily from exploiting composition equivalences, meaning different sequences of modules that reduce to identical end-to-end functions. When benchmarks eliminate these equivalences, performance drops significantly, showing their inability to generalize to compositions of known modules that produce novel end-to-end functions. We further demonstrate that performance degrades monotonically as module coverage shifts increase (when modules appear in different contexts between train and test), affecting both direct and step-by-step models (trained on intermediate and final outputs). These findings have concrete implications for benchmark design: benchmarks that include compositional equivalences can lead to significant overestimation of the generalization abilities of direct models, and benchmarks that include both module coverage violations and compositional equivalences can exacerbate shortcut learning. As a case study, we demonstrate the role of compositional equivalence on the generalization performance of the pre-trained model Llama3-8B on the code-output prediction task in the coding benchmark LiveCodeBench.

</details>


### [149] [Parallel Broyden Methods for Efficiently Evaluating Nonlinear State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13523)
*Ian Christopher Tanoh,Scott W. Linderman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于Broyden方法的拟牛顿法，用对角加低秩近似雅可比块，从轨迹割线构造近似，保持并行扫描兼容性，在低秩RNN、并行MCMC与扩散模型上收敛更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性状态空间模型通常只能串行求值，难以利用现代并行硬件。已有工作将求值重构为求根问题并用并行Newton法求解，但精确Newton需计算昂贵的稠密雅可比乘积；基于对角近似的拟牛顿法虽可扩展，却因忽略状态维度间耦合而收敛缓慢。

Method: 提出一种基于Broyden方法的拟牛顿方案：用对角加低秩（diagonal-plus-low-rank）近似雅可比矩阵的各个块以捕捉耦合项；近似由轨迹割线（trajectory secants）构造，无需自动微分；通过秩压缩（rank compression）保持与并行扫描的兼容性及对角近似同等的可扩展性。

Result: 理论证明：在压缩扫描下可有限步恢复精确轨迹；收敛性由压缩误差与沿当前轨迹误差方向的块近似误差刻画，而非谱范数。实验显示：在耦合近似低秩或雅可比求值昂贵的场景更占优——低秩RNN、并行MCMC算法、去噪扩散模型中，迭代次数少于其他拟牛顿法，在雅可比求值昂贵或状态维度大时是最快的并行方法。

Conclusion: 对角加低秩的Broyden型拟牛顿近似在保留对角法可扩展性与并行扫描兼容性的同时显著改善收敛，适用于低秩耦合结构或雅可比昂贵的非线性状态空间模型并行求值场景。

Abstract: Nonlinear state space models are typically evaluated sequentially, limiting their ability to exploit modern parallel hardware. Recent work has shown that state space models can be evaluated in parallel by reformulating evaluation as a root-finding problem, which can be solved with a parallel form of Newton's method. However, exact Newton methods require expensive multiplication of dense Jacobian matrices, and quasi-Newton methods based on diagonal approximations, while more scalable, are often slow to converge because they neglect interactions across state dimensions. We introduce a novel quasi-Newton approach based on Broyden's method, which captures coupling terms with diagonal-plus-low-rank approximations to blocks of the Jacobian. We construct the approximations from trajectory secants, avoiding automatic differentiation while retaining the favorable scaling of diagonal approximations and remaining compatible with parallel scan through rank compression. We prove finite-step recovery of the exact trajectory under the compressed scan, and show that convergence is tracked by the compression error and by the block approximation error along the current trajectory error, rather than in spectral norm. Empirically, we show that our method is advantageous when interactions are approximately low rank or when Jacobian evaluation is expensive: on low-rank RNNs, parallel MCMC algorithms, and denoising diffusion models, it converges in fewer iterations than other quasi-Newton methods and is the fastest parallel method when Jacobian evaluation is expensive or state dimensions are large.

</details>


### [150] [Closing the BF16-FP16 Convergence Speed Gap in RL Post-Training with Stochastic Rounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13545)
*Mahdi Nazemi,Oleg Rybakov,Tijmen Blankevoort,Asit Mishra,Carlo del Mundo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）中，标准混合精度BF16训练在相同学习率下比FP16收敛更慢，原因是权重“卡滞”（weight sticking）；作者通过在对FP32影子权重做BF16量化时引入随机舍入（stochastic rounding）来解决，既匹配FP16收敛速度，又降低相对参考模型的KL散度，同时保留BF16的动态范围优势且无需loss scaling。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BF16因动态范围与FP32相同、收敛性与FP32训练相近，被广泛用于大语言模型预训练。但在可验证奖励的强化学习中，学习率通常较低，作者发现相同学习率下标准混合精度BF16训练比FP16收敛更慢，需要解释并消除这一差距。

Method: 将差距归因于“权重卡滞”：FP32影子权重经round-to-nearest量化为BF16时，小幅更新常使用于rollout生成与训练的BF16权重保持不变，需多个优化器步才能体现。提高BF16学习率可缓解卡滞并达到FP16收敛速度，但会增大相对参考模型的KL散度。作者改为在FP32影子权重→BF16量化时使用随机舍入（stochastic rounding）。

Result: 随机舍入在不同模型和数据集上均达到FP16的收敛速度，且相对“提高学习率的BF16训练”降低了KL散度；与FP16不同，它保留BF16更宽的动态范围，且无需loss scaling。

Conclusion: 在RLVR低学习率场景下，BF16权重卡滞是BF16落后FP16的主因；用随机舍入替代round-to-nearest量化是一种低成本、无需调整学习率的改进方案，兼顾收敛速度、KL散度控制与BF16的数值优势。

Abstract: BF16 is widely used to pretrain large language models because it has the same dynamic range as FP32 and converges similarly to FP32 training. However, in reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards, where learning rates are typically low, we find that standard mixed-precision BF16 training converges more slowly than FP16 training at the same learning rate. We attribute this gap to weight sticking: when FP32 shadow weights are quantized to BF16 using round-to-nearest, small updates often leave the BF16 weights used for rollout generation and training unchanged. Consequently, several optimizer steps may be needed before updates to the shadow weights are reflected in the BF16 weights. Raising the BF16 learning rate reduces weight sticking and matches FP16's convergence speed, but it also increases the KL divergence from the reference model. We instead apply stochastic rounding when quantizing the FP32 shadow weights to BF16. This approach matches FP16's convergence speed across different models and datasets while reducing the KL divergence relative to BF16 training with a larger learning rate. Unlike FP16, it keeps BF16's wider dynamic range and needs no loss scaling.

</details>


### [151] [Massive Activations Confound Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13548)
*Nathan Delisle,Chenhao Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出大规模激活（massive activations）使PC1占据高达99.9%的激活方差，导致稀疏自编码器（SAE）的"方差解释率"与"消融损伤"两项评估指标失效，并提出去除PC1训练、重建时加回PC1的修正方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAE通常用方差解释率（variance explained）和消融损伤（ablation damage）来评估，但作者发现massive activations会让这两项指标严重失真：PC1主导方差、缺乏唯一稀疏分解、不同训练run重建出互不匹配的特征，使重要性排序被这些不稳定特征主导，评估结果不可靠。

Method: (1) 诊断：量化PC1的方差占比、跨run特征匹配率、以及方差解释率中PC1之外成分的残留；(2) 修正：在去掉PC1的激活上训练SAE，即绕过PC1，再在重建结果上加回PC1。

Result: PC1可解释高达99.9%的方差；两次独立run中，top-50最重要特征平均有47个不匹配（随机50个约27个）；字典可报告99.96%方差解释率却仍有33%的非PC1方差未被解释。修正后在受影响模型上top-50特征共享率从4–39.5%升至73–99.5%；同等TopK稀疏度下模型损伤最多降低44%，内容重建误差最多降低33.9%。

Conclusion: massive activations是SAE评估中的关键混杂因素，PC1应被单独处理而非交由SAE拟合；PC1去除+回加的方案显著提升特征稳定性与重建/损伤指标，建议作为SAE评估与训练的常规修正。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are commonly evaluated by variance explained and ablation damage. We show how massive activations undermine both evaluations, and propose a correction. Massive activations cause up to 99.9% of activation variance to lie along the first principal component (PC1). Because the PC1 component has no unique sparse decomposition, each SAE run rebuilds it from a different set of features. These features dominate importance rankings: an average of 47 of the 50 most important features are unmatched in a separate run, compared with about 27 of a random 50. A dictionary can also report 99.96% of variance explained while leaving 33% of the variance outside PC1 unexplained. We bypass PC1 by training the SAE on activations with PC1 removed, then adding PC1 to the reconstruction. In affected models, the top-50 feature sharing rate rises from 4-39.5% to 73-99.5%. At the same TopK sparsity, the resulting SAE degrades the model up to 44% less and reduces content reconstruction error by up to 33.9%.

</details>


### [152] [MouldIt: Data Efficiency of Multi-Task Temporal Representation Learning in Injection Moulding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13646)
*Abdullah Ayad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MouldIt——基于时间卷积与注意力的变分自编码器，用于注塑过程的多任务表征学习，在合成数据集上验证其数据效率与多模态优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能工业方案需同时处理多任务，要求模型理解底层工艺过程；本文以注塑成型为案例，考察表征学习相对传统机器学习的数据效率及多模态收益。

Method: 基于Erlanger Träger零件的真实参考周期合成工艺知情数据集；提出MouldIt（时间卷积+注意力VAE），重构模内信号并将零件分类为合格或某一合成失效模式；在9种数据规模、单模态与多模态设置下评测已知类分类与未知类检测两项任务，与accept-all基线及kNN、逻辑回归、随机森林、PCA等适配方法对比。

Result: 单模态下从4000周期起、多模态下从2000周期起MouldIt优于经典方法；分拣损失分别为6.0±0.7%与1.0±0.3%，在全部基线及PCA+随机森林组合中排名第一。

Conclusion: 基于时间卷积与注意力的VAE表征学习在注塑过程建模中展现出更好的数据效率和显著的多模态增益，适合作为工业多任务智能方案的核心建模方式。

Abstract: Smart industrial solutions that can address multiple tasks require models that understand the underlying processes. We examine representation learning for modeling the injection moulding process as an example of such solutions. We consider its data efficiency and multi-modal benefit compared to classical machine learning alternatives. To this end, we synthesize a process-informed dataset based on a real reference cycle of an Erlanger Träger part. We then introduce MouldIt, a temporal convolution and attention based variational autoencoder that reconstructs in-mould signals, while classifying parts as acceptable or as one of our synthetic failure modes. We evaluate MouldIt on two tasks, known-class classification and unknown-class detection, across nine dataset sizes in single-modal and multi-modal settings. We compare it with an accept-all baseline and the classical methods k-nearest neighbors, logistic regression, random forests, and principal component analysis, which we adapt to support both tasks. MouldIt outperforms classical methods across both tasks from 4000 cycles in the single-modal setting and from 2000 cycles in the multi-modal setting. It achieves part sorting losses of 6.0 $\pm$ 0.7% and 1.0 $\pm$ 0.3% in the single-modal and multi-modal settings respectively, ranking first across all baselines and a combination of principal component analysis and random forests.

</details>


### [153] [Tabular Foundation Models in Financial Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13550)
*Ed deHaan,Allen H. Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将表格基础模型（TFMs）引入会计研究，在"预测企业下一年盈余变动"这一基准任务上评估其表现。TabFM 精度优于微调开源大语言模型和多种树模型，且速度更快、算力消耗更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 会计与金融预测任务通常依赖结构化表格数据，现有方法要么需要训练/调参，要么使用可能记忆企业历史结果的大模型，存在前视偏差风险。作者希望引入免训练、可上下文学习的表格基础模型，并检验其在该基准任务上是否具备实用价值。

Method: 技术说明（technical note）性质：介绍 TFMs 并做单项基准任务对比。TFMs 在合成表格数据上预训练，发布后无需再训练或调参，通过上下文学习直接输出数值预测，从而规避因记忆企业结果导致的前视偏差。对比对象包括 Google Research 的 TabFM、近期会计研究中微调的开源权重大语言模型，以及若干树模型设定。

Result: TabFM 预测精度高于全部对比模型；相比大语言模型更快、算力消耗更低。部分树模型设定的预测速度快于 TabFM，因此在二者之间选择存在精度与速度的权衡。

Conclusion: TFMs 有望成为会计与金融预测的新工具；但评估仅基于单一基准任务，且不太可能有某一类模型在所有情境下占优，后续需多任务、多设定验证。

Abstract: This technical note introduces tabular foundation models (TFMs) to accounting research and evaluates their performance in one common benchmark task: predicting firms' next-year earnings changes. TFMs produce numeric predictions and learn in context, without post-release training or tuning, making them natural and easy to use for forecasting. The TFMs we study are pretrained on synthetic tabular data, so they avoid look-ahead bias from memorizing firms' outcomes. We compare Google Research's TabFM with a fine-tuned open-weight large language model developed in recent accounting research and with several tree-based models. TabFM is more accurate than all comparison models. It is also faster and less computationally intensive than the large language model. Several tree-based specifications produce predictions more quickly than TabFM, so choosing between them involves a trade-off between accuracy and speed. We conclude that TFMs have promise as a new tool for forecasting in accounting and finance. However, our evaluation is based on a single benchmark task, and no class of models is likely to dominate in all settings.

</details>


### [154] [Discovering Structurally Plausible and Interpretable Cognitive Models with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13561)
*Boyoung Kwon,Jiyoon Seo,Sang Ho Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DisCo是一个LLM引导的框架，通过带Validator和Cleaner的进化搜索自动发现可解释的符号认知模型，在三个行为任务上达到与专用基线相当的拟合性能并保持结构合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统计算认知建模依赖人工构建，耗时且需大量领域专业知能；现有LLM自动建模方法过度追求预测精度，牺牲了结构合理性与参数可识别性。

Method: 提出DisCo框架：以LLM引导的进化搜索发现可解释符号认知模型，包含两个结构组件——Validator过滤结构无效模型，Cleaner通过删除冗余参数提升参数可识别性；并在三个行为任务上做消融分析。

Result: 在三个行为任务上，DisCo发现的模型性能与任务专用基线相当；消融显示Validator对恢复心理学有意义的结构至关重要，缺少该组件时模型更易违反行为约束，且与外部认知测量的关联一致性更差。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的模型发现若结合恰当的结构约束，可产出超越预测拟合的、具备认知合理性的模型。

Abstract: Computational cognitive modeling has traditionally relied on manual model construction, which is a time-intensive process that requires substantial domain expertise. Although recent LLM-based approaches have begun to automate model generation, they often prioritize predictive accuracy at the expense of structural plausibility and parameter identifiability. We introduce DisCo, an LLM-guided framework that discovers interpretable symbolic cognitive models through evolutionary search with two structural components: a Validator that filters structurally invalid models, and a Cleaner that promotes parameter identifiability by removing redundant parameters. Across three behavioral tasks, DisCo consistently discovers models that achieve performance competitive with task-specific baselines. Ablation analyses show that the Validator is important for recovering psychologically meaningful structure. Without this component, models tend to violate behavioral constraints and show less consistent associations with external cognitive measures. These results suggest that LLM-driven model discovery, when coupled with appropriate structural constraints, can yield cognitively plausible models that go beyond predictive fit.

</details>


### [155] [Investigating Composite Modeling in Multi-Objective Bayesian Optimization for Scientific Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13565)
*Ricky Ye,Sahas Goli,Muhammad Ali,Poompol Buathong,Peter I. Frazier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究复合建模（利用可观测中间响应）在多目标贝叶斯优化（MOBO）中的收益，并从理论与实验两方面论证：当中间响应到最终目标的映射为非双射、非仿射时，复合建模的增益更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MOBO 需在昂贵评估下平衡多个竞争目标；许多应用中可调输入先映射到可观测中间响应，再经已知函数组合为最终目标。既有工作已证明单目标下复合建模能提升采样效率，但其在 MOBO 中的收益尚缺乏系统研究，增益来源亦不明确。

Method: 首先在单目标设定下，建立中间观测不提供额外预测信息的理论条件（复合方法与黑盒方法共享同一 warped Gaussian process 先验）；随后在 8 个合成及应用启发的科学设计基准上，对四个 MOBO 方法的复合版本进行受控数值实验，黑盒版本采用标准多输出高斯过程建模目标，考察上述条件与多目标优化增益的关系。

Result: 单目标理论分析与跨 8 个基准、4 种 MOBO 方法的受控实验均支持同一结论：当中间响应到最终目标的映射为非双射且非仿射时，复合建模更倾向于带来更大优化增益；反之增益有限。

Conclusion: 复合建模在 MOBO 中的收益取决于响应—目标映射的函数性质；实践上应在映射非双射、非仿射时优先采用复合建模，而映射为双射或仿射时中间观测的额外信息价值有限。

Abstract: Scientific design often requires balancing competing objectives that are expensive to evaluate through experiments or simulations. Multi-objective Bayesian optimization (MOBO) addresses this challenge by using probabilistic models to guide the selection of promising designs. In many applications, tunable inputs map to observable intermediate responses, which are then combined through known functions to compute the final objectives. Prior work on Bayesian optimization of composite functions shows that learning from these intermediate responses, which we call Composite modeling, can substantially improve sampling efficiency in single-objective optimization, compared to methods that condition only on black-box observations of the objective function. The benefits of Composite modeling in MOBO, however, remain understudied. In this work, we study the benefits of Composite modeling in MOBO and investigate the factors driving variation in its gains. We first establish single-objective conditions under which intermediate observations provide no additional predictive information beyond objective values, where composite and black box methods use the same warped Gaussian process prior. Controlled numerical experiments with versions of four MOBO methods across eight synthetic and application-inspired scientific-design benchmarks examine how these conditions relate to optimization gains in the multi-objective setting, where the black box approach models the objective functions with a standard multi-output Gaussian process. Both investigations support the claim that composite modeling tends to help more when the function mapping intermediate responses to final objectives is non-bijective and non-affine.

</details>


### [156] [Metamorphic testing of learned AC optimal power flow surrogates reveals representation dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13566)
*Stephen Peng,Vineet Jagadeesan Nair*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文用13条蜕变关系审计17种AC-OPF代理模型/流水线配置，评估其精度是否依赖单一电网序列化编码；所有配置均违反至少6条关系，并定位到GridSFM-Open v1.1的索引错误与预处理问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在单一电网序列化（serialization）上评估学习式AC-OPF代理，无法判断其精度是否依赖于该编码方式；需要显式检验表示鲁棒性。

Method: 设计13条蜕变关系（metamorphic relations），每条规定变换的有效条件与输出比较方式；覆盖17种配置（2个已发布训练检查点 + 15个随机权重架构/机制对照）；结合源码审查与中间张量干预定位缺陷；另做case500训练实验考察数据增强，并核查GPU/CPU特征求解器不一致的影响。

Result: 所有配置在报告容差×10放宽或收紧下均违反≥6条关系；GridSFM-Open v1.1存在索引bug及三项非规范/数值不稳定预处理；增强实验在4个模型中3个降低变换视图误差、4个均降低中位差异与约束违反P95，但部分关系变差，所有预测仍AC不可行；raw-Laplacian基线的大幅提升主要源于GPU/CPU特征求解器不匹配。

Conclusion: 建议在精度、可行性与成本之外同时报告表示一致性；结果既不支持部署就绪性，也不支持普遍不变性结论。

Abstract: Evaluating a learned AC optimal power flow (AC-OPF) surrogate on one grid serialization cannot show whether its accuracy depends on that encoding. We test this dependence with thirteen metamorphic relations, each specifying when a transformation is valid and how to compare its outputs. Our audit covers 17 model and pipeline configurations: two released trained checkpoints and 15 random-weight architecture or mechanism controls. Every configuration violates at least six relations, even at reporting tolerances ten times tighter or looser. For GridSFM-Open v1.1, source inspection and intermediate-tensor interventions point to an indexing bug and three noncanonical or numerically unstable preprocessing choices; a repaired checkpoint remains untested. In a separate case500 training experiment, augmentation lowers transformed-view error in three of four models and median discrepancy and constraint-violation P95 in all four. These benefits depend on the training protocol: some relations worsen, and every evaluated prediction remains AC infeasible. A GPU/CPU eigensolver mismatch accounts for most of the large raw-Laplacian-baseline gain. We recommend reporting representation consistency alongside accuracy, feasibility, and cost. These results establish neither deployment readiness nor universal invariance.

</details>


### [157] [Market-Conditioned JEPA: Learning from Unpredictable Market Shocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13572)
*Yuqi Wang,Fengyuan Liu,Mukai Li,Haochen Luo,Lei Li,Qi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Market-Conditioned JEPA（MC-JEPA）：在联合嵌入预测架构中用留一法构造的市场因子条件、经资产自身表征导出的因子载荷注入预测，使A股分钟级数据上多类目标的预测、排序与回测表现均优于逐资产JEPA及其他基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融时间序列的很大一部分变异来自无法由资产自身历史预测的市场整体冲击，而逐资产自监督学习将这部分变异当作不可约噪声处理，造成表征学习效率低、市场暴露等目标难以刻画。

Method: 在潜因子模型下论证对已实现市场因子条件化可使资产的市场暴露成为最优预测的必要成分，同时降低资产特异成分学习所面对的噪声；据此构建MC-JEPA联合嵌入预测架构：训练时为每个资产用其他资产的未来目标嵌入构造留一法市场条件，并通过由资产自身表征得到的因子载荷将其注入预测器；使用11年A股分钟级数据，覆盖收益、市场暴露、风险与流动性九类目标，并配合消融、合成因子模型实验与误差归一去归一化后的因子载荷分析。

Result: MC-JEPA在九类目标上全面优于逐资产JEPA，其中暴露、风险与流动性上的提升最强且统计显著性最高；在全部流动性目标和2小时至5天的收益期限上取得最优RankIC，超越时间序列基础模型与自监督基线，并在历史回测中获得最佳组合表现。消融显示收益与暴露的增益依赖于条件与已实现市场走势对齐；恢复归一化移除的波动尺度后，预测器因子载荷无需标签即可接近监督探针的beta排序能力；已知参数的合成因子模型实验亦表明条件化同时改善市场暴露与资产特异成分的恢复。

Conclusion: 将已实现市场因子作为条件变量嵌入自监督预测架构，可把市场共同冲击由"噪声"转化为可学信号，显著提升金融时序表征的预测力、市场暴露刻画与下游组合表现，为因子结构感知的自监督学习提供了一条有效路径。

Abstract: Much of the variation in financial time series is driven by market-wide shocks that cannot be predicted from an asset's own history. Per-asset self-supervised learning treats this variation as irreducible noise. Under a latent factor model, we show that conditioning prediction on the realized market factor makes the asset's market exposure necessary for optimal prediction, while reducing the noise against which the asset-specific component is learned. Building on this insight, we introduce Market-Conditioned JEPA (MC-JEPA), a joint-embedding predictive architecture for financial time series. During training, we construct a leave-one-out market condition for each asset from the future target embeddings of other assets and incorporate it through factor loadings derived from the asset's own representation. On 11 years of minute-level Chinese A-share data, MC-JEPA improves over per-asset JEPA across nine targets spanning returns, market exposure, risk, and liquidity, with the strongest and most statistically significant gains in exposure, risk, and liquidity. It achieves the best RankIC on all liquidity targets and on return horizons from two hours to five days, outperforming time-series foundation models and self-supervised baselines, and delivers the best portfolio performance in a historical backtest. Ablations show that the return and exposure gains require the condition to be aligned with the realized market movement. After restoring the volatility scale removed by normalization, the predictor's factor loadings rank stocks by market beta nearly as well as a supervised probe, without labels. A synthetic factor-model study with known parameters shows that conditioning improves the recovery of both market exposure and the asset-specific component.

</details>


### [158] [Unified Optimization of Traffic Signal and Lane Channelization for an Isolated Intersection Using Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13599)
*Jiwon Kang,Jiyoung Kim,Hyunseok Jun,Keemin Sohn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于深度Q网络（DQN）的强化学习方法，将交叉口信号配时与车道渠化（可变车道标线）进行实时联合优化，并借助人类反馈以规则方式过滤不可行的动作转移，从而在提升通行能力的同时保证驾驶安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信号控制是实时动态问题，而车道渠化设计是静态问题，二者难以联合求解。已有研究用混合整数线性规划（MILP）联合求解信号与道路宽度设计，但每当交通流模式变化就需重新求解，且问题规模增大时计算时间不可接受，缺乏实时性。

Method: 将联合优化建模为强化学习问题，设计可容纳"信号相位+车道标线方案"联合动作的深度Q网络（DQN），实现实时决策；同时引入基于人类反馈的训练机制，以规则方式过滤掉与信号相位不相容的、会危及驾驶人的车道标线突变动作，避免危险的动作切换。实现上要求交叉口各停止线处配备可变车道标线标志。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提方法通过提高交叉口吞吐量改善了交叉口运行性能，验证了实时联合优化的有效性。

Conclusion: RL方法可在实时条件下求解信号与车道渠化的联合优化问题，规避MILP反复求解的计算瓶颈；配合规则化安全约束与可变标线设施，可兼顾效率与安全，是替代传统静态优化建模的可行途径。

Abstract: Optimizing traffic signal control has been a long-lasting critical issue in traffic engineering, and a number of robust methodologies have emerged to solve the problem so far. On the other hand, the road design for lane channelization is a static problem that is difficult to solve jointly with the real-time traffic signal control. If traffic volume does not vary, it is possible that both problems can be solved in an integrated manner as a single static optimization problem. Recently, researchers have devised a mixed integer linear problem (MILP) to jointly determine the traffic signal and road width designs. However, this joint optimization problem must be solved repeatedly whenever the traffic flow pattern varies. Solving the MILP may also take impractical computing time as the problem size gets larger. We present a robust methodology for solving the joint optimization problem using reinforcement learning (RL) on a real-time basis. Abrupt changes in lane markings with an incompatible signal phase can jeopardize drivers. To sidestep such a dangerous action change, we train a model with human feedback to filter infeasible action transitions in a rule-based manner. We devised a deep Q-network that can accommodate joint actions for traffic signal and lane marking plans. To ensure the implementation of the proposed operation, a variable sign for lane markings is necessary at each stop line of an intersection. The experimental results confirm that the proposed methodology enhances the intersection performance by increasing throughput.

</details>


### [159] [Multifidelity Operator Inference for Non-intrusive Model Reduction under Data Scarcity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13624)
*Vivian Zhang,Tomoki Koike,Atticus Rex,Elizabeth Qian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出多保真算子推断（MF-OpInf），利用多保真线性回归框架融合稀缺高保真快照与丰富低保真数据来估计降阶算子，从而降低OpInf代理模型的方差，并在相同计算预算下获得比单保真OpInf更高的PDE解场预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程设计与不确定性量化中的多查询分析需反复求解高保真PDE模型，代价高昂，故需代理模型。基于机器学习的非侵入式代理建模中，算子推断（OpInf）通过拟合降阶算子实现，但计算预算有限导致高保真训练数据稀缺，仅用稀缺数据训练的OpInf模型方差大，在高风险工程设计场景中构成关键缺陷。

Method: 提出多保真算子推断MF-OpInf：以同一问题较粗离散化产生的丰富低保真数据补充稀缺高保真快照；采用多保真线性回归框架统一处理不同保真度数据，估计降阶算子。

Result: 在若干模型PDE问题上的数值实验表明，在相同计算预算下，MF-OpInf相比仅用高保真数据的OpInf具有更高的PDE解场预测精度。

Conclusion: 多保真数据融合能有效缓解OpInf在数据稀缺下的高方差问题，使代理建模在有限预算下更可靠地支撑不确定性量化与工程设计工作流。

Abstract: Many-query analyses, in which a computational model is repeatedly evaluated, play an important role in uncertainty quantification and optimization for engineering design. However, many-query analyses can be prohibitively expensive when considering high-fidelity numerical models, especially those governed by partial differential equations (PDEs). Surrogate modeling replaces costly simulations with cheap approximations, enabling practical uncertainty quantification and design workflows. Machine learning (ML) approaches to surrogate modeling have gained significant traction in the development of surrogate models due to their non-intrusive nature; they do not require access to the underlying simulation code. The Operator Inference (OpInf) approach, in particular, fits reduced operators to state and corresponding time derivative data. However, high-fidelity training data is scarce due to limited computational budgets, and OpInf models trained on such scarce data often exhibit high variance, a critical liability in high-risk engineering design. We propose Multifidelity Operator Inference (MF-OpInf), which reduces this variance by supplementing scarce high-fidelity snapshots with abundant low-fidelity data drawn from coarser discretizations of the same problem. MF-OpInf handles data across varying fidelity levels by utilizing a multifidelity linear regression framework to estimate the reduced operators. Numerical experiments on model PDE problems demonstrate that MF-OpInf predicts PDE solution fields with higher accuracy compared to the high-fidelity-only OpInf with the same computational budget.

</details>


### [160] [CP-MLPs: A Tensor-Rank Theory of Tied and Untied MLP Blocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13647)
*Md Rifat Arefin,Farzaneh Heidari,Irina Rish,Guillaume Rabusseau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 CP-MLPs 张量框架，把前馈块的每个乘性单元分解为"检测—读取—写出"三个角色，证明绑定(gated tied)与非绑定(gated untied)结构存在精确的按次数张量秩分离，说明解绑的宽度优势取决于目标函数结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer 前馈块混合了平方激活、门控、参数绑定/解绑等多种设计，但各要素对表达能力的独立贡献缺乏理论刻画，尤其"绑定检测器与值方向"这一选择的代价不清。

Method: 引入 CP-MLPs 张量（CP 分解）视角：每个乘性单元贡献一个秩-1 交互，并承担检测输入特征、读取取值、写出输出三种角色。据此区分：ReLU² 等 tied gated 块强制检测器与值共享方向；ReGLU、SwiGLU 等 untied gated 块允许二者不同。通过按次数(degree-wise)的张量秩分析给出精确的表达-宽度分离。

Result: 得到精确的按次数张量秩分离：对非定二次交互与角色非对称单项式，untied 单元在宽度上严格更高效；而 tied 单元在结构上与对称纯幂目标相匹配。实验中出现低于精确表示宽度的损失平台，但该平台本身不构成正近似差距，任意精度逼近由 border rank 决定；在匹配输入投影预算的选定比较中，训练后的 untied 门控 MLP 仍保有优势。

Conclusion: 解绑门控的宽度优势依赖于目标结构而非普遍成立；评估门控 MLP 的表达能力应以张量秩/border rank 为判据，而非仅凭损失平台。代码开源。

Abstract: Transformer feed-forward blocks combine several design choices: squared activations, gating, and tied versus untied parameter sharing, whose separate contributions to expressivity remain poorly understood. We introduce CP-MLPs, a tensor framework in which each multiplicative unit contributes a rank-1 interaction with three roles: detecting an input feature, reading a value, and writing to the output. Tied gated blocks such as ReLU$^2$ force the detector and value roles to share the same direction; untied gated blocks such as ReGLU and SwiGLU let them differ. We show that this distinction yields exact, degree-wise tensor-rank separations: untied detector-value units are strictly more width-efficient for indefinite quadratic interactions and role-asymmetric monomials, while tied units are structurally matched to symmetric pure-power targets. The width advantage of untying therefore depends on the target structure. Experiments show loss plateaus below the exact-representation width, but these plateaus alone do not establish a positive approximation gap: arbitrarily accurate approximation is governed by border rank. In trained gated MLPs, untied blocks retain an advantage in selected comparisons at matched input-projection budget. Code is available at https://github.com/rarefin/CP-MLPs.

</details>


### [161] [SepsisFlow: an event-stream foundation model for continuous risk warning and trajectory modelling in ICU sepsis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13661)
*Yawen Zhang,Wanzhe Xu,Zhenhua Zhang,Jinyu Wu,Yizhou Zhuang,Yitao Yang,Kenta Nakai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SepsisFlow 是一个以入院后逐小时为统一时间轴的脓毒症"预警—发生情境—早期病程"三段式框架：发生前用 Mamba+梯度提升树做6/12/24小时风险预测，发生时用门控网络融合多模态编码生成患者状态以界定发生情境，发生后用注意力双向循环网络编码72小时器官支持与生理轨迹刻画早期病程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脓毒症是ICU死亡的主要原因，起病方式与病程高度异质。现有预警模型多只预测"是否会发生脓毒症"，一旦报警，临床医生仍不清楚其发生情境与随后早期演变轨迹，难以据此决策。

Method: 构建统一按小时推进的三阶段模型：①发生前，Mamba（在257万份EHR上预训练）结合约1700个逐小时特征输入梯度提升树，预测6/12/24小时风险；②发生时，通过门控网络融合Mamba与表格编码器得到连续患者状态以定义发生情境；③发生后，用注意力双向循环网络编码72小时器官支持与生理轨迹，得到早期病程分层。在MIMIC-IV上评估预警性能并与16种重训算法及NEWS2、qSOFA、SIRS等比较，在eICU上检验风险排序的泛化性。

Result: 同等报警次数下，预警检出病例数多于NEWS2、qSOFA与SIRS，逐小时更新带来约每100例中多检出8例；优于16种重训算法，并在eICU保持风险排序。发生时情境可区分28天死亡率：心血管ICU相关情境约2%，其余情境15%–17%；各情境内，发生后持续器官支持者死亡率约为降级者的5倍。

Conclusion: SepsisFlow 确立了危重症中以基础模型范式实现连续风险预测与病程轨迹分层的路径，将预警从二元报警拓展为"情境+轨迹"的多维刻画。

Abstract: Sepsis is a leading cause of ICU death with heterogeneous onset and course, yet most warning models predict only whether sepsis will occur, leaving clinicians uncertain of its onset context and early trajectory once an alarm sounds. To answer these questions on one hourly clock from ICU admission, we built SepsisFlow: before onset, it combines a Mamba model pretrained on 2.57 million EHRs with about 1,700 hourly features in gradient-boosted trees to predict 6/12/24-h risk; at onset, it fuses Mamba and tabular encoders via a gated network into continuous patient states defining onset contexts; after onset, it encodes 72-h organ-support and physiological trajectories via an attention bidirectional recurrent network into early courses. For pre-onset warning in MIMIC-IV, SepsisFlow detected more cases than NEWS2, qSOFA, and SIRS at equal alarms, gaining about 8 cases per 100 from hourly updating, while outperforming 16 retrained algorithms and maintaining risk ranking in eICU. At onset, its contexts separated 2% cardiovascular-ICU from 15%-17% other 28-day mortality, and within each context after onset, persistent support carried fivefold higher mortality than de-escalation. SepsisFlow establishes a foundation-model paradigm for continuous risk and trajectory stratification in critical care.

</details>


### [162] [Bootstrapped On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13680)
*Yue Wu,Xueyu Zhou,Yangrong Hu,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现on-policy self-distillation（OPSD）在持续训练中会因特权教师与无参考学生的分布失配而性能退化，据此提出两阶段框架BOPD：先用OPSD把参考条件监督内化到中间模型，再把该无参考中间模型蒸馏进初始模型的新副本，从而提升数学推理与代码生成表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD无需外部教师，早期提升显著，但在持续训练下并不稳定，最终可能出现大幅性能下降。作者将这一退化归因于不断演化的无参考学生与冻结的特权教师之间分布失配逐渐扩大：早期特权教师与学生高度对齐、监督有用；随着训练推进，失配增大使教师给出误导性监督，反而损害学生。这一现象促使作者思考如何在保留特权参考监督价值的同时避免其后期负面作用。

Method: 提出Bootstrapped On-Policy Distillation（BOPD），一个两阶段框架。第一阶段利用OPSD，将参考条件（privileged reference）监督中的任务相关信息内化为一个中间模型；第二阶段将该无参考（reference-free）的中间模型作为教师，蒸馏到一个由初始模型复制而来的新学生中。即先"获取知识"再"迁移知识"，从而规避学生与特权教师分布失配导致的误导监督。

Result: 在多种模型上的实验表明，BOPD在数学推理与代码生成任务上均优于初始模型，也优于所构建的OPSD教师模型，验证了该两阶段策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 特权参考监督的价值可以被更有效地利用：先把其任务相关信号迁移到一个任务适配的无参考模型中，再从该模型进行蒸馏，比直接长久使用特权教师更优。这表明OPSD更适合作为知识内化的短程阶段，而非持续训练目标。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) improves language models by distilling teacher predictions on student-generated trajectories, while on-policy self-distillation (OPSD) removes the need for an external teacher by using a reference-conditioned copy of the model as the teacher. Despite its great early improvements, we find that OPSD does not remain consistently effective under continued training and can eventually suffer substantial performance degradation. We study this training behavior and link the degradation to a growing mismatch between the evolving reference-free student and the frozen privileged teacher. In the early stage of OPSD, the privileged teacher remains closely aligned with the student and provides useful guidance for improving the student's generation trajectories. However, as training progresses, the growing distributional mismatch between the privileged teacher and reference-free student can cause the teacher to provide misleading supervision, ultimately degrading student performance. This phenomenon motivates us to first leverage privileged reference supervision to acquire useful knowledge and then transfer it to a fresh student. We introduce Bootstrapped On-Policy Distillation (BOPD), a two-stage framework that first uses OPSD to internalize task-relevant information from reference-conditioned supervision into an intermediate model, and then distills this reference-free intermediate model into a fresh copy of the initial model. Experiments across models show that BOPD improves mathematical reasoning and code generation beyond both the initial models and the constructed OPSD teachers. These results suggest that privileged reference supervision can be more effectively exploited by first transferring its task-relevant signal into a task-adapted model and then distilling from that model without reference conditioning.

</details>


### [163] [FedVP: Federated Learning across Heterogeneous Models by Pooling Locally Trained Verifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13708)
*Yifan Li,Xi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出联邦验证器池（FEDVP），以“共享验证而非参数聚合”的新范式实现异构LLM客户端的联邦协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联邦学习依赖共享模型骨干聚合客户端更新，要求各机构使用同构模型；当机构部署来自不同家族、不同规模的LLM时，该前提失效，导致无法协作。

Method: 每个客户端用本地数据及自身所选LLM骨干生成的回答训练一个验证器；服务器将各客户端独立训练的验证器汇集成池，通过组合各验证器的判断进行答案选择，不合并任何模型参数；LLM骨干保持冻结，骨干与验证器在架构和规模上均可异构。

Result: 在五个公开医学问答数据集（视为联邦客户端）上，使用Qwen与LLaMA 3B–14B骨干进行评估，FEDVP在全部五个客户端上均带来一致提升，平均准确率较最强传统联邦基线高1.46–5.79个百分点。

Conclusion: FEDVP证明通过验证器层面的共享即可在异构LLM间实现有效联邦协作，规避参数聚合的同构限制，为跨机构LLM协同提供了可扩展的新路径。

Abstract: Conventional federated learning relies on the shared model backbone to aggregate client updates, limiting collaboration among institutions that deploy large language models (LLMs) from different families and at different scales. We propose Federated Verifier Pool (FEDVP), a new federated learning paradigm that enables heterogeneous clients to collaborate through shared verification rather than parameter aggregation. Each client trains a verifier using local data and responses generated by its chosen LLM backbone. The server pools these independently trained verifiers and combines their judgments to select answers without merging model parameters. This allows both LLM backbones and verifiers to differ in architecture and size, while keeping the LLM backbones frozen and deriving collaborative gains from improved answer selection. We evaluate FEDVP across five public medical question-answering datasets treated as federated clients, using Qwen and LLaMA backbones ranging from 3B to 14B parameters. FEDVP consistently improves performance across all five clients and outperforms the strongest conventional federated baselines by 1.46-5.79 percentage points in mean accuracy.

</details>


### [164] [TRUER: Test-Time Reward Correction Using an Error-Aware Rectifier for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13714)
*Mingxiang Shen,Jinhao Li,Jiaming Xu,Yibo Han,Guohao Dai,Yijia Zhang,Ningyi Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRUER：用有限标注源集训练一个"奖励矫正器"，在测试时强化学习中修正多数投票产生的错误奖励，从而提升奖励准确性与模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时强化学习（TTRL）依赖模型自身答案导出的奖励，但多数投票会把错误解一致化，形成"假阳性奖励"——审计显示错误奖励中假阳性占主导。同时大规模获取真实标签成本高，但存在少量已标注源集可加利用。

Method: 提出 TRUER 框架：(1) 利用有限的标注源集训练一个奖励矫正器（reward rectifier），使其能区分"正确性"与"共识性"；(2) 训练时重点采样/加权"错误多数"与"正确少数"这类共识与正确性相冲突的样本；(3) 训练完成后冻结矫正器，在后续测试时自适应阶段用它校正奖励信号。

Result: 在所有评估配置下，TRUER 在奖励准确率与模型性能两项指标上均一致优于朴素 TTRL（naive TTRL）。

Conclusion: 奖励准确性是测试时强化学习的关键瓶颈；借助少量监督即可有效抑制共识性假阳性，提升 TTRL 的可靠性，且该设计具备跨配置的一致性与良好可扩展性。

Abstract: Test-time reinforcement learning enables language models to adapt using rewards derived from their own answers. However, majority voting can reinforce incorrect solutions even when correct alternatives are available. Our training audit shows that false positives (incorrect responses receiving positive rewards) dominate reward errors. We focus on a practical regime where ground-truth rewards are difficult to obtain at scale, while a limited labeled source set is available. We introduce TRUER, a framework that uses limited supervision to train a reward rectifier that distinguishes correctness from consensus. To achieve this goal, we train the rectifier with an emphasis on incorrect majorities and correct minorities, targeting cases where consensus and correctness disagree. After that, we freeze the rectifier and use it to correct rewards during subsequent test-time adaptation. By improving reward accuracy and thereby model performance, TRUER consistently outperforms naive TTRL in both metrics across all evaluated configurations.

</details>


### [165] [Weighted-AoI-Driven UAV Trajectory and Reporting for Dynamic CKM Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13726)
*Zeyu Zhao,Yueling Che,Sheng Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究无人机集群在空域二维平面上的连续在线动态信道知识地图（CKM）跟踪问题，提出基于加权信息年龄（W-AoI）的调度方法，将时间陈旧度与2D U-Net预测的空间不确定性结合，并证明Top-K W-AoI选择可最大化单时隙误差下降上界，据此设计轻量贪心轨迹策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CKM构建假设环境准静态，在信道快速去相关时失效，因此需要面向动态环境的持续在线CKM跟踪。

Method: 将问题建模为无人机移动性与上报约束下的长期误差最小化问题；针对未测量小区的误差不可观测导致问题难解，提出W-AoI指标驱动调度：W-AoI融合精确时间陈旧度与2D U-Net预测的空间不确定性；证明Top-K W-AoI选择最大化单时隙误差下降上界；在此基础上设计融入连续沿路径感知的轻量贪心轨迹策略。

Result: 仿真显示，相比仅用AoI和FIFO基线，稳态误差分别降低3.8 dB²与4.8 dB²，且性能接近理想oracle方案。

Conclusion: W-AoI调度为动态CKM在线跟踪提供了有理论保证且轻量的解决方案，可在无人机集群约束下高效逼近oracle性能。

Abstract: Existing channel knowledge map (CKM) construction assumes quasi-static environments, failing when channels rapidly decorrelate. This paper investigates continuous online dynamic CKM tracking over an airborne 2D plane via a UAV fleet. We formulate a long-term error minimization problem under UAV mobility and reporting constraints. This problem, however, is intractable with unobservable errors for unmeasured cells. To tackle this, we propose a metric-driven scheduling approach featuring the Weighted-Age of Information (W-AoI). W-AoI evaluates cells by combining exact temporal staleness with spatial uncertainty predicted by a 2D U-Net. We prove that top-$K$ W-AoI selection maximizes the one-slot error reduction bound. Guided by this guarantee, we develop a lightweight greedy trajectory policy incorporating continuous along-path sensing. Simulations demonstrate our method reduces steady-state error by 3.8 dB$^2$ and 4.8 dB$^2$ over AoI-only and FIFO baselines, closely approaching an idealized oracle.

</details>


### [166] [Deterministic, Reproducible Inference in Exact Integer Arithmetic, and Verifiable Distributed Inference as Its Consequence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13730)
*Sayed Amir Karim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: tritify 是一个开源运行时，将真实模型（解码器语言模型、MoE、SAM 2.1）的前向推理路径全部改为精确整数运算，浮点仅用于离线量化与验证参考，从而实现跨硬件位级可复现、决策保真与位置无关的分布式推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 优化后的浮点推理在不同硬件、编译器、线程数下不可复现，同一模型会产生不同的比特结果，导致结果无法验证、难以在分布式/异构环境中建立信任。作者希望通过把运行时路径完全整数化，获得确定性的、可被独立校验的推理结果。

Method: 将浮点限制在离线量化与浮点参考验证中，运行时前向传播全程使用精确整数算术；以输出 SHA-256 作为复现凭证。（1）跨指令集架构与 CPU/GPU 验证输出位级一致；（2）在 OLMoE-1B-7B 与 106B 参数 MoE 上逐层（16 层）比对整数路径与浮点参考的决策一致性，并测量数值例外；（3）将模型计算切分到不同指令集、CPU/GPU 及联网机器上，验证输出比特不受影响。

Result: （1）整数路径逐次运行、跨 CPU ISA、跨 CPU/GPU 均输出位级相同结果，并有 SHA-256 复现；（2）在测试模型与输入上整数路径复现浮点参考的决策，逐层保真度在 16 层内无"保真度悬崖"；（3）GLM-4.5-Air（106,852,251,264 参数、427 GB 整数编码）在三台均无法在内存中容纳其整数编码的机器上分布式生成答案，结果与单机参考位级一致。
