Conclusion: 实现了可验证的分布式推理：不可信服务器或设备集群可执行模型，而开放的标量 oracle 可重新推导出精确字节（对不诚实服务器的保证属未来工作）。作者明确不主张单设备加速、浮点中间值的位相等或机密性。

Abstract: Optimized floating-point inference is not reproducible across environments: the same model on different hardware, compilers or thread counts can produce different bits. We present tritify, an open-source runtime that executes the forward pass of real models -- decoder-only language models, Mixture-of-Experts decoders, and the Segment Anything 2.1 image/video stack -- with the entire run-time path in exact integer arithmetic; floating point is confined to offline quantization and the float reference used for verification. We make three separate claims and verify each. (1) Cross-hardware reproducibility: the integer path produces bit-identical output run to run, across CPU instruction-set architectures, and across CPU/GPU, carried by a reproducing SHA-256 of the output. (2) Decision fidelity: the integer path reproduces the float reference's decision on the models and inputs tested, with the numeric exceptions measured; per-layer fidelity through sixteen layers of OLMoE-1B-7B and of a 106-billion-parameter MoE shows no fidelity cliff. (3) Location independence: a model's computation can be split across instruction sets, CPU and GPU, and networked machines with zero effect on the output bits. GLM-4.5-Air (106,852,251,264 parameters, 427 GB of integer codes) generated its answer across three machines, none of which can hold its integer codes in RAM, bit-identically to a single-host reference. The consequence is verifiable distributed inference: an untrusted server or a fleet of devices runs the model while an open scalar oracle re-derives the exact bytes; a guarantee against a dishonest server is future work. We do not claim a per-device speed-up, bit-equality of floating-valued intermediates, or confidentiality. Code: https://github.com/pelloran-inc/tritify

</details>


### [167] [Plug-and-Play ADMM for Inverse Problems with Flow Matching Denoiser](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13742)
*Dibyanshu Kumar,Magda Gregorova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于ADMM的PnP框架以集成flow matching先验，通过辅助变量与对偶更新强化数据保真与先验的一致性，并引入轨迹校正机制，在降低计算成本的同时实现高质量重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PnP方法配合生成先验在逆问题中表现优异，flow matching进一步带来快速可扩展的重建能力。但PnP的核心难题是数据保真更新与学习到的生成先验之间的一致性难以维持——数据一致性步骤会使迭代点偏离flow轨迹。作者希望更原理化地解决这一不一致问题。

Method: 1) 构建基于ADMM的PnP框架，用辅助变量与对偶更新在测量模型与先验之间施加更强的一致性约束；2) 提出轨迹校正机制，通过确定性投影与随机投影的组合，把迭代点重新拉回与flow一致的位置。

Result: 摘要未给出具体数值指标，仅声明方法实现了高质量重建，且相比现有做法降低了计算开销。

Conclusion: 该方法为flow先验在PnP框架中的集成提供了更原理化的方式，兼顾重建质量与计算效率；后续可通过实验数据与基线对比进一步验证其优势。

Abstract: Plug-and-Play (PnP) methods with generative priors have achieved strong performance in inverse problems, with recent flow matching models enabling fast and scalable reconstruction. However, a key challenge in PnP methods is maintaining consistency between data fidelity updates and the learned generative prior, as data-consistency steps can move iterates away from the flow trajectory. We propose an ADMM-based PnP framework for flow matching priors that enforces stronger consistency between the measurement model and the prior through auxiliary variables and dual updates. In addition, we introduce a trajectory correction mechanism that restores consistency with the flow via a combination of deterministic and stochastic projections. Our method provides a more principled integration of flow priors within PnP frameworks, achieving high-quality reconstructions with reduced computational cost.

</details>


### [168] [When Covariance Is Not Enough: Kernel-Based Regularization for Single-Cell Counterfactuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13748)
*Korbinian Traeuble,Edward de Brouwer,Archit Verma,Doron Haviv,Kipper Fletez-Brant*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出用归一化希尔伯特-施密特独立性准则（nHSIC）替代线性交叉协方差惩罚，构建非线性概念瓶颈生成模型，以提升单细胞反事实编辑的个体忠实度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单细胞反事实编辑只有在"忠实于个体"而非仅"忠实于条件"时才有价值；现有概念瓶颈模型用线性交叉协方差惩罚解耦已知与未知因子，但该一阶准则在存在非线性交互时无法完全分离信息。

Method: 先以含受控编辑与已知非线性协变量效应的仿真实验证明线性交叉因子惩罚的不足；再在概念瓶颈生成模型中引入非线性核判据——归一化希尔伯特-施密特独立性准则（nHSIC）进行解耦；最后应用于复杂单细胞RNA-seq数据。

Result: 仿真数据上，加入nHSIC能够恢复真实的干预效应；在复杂scRNA-seq数据上，非线性概念瓶颈模型提升了在不同组织环境下生成细胞反事实表达谱的能力。

Conclusion: 非线性核独立性判据优于线性一阶惩罚，可更可靠地分离概念因子，从而支持更可信的个体级反事实编辑与in-silico筛选。

Abstract: Counterfactual editing of single cells, predicting how an observed cell would look had one factor been different, enables in-silico screening where experiments are infeasible, but is useful only if the edit is faithful to the individual and not merely to the condition. Concept bottleneck generative models make the effects of interventions interpretable by decomposing a cell's representation into known and unknown factors. Previous work uses a linear cross-covariance penalty to disentangle known and unknown dimensions. We demonstrate that this first-order criterion does not fully separate information when non-linear interactions are present. We first demonstrate the inadequacy of linear cross-factor penalization with simulation experiments with controlled edits and known non-linear covariate effects. We then demonstrate that adding a nonlinear kernel criterion, the normalized Hilbert-Schmidt Independence Criterion, is able to recover the true intervention effects on simulation data. Finally, we apply the non-linear concept bottleneck model to a complex single-cell RNA-seq dataset and find that it improves the ability to generate counterfactual expression profiles of cells under different tissue environments.

</details>


### [169] [Beyond Interaction Scores: Selecting Feature Interactions via Independent Replication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13765)
*Jincheng Gao,Shuo Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LENS 是一个与模型无关的特征交互选择框架：用同一学习算法在不相交数据上训练两个模型，仅通过预测访问模型，将交互强度排序与独立重复检验结合，并仅用一次多重检验校正确定保留的交互对，全过程只需两次模型拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特征交互对预测精度和可解释性至关重要，但高交互得分可能只是拟合噪声而非可复现模式。现有方法存在两类缺陷：排序类方法只给特征对打分，阈值由用户自行决定；检验类方法虽能给出阈值，却依赖重复重拟合模型或对联合特征分布的建模。因此需要一个既能自动确定截断、又不依赖反复拟合的、与模型无关的选择框架。

Method: LENS 在同一学习算法下于不相交数据上训练两个模型，仅通过预测接口访问模型。对每个特征对，从联合效应曲面中剔除主效应以隔离交互模式，按交互强度排序，再检验第二个模型是否在具有实际意义的强度水平上复现该交互。用一次多重检验校正统一决定保留哪些特征对，且所需模型拟合次数固定为两次，与特征数量无关。理论方面：证明仅依赖复制比例会保留两次拟合共同具有的任意弱模式，而加入强度下限可恢复选择一致性；并刻画了 Friedman's H² 中归一化何时会逆转交互排序。

Result: 在16个合成设计上，LENS 与领先的特定模型排序方法（如 TreeSHAP）相当，恢复了91%的实质性交互，而基于 knockoff 的 DIAMOND 仅为45%，且未选出虚假交互对；在1,225个候选对且无真实交互的情形下，虚假发现显著更少。在大规模法国汽车保险数据上，识别出精算文献记载的交互，并恢复了使用全部特征对所获偏差增益的90%。在两个信用数据集上，LENS 仅选择少量特征对，同时在预测性能上保持或略优于加性基线。

Conclusion: LENS 提供了模型无关、仅两次拟合、阈值自动确定的特征交互选择方案，兼具理论保证（强度下限确保选择一致性）与实证有效性（合成数据的召回率、抗假阳性能力，以及真实保险与信用数据上的可解释性结果）。

Abstract: Learning feature interactions is essential for accurate prediction and interpretable modeling, yet a high interaction score may reflect fitting noise rather than a reproducible pattern. Ranking methods score feature pairs but leave the cutoff to the user; testing methods set the cutoff but rely on repeated model refits or a model of the joint feature distribution. We present LENS, a model-agnostic framework for selecting feature interactions through independent replication. LENS trains two models with the same learning algorithm on disjoint data and accesses them only through predictions. For each feature pair, it removes main effects from the joint-effect surface to isolate an interaction pattern, ranks pairs by its strength, and tests whether the second model reproduces it at a meaningful strength. A single multiple-testing correction determines which pairs to retain, using only two model fits regardless of the number of features. We prove that a replication ratio alone can retain arbitrarily weak patterns shared by both fits, whereas a strength floor restores selection consistency, and we characterize when normalization in Friedman's $H^2$ reverses interaction rankings. On 16 synthetic designs, LENS matches leading model-specific ranking methods (e.g., TreeSHAP) and recovers 91% of material interactions, against 45% for the knockoff-based DIAMOND, without selecting false pairs; with 1,225 candidate pairs and no interactions, it makes substantially fewer false discoveries. On large-scale French motor insurance data, it identifies interactions documented in the actuarial literature and recovers 90% of the deviance gain obtained using all pairs. On two credit datasets, LENS selects few pairs while maintaining or slightly improving predictive performance over the additive baseline.

</details>


### [170] [Omni-JEV: Text and Dual-Stream Speech Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13777)
*Shengzhou Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出全双工对话中的"交互动作决策"任务，并构建Omni-JEV模型：将文本或按角色索引的用户/助手参考语音映射为五类交互动作的概率分布，音频测试准确率84.08%、文本96.55%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户话语本身无法唯一决定全双工助手的下一步动作，其后果依赖于助手的响应状态与交互历史；语音活动、话语完成度、打断意图等信号虽提供相关证据，但要转化为响应决策仍需显式的动作策略。

Method: 在采集片段的端点处形式化该任务；构建Omni-JEV，基于对齐的语音-语音理解主干，分别编码声学角色，并与文本输入共享候选读出层；采用双语弱监督进行交叉熵微调，再用面向概率敏感决策的RLCD强化学习（对比Gaussian-logit与categorical Choice RLCD两种形式，相同监督初始化与训练曝光）。

Result: 两种RLCD变体在分类质量与概率质量之间呈现不同权衡；Gaussian Omni-JEV在测试集上达到音频84.08%、文本96.55%的准确率。

Conclusion: 统一的动作接口可支撑全双工语音助手在"应响应、保持当前语音、打断、恢复被打断的响应"之间的决策，为实现状态感知的全双工交互策略提供了可行范式。

Abstract: A user utterance does not uniquely determine the next action in full-duplex dialogue: its consequences depend on the assistant's response state and interaction history. Speech activity, utterance completion, and interruption intent provide related evidence, whose implications for a response require an explicit action policy. We formulate this task at collected-segment endpoints and introduce Omni-JEV, which maps text or role-indexed user and assistant-reference speech to probabilities over five interaction actions. An aligned speech-to-speech understanding backbone encodes the acoustic roles separately and shares its candidate readout with text inputs. Bilingual weak supervision supports cross-entropy fine-tuning followed by reinforcement learning for probability-sensitive decisions (RLCD). We compare Gaussian-logit and categorical Choice RLCD from the same supervised initialization under matched training exposure, observing different trade-offs between classification and probability quality. Gaussian Omni-JEV achieves test accuracies of 84.08% for audio and 96.55% for text. This common action interface supports decisions about when a full-duplex voice assistant should respond, preserve ongoing speech, interrupt, or resume an interrupted response.

</details>


### [171] [AnchorMTS: Multi-View Signal Representation and Forecast-Anchored Fusion for Long-Term Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13794)
*Gao Wu,Tingting Xu,Changning Wu,Tengfei Long,Yong Liu,Jiabin Jin,Kexin Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 AnchorMTS，从同一观测窗口派生自对齐的视觉与文本视图，仅用于精化一个完整的数值预测：可训练证据路径给出候选预测，无参数输出投影将其相对数值锚点的位移按变量严格限幅。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期时间序列预测器通常只优化单一数值表示，忽略了同一段历史中蕴含的互补几何、周期/递归与语义结构信息。

Method: 构建视觉与文本的自对齐多视图表示作为辅助证据路径；保留完整数值预测主干产生锚点，由可训练证据路径产生候选预测，再经无参数输出投影对每变量位移施加精确上界约束，使辅助信息只能受控地修正数值预测。

Result: 在 6 个基准、4 个预测时域上，宏观 MSE/MAE 达 0.336/0.367，为 7 个监督基线中最优；在 24 个 MSE 设置中对 cellwise 基线包络胜出 18 个；移除辅助路径后宏观 MSE 升至 0.340；投影使 95.8% 的候选修正保持不变且零越界。

Conclusion: 互补的多视图证据能提升预测性能，同时其最终影响被显式控制；辅助路径确有增益，且无参数投影在几乎不损失修正效果的前提下提供严格可靠性保证。

Abstract: Long-term time-series forecasters typically optimize a single numerical representation, even though the same history exposes complementary geometry, recurrence, and semantic structure. We propose AnchorMTS, which derives self-aligned visual and textual views from each observed window and uses them only to refine a complete numerical forecast. A trainable evidence path produces a candidate forecast, while a parameter-free output projection enforces an exact per-variable bound on its displacement from the numerical anchor. Across six benchmarks and four horizons, AnchorMTS achieves the best macro MSE/MAE (0.336/0.367) among seven supervised baselines and wins 18 of 24 MSE settings against the cellwise baseline envelope; removing the auxiliary path raises macro MSE to 0.340. The projection leaves 95.8% of candidate refinements unchanged while yielding zero bound violations, showing that complementary multi-view evidence can improve forecasting while its final influence remains explicitly controlled.

</details>


### [172] [JAX-WAVE: A Differentiable Physics-Supervised Framework for Inverse Wave Design in Heterogeneous Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13826)
*Honghui Du,Binyao Guo,Yushu Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 JAX-WAVE：用共享神经网络把目标末端波场与已知波速模型直接映射为有界初始压力，通过可微 FDTD 求解器按物理设计目标训练，实现摊销式逆波设计，显著降低在线计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非均匀介质中设计指定波场需要补偿介质传播响应的初始压力分布，传统做法是对每个目标与环境重复做数值反演，计算代价高且不可复用，缺乏可迁移的在线低成本方案。

Method: 构建共享权重神经网络，输入期望终端场与波速图，输出受对称压力界约束的初始压力；训练端嵌入可微有限差分时域（FDTD）求解器，直接用物理设计目标（无需最优控制标签）。利用线性传播与对称压力界的性质推出正则最优点满足精确符号反转关系，并以共享权重投影强制执行该关系、保持可行性并满足配对符号目标下的精确恒等式。同时刻划离散传播算子及其加权伴随，以连接学习、数值反演与最优性评估。

Result: 在独立确认集上，三个训练模型对未见材料的误差为：几何目标 25.24%，可达目标 4.77%。同容量对比显示符号参数化带来更好的重建。在 5 ms 中位在线预算内，学习映射相比加速数值反演在异质基准上平均误差降低 45%；更长的数值求解仍可获得更高精度。

Conclusion: 结果建立了一种可复用的逆设计流程，并表明其精度与计算价值取决于目标结构与查询负载，为该类问题在精度-成本权衡下的应用提供依据。

Abstract: Designing a prescribed wave field in a heterogeneous medium requires an initial pressure distribution that compensates for the medium's propagation response. Repeated numerical inversion solves this task afresh for each target and environment. We introduce JAX-WAVE for amortized inverse wave design: a shared neural network maps desired terminal fields and known wave-speed maps directly to bounded initial pressures. Training through a differentiable finite-difference time-domain solver uses the physical design objective without requiring optimized control labels. Linear propagation and symmetric pressure bounds imply an exact sign-reversal relation for the regularized optimum. A shared-weight projection enforces this relation, preserves feasibility, and satisfies an exact identity for the paired-sign objective. We characterize the discrete propagator and its weighted adjoint to connect learning, numerical inversion, and optimality assessment. On an independent confirmation set, three trained models achieve unseen-material errors of 25.24% for geometric targets and 4.77% for reachable targets. Matched-capacity comparisons show improved reconstruction with the sign parameterization. Within a 5 ms median online budget, the learned map reduces mean error by 45% relative to accelerated numerical inversion on the heterogeneous benchmark; longer numerical solves recover greater accuracy. The results establish a reusable inverse-design procedure and show how its accuracy and computational value depend on target structure and query workload.

</details>


### [173] [Where to Approximate in Neurosymbolic Inference?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13862)
*Samy Badreddine,Emile van Krieken,Luciano Serafini,Antonio Vergari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将既有概率神经符号方法统一为三步自底向上的近似编译流水线，借鉴张量网络，用张量列（TT）分解实例化其中两步，并推导出精确相乘近似因子、再经 SVD 或插值重压缩的新流水线，在六个任务上得到精度高数个数量级的 WMC 估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率神经符号方法依赖加权模型计数（WMC）将神经预测器与符号约束结合，但精确 WMC 是 #P-hard 问题、可扩展性差，现有方法普遍采用近似。作者希望厘清近似预算应如何在这类编译流程中分配，从而提升近似 WMC 的精度并改善下游学习。

Method: 把现有近似方法统一为三步自底向上的近似编译流水线；受张量网络启发，用张量列分解实例化其中两步，推导出"精确相乘近似因子 + 对乘积做 SVD 或插值重压缩"的新型流水线；在数独、算术推理和场景理解共六个任务、多种递增问题规模上进行评测。

Result: 新流水线给出的 WMC 估计比先前方法精确数个数量级；在依赖符号因子间紧密交互的推理任务上，下游学习效果显著更强，而较粗糙的近似会忽略这些依赖关系。

Conclusion: 该工作为高难度概率推理任务的有效近似编译开辟了新的设计空间，表明在编译流水线中合理分配近似预算并结合张量列分解与重压缩是提升近似 WMC 与下游性能的有效途径。

Abstract: Probabilistic neurosymbolic methods rely on weighted model counting (WMC) to combine neural predictors with symbolic constraints. Exact WMC is #P-hard and typically scales poorly, so many methods resort to approximations. By unifying existing approaches under a three-step bottom-up approximate compilation pipeline, we study where approximation budget is best allocated. Inspired by tensor networks, we instantiate two steps with tensor train decompositions and derive new pipelines that exactly multiply approximated factors and recompress their products via SVD- or interpolation-based schemes. Across six tasks in Sudoku, arithmetic reasoning, and scene understanding, and across increasing problem sizes, our pipelines produce WMC estimates orders-of-magnitude more accurate than prior methods. This translates into stronger downstream learning on tasks where reasoning relies on tight interactions between symbolic factors, where cruder approximations ignore these dependencies. Our pipelines open novel design space for effective approximate compilation of challenging probabilistic reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [174] [Mechanistic Interpretability of Deep Neural Networks: Methods, Frameworks, and Perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13868)
*Lorenzo Cirillo,Irene Amerini,Andrea Cavallaro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇综述，概述了面向深度神经网络因果推断的机制可解释性（mechanistic interpretability）研究进展，聚焦于识别其激活对特定行为具有因果责任的内部组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络广泛应用于图像、音频、语言等信号领域的基础任务，但其内部机制难以解释：层间激活是结构化信号，且单个神经元常表征多个概念，形成大量相互依赖的关系。因此需要揭示其内部机制。

Method: 综述性方法：以机制可解释性为框架，梳理通过定位对特定行为具有因果责任的组件（而非仅相关性）来分析网络内部因果推断的相关工作。

Result: 该摘要为概述性文章，未报告具体实验结果；其结果是整理并归纳了机制可解释性在深度网络因果分析方面的研究脉络与进展。

Conclusion: 机制可解释性为理解与检验深度神经网络的内部因果机制提供了系统视角，是连接模型可解释性与因果推断的重要研究方向。

Abstract: Deep neural networks underpin fundamental tasks in a wide range of signal domains, such as image, audio, and language processing. However, their internal mechanisms are notably difficult to interpret. Internal activations are structured signals transformed across layers that may encode a multitude of interdependent relationships, as individual neurons frequently represent multiple underlying concepts. Mechanistic interpretability aims to uncover the internal mechanisms of neural networks by identifying components whose activations are causally responsible for specific behaviors. In such a context, this article provides an overview of advances in mechanistic interpretability for the analysis of causal inference in deep neural networks.

</details>


### [175] [Are Causal Neuron Circuits Unique and Transferable?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13885)
*Ömer Tekin Yavuz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文质疑"神经元组因果干预"类可解释性演示的必要性叙事，通过穷举子集干预测量组内必要程度：全组往往非必要，少数神经元即可翻转答案，且存在多个互不相同的充分子集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性演示（如Qwen3-32B中移除18个神经元使"Scar"变为"Simba"）只证明"该组有影响"，却未回答三个关键问题：组内有多少是必要的、是否存在其他等效组、效应能否泛化到其他输入。

Method: 对每个选定的12个MLP神经元组，穷举全部4,096个子集做零消融/激活替换。任务一：Qwen3-0.6B上的二选一首都任务，比较零消融、随机神经元、跨问题激活替换。任务二：两跳关系链（Qwen3-1.7B、Llama-3.2-1B、Gemma-3-1B），用第一跳问题的激活补丁到12个神经元，观察答案是否转向桥接实体。并检验重述句与其他链的迁移性。

Result: 首任务中零消融12个神经元使答案边际下降27%，随机神经元几乎无影响；但用其他问题的激活替换几乎无影响，激活跨问题近乎恒定，未发现问题特异内容。两跳链中补丁使贪心答案以桥接实体开头：Qwen3-1.7B 9/11、Llama-3.2-1B 9/11、Gemma-3-1B 8/10，随机神经元从不出现。全组很少必要：少数神经元常已足够，许多链存在多个互不相交的独立充分子集；探索性分析显示这多发生在模型置信度较低时。效应可迁移到同一链的重述，但在其他链上显著减弱。</result>
<｜｜DSML｜｜ parameter name="conclusion" string="true">组级干预证据不足以推断必要性或回路结构。神经元级因果干预应报告子集必要性分布与多解性，而非单次全组演示；本文不恢复完整回路连接。

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Interpretability demonstrations often show that intervening on a group of neurons changes a model's answer. A recent example removes an 18-neuron group in Qwen3-32B and the model answers "Simba" instead of "Scar" to a two-hop Lion King riddle. Such a demonstration establishes that the group matters, but not how much of it is necessary, whether other groups could do the same, or whether the effect carries over to other inputs. We ask: when intervening on a group of neurons changes a model's answer, how much of that group is actually necessary? For every selected group we measure all 4,096 subsets of 12 MLP neurons. On a two-alternative capital task (Qwen3-0.6B), zero-ablating 12 selected neurons lowers the answer margin by 27% while random neurons barely change it, yet replacing the same neurons with their values from other questions also barely changes it: their activations are nearly constant across questions, and we find no evidence that they carry question-specific content. On two-hop relation chains, patching 12 neurons with activations from the first-hop question shifts the answer to the bridge entity (from Scar to Simba): the greedy answer starts with the bridge in 9/11 chains for Qwen3-1.7B, 9/11 for Llama-3.2-1B and 8/10 for Gemma-3-1B; random neurons never do. The full group is rarely necessary: a few neurons often suffice, and in many chains disjoint subsets each flip the answer on their own, in an exploratory analysis most often when the model is less confident. Group effects transfer to rephrasings of the same chain but are much weaker on other tested chains. We study neuron-level causal interventions and alternative subsets; we do not recover the full connectivity of circuits.

</details>


### [176] [Conformal Risk Control for QoT-Aware Lightpath Feasibility Under Technology-Driven Domain Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13893)
*Kiarash Rezaei,Omran Ayoub,Paolo Monti,Carlos Natalino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种事后保形风险控制（CRC）框架，对预训练AI/ML回归器不作改动，仅将其点预测映射为具有有限样本误覆盖率保证的不确定性区间，并通过策略规则转为运维决策；在QoT感知光路可行性评估中验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光网络中基于AI/ML的回归模型仅输出点预测，无法提供统计上可控的可靠性信息；同时光纤、放大器与交换设备升级会引发域偏移，使原有预测与量化基线失效，运营商需要可解释、可调的风险控制手段。

Method: 1）事后CRC：保持预训练回归器固定，构建不确定性带宽并给出有限样本区间误覆盖率保证，决策错误率则通过实验评估；2）实例化于QoT感知光路可行性判定，回归器在域偏移下预测OSNR；3）用特征偏移贡献分数识别技术演进中与QoT关系变化最大的光路特征；4）设计两种策略规则将带宽转为可行性决策（保守拒绝 vs 激进接受可调）；5）在CONUS拓扑上的开放SDM QoT数据集上，跨17种域偏移、10种校准-测试划分共170次试验，与source-only分位数回归（QR）基线对比。

Result: 目标覆盖率90%时，CRC不确定性带在全部170次试验中均满足目标，而仅源域分位数回归基线无一次满足；严重偏移下，效率导向（EO）策略将零样本可行性决策准确率由约85%提升至99%以上，接近迁移学习（TL）策略且无需重训练。

Conclusion: 该框架为基于回归的光网络决策支持提供有限样本的区间误覆盖控制，并具备超出QoT感知光路可行性评估的推广潜力。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence and machine learning (AI/ML)-based regression models support operational decision-making in optical networks but typically expose only point predictions, which carry no statistically controlled information about their reliability. This paper proposes a post-hoc conformal risk control (CRC) framework that keeps a pretrained AI/ML regressor fixed, provides a finite-sample guarantee on uncertainty band miscoverage, and maps the band to operational decisions through policy rules. The guarantee applies to interval miscoverage, while decision-error rates are evaluated empirically. We instantiate the framework for quality of transmission (QoT)-aware lightpath (LP) feasibility assessment, in which a regressor predicts optical signal-to-noise ratio (OSNR) under domain shifts caused by fiber, amplifier, and switching upgrades. Feature shift contribution scores identify the LP features whose relation to QoT changes the most across a technology transition, and two policies translate the bands into feasibility decisions, allowing operators to tune the trade-off between conservatively rejecting or aggressively accepting LP realizations. Experiments on open space-division multiplexing (SDM) QoT datasets over the CONUS topology show that, for a target coverage of 90%, the uncertainty bands meet the target in all 170 trials spanning 17 domain shifts and ten calibration-test partitions, whereas a source-only quantile regression (QR) baseline meets it in none. Under severe shifts, the efficiency-oriented (EO) policy empirically improves zero-shot (ZS) feasibility-decision accuracy from about 85% to above 99%, approaching a transfer learning (TL) policy while avoiding retraining. The framework provides finite-sample control of uncertainty band miscoverage for regression-based optical network decision support, with potential applications beyond QoT-aware LP feasibility assessment.

</details>


### [177] [Target Selection with a Disappearing-Stimulus Mixed-Reality P300 Interface: Feasibility and Object-Distinguishable Versus Uniform Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13906)
*Runqi Pan,Takuto Niinuma,Yunshan Huang,Masato Sugino,Kenta Shimba,Kiyoshi Kotani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在头戴式MR中验证"线索消失"型P300目标选择可行性：48个留出试次中18次判别正确（随机期望1.9，p<1e-12）；但为每个线索赋予稳定的颜色-形状身份相比统一球体并无可检测增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MR P300界面可直接在用户真实环境中标注可选目标（如轮椅目的地），但当闪烁间隙线索消失时，用户必须记住不可见的意图目标。作者希望检验这类"消失刺激"范式是否仍支持目标选择，并检验给每个线索赋予稳定的颜色—形状身份（物体线索）能否优于视觉上同质的球体线索。

Method: 6名健康被试在头戴式MR显示器上完成5×5行列闪烁任务，两种线索条件各4个试次；采集Fz、Cz、Pz三导EEG。采用收缩线性判别分析（shrinkage LDA），并做嵌套验证：完整试次在类别平衡、超参数选择与拟合过程中均被留出，以保证严格的试次级评估。比较两种线索条件下的解码正确率与留出AUC。

Result: 两种条件均诱发顶区目标相关正波。留出试次中解码正确18/48，而随机期望为1.9（二项检验p<1e-12），且3名被试单独达到显著。线索格式无可检测差异：完整目标正确率两种条件均为9/24（配对差异0.0个百分点，95% CI [-37, 37]）；留出AUC球体线索0.609、物体线索0.585。

Conclusion: 消失刺激的MR P300目标选择在严格试次级评估下是可行的；但物体身份线索带来的收益即便存在也过小，无法在本规模试点中分辨，需更大样本或更强线索设计进一步验证。

Abstract: Mixed-reality (MR) P300 interfaces can register selectable destinations, such as wheelchair goals, directly in the user's surroundings. When the cues disappear between flashes, however, the user must retain an intended target that is no longer visible. We tested whether such an interface supports target selection and whether giving each cue a stable colour-shape identity improves it compared with visually uniform spheres. Six healthy adults completed four trials in each cue condition of a 5x5 row/column task on a head-mounted MR display, with EEG from Fz, Cz, and Pz. Shrinkage linear discriminant analysis was evaluated with nested validation in which complete trials were held out from class balancing, hyperparameter selection, and fitting. Both conditions evoked a parietal target-related positivity. The decoded cell was correct in 18 of 48 held-out trials, against 1.9 expected by chance (binomial p < 1e-12), and in three participants individually. Cue format made no detectable difference: complete-target accuracy was 9/24 in both conditions (paired difference 0.0 percentage points, 95% CI [-37, 37]), and held-out AUC was 0.609 for spheres and 0.585 for object cues. Disappearing-stimulus MR P300 selection is therefore feasible under strict trial-level evaluation, but the benefit of object identity, if any, is too small to resolve in a pilot of this size.

</details>


### [178] [Learning from First Discrepancies with Joint Reset and Pilot Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13913)
*Michal Čertík*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究在“首次错误决策即终止”的短程交互记录下学习二分类假设类所需的统计资源（运行次数与不同引导假设数），给出上界 learner 与匹配的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画一种受限反馈模型下的学习成本：每次运行只检验一个预先选定的假设（pilot），仅在无差异时揭示记录终止信息，且信息在首个错误决策处截断。目标是量化所需运行数与不同 pilot 数。

Method: 设定 VC 维 ≤ d 的固定二分类类、至多 D 个查询的记录、与 pilot 无关的未知分布，以及预先固定的有界损失。证明工具结合：由停止显示可计算的 gain、指数权重、稀疏深度鲁棒图（depth-robust graph）、调和平均界；learner 通过枚举类在显示查询上的标记模式（可指数时间）实现。

Result: 若类中成员差异概率下确界 ≤ f、风险下确界 ≤ r，则 learner 在每条历史上使用 Õ((d+1)[D(f+ε)+r]/ε²) 次运行与 Õ(D(1+f/ε)) 个不同 pilot，输出类中成员及宽度 ≤ ε 的区间，以 ≥ 1−δ 概率同时覆盖输出风险与最优风险；并处理未知上限与期望成本。有限类的下界表明这两个阶在正维度、小精度、固定置信度下最坏情形必要（可能在不同实例上）。

Conclusion: 该模型下运行数与 pilot 数的阶是最优的（至多对数因子）：上界与下界匹配；结论在标准可测性条件下仅涉及统计资源，计算复杂度未加约束。

Abstract: A run that stops at its first wrong decision shows the queries and their correct labels up to and including that decision, and nothing after it. We study how many such runs are needed for learning. Each run tests a hypothesis, the pilot, chosen beforehand from a fixed binary class of VC dimension at most $d$, on a fresh record of at most $D$ queries drawn from one unknown distribution that does not depend on the pilot. The terminal information of a record is shown only if the pilot makes no discrepancy, and a bounded loss, fixed in advance, is attached to the position where a hypothesis stops. If the discrepancy probabilities of members of the class have infimum at most $f$ and their risks have infimum at most $r$, one learner uses $\widetilde O((d+1)[D(f+\varepsilon)+r]/\varepsilon^2)$ runs and $\widetilde O(D(1+f/\varepsilon))$ distinct pilots on every history. It returns a member of the class and an interval of width at most $\varepsilon$ containing, with probability at least $1-δ$, both the output's risk and the optimal risk. We also treat unknown ceilings and expected costs. Lower bounds for finite classes show that each order is necessary in the worst case, possibly on different instances, at positive dimension, small accuracy and fixed confidence. The learner enumerates label patterns of the class on displayed queries, possibly in exponential time; the results, under standard measurability conditions, concern statistical resources only. The proof combines a gain computable from stopped displays, exponential weights, a sparse depth-robust graph and a harmonic-mean bound.

</details>


### [179] [Dual-Core Tensor Wheel Decomposition with Structure-Cached Acceleration for Tensor Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13938)
*Wenchao Xie,Qingsong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出双核张量轮分解（DCTW）及结构缓存加速策略，将中心核分解为两个低阶核并由共享潜键连接，避免大规模矩阵显式构造，从而提升视频补全与鲁棒去噪的效率和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始张量轮分解依赖外环因子与高阶中心核的交互来刻画高维数据相关性，但更新组件时需显式构造和操作大规模矩阵，中心核更新的计算与内存开销尤其高，限制了效率。

Method: 提出DCTW，将中心核因式分解为两个较低阶的核，通过共享潜键（latent bond）连接，每个核耦合不同的外环因子组；同时设计结构缓存加速策略，避免显式大规模矩阵乘法，并缓存张量网络收缩过程中产生的中间张量以跨块更新复用。

Result: 复杂度分析与实验表明，基于结构缓存的策略相较显式与隐式更新方案加速优化，且在秩增长时峰值内存增幅受限；在彩色视频补全与鲁棒去噪任务上，DCTW在重建质量、计算效率和可扩展性上达到或优于代表性张量网络方法。

Conclusion: DCTW通过中心核的双核分解与结构缓存机制，在控制内存增长的同时提升优化效率，是一种兼顾精度、效率与可扩展性的高维数据张量分解方案。

Abstract: Tensor wheel decomposition captures complex correlations in high-dimensional data through interactions between outer-ring factors and a high-order central core. However, updating these components requires the explicit construction and manipulation of large-scale matrices, which limits computational efficiency and incurs particularly high computational and memory costs for the central-core update. To address these limitations, we propose dual-core tensor wheel decomposition (DCTW), which factorizes the central core into two lower-order cores linked by a shared latent bond. Each core is coupled to a different group of outer-ring factors. We further propose a structure-cached acceleration strategy that avoids explicit large-scale matrix multi- plications and caches intermediate tensors generated during tensor network contractions for reuse across block updates. This reuse reduces redundant computation and avoids the explicit construction of large matrices. Complexity analysis and experiments show that the strategy based on structural caching accelerates optimization compared with explicit and implicit up- date schemes while limiting the increase in peak memory as the ranks grow. Experiments on color video completion and robust denoising show that DCTW matches or outperforms representative tensor network methods in reconstruction quality, computational efficiency, and scalability.

</details>


### [180] [PARDIG: Global Precipitation Estimates Learned from Sparse Satellite Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13941)
*Guy Shalev,Stephan Rasp,Boris Babenko,Andrew El-Kadi,Mohammed Alewi Hassen,Samier Merchant,Fred Zyda,Dominic Masters,Ferran Alet,Salva Rühling Cachay,Ilan Price,Akib Uddin,Nofar Peled Levi,Ben Gaiarin,Aaron Bell,Peter Battaglia,Alvaro Sanchez-Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PARDIG：基于球面图-网格Transformer的AI框架，用稠密多模态输入（大气状态分析+多光谱静止卫星影像）预测稀疏星载雷达降水，生成0.1°、15分钟全球近实时降水场（2016至今，历史版本回溯至1999），在三个独立雷达网和3.6万余地面站评估中全面优于IMERG V07、MSWEP V3、ERA5与HRES，极端强度提升最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球尺度降水估计对地球系统科学与灾害响应至关重要，但观测覆盖与测量精度受限；现有产品在技能、时延、地理/时间覆盖、时空分辨率等特性之间存在权衡，难以兼得。

Method: 提出PARDIG框架，采用球面图-网格（spherical graph-mesh）Transformer架构；训练输入为稠密多模态数据（大气状态分析、多光谱地球静止卫星影像），监督目标为稀疏高精度星载雷达降水测量；推理时在全球稠密网格上以0.1°、15分钟分辨率应用训练好的模型，输出完整近实时降水场（2016年至今），并提供回溯至1999年的历史版本。

Result: 在美国、欧洲、日本三个独立雷达网及全球逾36,000个地面站评估中，PARDIG在各降水阈值与区域上一致优于IMERG V07（Final与Early）、MSWEP V3、ERA5和HRES，极端强度处改进最大；极端降水事件案例研究表明其能成功刻画事件的空间结构与时间演变；此外，全球天气模型WeatherNext 3以PARDIG为训练目标后，降水预报技巧提升。

Conclusion: PARDIG以单一AI框架同时兼顾技能、时延、覆盖与时空分辨率，实现近实时全球高分辨率降水估计，并可反哺数值天气预报模型训练；历史与近实时数据集均以CC BY 4.0许可公开开放。

Abstract: Estimating precipitation at the global scale is vital for Earth system science and disaster response, yet observational coverage and measurement precision remain constrained. Existing solutions provide options that trade off desirable traits such as skill, latency, geographical coverage, temporal coverage, and spatial and temporal resolution. We present PARDIG, an artificial intelligence framework based on a spherical graph-mesh transformer architecture that produces near-real-time global precipitation estimates. PARDIG is trained on dense multi-modal inputs, including atmospheric state analyses and multi-spectral geostationary satellite imagery, to predict sparse, high-precision precipitation measurements from spaceborne radar. At inference, the trained model is applied densely across the globe at $0.1^\circ$ spatial and 15-minute temporal resolution, producing complete near-real-time precipitation fields from 2016 to the present, alongside a historical variant extending the record back to 1999. Evaluation against three independent radar networks (over the US, Europe, and Japan) and against over 36,000 surface stations worldwide shows that PARDIG consistently outperforms IMERG V07 (Final and Early), MSWEP V3, ERA5, and HRES across precipitation thresholds and regions, with the largest improvements at extreme intensities. Case studies of extreme precipitation events demonstrate that PARDIG successfully captures their spatial structure and timing. The improvements also extend to forecasting: WeatherNext 3, a global weather model, produces more skillful precipitation forecasts when trained to predict PARDIG. Both the historical and near-real-time PARDIG datasets are openly available under a CC BY 4.0 license.

</details>


### [181] [Chem World: A Large-Scale Benchmark and Physics-Aware Framework for Trustworthy Molecular Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13945)
*Tianyou Bai,Huan Wang,Mingchen Gao,Tongrui Yang,Fangyue Lin,Pinze Ren,Zhenlin Zhao,Siming Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出大规模基准 Chem World（17个公开数据集、80万+样本、10条性质轨道，涵盖单分子与多组分体系），并提出物理感知组合建模方法 PACM，实验显示分子语言模型表征强且可迁移，PACM 在多种化学体系上稳定提升预测性能，并落地为药物性质预测 Agent。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子性质预测基准在数据集、性质类型与评测协议上彼此割裂，缺乏统一测试平台，难以系统评估模型的泛化能力；同时多组分/配方体系的组合建模与物理约束尚未被统一纳入评测。

Method: 构建 Chem World 基准：整合17个公开数据集、10条性质轨道、80万+分子与化学体系实例，统一评测协议，覆盖单分子及多组分体系（含药物溶解度）。在此基础上提出 PACM（Physics-Aware Compositional Modeling），将分子表征与组分感知建模、领域特定物理约束相结合；并基于 PACM 构建药物性质预测 Agent。

Result: 大量实验表明：分子语言模型可提供强且可迁移的表征；PACM 在多样化学体系上均能持续提升预测性能；统一基准可有效区分不同表征与组合建模策略。

Conclusion: Chem World 与 PACM 驱动的预测 Agent 为可泛化的化学建模提供统一基础设施与方法支撑，可作为 AI 辅助生物医学研究及药物制剂开发的基座。

Abstract: Molecular property prediction is fundamental to computational chemistry and drug formulation research, yet existing benchmarks remain fragmented across datasets, properties, and evaluation protocols, limiting systematic evaluation of model generalization. We introduce Chem World, a large-scale benchmark integrating 17 public datasets with over 800,000 molecular and chemical-system instances across 10 property tracks, covering single-molecule and multi-component systems, including drug solubility. Chem World provides a unified testbed for evaluating molecular representations and compositional modeling across diverse chemical properties. Building on the benchmark, we propose PACM (Physics-Aware Compositional Modeling), which combines molecular representations with composition-aware modeling and domain-specific physical constraints. Extensive experiments show that molecular language models provide strong and transferable representations, while PACM consistently improves prediction across diverse chemical systems. Chem World and our PACM-powered drug-property prediction Agent support generalizable chemical modeling and offer a foundation for AI-assisted biomedical research and drug formulation development.

</details>


### [182] [Geometry-Dependent Approximation and Stability Properties of Shallow ReLU$^k$ Networks on Bounded Euclidean Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13952)
*Yuqing Liu,Tong Mao,Jinchao Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文系统研究有界欧氏区域上隐藏参数几何（以填充距离h与对跖分离距离h̄刻画）对浅层ReLU^k网络的影响，涵盖逼近率、饱和型下界、Gram与刚度矩阵谱、Bernstein与Marcinkiewicz–Zygmund不等式、离散最小二乘恢复及逆逼近等结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 浅层ReLU^k网络的逼近能力与系数表示稳定性本质上受隐藏层参数（频率、方向等）分布的几何结构影响，但既有工作多关注网络规模，缺乏以参数几何为显式变量的统一定量刻画。作者因而希望以填充距离h和对跖分离距离h̄为度量，建立逼近精度、谱条件、离散恢复与正则性反演之间的系统联系。

Method: 采用光滑局部化技术与二进Fourier估计作为核心分析工具，对给定网络空间L_n^k证明逼近上界与饱和型下界；上界利用目标函数至临界阶(d+2k+1)/2的正则性，下界针对比该阶更光滑的固定目标渐近成立，且上界在内部子域Ω'上、下界在较大区域Ω上进行。进一步推导依赖于几何的Gram矩阵与刚度矩阵谱估计以量化条件数与系数稳定性，建立Bernstein与Marcinkiewicz–Zygmund不等式及范数离散化，并研究基于函数值的正则化最小二乘恢复，最后在系数或分离条件下由逼近率反推目标正则性。

Result: 得到形如inf_{g_n∈L_n^k}‖f−g_n‖_{L²(Ω')} ≲ h^r‖f‖_{H^r(Ω)}的逼近上界，以及inf_{g_n∈L_n^k}‖f−g_n‖_{L²(Ω)} ≳ h̄^{(d+2k+1)/2}‖f‖_{L²(Ω')}的饱和型下界；并获得依赖参数几何的谱估计、范数离散化与正则化恢复的定量保证，以及在系数/分离条件下由收敛率推断目标正则性的逆逼近结论。

Conclusion: 参数几何（填充距离与对跖分离距离）决定了浅层ReLU^k网络的逼近精度、系数表示稳定性、离散恢复可靠性以及收敛率中所蕴含的正则性信息，从而为网络设计中的参数采样与条件控制提供理论依据。

Abstract: On bounded Euclidean domains, we study how hidden-parameter geometry affects shallow ReLU$^k$ networks through results on approximation rates, saturation-type lower bounds, Gram and stiffness matrix spectra, Bernstein and Marcinkiewicz--Zygmund inequalities, discrete least-squares recovery, and inverse approximation.
  Our analysis uses smooth localization and dyadic Fourier estimates. We quantify parameter geometry by the fill distance $h$ and antipodal separation distance $\underline h$. For the prescribed network space $L_n^k$, representative approximation estimates take the form \begin{equation*} \begin{aligned} \inf_{g_n\in L_n^k}\|f-g_n\|_{\mathcal L^2(Ω')}&\lesssim h^r\|f\|_{\mathcal H^r(Ω)},\\ \inf_{g_n\in L_n^k}\|f-g_n\|_{\mathcal L^2(Ω)}&\gtrsim \underline h^{\frac{d+2k+1}{2}}\|f\|_{\mathcal L^2(Ω')}. \end{aligned} \end{equation*} The upper bound exploits target regularity up to the critical order $\frac{d+2k+1}{2}$, while the lower bound applies asymptotically to fixed targets smoother than this order. The upper estimate holds on an interior subdomain $Ω'$, whereas the lower estimate concerns the larger domain $Ω$.
  We also obtain geometry-dependent spectral estimates that quantify conditioning and coefficient stability. Further results establish norm discretization and regularized recovery from function values, and infer target regularity from approximation rates under coefficient or separation conditions. Altogether, these results describe how parameter geometry governs approximation accuracy, the stability of coefficient representations, discrete recovery, and the regularity information contained in convergence rates.

</details>


### [183] [RB-LDC: Redundancy-Balanced Latent Coding for Noise-Robust Diffusion Latents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13955)
*Hyunseok Jeong,Jaeho Jeon,Young-Sik Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出在编码器与扩散过程之间插入一个可逆线性层，按解码器对各方向的敏感度重新分配信噪比，实现敏感度加权LMMSE风险最小化，并在ImageNet 256×256上验证（RB-LDC）可显著降低受扰动潜变量的重建误差与FID。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型解码器对潜变量的扰动敏感，而白化后的前向扩散对所有方向施加相同SNR的噪声，忽略了各方向解码敏感度不均，造成潜空间编码能量分配低效。

Method: 研究编码器与扩散之间的可逆线性层，用以平移各方向的log-SNR；给出任意白化潜分布下LMMSE恢复风险的精确谱表达式；在能量预算与保证可逆的下界约束下，最小化解码敏感度加权的、沿扩散日程积分的LMMSE风险，得到唯一最优分配，该层与解码器期望回拉度量对齐并使各方向边际风险下降均衡，由确定性谱求解器计算；用Jacobian估计敏感度，实例化为RB-LDC。

Result: 大预算下分配服从关于敏感度的幂律，指数由低SNR尾部损失权重决定：cosine日程下噪声预测为1/2、数据预测为2/3，截断日程出现交叉。ImageNet 256×256、SD-VAE与VA-VAE上，RB-LDC在4项指标、5种编码配置、15个SNR与3个随机种子平均下均优于同预算各向同性编码；VA-VAE块局部编码下平均受扰FID降低17.1且保持干净重建；部分中间SNR处受扰FID反而变差；冻结扩散模型、采样期加噪的12种组合中FID点估计全部更低。

Conclusion: 按解码器敏感度重新分配潜空间各方向的SNR是一种有理论保证、可解析求解的有效编码原则；RB-LDC在重建与生成质量上带来一致增益，但并非在所有SNR区间都单调改进。

Abstract: Latent diffusion models decode approximately correct latents, making decoder sensitivity to perturbations relevant to image quality. After whitening, forward diffusion corrupts every direction at the same signal-to-noise ratio (SNR), despite unequal decoder sensitivities. We study an invertible linear layer between the encoder and diffusion process that shifts each direction's log-SNR. Its linear minimum mean-square-error (LMMSE) recovery risk has an exact spectral expression for any whitened latent distribution. Under an energy budget and a floor ensuring invertibility, minimizing decoder-sensitivity-weighted LMMSE risk integrated over the diffusion schedule yields a unique optimal allocation. The layer aligns with the decoder's expected pullback metric and equalizes marginal risk reduction across directions, computed by a deterministic spectral solver. At large budgets, the allocation follows a power law in sensitivity whose exponent is determined by the low-SNR tail of the loss weighting: one half for noise prediction and two thirds for data prediction under the cosine schedule, with a crossover on truncated schedules. We instantiate this principle as RB-LDC using Jacobian-based sensitivity estimates. On ImageNet at 256 x 256 resolution with SD-VAE and VA-VAE, trained RB-LDC improves reconstruction from noisy latents over budget-matched isotropic coding in all four metrics and five coding configurations, averaged over 15 SNRs and three pipeline seeds. It reduces average perturbed FID by 17.1 for VA-VAE block-local coding while preserving clean reconstruction. We also report intermediate SNRs where perturbed FID worsens. With a frozen diffusion model, RB-LDC yields lower FID point estimates in all 12 tested combinations of sampling progress and channel SNR under corruption during sampling.

</details>


### [184] [Universal Approximation Theorem for Metaplectic Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13991)
*Ahmed Abdeljawad,Malek Yacine Rezoug*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 证明了在非空有界Lipschitz区域上，metaplectic神经算子具有普适逼近性：以整空间metaplectic乘子（零延拓与限制）为非局部分支，配以光滑、全局Lipschitz、非多项式激活函数，可从C^s逼近到C^r、从H^s逼近到H^r的连续算子；单隐层、单非局部分支即足，标量符号原子与位置特征由变换和区域决定，与目标算子、紧集及精度无关。该结果涵盖Fourier与固定非退化分数阶Fourier乘子构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有神经算子（如Fourier神经算子）的普适逼近结论多依赖特定变换与核参数化，缺乏对更一般的辛/元胞（metaplectic）乘子族在一般区域上的统一逼近理论；本文旨在建立metaplectic神经算子在Lipschitz区域上对连续算子的普适逼近框架，并将其作为Fourier与分数阶Fourier构造的统一推广。

Method: 非局部分支取整空间metaplectic乘子，配合零延拓与限制，关联任意固定的自由辛矩阵；激活函数为光滑、全局Lipschitz且非多项式。证明思路为将平均神经算子（averaging neural operator）理论与精确的chirp共轭平均恒等式（chirp-conjugated averaging identity）结合，辅以导数估计，从而在C^s→C^r与H^s→H^r上获得紧一致逼近；Sobolev情形下要求紧输入集额外在L^∞中一致有界。

Result: 对任意非负整数s,r与有限输入/输出通道维数，可从C^s到C^r、从H^s到H^r分别在对应输出范数下紧一致逼近连续算子；单个隐算子层加单个非局部分支即足够；单个标量符号原子与有限个位置特征由变换和区域预先确定，与目标算子、紧集及误差无关；结论统一包含Fourier乘子与固定非退化分数阶Fourier乘子构造。

Conclusion: metaplectic神经算子构成一类在Lipschitz区域上具备普适逼近能力的算子族，其非局部分支由自由辛矩阵参数化，可覆盖并推广Fourier与分数阶Fourier神经算子；逼近所需的架构复杂度极低（单层单分支），且符号与位置特征与任务无关，为分数阶/辛类算子学习提供了理论保证。

Abstract: We prove universal approximation for metaplectic neural operators on nonempty bounded Lipschitz domains. The nonlocal branches are whole-space metaplectic multipliers with zero extension and restriction, associated with any fixed free symplectic matrix. Assuming a smooth, globally Lipschitz, nonpolynomial activation, we obtain compact-uniform approximation of continuous operators from $C^s$ to $C^r$ and from $H^s$ to $H^r$ in the respective output norms, for arbitrary nonnegative integers $s,r$ and finite input and output channel dimensions. In the Sobolev setting, compact input sets must additionally be uniformly bounded in $L^\infty$. One hidden operator layer with one nonlocal branch suffices. A single scalar symbol atom and finitely many positional features are prescribed by the transform and domain independently of the target operator, compact set and accuracy. The proof combines averaging neural operator theory with an exact chirp-conjugated averaging identity and derivative estimates. The result includes Fourier and fixed nondegenerate fractional Fourier multiplier constructions.

</details>


### [185] [Temporal Behavior Trees for Reinforcement Learning: Specifications as Rewards, Curricula, and Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13993)
*Vladimir Krsmanovic,Bernd Finkbeiner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TBTCL 以时序行为树（Temporal Behavior Trees, TBTs）作为规格引导强化学习的统一接口，用单一 TBT 同时完成任务定义、稠密奖励生成、课程构造与策略诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于形式化规格的强化学习把任务定义、奖励塑形、课程设计与策略诊断当作彼此独立的数学对象，导致额外的转换步骤，并丢弃了规格中已有的结构信息（子任务边界、顺序约束、定量度量）。

Method: 为规格引导 RL 改造 TBT 的语法与语义，得到一个比 LTL 等常见规格语言表达力更强且天然具备层次性的形式化体系；提出从 TBT 语义中自动提取课程 DAG、推导基于鲁棒性的稠密奖励的算法；利用 TBT 轨迹分割构建可理解的能力剖面（competence profiles），并自动将奖励调整至瓶颈处。

Result: 在离散与连续领域的六个规格上进行评测，表明 TBT 可同时充当规格、奖励、课程与诊断工具。

Conclusion: TBT 能够作为规格引导强化学习的统一形式化载体，避免多对象间的转换开销并保留规格结构，从而在任务定义、奖励设计、课程学习与策略解释上提供一体化支持。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from formal specifications often separates task definition, reward shaping, curriculum design, and policy diagnostics into different mathematical objects. This fragmentation requires additional conversion steps and discards structure already present in the specification, such as subtask boundaries, ordering constraints, and quantitative measures. We introduce TBTCL (Temporal Behavior Tree Centric Learning), a framework that uses Temporal Behavior Trees (TBTs) as a unified interface for specification-guided reinforcement learning. A single TBT specifies the task, induces dense robustness-based rewards, defines a curriculum, and supports better understanding of learned policies. By adapting TBT syntax and semantics for specification-guided reinforcement learning, we provide a formalism that is more expressive than popular specification languages such as LTL and inherently hierarchical. We present algorithms to automatically extract a curriculum DAG and derive dense, robustness-based rewards directly from TBT semantics. Furthermore, we utilize TBT trace segmentation to build understandable competence profiles and automatically adapt the reward toward bottlenecks. We evaluate TBTCL on six specifications across discrete and continuous domains and show that Temporal Behavior Trees can serve as a specification, a reward, a curriculum, and a diagnostic.

</details>


### [186] [BioXArena-Pro: a research-grade benchmark for autonomous biomedical machine learning agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13994)
*Loka Li,Duzhen Zhang,Xingbo Du,Leonard Song,Kun Zhang,Le Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 BioXArena-Pro 基准，包含来自八个生物医学领域的 64 个研究级建模任务，每个任务均基于已发表研究重建，并多以物种或队列等完整生物单元作为留出集；在 22 种智能体配置中最佳得分为 55.1/100，框架（scaffold）影响几乎与模型本身相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体被越来越多地用作生物医学模型的自主构建者，但已有基准规模小、接近饱和或存在信息泄漏风险，难以真实评估智能体的研究级建模能力。

Method: 构建 BioXArena-Pro：从已发表研究重建 64 个研究级建模任务，覆盖八个生物医学领域，留出完整生物单元（如物种、队列）以防泄漏；评估 22 种智能体配置；对每个任务重跑已发表方法作为对照；并做消融实验（禁用预训练模型、包括生物基础模型）。任务经多轮 AI 辅助审计与最终人工复核。

Result: 最佳配置得分 55.1/100；scaffold 的影响几乎与模型同等重要。在已发表配方可原样适用的任务上，最佳智能体与已发表方法持平；在重建任务上，已发表方法落后 8.2 分，说明智能体通过适配新数据获益。在最强智能体使用预训练模型的那些任务上，禁用预训练模型（含生物基础模型）会使其得分下降 9 分。

Conclusion: BioXArena-Pro 提供了规模更大、抗泄漏、研究级的生物医学建模智能体基准；结果显示当前智能体能力仍有限（最高约 55 分），scaffold 设计与预训练/生物基础模型的使用对性能有实质影响，且智能体在需数据适配的重建任务上优于原已发表方法。

Abstract: Coding agents are increasingly proposed as autonomous builders of biomedical models, but existing benchmarks are small, near saturation or open to leaked information. We present BioXArena-Pro, 64 research-level modelling tasks from eight biomedical domains, each rebuilt from a published study; most hold out whole biological units such as species or cohorts. Across 22 agent configurations, the best scores 55.1 of 100, and the scaffold matters almost as much as the model. We re-ran a published method for every task: where the published recipe applies unchanged, the best agent and the methods tie; on rebuilt tasks the methods trail by 8.2 points, suggesting that agents gain by adapting to new data. Pretrained models (including biological foundation models) matter where the strongest agent uses them: forbidding them decreases its score on those tasks by 9 points. Every task was audited in several AI-assisted rounds and a final human review.

</details>


### [187] [Coding Agent Memory Post-Training II: Unlocking the Memory Potential of Pre-trained File Operations for Long-Horizon Tasks via On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14009)
*Lirui Luo,Kelong Mao,Xinwei Yang,Rongqing Li,Luyu Chen,Kieran Wong,Yudong Guo,Xinrui Wang,Jiayin Zhu,Pu Yao,Gu Simiu,Sulong Xu,Cong Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出CAMG-II长程智能体强化学习环境套件与CAMP-II后训练方法，通过全异步在策略蒸馏让单一策略在五个环境中学习基于文件的记忆行为，收益达最强OPD基线的2.97倍，并可迁移至Terminal-Bench 2.1与SWE-Bench Pro V2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程任务的交互历史超出模型活跃上下文，智能体需自主决定写记什么、读回什么及何时操作。现有用OPD把记忆控制纳入策略的做法依赖模型预训练之外的记忆工具与压缩协议，导致教师对未见接口无法给出有效指导、学生采样到的有意义记忆动作过少，双向削弱蒸馏效果。

Method: 构建CAMG-II，覆盖Shop、Coding、AutoResearch、General、Visual五类长程智能体RL环境；每个环境在原生任务接口之外提供普通Bash访问和回合持久化工作区，使策略能在上下文替换间创建、修改、检索和复用文件作为记忆。提出CAMP-II，用冻结的、具备文件操作先验的教师模型，跨五个环境以全异步OPD联合训练单一策略，直接学习文件式记忆行为。

Result: 在CAMG-II上，CAMP-II取得的增益为最强OPD基线的2.97倍；所学记忆行为可泛化到Terminal-Bench 2.1和SWE-Bench Pro V2两个外部基准，成功率均提升。

Conclusion: 以Bash+持久化工作区作为无需额外协议的原生记忆接口，可缓解教师-学生双侧的蒸馏失配；全异步OPD跨环境联合训练所得的文件记忆策略具备跨基准的可迁移性。

Abstract: Language-model agents increasingly tackle long-horizon tasks whose interaction histories exceed the model's active context. They must therefore decide what to write down and what to read back, and when to do either. Recent work has begun to use on-policy distillation (OPD) to make memory control part of the policy, relying on memory tools and compaction protocols that lie outside either model's pre-training. However, this weakens the distillation on both sides: the teacher cannot give useful guidance on an interface it has never used, and the student samples too few meaningful memory actions for that guidance to take effect. To study this problem, we introduce Coding Agent Memory Gym II (CAMG-II), a suite of long-horizon agentic-RL environments spanning Shop, Coding, AutoResearch, General, and Visual. Alongside each environment's native task interface, CAMG-II provides ordinary Bash access and an episode-persistent workspace, enabling the policy to create, revise, search, and reuse files as memory across context replacements. We also introduce Coding Agent Memory Post-Training II (CAMP-II), which trains a single policy jointly across all five environments with fully asynchronous OPD to learn this file-based memory behavior directly from a frozen teacher with a prior for file operations. On CAMG-II, CAMP-II obtains 2.97 times the gain of the strongest OPD baseline. The memory behavior CAMP-II learns generalizes to two external benchmarks, Terminal-Bench 2.1 and SWE-Bench Pro V2, with improved success rates.

</details>


### [188] [Margin-Guided Token Credit for Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14015)
*Chi Liu,Xin Chen,Yuchuan Weng,Srinivasan Manoharan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MCPO 用 top-2 margin 度量每个 token 处的"犹豫程度"，把解的整体奖励按此加权分配给各 token，仅需训练器已有的前向计算，无需额外评估或批次统计；在 MiniCPM5-2B-Base 与 Qwen3-4B-Base 上均稳定优于 GRPO，宏平均 avg@32 提升 6.55 分（95% CI [4.1, 9.0]）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可验证奖励强化学习（GRPO）将最终答案的对错奖励等额分配给解中的每一个 token，忽略了不同 token 对最终正确性贡献的差异——模型在高不确定（犹豫）位置所做的决策往往更关键，理应获得更多信用。作者据此提出按"犹豫程度"重分配优势的动机。

Method: 提出 MCPO（Margin-Credit Policy Optimization）：(1) 用策略下一 token 分布的 top-2 margin M = p1 − p2 作为犹豫度量；(2) 用两个固定阈值把 M 映射为分数；(3) 将每个解的优势按其 token 分数进行重分配，同时约束加权后均值保持不变；(4) margin 直接取自训练器已执行的前向传播，无额外模型评估、无 batch 级统计，实现代价低。

Result: 实验设置：MiniCPM5-2B-Base + LoRA，训练 200 步，2,048 道 DAPO-Math 题，评测 6 个竞赛数学基准。结果：(1) 5 个数据种子全部优于原生 GRPO，宏平均 avg@32 平均提升 6.55 分（95% CI [4.1, 9.0]）；(2) Qwen3-4B-Base 复现实验 3 个种子全部提升（+4.75）；(3) 增益集中在 GRPO 部分成功的题目上，且随 pass@k 的 k 增大而增大，pass@32 处达 13.8 分；(4) MCPO 模型答案长度是基线的 2–3 倍，且写得更长的 run 得分更高。

Conclusion: 基于 token 级不确定性的信用分配是一个简单、低成本且稳健有效的改进，可跨模型规模与数据种子复现。局限在于：增益与答案变长两种效应在实验中未能解耦，"长答案"究竟为因果机制还是伴随现象尚待验证，后续需长度控制或消融实验加以区分。

Abstract: In reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards, a language model is rewarded for a correct final answer, and GRPO passes that reward to every token of the solution in equal measure. We instead place the credit where the model hesitated, in a method we call MCPO (Margin-Credit Policy Optimization). We measure hesitation by the top-2 margin $M=p_1-p_2$ of the policy's next-token distribution, map it to a score with two fixed thresholds, and spread each solution's advantage over its tokens so that the mean stays unchanged. The margin is read from a forward pass the trainer already performs, so the method needs no extra model evaluation and no batch statistics. We train MiniCPM5-2B-Base with LoRA for 200 steps on 2,048 DAPO-Math problems and evaluate on six competition-math benchmarks. MCPO beats an unmodified GRPO baseline for all five data seeds, by 6.55 points of macro avg@32 on average (95\% CI $[4.1, 9.0]$), and a Qwen3-4B-Base replication improves for all three seeds ($+4.75$). The gain is largest on problems that GRPO solves only some of the time and grows with $k$ in pass@$k$, reaching 13.8 points at pass@32. MCPO models also write two to three times longer answers, and runs that write longer score higher; our experiments do not separate the two effects.

</details>


### [189] [ScaleBrain: A Scale-Consistent Framework for EEG Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14035)
*Minyu Zhou,Yajing Wu,Yongqiang Tang,Wang Jia,Wensheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ScaleBrain，一种尺度一致的自监督预训练框架，从模型输入与优化目标两侧显式协调EEG多尺度表征：多尺度EEG分词（不同时间跨度、共享时间锚点的分析窗）+尺度特定重建目标+轴向时空注意力融合，在6个公开BCI基准上以仅5.0M参数取得5个数据集最优平均平衡准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG基础模型多通过特征级操作构造时间尺度，导致学到的特征与原始信号尺度特定特性之间的对应关系不显式；且当自监督目标与多尺度输入表征不对齐时，某些时间尺度信息在预训练中监督不足。

Method: 1) 多尺度EEG分词：以不同时间跨度、共享时间锚点的分析窗表征同一EEG片段，使表征与原始信号尺度特征显式对应；2) 尺度特定重建（scale-specific reconstruction）：作为自监督目标与多尺度输入结构对齐，在不同时间分辨率上提供监督；3) 轴向时空注意力（axial spatio-temporal attention）：沿空间与时间维度对融合后的多尺度表征进行上下文化。

Result: 在6个公开BCI基准上与12个代表性基线对比，ScaleBrain在5个数据集上取得最高平均平衡准确率，剩余1个排名第三；模型参数量仅5.0M，表现出较强性能与良好参数效率。

Conclusion: 从输入表征与优化目标两端协同设计的多尺度一致性预训练，可有效缓解尺度—特征对应隐式与尺度监督不足问题，以轻量参数实现领先的BCI解码性能。

Abstract: Foundation models pre-trained on large unlabeled electroencephalography (EEG) corpora have emerged as a transformative paradigm for Brain-Computer Interface (BCI) applications. Recent EEG foundation models have shown that multi-scale representations derived from fixed-length patches can improve EEG representation learning. Existing frameworks commonly construct temporal scales through feature-level operations. However, these operations leave the correspondence between learned features and scale-specific characteristics of the original EEG signals implicit. Moreover, when the self-supervised objective is not aligned with the multi-scale input representations, information at some temporal scales may receive insufficient supervision during pre-training. In light of this, we propose ScaleBrain, a scale-consistent pre-training framework that explicitly coordinates multi-scale representations of EEG from both the model input and optimization objective sides. Specifically, ScaleBrain introduces multi-scale EEG tokenization to represent the same EEG segment through analysis windows with different temporal spans and shared temporal anchors, and pairs these representations with scale-specific reconstruction to align with the multi-scale input structure and provide supervision across temporal resolutions during self-supervised learning. We further develop an axial spatio-temporal attention mechanism to contextualize the fused multi-scale representation along spatial and temporal dimensions. Extensive evaluations on six public BCI benchmarks against 12 representative baselines show that ScaleBrain achieves the highest mean Balanced Accuracy on five datasets and ranks third on the remaining one, with only 5.0M parameters, demonstrating strong performance and favorable parameter efficiency.

</details>


### [190] [Structured Confidence Perturbation via Fisher Geometry for Adversarial Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14046)
*Chenlong Wei,Hanling Wang,Chong Zhang,Qinkai Yu,Fangyu Wu,Xiaobo Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文针对对抗训练中的"伪攻击"问题，提出从损失最大化与置信度流形几何两个互补视角攻击并防御"置信度结构形变"，并给出高效算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗训练常受"伪攻击"困扰：扰动显著抬升损失值，却未真正改变模型的置信度结构，导致鲁棒性提升受限、训练信号失真。因此需要直接针对置信度结构形变进行攻击与防御。

Method: 提出两条互补路线：(1) 在传统损失最大化框架下引入交叉熵与KL散度的混合目标，使对抗样本同时破坏预测准确性与分布一致性；(2) 几何重构对抗训练，生成在置信度流形上最大化分布距离的扰动。理论分析表明最优扰动方向与Fisher信息矩阵的主特征向量对齐，并提出避免高维矩阵运算的高效算法。

Result: 在多个数据集与模型架构上的实验表明，该方法在对抗鲁棒性与鲁棒迁移性上优于现有方法，尤其在高置信度区域的结构性扰动方面表现突出。

Conclusion: 将对抗训练从单纯损失最大化拓展到置信度结构的形变层面，兼具理论刻画（Fisher信息矩阵主特征方向）与实用效率，为缓解伪攻击、提升鲁棒性提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Adversarial training is often plagued by "pseudo-attacks", perturbations that significantly increase the loss value but fail to truly alter the model's confidence structure. To address this, we propose two complementary approaches to directly attack and defend against confidence structure deformation. First, building upon the traditional loss maximization perspective, we introduce a hybrid objective that integrates cross-entropy and KL divergence, encouraging adversarial examples to simultaneously disrupt prediction accuracy and distribution consistency. Second, we geometrically reconstruct adversarial training by generating perturbations that maximize the distribution distance on the confidence manifold. Theoretical analysis shows that the optimal perturbation direction aligns with the principal eigenvectors of the Fisher information matrix. We also propose an efficient algorithm to avoid high-dimensional matrix computations. Experiments on multiple datasets and model architectures demonstrate that our method outperforms existing methods in adversarial robustness and robust transferability, particularly excelling in structural perturbations within high-confidence regions.

</details>


### [191] [Generalizing Causal Decision-Making: Markov Equivalence, Transportability and Counterfactuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14066)
*Min Woo Park,Sanghack Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将结构化因果 bandit 扩展到部分已知因果结构、跨环境迁移信息与可实现反事实动作三种情形，分别给出图准则、部分可迁移性界与反事实最优性刻画，并据此剪枝动作空间后保证仍保留最优动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典结构化因果 bandit 假设因果结构完全已知，而现实中往往只掌握部分结构（如仅到 PAG/MAG 层面）、可从其他环境分布中获取信息，且可用于干预的动作还受实验能力的限制。需要回答：在这些不完整信息下，哪些动作值得探索、能否在保留最优动作的前提下缩小动作空间。

Method: (1) 在最小干预集（MIS）框架下，针对 Markov 等价类给出 MIS 的图准则，并刻画极大祖先图（MAG）中"可能最优"的 MIS；设计搜索算法找出在部分祖先图（PAG）所表示至少一个图中可能最优的集合。(2) 通过部分可迁移性（partial transportability）从源分布推导目标环境奖励上界，处理不可操纵变量；利用支配关系进一步给出动作剪枝与受限探索的界，并在先验界有效时给出 regret 保证。(3) 在指定实验能力支撑的动作上建立反事实结构化因果 bandit，给出极小性与可能最优性的图刻画，导出删除等价/被支配动作但保持最优动作的算法。

Result: 得到三类理论结果：(a) Markov 等价与 PAG/MAG 下的 MIS 图准则及"可能最优 MIS"的搜索算法；(b) 基于部分可迁移性的目标奖励界、由支配关系导出的动作剪枝界与相应 regret 界；(c) 反事实设定下保留最优动作的动作约简算法。在设定结构因果模型上的实验考察了这些约简与迁移界对学习的影响。

Conclusion: 因果知识与实验能力共同决定了哪些动作值得探索；在部分结构、跨环境信息与反事实动作约束下，可以在理论上安全地约简动作空间并引导探索，同时保持最优动作不被排除。

Abstract: Structural causal bandits use causal knowledge to guide exploration when reward mechanisms are unknown. This paper extends the approach to partially known causal structure, information from other environments, and realizable counterfactual actions. First, we develop graphical criteria for minimal intervention sets under Markov equivalence and characterize possibly-optimal minimal intervention sets for maximal ancestral graphs. A search algorithm finds sets that are possibly optimal in at least one graph represented by a partial ancestral graph. Second, we derive target reward bounds from source distributions through partial transportability, accounting for non-manipulable variables. Dominance relations provide further bounds for action pruning and restricted exploration, with a regret guarantee under valid prior bounds. Third, we formulate counterfactual structural causal bandits over actions supported by specified experimental capabilities. Graphical characterizations of minimality and possible optimality yield algorithms that remove equivalent or dominated choices while preserving an optimal action. Experiments with specified structural causal models examine how these reductions and transferred bounds affect learning. Together, these results clarify how causal knowledge and experimental capabilities determine which actions merit exploration and guide learning within the retained action space.

</details>


### [192] [Opportunity-Cost Option-Critic: Adaptive Switching Costs for Robust Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14099)
*Khaled Ammoura,Aya Kayal,Naseem Daher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出机会成本选项-评论家框架（OC²），用当前选项与其最优替代选项之间的价值差作为状态依赖的“斟酌成本”，使选项在明显更优时保持、在出现更优选项时快速切换，从而改善非平稳环境下的适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分层强化学习（HRL）中常用“斟酌成本”（deliberation cost）防止选项（option）退化为基元动作。现有方法依赖固定惩罚或基于持续时间的自适应，二者都只是选项效用的间接代理，无法真实反映“放弃当前行为”的代价。

Method: 提出 OC² 框架，将斟酌成本定义为状态依赖的机会成本：活跃选项与其最佳替代选项之间的正价值差（value gap）。直接学习该成本会退化为零成本解，故引入一个辅助目标，使学到的成本与价值差目标对齐。

Result: 在 Four Rooms 与 Pinball 域上，于平稳、目标漂移与几何漂移三种条件下评估。Pinball 目标漂移任务中，OC² 前十回合成功率达 52%，固定成本基线为 18%。

Conclusion: 基于机会成本的持续性机制能提升非平稳环境中的适应能力；相较固定惩罚或时长启发式，价值差式的状态依赖成本是更直接的选项效用信号。

Abstract: Deliberation costs are commonly used in hierarchical reinforcement learning (HRL) to prevent options from collapsing to primitive actions. Existing approaches rely on fixed penalties or duration-based adaptations, both of which are indirect proxies for an option's utility. This paper argues that option persistence should instead depend on the opportunity cost of abandoning the current behavior. We propose the Opportunity-Cost Option-Critic ($OC^2$) framework, which defines a state-dependent deliberation cost using the positive value gap between the active option and its best alternative. This encourages persistence when the current option is clearly preferable and allows rapid switching when a better option emerges. We show that directly learning such costs leads to a degenerate zero-cost solution and introduces an auxiliary objective that aligns the learned cost with the value-gap target. The approach is evaluated in Four Rooms and Pinball domains under stationary, goal-shift, and geometry-shift conditions. In the Pinball goal-shift task, the $OC^2$ framework achieves a 52\% first-ten-episode success rate compared with 18\% for a fixed-cost baseline. The results suggest that opportunity-cost-based persistence improves adaptation in non-stationary environments.

</details>


### [193] [Causal Time Series Modeling of Supraglacial Lake Evolution in Greenland under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.15265)
*Emam Hossain,Muhammad Hasan Ferdous,Devon Dunmire,Aneesh Subramanian,Md Osman Gani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RIC-TSC框架，将滞后感知的因果发现（J-PCMCI+）嵌入时序建模，用于格陵兰冰面湖演化分类，在分布外评估中比相关性基线准确率最高提升12.59%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时空地球观测模型多依赖纯相关性特征，在异质域间迁移性差；因果建模能刻画稳定不变关系、提升分布偏移下的鲁棒性与泛化，但其潜力尚未被充分利用。

Method: 构建区域信息引导的因果时序分类框架RIC-TSC：融合Sentinel-1微波后向散射、Sentinel-2与Landsat-8光学反射率及CARRA气象变量等多模态卫星与再分析数据；用J-PCMCI+分别在全局和各流域尺度估计因果图，识别区域特定及不变的预测因子及其时间滞后，再将经校验的预测因子与滞后输入轻量级分类器。

Result: 在覆盖两个对比消融季（2018—2019）、1000个人工标注冰面湖的平衡基准上，分布外评估中因果模型准确率最高比相关性基线高12.59%。

Conclusion: 因果发现不仅是特征选择手段，更是构建可泛化、具机制解释性的动态地表过程模型的路径；该方法兼顾预测精度与科学可解释性。

Abstract: Causal modeling offers a principled foundation for uncovering stable, invariant relationships in time-series data, thereby improving robustness and generalization under distribution shifts. Yet its potential is underutilized in spatiotemporal Earth observation, where models often depend on purely correlational features that fail to transfer across heterogeneous domains. We propose RIC-TSC, a regionally-informed causal time-series classification framework that embeds lag-aware causal discovery directly into sequence modeling, enabling both predictive accuracy and scientific interpretability. Using multi-modal satellite and reanalysis data-including Sentinel-1 microwave backscatter, Sentinel-2 and Landsat-8 optical reflectance, and CARRA meteorological variables-we leverage Joint PCMCI+ (J-PCMCI+) to identify region-specific and invariant predictors of supraglacial lake evolution in Greenland. Causal graphs are estimated globally and per basin, with validated predictors and their time lags supplied to lightweight classifiers. On a balanced benchmark of 1000 manually labeled lakes from two contrasting melt seasons (2018-2019), causal models achieve up to 12.59% higher accuracy than correlation-based baselines under out-of-distribution evaluation. These results show that causal discovery is not only a means of feature selection but also a pathway to generalizable and mechanistically grounded models of dynamic Earth surface processes.

</details>


### [194] [Hierarchical Graph Representation Learning with Pooling-Induced Substructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14126)
*Luca Sbicego,Xiaowen Dong,Dorina Thanou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Pooled-Substructure-Aware GNNs（PSA-GNN），在结构池化过程中显式保留并处理池化诱导的子结构，理论上提升池化GNN在WL测试下的表达力，并配套两个可控层级结构的合成数据集与一个衡量真实基准层级模式普遍性的指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准GNN难以对超出局部邻域的子结构进行推理；图池化虽通过逐层粗化构造层级表示来应对，但现有方法往往不显式保留池化所诱导的子结构，导致经验性能不稳定；同时缺乏具有显式层级结构的基准，评估困难。

Method: 将基于结构的池化所划分出的子结构显式保留并作为独立对象继续处理（而非仅依赖粗化后的超节点表示），把子结构信息融入池化GNN的消息传递与读出流程；并从Weisfeiler-Lehman测试角度进行表达力分析。评估方面构建两个层级结构可控的合成数据集，并提出刻画真实图级基准中层级模式普遍性的度量指标。

Result: 在具有相关层级结构的任务上，方法优于同配置的池化基线；在结构更简单的基准上保持竞争力；理论上表明该设计可提升池化GNN在WL意义下的表达力。

Conclusion: 显式保留池化诱导子结构是提升池化GNN表达力与层级推理性能的有效设计原则，配套的合成数据集与层级模式指标为相关方法提供了更有针对性的评估手段，代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: Hierarchical graph-level tasks that require reasoning over substructures that extend beyond local neighborhoods are posing challenges for standard Graph Neural Networks (GNNs). While graph pooling aims to address this by coarsening graphs into hierarchical representations, existing methods often fail to explicitly preserve the substructures they induce, leading to inconsistent empirical performance. Moreover, evaluating such approaches remains challenging due to the lack of benchmarks with explicit hierarchical structure. We propose Pooled-Substructure-Aware GNNs, an approach to graph pooling that explicitly preserves and processes substructures uncovered by structure-based pooling schemes. We show that this design enhances the expressive power of pooling-based GNNs in the sense of the Weisfeiler-Lehman (WL) test. To enable principled evaluation, we introduce two new synthetic datasets with controllable hierarchical structure and propose a metric to characterize the prevalence of hierarchical patterns in real-world graph-level benchmarks. On tasks with relevant hierarchical structure, our approach outperforms matched pooling baselines, while remaining competitive on structurally simpler benchmarks. Code and datasets are publicly available at https://github.com/lsbicego/PSA-GNN/.

</details>


### [195] [Right for the Wrong Reasons? Evaluating Plausibility and Behavioral Change in Explanation-Guided Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14134)
*David Dembinsky,Adriano Lucieri,Andreas Dengel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在受控的合成捷径数据集上系统比较不同解释引导学习（EGL）目标，发现EGL的效果高度依赖于具体目标：基于理由的数据增强最能提升鲁棒性，而归因约束目标主要提升解释合理性，二者均不能可靠地带来扰动保真度的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EGL 声称能让模型既准确又依赖任务相关证据，但"解释与人类理由更一致"是否真的意味着模型决策策略发生了改变仍不明确。现有评估往往缺乏对捷径类型的受控对比，难以区分解释层面的改善与行为层面的纠正。

Method: 构建受控的合成捷径数据集，涵盖局部化、背景型和全局型三类捷径线索（借鉴真实应用场景），并在该基准上对比多种 EGL 目标，从行为层面（准确率、前景依赖）与解释层面（重要性质量、扰动保真度等）双轴评估。

Result: EGL 效果强烈依赖于具体目标。基于理由的数据增强在捷径证据可与任务相关前景信息分离时鲁棒性收益最明显，平均准确率提升达 +4.8 个百分点，并伴随前景依赖增强；归因约束类目标主要提升解释合理性，重要性质量平均提升最高 +14 个百分点，但行为纠正较弱且不稳定，平均准确率增益至多 +1.6 个百分点。此外，鲁棒性或合理性的提升都不能可靠地预示更高的扰动保真度。

Conclusion: EGL 是有用的，但其成功不能仅凭理由一致性来推断，应在互补的行为层面与解释层面双轴进行评估；作者公开了全部实验结果（https://right-for-wrong-reasons.dfki.de/）以供进一步分析。

Abstract: Explanation-Guided Learning (EGL) promises models that are not only accurate but also rely on task-relevant evidence. However, whether improved agreement between model explanations and human-provided rationales also implies a changed decision strategy remains unclear. We study this tension under controlled synthetic shortcut introduction using a constructed shortcut dataset. Inspired by real-world applications, our benchmark covers shortcuts of different kinds, including localized, background-based, and global cues. This allows us to evaluate whether different EGL objectives produce behavioral changesand explanatory changes. Across these axes, we find that the effects of EGL are strongly objective-specific. Rationale-based data augmentation provides the clearest robustness gains when shortcut evidence can be separated from task-relevant foreground information, reaching average accuracy improvements of up to +4.8 percentage points (pp) together with increased foreground reliance. Attribution-constraining objectives primarily improve explanation plausibility, increasing Importance Mass by up to +14 pp on average, but provide weaker and less consistent behavioral correction, with average accuracy gains of at most +1.6 pp. Moreover, neither improved robustness nor improved plausibility reliably implies higher perturbation-based fidelity. Overall, our results show that EGL can be useful, but its success cannot be inferred from rationale agreement alone and should instead be evaluated along complementary behavioral and explanation-level axes. We publicly release all experimental results to enable further analysis (https://right-for-wrong-reasons.dfki.de/).

</details>


### [196] [HaiRuo: Forecasting Ocean Eddies as Fields and Objects with a Satellite-Altimetry Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14136)
*Xukun Qin,Haochen Sun,Yuyang Tian,Ge Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HaiRuo 提出"场+对象"双模态中尺度涡预报框架：以 24 年全球卫星高度计数据预训练的 1.05B 参数基础模型 HaiRuo-1B 一次前向输出 15 天海面高度、地转流速与逐日涡旋掩膜，再由拉格朗日对象解码器 XuanJi 在随动极坐标框架下迭代细化涡心轨迹，实现 15 天涡心误差 25.2 km（优于 14 种方法 18%），全局预注册评测下稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中尺度涡承载海洋大部分动能并输运热量、盐度与营养盐，但两周尺度涡旋轨迹仍依赖海洋模式的事后追踪启发式，缺乏端到端、可直接预测涡旋轨迹的方法；且现有方法要么只预测物理场、要么只做对象追踪，二者割裂。

Method: 双组件分工：HaiRuo-1B 为无标签预训练的 1.05B 参数基础模型（24 年全球卫星高度计观测），输入 30 天 SLA 图，输出 15 天海面高度场、地转流场及逐日涡旋掩膜；XuanJi 为拉格朗日对象解码器，在每个涡旋的随动极坐标框架内采样演化预报，经三轮前瞻迭代（look-ahead rounds）与对象注意力细化 15 天路径。可冻结骨干、端到端联合微调或即插即用于其他骨干。

Result: 同解码器下，HaiRuo-1B 提升场技巧 +0.14、mIoU +1.1 点；冻结时 15 天涡心误差降低 1.2 km（从头训练降低 1.35 km）。XuanJi 在 HaiRuo-1B 上再降 2.5 km（联合微调 2.9 km），在六个已发表骨干上经对象注意力降低 2.5–3.3 km。全局预注册评测：15 天涡心误差 25.2 km vs. 最优基线 30.6 km（-18%），14 种方法全面领先；在标注区域外及 ENSO 两相位下保持优势；复现西向漂移、50–60°S 东向反转及气旋-反气旋向极偏转。经固定追踪器 10 天外推，HaiRuo 预报场使 WenHai、XiHe 涡心误差降低 37–43%（-2.8 km，而二者退化 +4.3–8.7 km），对象轨迹使流水线误差全局降低 77–78%。

Conclusion: 将涡旋预测统一为"连续物理场"与"离散对象轨迹"的联合建模是可行且高效的：大规模无标签预训练提供可迁移的动力学先验，拉格朗日对象解码器可即插即用地改进现有骨干。工作开源权重、代码与项目页，为该方向提供可复现基线。

Abstract: Mesoscale eddies carry most of the ocean's kinetic energy and move heat, salt and nutrients across basins, yet where an eddy will be two weeks ahead is still read off ocean models with post-hoc tracking heuristics. We introduce HaiRuo, which forecasts eddies as fields and as objects at once: from 30 days of satellite sea-level maps, one forward pass returns 15 days of sea level and geostrophic velocity, daily eddy masks and the trajectory of every eddy alive today. HaiRuo pairs two components with a clear division of labour: (1) HaiRuo-1B, an open-weight 1.05-billion-parameter foundation model pretrained without labels on 24 years of global satellite-altimetry observations, which supplies the 15-day physical fields and masks (+0.14 field skill and +1.1 mIoU points under the same decoder) and further lowers 15-day centre error by 1.2 km frozen (1.35 km over training from scratch); and (2) XuanJi, a Lagrangian object decoder that samples the evolving forecast in each eddy's co-moving polar frame and refines the 15-day path across three look-ahead rounds, removing 2.5 km on HaiRuo-1B (2.9 km with joint fine-tuning) and 2.5-3.3 km via its object attention across six published backbones. Under one pre-registered global evaluation over the whole ocean, HaiRuo improves on fourteen methods on every endpoint (25.2 vs. 30.6 km 15-day centre error, -18%), holding its margin outside the labelled regions and through both phases of the El Nino-Southern Oscillation (ENSO), while reproducing westward drift, 50-60 deg S eastward reversal and poleward cyclone-anticyclone deflection. Over ten days through one fixed tracker, HaiRuo's forecast field cuts the eddy-centre error of WenHai and XiHe by 37-43% (-2.8 km vs. day 0 while they degrade +4.3-8.7 km), and its object trajectories cut pipeline error by 77-78% globally. Open-weight checkpoints, code and project page: https://github.com/xkqin/HaiRuo

</details>


### [197] [Deviation-Aware Pretraining for Cardiovascular Disease Detection from Electrocardiograms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14141)
*Siyuan Yan,Haibo Zheng,Feng Lu,Wei Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自监督心电图（ECG）特征提取框架，基于近百万条无标签12导联ECG数据训练，包含偏差定位嵌入（DLE）与关联重建嵌入（ARE）两个模块，在多个公开数据集上AUROC最高提升6.5%、F1提升12.8%，且无需额外微调即可泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习ECG模型多依赖有监督、任务特定训练，难以跨疾病类型、患者人群与采集条件泛化；同时标注数据获取成本高，限制了模型在多样医疗场景中的可扩展性与公平性。

Method: 构建自监督预训练框架，利用约100万条无标签12导联ECG学习通用表征。框架含两模块：（1）偏差定位嵌入（DLE）：检测波形层面相对正常模式的偏离；（2）关联重建嵌入（ARE）：以偏差为引导进行跨导联重建，建模导联间关联，捕捉多导联共同表达的异常模式。二者结合使特征同时编码细微偏差与跨导联上下文信息。

Result: 在多个公开数据集上，相较既有方法AUROC最高提升6.5%、F1最高提升12.8%，且未对预训练特征提取器做额外微调即取得该结果；嵌入可视化显示学到的特征具备有意义的疾病聚类与异常识别能力。

Conclusion: 该自监督框架降低了对标注数据的依赖，具备跨数据集泛化能力，可辅助临床医生在多样医疗环境中使用，有望提升心血管疾病的早期检测与长期监测水平，推动更可及、更公平的心脏健康照护。

Abstract: Electrocardiogram (ECG) signals are critical for cardiovascular disease (CVD) detection. Most current deep learning models rely on supervised, task-specific training, which limits their ability to generalize across diseases, patient populations, and acquisition settings. This paper presents a self-supervised ECG feature extraction framework that learns from nearly one million unlabeled 12-lead ECG recordings. The framework includes two modules: Deviation Localization Embedding (DLE), which detects waveform-level deviations from normal patterns, and Association Reconstruction Embedding (ARE), which models cross-lead associations through deviation-guided reconstruction to capture abnormal patterns expressed across multiple leads. These modules enable the framework to capture subtle deviation-related patterns and their cross-lead context. The framework achieves up to 6.5% AUROC and 12.8% F1 improvements over prior methods on multiple public datasets, even without additional fine-tuning the pretrained feature extractor. Embedding visualizations show that the learned features support meaningful disease grouping and anomaly identification. The approach reduces the need for labeled data and can support clinicians in diverse healthcare settings. The framework has potential to improve early detection and monitoring of cardiovascular diseases, contributing to more accessible and equitable heart care.

</details>


### [198] [Unipolar Neuron Networks Trained by Recirculation for Reward-Based Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14153)
*Florin Leon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出由"单极神经元树"构成的网络及称为 recirculation 的纯奖励学习规则，并在 MNIST/Iris 顺序任务上验证其抗遗忘能力显著优于梯度训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单极神经元是章鱼等无脊椎动物的主要神经元类型，其单一神经突分叉成树，同时承载输入与输出突触，并兼具局部（近距离分级输出）与全局（远端目标）广播式输出。现有主流人工网络多以点神经元加反向传播为范式，与这种生物结构差异大，且顺序学习时易灾难性遗忘。作者希望用更贴近生物形态的树状无源段模型加局部学习规则，实现纯奖励驱动的学习并缓解遗忘。

Method: 1) 神经元建模：每个神经元是一棵无源段（passive segments）构成的树，各段激活值按线性系统稳态精确求解。2) 学习规则 recirculation：前向传播后，第二次传播把"期望的输出变化"沿同一组连接回送；每个突触仅用其本地可获得量调整权重。3) 由于每个神经元的线性系统对称，该局部更新近似等于预测奖励误差的梯度。4) 抗遗忘双机制：任务编码（task code）在每个任务中抑制不同子集的树分支；巩固（consolidation）降低近期变化较大突触的学习率。5) 在 MNIST 与 Iris 任务序列上评估。

Result: 在 MNIST 与 Iris 多任务序列上，该网络保持高准确率且遗忘很低；作为对照，对同一网络使用基于梯度的训练几乎完全遗忘先前任务。

Conclusion: 树状无源段 + 局部 recirculation 规则可近似实现梯度式信用分配，无需显式反向传播；配合任务编码式分支门控与基于近期权变幅度的巩固机制，可在序列学习中实现稳定的高精度与低遗忘，显著优于同结构网络的梯度训练。

Abstract: Unipolar neurons are the predominant type of nerve cell in the octopus and in most other invertebrates. A single neurite (a fiber extending from the cell body) branches into a tree that carries both input and output synapses. The branches send graded local outputs to nearby cells, and one site on the main neurite sends a global output to distant targets. This paper proposes a network of such neurons and a learning rule for it. Each neuron is modeled as a tree of passive segments, whose activations are computed exactly as the steady state of a linear system. The network learns from reward alone, with a rule called recirculation. After each forward pass, a second pass sends requested changes of the outputs back through the same connections. Each synapse then adjusts its weight using only quantities available at its own location. Because the system of each neuron is symmetric, this update closely follows the gradient of the error in the predicted reward. Two mechanisms limit forgetting when tasks are learned in sequence: a task code inhibits a different subset of branches in each task, while consolidation lowers the learning rate of synapses that changed strongly in the recent past. The model is evaluated on a sequence of MNIST and Iris tasks. The network maintains high accuracy with low forgetting, whereas gradient-based training of the same network forgets earlier tasks almost completely.

</details>


### [199] [Tree-Ising: Repairing Language Models with Bit-Level Ising Edits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14210)
*Hadi Salloum,Ammar Ali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Tree-Ising将LLM权重修复形式化为BF16权重上的稀疏离散优化问题，分解为逐输出行的QUBO/Ising问题并用GPU模拟退火或模拟分岔求解，在数学推理修复上优于agent编辑且成本极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MIT的weight-space programming让frontier LLM agent直接读写网络权重进行无梯度修复，但代价高昂（158轮agent交互、8970万tokens、50次前向请求）。作者希望用纯粹的稀疏离散优化替代agent的推理式编辑，以更少权重改动、更低成本获得同等或更好的修复效果。

Method: 将修复建模为模型自身BF16权重上的稀疏离散优化：用前向统计定义目标函数（削弱循环方向、强化结论方向、在有效token上保持层输出不变）；在状态树表示下（无损BF16存储为特例），允许的编辑为符号翻转与指数位移，且保持共享的组指数和尾数不变；将目标精确分解为每个输出行独立的QUBO(Ising)子问题，在GPU上用模拟退火或量子启发式模拟分岔求解；并在IBM Heron处理器上用QAOA执行8变量子问题作为概念验证。

Result: 在agent的两个层上，Tree-Ising在MATH-500达54.6%（agent编辑51.7%，基座7.9%）；模拟分岔求解器得55.1%，统计上不可区分。两种求解器在全部五个数学基准上均优于agent编辑，校正多重比较后在MATH-500与GSM8K上显著。改动权重少13%–17%，BF16位翻转减少2.2–2.3倍；仅需4次前向请求、零LLM token，退火方案GPU耗时约0.90美元。在Qwen3.5-2B上（仅用基座选层与剂量），贪心GSM8K从21.6%提升至69.4%。

Conclusion: 将权重修复转化为稀疏离散优化（Ising/QUBO）可复现甚至超越LLM agent的权重编辑效果，同时大幅减少权重改动量、前向调用次数与成本，并具备向量子硬件迁移的概念验证潜力。

Abstract: Weight-space programming, introduced in recent MIT work by Takehi et al., enables a frontier LLM agent to read a network's weights and write a repair. Their GPT-6 Astra agent corrected the reasoning loops of Qwen3.5-0.8B without gradients, using 158 agent turns, 89.7 million tokens, and 50 forward-pass requests. Our method, Tree-Ising, casts this repair as a sparse discrete optimization problem over the model's own BF16 weights. Forward statistics define an objective that weakens a loop direction, strengthens a concluding direction, and preserves the layer's output on productive tokens. Within a state-tree representation, where lossless BF16 storage acts as a special case, the admissible edits are sign flips and exponent shifts that preserve all shared group exponents and mantissas. We decompose this objective exactly into independent QUBO (Ising) problems, one per output row, which we solve on a GPU using simulated annealing or quantum-inspired simulated bifurcation. On the agent's two layers, Tree-Ising achieves 54.6% on MATH-500, outperforming the agent's edit (51.7%) under the same evaluation protocol, while the base model scores 7.9% as reported. The simulated bifurcation solver yields a statistically indistinguishable repair at 55.1%. Both solvers outperform the agent edit across all five math benchmarks, with statistically significant improvements on MATH-500 and GSM8K after correcting for multiple comparisons. Our approach modifies 13% to 17% fewer weights and reduces BF16 bit flips by a factor of 2.2 to 2.3. It requires only four forward-pass requests and zero LLM tokens, with the annealing recipe costing about $0.90 in GPU time. On Qwen3.5-2B, with layers and dose selected using only the base model, the same recipe increases greedy GSM8K accuracy from 21.6% to 69.4%. As a proof of concept, we execute an eight-variable repair subproblem using QAOA on an IBM Heron processor.

</details>
