### [200] [Verifiable Agentic Traffic Class: A Policed Quality-of-Service Mechanism for Agentic AI Traffic, Evaluated from Simulation to Real-Kernel Adversarial Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14211)
*Naveen Babu Narayanan Nair,Sajay Kurup*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出可验证智能体流量类（VATC）：用保留DSCP标记智能体流量，路由器侧分类器将违背声明的流降级到尽力而为之后，并配限速优先队列，在仿真与真实Linux内核中与DiffServ、fq_codel对比评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的软件智能体产生的流量与人类网页流量特征不同，但路由器没有对应类别，也无法验证发送方"我是智能体"的声明，因此需要一种既可声明又可验证的区分服务类别。

Method: VATC由三部分组成：(1) 保留的DSCP值声明智能体流量；(2) 路由器侧分类器，把与声明相矛盾的流降级为排在尽力而为流量之后服务的类；(3) 限速优先队列。在两种仿真器和真实Linux内核上与静态DiffServ（信任标记的专用类）及fq_codel（逐流公平排队）对比，并测试欺骗洪泛、四特征在线分类器、引入仿真网络时延，以及真实智能体框架在拥塞链路上的表现。

Result: 稳态负载下VATC与DiffServ相当。构造突发下，仿真中（假设即时判决）智能体平均排队时延较DiffServ降低98%，人类时延增加12%，增益主要来自独立类别。内核中欺骗洪泛下，限速使人类时延比DiffServ低11倍。但无验证时，DiffServ与紧配置fq_codel给合法智能体的时延低于VATC；在线四特征分类器降级了全部20个"批量型"欺骗者、给智能体0.30 ms，却漏检6个复制智能体模式的欺骗者；加入仿真网络时延后，时序特征失去区分能力。真实框架在拥塞链路上，路由器排队占任务时间的13%–63%。

Conclusion: VATC在稳态、突发和欺骗洪泛场景下优于静态DiffServ的时延保护，但代价是人类时延上升，且在无验证情形下不优于DiffServ/fq_codel；分类器对模仿型欺骗与时延扰动脆弱。结论基于合成或沙箱规模流量，实际部署有效性仍需验证。

Abstract: Software agents built on large language models generate traffic unlike human web traffic, yet routers have no class for it and cannot verify a sender's claim to be an agent. This paper proposes the Verifiable Agentic Traffic Class (VATC), which combines a reserved Differentiated Services Code Point (DSCP) value declaring agent traffic, a router-side classifier that demotes flows contradicting the declaration to a class served after best-effort traffic, and a rate-limited priority queue. VATC was compared with static Differentiated Services (DiffServ), a dedicated class that trusts the marking, and fq_codel, a per-flow fair-queuing scheduler, in two simulators and on a real Linux kernel. VATC matched DiffServ under steady load. In a constructed burst it cut mean agent queuing delay by 98% relative to DiffServ in simulation (assuming an immediate verdict), at a 12% increase in human delay; most of the gain came from the separate class. Under a spoofed flood in the kernel, the rate limit held human latency 11 times below DiffServ's. Without verification, DiffServ and a tightly configured fq_codel gave legitimate agents lower latency than VATC; a live four-feature classifier then demoted all 20 bulk-shaped spoofers and gave agents 0.30 ms, but missed six spoofers that copied the agent pattern. With emulated network delay, a timing feature of the classifier stopped separating agent from human flows. In real agent harnesses on a congested link, router queueing took 13% to 63% of task time, depending on harness and assumed model latency. The traffic was synthetic or sandbox-scale.

</details>


### [201] [SinkState: On-the-Fly SVD of Recurrent States for Efficient Hybrid LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14246)
*Haolin Jiang,Weihang Li,Zhongzhu Zhou,Jinwen Pan,Santosh Pandey,Hang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SinkState，通过将混合模型中线性注意力层的循环状态以自适应低秩形式存储与更新，降低解码期的内存占用与访存开销，从而在保持精度的前提下加速推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合模型用线性注意力把历史压缩到固定大小的循环状态中，但现有服务系统仍以稠密形式保存这些状态，每一步解码都要完整读写，内存流量随批大小增长并主导解码开销。作者发现线性层 Q/K/V 集中于稳定的低维子空间（线性注意力 sink），使每个状态实际近似低秩且秩随状态变化，因此稠密存储存在冗余。

Method: SinkState 在整个推理过程中将每个循环状态以自适应低秩形式存储，采用缓冲更新机制，并只在顶层子空间上做局部 SVD；其余组件在 CPU 上异步整合。

Result: 在四个含 Mamba-2 与 Gated DeltaNet (GDN) 层的混合模型（4B 至 120B 参数）上，相比 Mamba-SSM 与 FLA，循环状态峰值内存最多降低 2.6x（平均 1.9x），解码速度最多提升 1.58x（平均 1.25x），且保持精度。

Conclusion: 线性注意力循环状态的固有低秩特性可被系统层面显式利用；SinkState 以自适应低秩状态存储与异步更新实现了显著的内存节省与解码加速，为混合模型的高效服务提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Hybrid models combine softmax attention with linear attention, which compresses history into fixed-size recurrent states to balance model quality and inference efficiency. However, linear attention has not pushed this compression to its limit: we find that the queries, keys, and values of linear layers concentrate in a stable, low-dimensional subspace, a phenomenon we call the linear attention sink, so each state is effectively low-rank, with a rank that varies across states. Yet existing serving systems keep these redundant states dense, reading and rewriting them in full at every decoding step, so their memory traffic grows with the batch size and dominates the decoding cost. Thus SinkState stores each recurrent state in an adaptive low-rank form throughout inference with buffered updates and a local SVD restricted to the top subspace. SinkState further consolidates the remaining components asynchronously on the CPU. On four hybrid models with Mamba-2 and Gated DeltaNet (GDN) layers, from 4B to 120B parameters, SinkState reduces the peak memory of recurrent states by up to 2.6x (1.9x on average) and speeds up decoding by up to 1.58x (1.25x on average) over Mamba-SSM and FLA, while maintaining accuracy.

</details>


### [202] [A Margin Perspective on LoRA: Robustness to Catastrophic Forgetting and Adapter Merging (MaLoRA)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14397)
*Ziqing Xu,Hancheng Min,Lachlan Ewen MacDonald,Salma Tarmoun,Enrique Mallada,Weijie Su,Rene Vidal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从 margin（间隔）视角为 LoRA 的两大经验性质——抗灾难性遗忘与多适配器可合并——提供统一的理论解释，并据此提出免训练的合并规则 MaLoRA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA 在实践中表现出对灾难性遗忘的鲁棒性以及可将独立训练的适配器合并为多任务模型的能力，这两点对持续学习与多任务学习至关重要，但此前缺乏理论理解。

Method: 在多类线性分类、近正交任务设定下，结合 ℓ2 正则化分析 LoRA 的最优适配器，刻画不同正则化区间下的解；在中间正则化区间证明最优适配器与微调数据上的最大间隔解对齐；进而推导预训练数据与微调数据间隔的闭式表达式，并分析适配器合并的间隔性质与最优混合系数。

Result: 得到三类结论：(1) 正则化参数在保留与适配之间形成精确的间隔权衡；(2) 合并模型在每个任务上均获得正间隔，并可导出平衡各任务间隔、最大化并集间隔的最优混合系数；(3) 由此给出免训练的合并规则 MaLoRA，在现代架构与真实数据集的视觉、语言分类任务上与多个合并基线相当或更优。

Conclusion: 该工作以间隔为统一框架解释了 LoRA 的抗遗忘与可合并性，将理论刻画转化为实用的免训练合并方法，并经实验验证。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) is the de facto method for parameter-efficient fine-tuning of large neural networks, achieving strong task adaptation with minimal trainable parameters and low optimization cost. Beyond this efficiency, practitioners have observed two striking properties: \textit{robustness to catastrophic forgetting} and the ability to \textit{merge independently trained adapters} into a single model that performs competitively across multiple tasks. These properties are central to continual and multi-task learning, yet remain poorly understood. In this work, we provide a theoretical explanation through a unified \textit{margin-based perspective}. We analyze LoRA in multiclass linear classification under a near-orthogonal task regime with $\ell_2$-regularization, and characterize the optimal LoRA adapter across regularization regimes. Our analysis shows that, in an intermediate regime of regularization parameters, the optimal adapter aligns with the \emph{max-margin solution} on the fine-tuning data. Building on this characterization, we derive two key consequences. First, we obtain closed-form expressions for the margins on pre-training and fine-tuning data, revealing a precise margin trade-off: the regularization parameter controls the balance between retention and adaptation. Second, we analyze adapter merging, proving that merged models achieve positive margin on each task and deriving optimal mixing coefficients that balance margins across tasks and maximize the margin over their union. These results lead to a simple, training-free merging rule, which we term \textit{margin-based LoRA merging} (MaLoRA). Experiments on modern architectures and real datasets validate our theoretical predictions, showing that MaLoRA matches or outperforms several adapter-merging baselines across a range of vision and language classification tasks.

</details>


### [203] [REAL Loss: Optimizing Real Expected Acceptance Length for Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14399)
*Yu Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出REAL损失，直接精确优化草稿模型的期望接受长度(EAL)，克服现有块级代理目标的协方差偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投机解码的加速比由期望接受长度(EAL)决定，但标准训练目标(交叉熵、KL、总变差)只做分布匹配或单步重叠优化；现有块级目标仍是EAL的代理而非精确形式，例如对目标分布续写做后期接受平均而非按已接受token选定的验证分支，导致协方差差距，甚至使梯度方向反转。

Method: 分析上述协方差差距，推导出REAL——真实EAL训练损失：用路径权重实现无偏估计，并用Rao-Blackwell化降低方差；仅修改训练损失，不改动草稿模型架构与推理流程。

Result: 在Qwen3.6-35B-A3B目标模型与Domino草稿模型的受控损失层面比较中，REAL在9个任务子集、5个评估随机种子上于概率化服务接受长度指标上优于WTV、VSD、DBLast及静态LK基线。

Conclusion: REAL使EAL训练从代理目标走向精确目标，实证表明真实EAL训练实用有效，且实现成本极低（仅改损失函数）。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates LLM inference by having a draft model propose multiple tokens for the target model to verify in one pass. The speedup is governed by the expected acceptance length (EAL), yet standard training objectives such as cross-entropy, KL divergence, and total variation focus only on distribution matching or single-step overlap. Recent studies instead propose block-level training objectives, aiming to optimize EAL directly. However, these objectives remain surrogates rather than exact formulations of EAL. For example, some average later acceptance over target-distributed continuations rather than the verification branches selected by accepted tokens, introducing a covariance gap that can reverse the gradient direction. We analyze this gap and derive REAL, a real EAL training loss that uses path weights for unbiased estimation and Rao-Blackwellization for variance reduction. In a controlled loss-level comparison with a Qwen3.6-35B-A3B target and a Domino drafter, REAL outperforms WTV, VSD, DBLast, and the static LK baseline on probabilistic serving acceptance length across 9 task subsets and 5 evaluation seeds. These gains require only a change to the training loss, with no modification to the drafter architecture or inference procedure, demonstrating the practicality of real EAL training. A reference implementation of the REAL loss is available at https://github.com/King-ty/speculators/tree/real-loss.

</details>


### [204] [Discovering Equations for the Thermodynamics of South Asian Monsoon Precipitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14401)
*Savannah L. Ferretti,Jane W. Baldwin,Michael S. Pritchard,Tom Beucler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"符号蒸馏"方法（神经网络+符号回归），从21年夏季再分析资料中挖掘可解释的南亚季风降水方程，性能优于传统浮力诊断且参数少三个量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 南亚季风降水依赖低层大气的热力学结构，但其各要素如何组合影响降水尚不清楚，部分原因是这种依赖在垂直方向上是非局地的；而现有浮力型诊断（简单把水汽与不稳定度相加）物理上过于简化。

Method: 符号蒸馏分两步：先用神经网络学习与降水最相关的垂直热力学结构，将每个变量压缩为一个不依赖具体垂直网格的可解释特征；再用符号回归从这些特征及地表状态推导闭式方程。

Result: 最佳方程解释的降水方差是常用浮力诊断的1.5倍，在参数量约少1000倍的情况下达到神经网络技能约85%；方程把水汽与不稳定度视为相互竞争的控制因子而非相加，最强降水出现在低层自由对流层湿润时；在海洋上，感热加热只有在近地面空气暖湿时才对应更多降水，并改善了对降水及其日循环的预测。

Conclusion: 尽管特征仅为降水预测而优化，方程对各特征的响应大体符合对流物理预期并与再分析一致；这些响应把季风降水对整层垂直结构的依赖简化为少数简单、可检验的约束，可用于指导数据驱动降水模型提升物理一致性。

Abstract: South Asian monsoon precipitation depends on the thermodynamic structure of the lower troposphere, but how its ingredients combine to shape precipitation remains unclear, partly because this dependence is nonlocal in the vertical. We use symbolic distillation, which combines neural networks and symbolic regression, to discover interpretable precipitation equations from 21 years of June-August reanalysis data. Neural networks first learn the vertical thermodynamic structure most relevant to precipitation, condensing each variable into a single interpretable feature not tied to a particular vertical grid. Then, symbolic regression derives closed-form equations from these features and the surface state. Our best equation explains 1.5 times as much precipitation variance as a widely used buoyancy-based diagnostic and recovers about 85% of neural network skill while using about a thousand times fewer parameters. Unlike the buoyancy diagnostic, which adds moisture and instability, the equations treat them as competing controls, with the heaviest precipitation occurring when the lower free troposphere is humid. Over ocean, sensible heating is associated with additional precipitation mainly when near-surface air is warm and moist, improving predicted precipitation and its diurnal cycle. Although the learned features were optimized only for precipitation prediction, the equations largely respond to each feature as expected from convective physics, consistent with the reanalysis. These responses reduce the dependence of monsoon precipitation on the entire vertical column to a few simple, testable constraints that could guide data-driven precipitation models to make them more physically consistent.

</details>


### [205] [Causal Concept Explanations for Deep Neural Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14407)
*Josh Rountree,Pulkit Verma,Oswin So,Chuchu Fan,Julie A. Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CCW-Net，首次将中介分析（interventional mediation analysis）用作训练目标，使基于概念的解释与模型输出之间具有可量化的因果关系，并在航空器控制、驾驶和图像分类任务上验证了必要性、充分性与独立性三条因果对齐准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经网络的概念解释本质上是相关性的：某个概念解释可能看似正确，但对模型输出既不必要也不充分，从而具有误导性。缺少引导并量化概念解释与输出之间因果关系的训练与评估手段，制约了自主系统的开发、认证与部署。

Method: 引入CCW-Net（Causal Concept Wrapper Network）：1）将经济学、医学、流行病学中的干预式中介分析作为训练目标，对齐概念解释的因果效应与模型输出；2）以反事实概念表示锚定三条因果对齐准则——必要性（无关概念效应趋于零）、充分性（相关概念表示可完整重构模型输出）、独立性（总效应在各概念间线性分解）；3）同时把中介分析用作评估工具，按概念标签组合量化模型输出中可因果归因于各概念解释的比例。

Result: 在航空器控制、驾驶与图像分类三类任务上进行评估，CCW-Net能够塑造概念表示使其同时满足必要性、充分性与独立性；并可按概念标签组合定量给出输出对单个概念解释的因果归因份额。

Conclusion: CCW-Net使概念解释不再仅是可解释的，而是可量化因果的，为透明可信神经模型的安全性评估、认证与部署提供了支持。

Abstract: Concept-based explanations in neural models are important for the development, certification, and adoption of autonomous systems. Yet, interpretability remains correlational. Without methods to guide and quantify causal relationships between explanations, a concept explanation can be provided without being necessary or sufficient for the observed output thereby appearing correct while misleading. We introduce Causal Concept Wrapper Network (CCW-Net), the first use of interventional mediation analysis from fields such as economics, medicine, and epidemiology as a training objective to align causal effects of concept-based explanations with a model's output. We further apply mediation analysis as an evaluation tool to quantify, per concept label combination, how much of any model's output is causally attributable to each concept explanation. As a training objective, CCW-Net shapes a model's concept representations such that they are both necessary and sufficient for the outputs they are credited with and independent of other concepts' contributions. Counterfactual concept representations anchor a set of causal alignment criteria: necessity, sufficiency, and independence. Necessity drives irrelevant concept effects toward zero, sufficiency ensures relevant concept representations fully reconstruct a model's output, and independence enforces the total effect decomposes linearly across concepts. We evaluate CCW-Net across aircraft control, driving, and image classification. CCW-Net provides a step toward concept-based explanations that are not merely interpretable, but quantifiably causal, supporting safer evaluation, certification, and deployment of transparent and trustworthy neural models.

</details>


### [206] [DnNP: Denoising Input Uncertainty in Neural Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14424)
*Fatemeh Tohidian,Chengzhi Shi,Soomi Lee,Matthew Elia,Amy Mueller,Stratis Ioannidis,Jennifer Dy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出去噪神经过程（DnNP），显式建模输入噪声，通过变分推断联合学习去噪与元学习，在噪声数据下提升预测与不确定性量化，并提供理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准神经过程（NP）框架假设输入无噪声，而实际场景中传感器噪声与测量误差不可避免，假设与真实数据的偏差会显著降低预测质量与不确定性估计的可靠性。

Method: 将NP框架扩展为显式建模输入不确定性的DnNP，采用变分推断训练，联合学习对受污染输入的去噪与元学习，使模型对含噪训练数据具有鲁棒性。

Result: 在合成与真实数据集上均较标准NP带来一致的预测与不确定性量化提升；在真实空气质量传感器的PM2.5空间插值任务上，RMSE最高降低30%，NLL最高降低73%。

Conclusion: 理论上有两点支撑：含噪输入下的Bayes最优预测器属于DnNP模型类；采用固定观测方差的plug-in模型存在不可消除的KL散度间隙，而DnNP可证明地避免该间隙。

Abstract: Neural Processes (NPs) are flexible meta-learning models capable of uncertainty quantification across diverse applications. However, the standard NP framework assumes access to noise-free inputs. This is unrealistic in many practical settings, where sensor noise or measurement errors are unavoidable. This discrepancy between model assumptions and real-world data can significantly degrade prediction quality and uncertainty estimates. We introduce Denoising Neural Processes (DnNPs), a principled extension of the NP framework that explicitly models input uncertainty. We train DnNPs via variational inference. DnNPs jointly learn to denoise corrupted inputs and perform meta-learning, making them robust to noisy training data. We demonstrate DnNPs' effectiveness on both synthetic and real-world datasets, showing consistent improvements in prediction and uncertainty quantification compared to standard NPs. On PM2.5 spatial interpolation from real-world air quality sensors, DnNP reduces RMSE by up to 30% and NLL by up to 73% relative to standard NPs. We also provide theoretical grounding: we show that the Bayes-optimal predictor under noisy inputs belongs to the DnNP model class, and that plug-in models with fixed observation variance incur an irreducible KL divergence gap that DnNP provably avoids.

</details>


### [207] [Quantifying the Sampling Frequency Invariance of Time-Series Foundation Models for Sensor Data Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14425)
*Sourish Gunesh Dhekane,Abinaya Kumar,Torsten Woertwein,Yan-Bo Lin,Thomas Ploetz,Shabnam Ghaffarzadegan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首次以"采样频率不变性"（SF-invariance）为核心，对4个主流时间序列基础模型（MOMENT、Chronos-2、Moirai-MoE、TimesFM）做压力测试，提出FSI、FDI、CDI三个指标，量化嵌入漂移、预测性能退化与下游任务退化，并将其刻画为（信号×模型）配对的可测量属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TSFM已广泛用于传感器场景，而不同传感器、设备与工作模式（如节能模式）导致采样频率（SF）差异显著。理想情况下，只要SF仍保留任务所需带宽，TSFM应保持有效，但现有TSFM评估并未系统检验其对SF变化的鲁棒性，"SF不变性"缺乏统一、可量化的评价框架。

Method: 对MOMENT、Chronos-2、Moirai-MoE、TimesFM四个TSFM进行压力测试，沿三个维度考察SF下降的影响：(i) 嵌入空间漂移，(ii) 预测性能退化，(iii) 下游任务性能退化；分别由三个新指标FSI（频率偏移/敏感度指标）、FDI（预测退化指标）、CDI（下游退化指标）量化。分析中引入"信号原生SF与任务所需带宽之间的裕度"这一变量，并分解SF不变性中有多少由模型选择解释。

Result: 结果刻画三点：(i) SF不变性取决于信号原生采样频率与任务所需带宽之间的裕度，而非SF数值本身；(ii) 量化了不同TSFM选择对SF不变性的解释程度（模型间差异存在但有限）；(iii) 在低SF下，TSFM性能退化中有相当部分源于信息带宽损失，而非模型能力本身的缺陷。

Conclusion: 将SF不变性重构为（信号×TSFM）配对的一个可测量、信号感知的属性，而非模型的单一固有特性；为实践者判断TSFM在何种条件下表现出SF不变性提供了依据与指导。

Abstract: Time-Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) are increasingly used in sensor applications, where sampling frequency (SF) varies across sensors, devices, and operating modes (e.g., power saving). Ideally, TSFMs must remain effective during such SF changes, provided the SF retains task-critical bandwidth. In this paper, we stress-test 4 prominent TSFMs: MOMENT, Chronos-2, Moirai-MoE, and TimesFM, to quantify their SF-invariance and investigate whether their behavior tracks the resulting loss of information bandwidth rather than the SF value itself. Our stress-test probes 3 dimensions: (i) the shift in embeddings, (ii) the degradation in forecasting performance, and (iii) the degradation in downstream performance, quantified by three novel metrics: FSI, FDI, and CDI, respectively. To our knowledge, this is the first study to place SF-invariance at the core of TSFM evaluations. Our results characterize: (i) how SF-invariance depends on the margin between signal's native-SF and the bandwidth its task requires, (ii) how much of that invariance the choice of TSFM explains, and (iii) how much of TSFM's performance degradation under a lower SF reflects information loss rather than a limitation of their abilities. These findings reframe SF-invariance as a measurable, signal-aware property of the (signal X TSFM) pair, and guide practitioners on when TSFMs display SF-invariance.

</details>


### [208] [Accurate predictions of properties of energetic materials with tabular foundation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14462)
*Vagn Krister Nes Glomsrud,Mathias Bynke,Lars Henry Berge Olsen,Dennis Christensen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将表格基础模型TabPFN用于含能材料的撞击感度、分解温度与晶体密度预测，无需调参即超越已发表最优模型及XGBoost、随机森林、高斯过程等主流机器学习方法，且速度更快并自带不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 含能材料三项关键性质（撞击感度、分解温度、晶体密度）的传统预测依赖大量超参数调优的专用模型；作者希望验证新兴的表格基础模型TabPFN是否能以零调参方式提供更准确、更快速且带不确定性量化的预测。

Method: 以TabPFN为核心预测器，输入分别采用手工分子描述符与Chemprop消息传递神经网络学习到的嵌入表示；与已发表最优模型、调优后的XGBoost、随机森林和高斯过程进行系统对比；利用TabPFN输出的完整预测分布实现不确定性量化，并与重复测量化合物的经验分布比较；最后用Shapley值对特征重要性进行解释。

Result: 三项性质预测中TabPFN均优于已发表最优模型；显著优于调优XGBoost、随机森林与高斯过程，且在手工描述符和Chemprop嵌入两种输入下结论一致；对三项性质，将TabPFN作用于Chemprop嵌入的精度甚至高于Chemprop自身预测；TabPFN是所考察模型中最快之一，比XGBoost快一个数量级以上；预测分布的不确定性量化与重复测量经验分布具有可比性。

Conclusion: TabPFN作为免调参的表格基础模型，在含能材料性质预测中兼具高精度、高速度与内置不确定性量化，优于专用模型与传统机器学习方法，配合Shapley值可提供可解释性，具备作为该类性能预测通用基线的潜力。

Abstract: We use TabPFN, a recently developed tabular foundation model, to predict three properties of energetic materials: impact sensitivity, decomposition temperature and crystal density. Without any hyperparameter tuning, TabPFN outperforms the best published models for all three properties. It also substantially outperforms a wide range of established machine-learning models, including tuned gradient boosting (XGBoost), random forests and Gaussian processes. This holds both when the models are given hand-crafted molecular descriptors and when they are given learned representations from Chemprop's message-passing neural network. In particular, for all three properties, TabPFN applied to Chemprop's own embedding is more accurate than Chemprop's predictions. TabPFN is also among the fastest models we consider, more than an order of magnitude faster than XGBoost. As TabPFN predicts the entire distribution of the target rather than just a single value, it offers built-in uncertainty quantification, which we compare with the empirical distributions of compounds with repeated measurements. Finally, we interpret the predictions using Shapley values, which rank the features by their importance.

</details>


### [209] [A Theory of Policy Optimization in Post-Training: Coverage Collapse and Reference Resets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14467)
*Audrey Huang,Nan Jiang,Sham M. Kakade,Sadhika Malladi,Akshay Krishnamurthy,Dylan J. Foster*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论角度重新审视语言模型后训练中的策略优化，提出"覆盖保持"(coverage-preserving)算法概念：其样本与梯度步数由基座模型对最优回答的覆盖度控制。作者揭示REINFORCE（PPO/GRPO基础）存在"覆盖塌缩"问题，并通过"参考重置"与近似策略镜像下降(APMD)框架，首次为PPO、GRPO及平方损失类算法（如Kimi所用）建立全局收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 策略优化是语言模型后训练的核心，但缺乏有原则的理论指导。作者希望识别出能够利用基座模型采样能力、以受控样本和梯度步数学习近最优策略的算法，从而理论上刻画"放大基座模型已有能力"这一后训练目标的实现条件。

Method: (1) 提出覆盖度与"覆盖保持"算法的定义；(2) 理论分析REINFORCE（PPO/GRPO基础）的覆盖塌缩现象，包括精确梯度下及KL正则下仍存在；(3) 提出参考重置干预，并形式化为近似策略镜像下降(APMD)元算法——近似求解一系列KL正则RL子问题；(4) 针对预训练特征上的softmax线性策略，用APMD证明PPO、GRPO变体及平方损失算法的全局收敛性。

Result: REINFORCE可将最优回答概率压低至近零（即便奖励提升），导致优化停滞，收敛可能需要指数级步数，且对精确梯度和KL正则均不鲁棒；参考重置能缓解该问题，使APMD支持更强正则、子问题更易求解且收敛至全局最优；首次给出PPO、GRPO（含显式KL正则与参考重置）及平方损失类算法的覆盖保持全局收敛保证。

Conclusion: 研究阐明了正则化、裁剪与目标函数选择如何影响后训练的采样与优化成本，为语言模型策略优化提供了原则性理论指导，并解释了参考重置等实用技巧的有效性来源。

Abstract: Policy optimization is central to language model post-training, yet principled guidance remains limited. We revisit its theoretical foundations to identify algorithms that learn near-optimal policies using total samples and gradient steps governed by the base model's response coverage, a measure of how readily it generates optimal responses. We call such algorithms coverage-preserving. This guarantee captures the goal of amplifying capabilities accessible through sampling from the base model.
  (1) Coverage collapse. REINFORCE, the basis for PPO and GRPO, can drive the probability of optimal responses to near zero, even as reward improves. This coverage collapse stalls optimization, and convergence can require exponentially many steps despite good initial coverage. The failure arises even with exact gradients and persists under KL regularization to the base model.
  (2) Reference resets. We show that repeatedly resetting the KL reference to the most recent policy mitigates coverage collapse. We formalize this intervention in Approximate Policy Mirror Descent (APMD), a meta-algorithm that approximately solves a sequence of KL-regularized RL subproblems. APMD enables stronger regularization, making each subproblem easier to solve, while reference resets allow convergence to global optimality.
  (3) Coverage-preserving algorithms. For softmax linear policies over pretrained features, we use APMD to establish the first global convergence guarantees showing that widely used families of post-training algorithms are coverage-preserving. These include stylized PPO and GRPO variants with explicit KL regularization and reference resets, and squared-loss algorithms that implement them implicitly, including those used to train the Kimi models.
  These results clarify how regularization, clipping, and objective choice affect the sampling and optimization costs of post-training.

</details>


### [210] [Off-policy Learning with Excursion Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13275)
*Jiamin He,Mark Rowland,Zhaohan Daniel Guo,Hado van Hasselt,Diana Borsa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出“excursion policies”：在行为策略数据上，先按目标策略连续执行多步“短途偏离”，再回落到行为策略，从而在离策略RL中提供一种介于偏差与方差/稳定性之间的结构化折中方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离策略RL需要权衡偏差与方差/稳定性。常见做法是在行为策略与目标策略之间做线性混合（mixture policy），但这仅覆盖了两策略插值空间中的一个点，缺乏更一般的插值机制。

Method: 定义excursion policies：智能体先按目标策略执行多步excursion，随后再切回行为策略；推导该策略类对应的动态规划结果，作为其在RL中使用的理论基础；进一步设计具有可证明正确性（sound）的离策略时序差分（TD）算法，用于excursion策略的价值估计。

Result: 在连续控制基准上，excursion策略在效率—稳定性权衡上与混合策略（mixture policies）相当；理论分析揭示了二者在行为上的概念性差异。

Conclusion: excursion policies为离策略RL提供了一种结构化的偏差—方差权衡机制，并具备动态规划与sound TD算法的理论支撑，可作为混合策略之外的有益替代方案。

Abstract: Off-policy reinforcement learning (RL) tries to learn the value of a target policy while data comes from a different behavior policy. This often requires trading off bias for variance or stability. One way to trade these off is to linearly interpolate between the behavior and target policies, but this represents only one point in a broader space of interpolation mechanisms. We introduce excursion policies--a structured alternative where the agent follows the target policy for a multi-step "excursion" before reverting to the behavior policy. We derive dynamic programming results for these policies, establishing the theoretical basis for their use in RL. We further develop provably sound off-policy temporal-difference (TD) algorithms for excursion value estimation. We find that excursion policies provide an efficiency-stability trade-off comparable to mixture policies in continuous control benchmarks, and our analysis illustrates conceptual differences in their behavior.

</details>


### [211] [TimeSeek: Multi-Task and Multi-Domain Pretraining for Generalized Time Series Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14491)
*Peng Chen,Yihang Wang,Zhongwen Rao,Yang Shu,Yuying Qiu,Yuxuan Chen,Lujia Pan,Bin Yang,Chenjuan Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TimeSeek：以"域/任务异质下的选择性信息共享"为指导思想，通过多域特征提取器与多任务协同预训练，统一了预测、异常检测与分类三类任务，在少样本与零样本场景下达到SOTA并保持良好推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）存在两点不足：1）主要靠堆叠Transformer层扩大参数量，缺乏对多域数据中复杂时序模式的自适应建模能力；2）多为单一任务（如预测或异常检测）预训练，少数多任务模型未能充分利用任务特异性与任务共性，性能次优。

Method: 提出统一分析模型TimeSeek，核心理念是域与任务异质性下的选择性信息共享。数据侧设计多域特征提取器，依据域特性自适应捕捉时间依赖与通道相关性，含渐进式多尺度建模与自适应槽位（slot）通道建模；任务侧提出多任务协同训练，通过任务定制与跨任务训练同时利用任务特异性与共性，在预训练中统一多任务。

Result: 在大规模时序数据上预训练后，TimeSeek在预测、异常检测与分类任务上于少样本和零样本设置下均取得SOTA性能，并具备良好的推理效率。

Conclusion: 通过多域自适应特征提取与多任务协同预训练实现"选择性信息共享"，可在单一模型中统一异构时序任务与多域数据，验证了兼顾任务特异性和共性的统一建模范式在泛化性与效率上的有效性。

Abstract: Time series analytics has been widely applied across various domains. While existing time series foundation models (TSFMs) have demonstrated promising results, they still face two major challenges: 1) Most TSFMs mainly rely on stacking Transformer layers to increase parameter size, lacking the ability to adaptively model complex time series patterns from multi-domain data. 2) Current TSFMs are primarily pre-trained for specific tasks, e.g., forecasting or anomaly detection, and a few models support multiple tasks, but they fail to fully leverage task specificity and commonality, resulting in suboptimal performance. To address these challenges, we propose TimeSeek, a novel generalized time series analytics model that adaptively models time series patterns from multi-domain data and unifies multiple tasks in pre-training. Conceptually, TimeSeek is guided by selective information sharing under domain and task heterogeneity. From the perspective of multi-domain data, we propose a Multi-Domain Feature Extractor that adaptively captures temporal dependencies and channel correlations based on domain-specific characteristics, including progressive multi-scale modeling and adaptive slot channel modeling. From the perspective of the diversity of tasks, we propose multi-task collaborative training, which leverages task specificity and task commonality through task customization and cross-task training, effectively unifying multiple tasks in pre-training and better adapting to diverse downstream scenarios. After pre-training on large-scale time series data, TimeSeek achieves state-of-the-art performance in forecasting, anomaly detection, and classification tasks across both few-shot learning and zero-shot situations, together with favorable inference efficiency.

</details>


### [212] [Emergent Biological Capabilities in a Foundation Model for Molecular Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14504)
*Adi Mashiach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Bonbon 是一个基于约 3.2 万亿 token 蛋白-配体配对序列训练的多模态生物学生成式基础模型，仅用序列、无任务监督与结构坐标即可零样本涌现功能、残基与键三个层级的能力，并在湿实验中达到 61.5% 剂量响应命中率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将"大规模无标注语料训练→涌现能力"的范式从语言/视觉迁移到多模态生物学，探索分子相互作用领域能否仅凭未标注的蛋白-配体配对序列获得可泛化的零样本能力，并检验基于损失的缩放律是否足以预测涌现能力出现的时机。

Method: 在约 3.2 万亿 token 的蛋白-配体配对序列上训练 Bonbon，不使用任何任务特定监督，也不使用结构坐标；在单一表示空间中评估三个分辨率层级的零样本能力：功能层（作用机制 MoA）、残基层（结合位点定位，含正构/别构判别）、键层（共价/非共价结合）；在 42M–1.6B 共六个模型规模上做受控缩放实验；参数-词元比设为 1:2,000 以适配配对交互数据的稀疏性；湿实验在 7 个蛋白家族、9 个疾病靶点上进行前瞻性验证。

Result: 三项能力均可泛化到分布外复合物；在连续指标上，所有能力均在最大规模处出现陡峭跃升，而预训练损失平滑下降；冻结表示在结合与亲和力预测上达到 SOTA，速度比共折叠方法快约五个数量级；湿实验中对 de novo 化合物取得 61.5% 剂量响应命中率，得到此前未被报道可抑制这些蛋白的结构新颖分子，包括分布外靶点。

Conclusion: 分子相互作用的涌现能力可由纯序列预训练获得，但仅凭基于损失的缩放律不足以预测其出现；受数据稀疏性限制需采用远高于常规的参数-词元比，且该范式在计算效率与前瞻性湿实验有效性上均得到验证。

Abstract: Foundation models trained on large unlabeled corpora develop emergent capabilities, tasks that go beyond the training objective. We extend this paradigm to multimodal biology with Bonbon, a foundation model for molecular interactions trained on approximately 3.2 trillion tokens of paired protein-ligand sequences. From a single representation space, Bonbon exhibits three zero-shot emergent capabilities at hierarchical levels of resolution: functional (mechanism of action), residue (binding site localization, including orthosteric/allosteric discrimination), and bond (covalent versus non-covalent engagement). All three capabilities generalize to out-of-distribution complexes. Bonbon was trained on protein-ligand sequences alone, without any task-specific supervision and without structural coordinates. We conducted controlled scaling experiments across six model scales from 42M to 1.6B parameters; every capability evaluated shows a sharp jump at the largest scale on continuous metrics, even as pretraining loss decreases smoothly. This demonstrates that loss-based scaling laws alone are insufficient to predict when zero-shot emergent capabilities become available. The capabilities reported here emerge after training at a 1:2,000 parameter-to-token ratio to accommodate the sparsity of paired biological interaction data. The same frozen representations achieve state-of-the-art binding and affinity prediction at five orders of magnitude greater speed than co-folding methods. In prospective wet-lab validation across nine disease targets spanning seven protein families, Bonbon achieves a 61.5% dose-response hit rate on de novo compounds, yielding structurally novel molecules not previously reported to inhibit these proteins, including for out-of-distribution targets.

</details>


### [213] [Continual-ARC: Measuring Skill Acquisition over a Working Lifetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14505)
*Daniel Lenton,Yusha Arif,Haris Mahmood*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Continual-ARC 终身学习基准，用 ARC-AGI 三版任务流在同一尺度上比较基于记忆与基于权重的持续学习系统，并发现 harness 持久化几乎无用、多数 harness 方法劣于简单缓存基线，而权重学习在未见任务上有显著收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习基准存在三点局限：通常只覆盖单一应用领域；每个实例只被服务一次，无法考察任务"休眠后重现"；假定单一学习者家族，导致基于记忆（agent harness、上下文缓存）与基于权重（参数更新）的系统难以在同一尺度上公平比较。

Method: 构建 Continual-ARC 数据流：任务取自三届 ARC-AGI，无需领域知识；同一任务在休眠间隔后以新实例重现，同时不断有新任务加入。学习者对每个实例可选择付费获取示例、提交答案或在 harness 内行动，错误提交可重试但需付费，得分由该实例所消耗的反馈预算比例决定。学习者可跨实例保留任意信息，使记忆型与权重型方法同尺度计分。流结束后冻结学习器，在新实例上测记忆（已见任务）与泛化（未见任务）。实验：冻结权重的 agent harness（有/无持久化）跑于 Qwen3.5-9B 与 GLM-5.3 Flash，对照两个提示基线、一个程序归纳方法与一个用 GLM-5.3 Flash 模仿训练的 Qwen3.5-9B 权重模型。

Result: harness 的持久化通常不能提升记忆表现，除非给出显式的专家指导；在所有测试模型上，多数基于 harness 的方法学习效果仍不如"直接缓存每个任务示例"的简单基线；没有任何 harness 方法在未见任务上获得增益，而基于权重的学习表现出实质性提升。

Conclusion: harness 与测试时参数学习均未在公平基准下展现出对持续学习的可靠优势；作者据此指出未来方向——基于权重的持续学习可教会模型在新领域中驱动 harness，从而将权重学习与 harness 机制结合。

Abstract: Agent harnesses have emerged as a promising mechanism for continual learning, alongside test-time parametric learning. However, the current benchmarks have several limitations: they generally focus on a single applied domain, serve each instance only once, and assume a single family of learner, so memory-based and weight-based systems are rarely compared on the same scale. We propose Continual-ARC, a lifelong stream built from the three ARC-AGI editions, whose tasks need no domain knowledge. The same tasks return after dormant gaps with fresh instances, while new tasks keep arriving. On each instance, the learner may pay for demonstrations, submit answers, or act inside the harness, with retries permitted on incorrect submissions, which are paid for. Each instance is scored based on the share of this feedback budget consumed. Since a learner may keep anything between instances, memory-based and weight-based systems are scored on the same scale. After the stream, the learner is frozen and tested on fresh instances of the tasks it met, which measures memorization, and on unseen tasks, which measures generalization. We run an agent harness with frozen weights, with and without persistence, on Qwen3.5-9B and GLM-5.3 Flash, comparing them with two prompting floors, a program-induction method and Qwen3.5-9B's weights trained to imitate GLM-5.3 Flash. Persistence in the harness generally does not improve memorization unless provided with explicit expert guidance. Most harness-based methods still learn less than a floor that simply caches each task's demonstrations, across all models we tested. No harness-based method gains on unseen tasks, whereas weight-based learning shows substantial gains. This opens the door to future weight-based continual learning that could teach models to drive harnesses in new domains.

</details>


### [214] [When Are Predictions Enough? An Evaluation Protocol for Frozen Expert Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13356)
*Alessandro Pereira,Keith J Ransom,Lewis Mitchell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出可复用的公平评估协议，比较"融合特征"与"融合预测"两类专家组合方式。在DF40的21个冻结deepfake检测器上，留一生成器评估下，对6个架构多样专家的特征做逻辑回归，宏AUROC比最佳预测融合器高0.081（8个生成器中7个提升）；更大网络无额外收益，说明信息丰富度比融合器容量更关键；削弱预测基线会使特征优势虚高至0.172。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实践中常将独立训练的模型以冻结权重方式通过最终预测进行组合，但这些专家的内部特征可能含有预测所遗漏的有用信息。作者追问：特征融合何时优于预测融合，以及如何使二者比较公平——现有结论可能被评估选择（弱基线、数据重叠）所扭曲。

Method: 提出可复用评估协议并应用于DF40的21个冻结deepfake检测器，同时审计评估选择对结论的影响。协议要点：①使用强预测基线（而非弱基线）；②对比简单线性（逻辑回归，约10K参数）与更大特征融合器（约3.5M参数）；③分别变化架构与训练数据；④按留出生成器报告结果并施加训练/测试重叠控制（leave-one-generator-out）。

Result: 留一生成器评估下，逻辑回归特征融合较每折最佳预测融合器宏AUROC提升0.081，8个生成器中7个受益。更大网络（约3.5M）相对线性模型（约10K）无明显提升，表明在该专家池上获取更丰富信息比增加融合容量更重要。削弱重叠污染的标准划分上的预测基线，会使表观特征优势虚高最多0.172 AUROC；重叠感知评估也改变增益估计，但该对比未能单独隔离重叠效应。分生成器与专家池结果展示了优势成立、减弱或反转的位置。

Conclusion: 当特征保留了预测所遗漏且能迁移到新生成器的信息时，特征融合有益；当最强预测基线本身表现良好时，预测融合可能已足够；两者均无法可靠弥补专家迁移性差的问题。缺少这些控制时，同一基准可支持不同结论，故需以强基线、重叠感知、留一生成器的协议进行公平比较。

Abstract: Practitioners often combine independently trained models while keeping their weights frozen. These experts are commonly combined through their final predictions, although their internal features may retain useful information that those predictions omit. We ask when combining features improves on combining predictions, and how to make that comparison fair. We introduce a reusable evaluation protocol, apply it to 21 frozen deepfake detectors from DF40 (Yan et al., 2024), and audit how evaluation choices change the conclusions. The protocol uses strong prediction baselines, compares simple and larger feature combiners, varies architecture and training data separately, and reports results by held-out generator with train/test overlap controls. Under leave-one-generator-out evaluation, logistic regression on the features of six architecture-diverse experts improves macro AUROC by 0.081 over the best tested prediction combiner in each fold, with gains on seven of eight generators. The larger network shows no clear improvement over the linear model (approximately 3.5M versus 10K parameters), suggesting that access to richer information matters more than additional combiner capacity on this pool. Features may help when they retain information that the predictions omit and that transfers to new generators. Predictions may be enough when the strongest prediction baseline already performs well, while neither approach reliably compensates for poor expert transfer. Weakening prediction baselines on the overlap-affected standard split inflates the apparent feature advantage by up to 0.172 AUROC. Overlap-aware evaluation also changes the estimated gain, though the contrast does not isolate overlap. Results by generator and expert pool show where the advantage holds, weakens, or reverses. Without these controls, the same benchmark can support different conclusions.

</details>


### [215] [When Does the Real Wiring Win? Testing connectome structure-function claims against unmeasured physiology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14508)
*Yeyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出，用"打乱/重连连线组与真实连线组"的模型比较来论证"结构决定功能"存在系统偏差，原因是连线固定了谁连谁，却未固定兴奋性、增益、可塑性等生理参数，建模者常把真实连线模型调好而把零模型欠调。作者将其形式化为三种估计量（P1：真实连线调优的生理迁移到零模型；P2：各连线各自调优；P3：P2 但生理须与独立数据一致），用合成真值、安慰剂零模型与三个果蝇回路模型量化偏差对生物学结论的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连线组约束模型（connectome-constrained models）被广泛用来支持"测量到的连接图优于打乱连接图，故结构塑造功能"这一结论。但此类比较混淆了连线与调参：连线只决定谁连谁，不决定兴奋性、增益或可塑性速率，而这些参数往往被针对真实连线反复调优，导致零模型天然处于欠调状态。已有机器学习中众所周知的偏差（欠调基线、继承权重、赢家诅咒）在此类生物学推断中的实际影响尚不清楚，且缺少能区分"接线缺陷"与"模型缺陷"的方法框架。此外，若结论依赖于调参协议，则可能把模型不适定性（model inadequacy）误读为生物体连线的缺陷。

Method: 1) 提出三种可分离的估计量：P1 = 在真实连线上调优生理参数后原样迁移至零模型（生理固定、接线变）；P2 = 每种接线各自独立调优（接线与生理共变）；P3 = P2 但生理参数须落在独立数据支持的范围内。2) 明确三类偏差来源：欠调基线（under-tuned baselines）、继承权重（inherited weights）、赢家诅咒（winner's curse）。3) 采用三重验证：合成真值（synthetic ground truth）、安慰剂零模型（placebo nulls，即已知无差异的对照）与三个果蝇（Drosophila）回路模型（含蘑菇体 Kenyon 细胞输出、头部方向系统、flyvis）。4) 统计上使用 95% 置信区间、held-out 重跑与不同倍数重训练（10 倍、50 倍）检验排序稳健性；并分析 alpha'beta' 细胞在活跃 Kenyon 细胞中所占比例对结论的调控作用。

Result: 部分结论在三种协议下均成立：奖赏与惩罚多巴胺通路分离可提升学习表现；头部方向拓扑结构是维持持续性 bump（活动包）的必要条件。另一些结论则不稳定。在蘑菇体中，真实 Kenyon 细胞输出接线在 P1 与 P2 下比"跨亚型身份打乱"接线好 0.09，但在 P3 下反而差 0.09（95% CI 均不含零，held-out 重跑证实）。结论由 alpha'beta' 细胞在活跃 Kenyon 细胞中的占比决定：P1 选出的比例偏低；在成像暗示的约 44% 比例下，真实接线模型无法学习，说明这是模型不适定性，而非果蝇连线的缺陷。在 flyvis 中，P1 会惩罚镜像连线组，而这种构造性解的中位误差仅比真实高出 0.07，且优于空间打乱连线组于 50/50 个成员；该排序在十倍长重训练下仍保持，但在五十倍时依赖于检查点（checkpoint）。

Conclusion: "真实连线优于打乱连线"这一常被引用的结论在很大程度上是评价协议的产物，而非结构-功能的可靠证据：协议选择（生理是否迁移、是否受数据约束）可以反转符号（+0.09 到 −0.09），并使镜像连线组被误判为劣于空间打乱。可靠的结构性论断需要三项条件同时满足：匹配的优化（matched optimisation）、数据约束的生理参数（data-constrained physiology）、以及在该约束下仍然可工作的模型（避免模型不适定性被误读为连线缺陷）。作者建议报告多估计量与置信区间、对零模型做等量调优，并用独立生理数据检验模型可行性。

Abstract: Connectome-constrained models are often used to argue that a measured wiring diagram outperforms shuffled or rewired wiring, and hence that structure shapes function. Such comparisons can confound wiring with how the models are tuned: a connectome fixes who connects to whom, not the excitability, gains or plasticity rates the model also needs, and modellers often tune these until the real-wiring model works. We separate three estimands: physiology tuned on the real wiring and transferred to nulls (P1), each wiring tuned on its own (P2), and P2 restricted to physiology consistent with independent data (P3). The biases involved are known in machine learning (under-tuned baselines, inherited weights, winner's curse); we measure what they do to biological conclusions, using synthetic ground truth, placebo nulls and three Drosophila circuit models. Some claims survive every protocol we test: segregated reward and punishment dopamine improves learning, and head-direction topography is required for a persistent bump. Others do not. In the mushroom body, the real Kenyon-cell output wiring beats a cross-subtype identity shuffle by 0.09 under P1 and P2 but loses to it by 0.09 under P3 (95% CIs exclude zero; held-out runs confirm). The verdict is set by the share of alpha'beta' cells among active Kenyon cells, which P1 selects low; at the share implied by imaging (about 44%) the real-wiring model fails to learn, exposing a model inadequacy rather than a deficit of the fly's wiring. In flyvis, P1 penalises a mirrored connectome, which has a constructed solution within 0.07 (median) of the real error, more than a spatially shuffled one in 50 of 50 members; the ranking survives ten-fold longer retraining but depends on the checkpoint at fifty-fold. A credible structural claim needs matched optimisation, data-constrained physiology and a model that still works there.

</details>


### [216] [Pyrite: Building and Benchmarking Protein-Ligand Pose Ranking Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14511)
*Megha Srivastava,Michael H. Lim,Allison M. Keys,Pranav Murugan,Nate Gruver*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Pyrite——一个含1500对构象对的蛋白-配体成对姿态排序基准，并配套结构化表示PyriteRep与两个LLM智能体（PyriteReasoner直接文本推理、PyriteFeaturizer生成可执行代码构建特征+逻辑回归），后者准确率达74%，超过专家接近随机水平的表现，且LLM引导的特征发现优于直接推理，GRPO可使开源模型追平或超越闭源前沿模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代共折叠（cofolding）模型能生成多样的候选配体姿态，但如何可靠地对这些候选进行排序仍是难题；同时缺乏一个专门的、有挑战性的成对姿态排序评测基准，且不清楚现有化学信号与专家判断的实际有效性。

Method: 构建Pyrite成对姿态排序基准（1500对姿态对，覆盖多样蛋白-配体复合物）；以具备3D可视化与物理打分的化学专家作为人类基线；提出PyriteRep结构化表示（分子几何、蛋白-配体相互作用、物理化学打分）；开发两个LLM智能体：PyriteReasoner进行基于文本的直接比较推理，PyriteFeaturizer生成可执行代码以构建特征并训练逻辑回归（可选融合LLM子调用派生特征）；并使用GRPO对开源权重模型进行优化。

Result: 专家表现接近随机水平，凸显现有化学信号解读之难；PyriteReasoner与PyriteFeaturizer分别达到最高约67%与74%准确率，超过专家表现；LLM引导的特征发现总体优于直接推理；GRPO显著提升开源权重模型，使其在评测中追平或超越闭源前沿模型。

Conclusion: Pyrite确立了姿态排序作为一项具挑战性的能力测试，并表明LLM引导的特征发现是解释与整合计算化学工具输出的一条有前景路径。

Abstract: Predicting how a ligand binds to its target is central to small-molecule drug discovery. Modern cofolding models can generate diverse candidate poses, but reliably ranking these candidates remains challenging. We introduce Pyrite, a pairwise pose-ranking benchmark comprising 1,500 pose pairs across diverse protein-ligand complexes. Expert chemists equipped with 3D visualization tools and physics-based scores perform near chance, highlighting the difficulty of interpreting available chemical signals. To investigate automated ranking, we introduce PyriteRep, a structured representation of molecular geometry, protein-ligand interactions, and physicochemical scores, and develop two LLM-based agents: PyriteReasoner, which directly compares poses through text-based reasoning, and PyriteFeaturizer, which generates executable code to construct features for logistic regression, optionally incorporating features derived from LLM subcalls. These agents achieve up to 67% and 74% accuracy, respectively, exceeding observed expert performance. LLM-guided feature discovery generally outperforms direct reasoning, while Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) improves open-weight models, enabling them to match or surpass closed-source frontier models in our evaluations. These results establish Pyrite as a challenging test of pose-ranking ability and highlight LLM-guided feature discovery as a promising approach to interpret and combine the outputs of computational chemistry tools.

</details>


### [217] [AutoScaffold: AI-Guided Scaffolding for Reinforcement Learning of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14515)
*Zijian Zhang,Rizhen Hu,Jiangweizhi Peng,Shiyang Li,Sujay Bhatt,Sihan Zeng,Mingyi Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AutoScaffold：用冻结教师模型周期性分析学生RLVR训练经验，自动生成并动态调整提示脚手架（跨任务策略+单任务提示），评估时移除脚手架，在ALFWorld、检索式QA、数学推理、GPU内核生成四个域均优于对应RL基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR的outcome reward把每次尝试压缩成单一标量，浪费了丰富的训练经验信息。训练期脚手架（策略、提示）是把这些信息转化为指导的自然手段，但现有设计依赖人工、需领域知识且针对特定任务定制，缺乏统一、自动化的框架。

Method: 提出统一AI-for-AI框架AutoScaffold：冻结的教师模型定期检查学生的训练经验，诊断瓶颈，并生成两类脚手架——(i)在一类任务间共享的策略，(ii)针对单个困难任务的提示；同时根据学生学习进展调整支持的内容与力度。学生自监督学习于带脚手架的提示上，评估阶段完全移除脚手架以检验真实能力。

Result: 无脚手架评估下，四域均超越对应RL基线：ALFWorld成功率+7.2点，检索式QA平均精确匹配+4.0点；GPU内核生成中"生成正确且快于PyTorch参考实现的内核"比例最高+5.0点；数学推理以约1/3的学生训练步数超过QuestA报告的准确率。

Conclusion: 分析表明教师在每类任务上识别出不同失败模式，并随学生进步把脚手架从分步工作流转向针对性修补；更新后的脚手架比沿用旧脚手架或无脚手架带来更多学习收益，证明训练经验可被自动转化为有效的自适应指导。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) produces rich training experience, yet outcome rewards reduce each attempt to a single number and leave most of this information unused. Training-time scaffolds, such as strategies or hints added to the prompt during training, offer a natural way to turn this information into guidance, but their design and adaptation are largely left to humans and tailored to specific domains. We introduce AutoScaffold, a unified AI-for-AI framework that turns a student's training experience into adaptive scaffolds, with minimal domain-specific information and no human intervention during training. A frozen teacher periodically examines this experience, diagnoses bottlenecks, and designs scaffolds in two forms: (i) strategies shared across a class of tasks and (ii) hints for individual hard tasks, adjusting the content and amount of support as the student learns. The scaffolds are removed at evaluation. We apply the same framework to ALFWorld, search-based QA, mathematical reasoning, and GPU kernel generation. When evaluated without scaffolds, students trained with AutoScaffold outperform those trained with the corresponding RL baselines in all four domains, with gains of 7.2 points in ALFWorld success and 4.0 points in average QA exact match. On GPU kernel generation, AutoScaffold increases the fraction of tasks with a correct kernel that runs faster than the PyTorch reference by up to 5.0 points. On math, our student surpasses QuestA's reported accuracy using approximately one-third as many student training steps. Analyses show that the teacher identifies a different failure in each task class and shifts its scaffolds from step-by-step workflows to targeted fixes as the student improves, and that its revised scaffolds help the student learn more than keeping the previous scaffold or using no scaffold.

</details>


### [218] [TorchCurves: Differentiable Parametric Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14516)
*Alex Shtoff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TorchCurves是一个PyTorch库，将向量值参数曲线设为可学习原语：把标量输入批次映射为B样条或Legendre多项式基下的曲线上的点，并可对输入与系数求导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有曲线/基函数计算多依赖NumPy/SciPy等非自动微分工具，难以与深度模型联合训练；缺少将曲线求值作为可微分可学习原语、兼具可读性与向量化实现的通用库。

Method: 核心算子将成批标量输入映射到B样条或Legendre基表示的曲线点，支持对输入与系数双向自动微分；系数可与外围模型联合优化或由另一网络生成。提供可训练模块、可复用基模块与函数式接口；实现上结合经典求值算法与PyTorch向量化操作，不使用自定义硬件内核，强调可读性与可维护性。

Result: 在不同问题规模下，将曲线求值性能与NumPy、SciPy对比，KAN训练与EfficientKAN对比，展示了该通用性在何种规模下计算上仍实用、在何处开销显著。

Conclusion: TorchCurves为曲线作为深度学习原语提供了可微、模块化、易维护的开源工具（GitHub: alexshtf/torchcurves），适用于连续特征嵌入、Kolmogorov-Arnold网络与函数型回归等场景，但其通用性带来的性能开销在特定规模下不可忽视。

Abstract: We present TorchCurves, a PyTorch library that treats vector-valued parametric curves as learnable primitives. Its central operation maps batches of scalar inputs to points on curves represented in B-spline or Legendre polynomial bases, with differentiation through both inputs and coefficients. Curve coefficients can be optimized jointly with the surrounding model or produced by another network. The library provides trainable modules, reusable basis modules, and a functional interface, supporting applications ranging from continuous feature embeddings to Kolmogorov-Arnold networks and functional regression. Its implementation combines classical evaluation algorithms with vectorized PyTorch operations, emphasizing readability and maintainability without custom hardware kernels. Timing experiments compare curve evaluation against NumPy and SciPy and KAN training against EfficientKAN across a range of problem sizes. The results illustrate where this generality remains computationally practical and where overhead is substantial. Library is available on GitHub at https://github.com/alexshtf/torchcurves.

</details>


### [219] [Mixed Signals: Weight-Space Programming for EEG and ECG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13758)
*Dara Varam,Taraneh Aminosharieh Najafi,David Atienza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将"权重空间编程"（weight-space programming）这一范式的适用范围从游戏网络与扩散模型拓展到生物信号模型（EEG/ECG），证明前沿语言模型可在完全不给梯度下降的情况下，仅凭架构、任务与少量受试者数据直接"写出"网络全部权重，并能无监督地定位与修复模型中植入的特定缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者动机在于检验"通过推理而非梯度下降来完成训练"这一设想是否具有跨领域的普适性。此前权重空间编程的验证集中于游戏博弈网络、语言模型的特定失效修复与扩散模型，尚不清楚它能否处理生物信号这类低信噪比、个体差异大、且模型体量较小（适合边缘部署）的任务。若成立，将为可穿戴/边缘设备上的模型定制与调试提供一条不依赖大规模训练流程的新路径，因此需要系统评估其能力边界与失效模式。

Method: 实验在EEG与ECG数据训练的网络展开，并设计两类任务：（1）调试任务——在生物信号模型权重中植入特定故障（fault），在不告知故障类型的前提下要求语言模型定位并修复；（2）从零写权重任务——仅提供网络架构、任务描述与极少量训练受试者的数据，由语言模型直接生成整张网络的全部权重，过程中不执行任何梯度下降步骤。评测以梯度下降训练的网络与经典手工特征EEG流水线（alpha频带功率；CSP + LDA）为基线，考察分类表现、所需受试者数量，以及模型从权重反推训练任务的能力。

Result: 主要结果有三：其一，前沿模型能在未被提示故障类型的情况下调试出植入生物信号模型中的具体故障；其二，语言模型可仅凭架构、任务与少量受试者从零写出全部权重，在一组实验中仅用6倍更少的训练受试者即达到与标准梯度下降训练相当的性能；其三，Claude Opus 5.5 所"写"出的网络在同样仅用5名受试者构建时，性能超过 alpha 频带功率与 CSP+LDA 等经典手工 EEG 流水线。局限方面：语言模型无法继续提升已经训练良好的分类模型，也难以仅凭权重可靠识别网络原先训练的任务。

Conclusion: 结论认为，权重空间编程对小规模网络（尤其是部署于边缘设备与可穿戴设备上的模型）具有实用潜力，但在成为可落地实践之前仍需进一步研究：其当前能力受限于"锦上添花"式改进的缺失与任务推断能力的不足，说明该方法更适合作为低数据条件下的模型构建与调试工具，而非替代梯度下降的通用训练手段。

Abstract: Weight-space programming allows language models to read a trained neural network's weights and edit them. This opens the door to ``training'' by reasoning rather than through gradient descent. Prior work has demonstrated its functionality and promise for game-playing networks and fixing specific failures in language models, even extending to diffusion models. In this work, we present its applicability to models trained on biosignal data, specifically EEG and ECG data. We show that frontier models are not only able to debug specific faults planted in biosignal-based models without being told what these faults are, but are even capable of writing all the weights of a network from scratch, given only the architecture, the task, and a minimal set of training subjects. In particular, in one set of experiments, a language model was able to match standard gradient-descent training using $6\times$ fewer training subjects, through reasoning alone and without any gradient descent step. Networks written by Claude Opus 5.5 also scored higher than classic hand-crafted EEG pipelines (alpha band power, and common spatial patterns with linear discriminant analysis) when built from the same 5 subjects. We also highlight some of the limitations, which include the inability of language models to improve already well-trained classification models, and their incapacity to reliably recognize, from the weights alone, the task a network was trained on. Overall, our results indicate that weight-space programming can be useful for small networks, such as those deployed on edge devices and wearables, but the concept still requires further investigation before it can be used in practice.

</details>


### [220] [Forward-Value Discretization: Faster Stochastic Diffusion Sampling Without High-Order Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14328)
*Yuchen Jiao,Na Li,Changxiao Cai,Yuting Wei,Gen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文证明随机扩散采样器即使采用高阶离散化仍是收敛阶，并据此提出无需训练的 forward-value discretization 框架，在低 NFE 预算下提升采样质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机扩散采样器达到相同质量所需 NFE 显著多于确定性采样器；虽有高阶离散化加速确定性采样的经验，但其对随机采样的收益不清楚，作者希望厘清该问题并弥合二者的效率差距。

Method: 先建立匹配的上界与最坏情形下界，证明即使 score 精确，随机采样器配高阶近似仍为一阶收敛。进而提出 forward-value discretization：用先前预测构造 lookahead 估计点来评估数据/噪声预测模型。确定性版本 F-DPMSolver 采用 DDIM 变体；随机版本 F-DDPM 在按后续噪声注入校正后的更干净 lookahead 点评估模型，并复用上一次模型预测与上一次注入的噪声，每步仅一次模型评估且具一阶收敛保证。

Result: 在 CIFAR-10、ImageNet、FFHQ、LSUN 上，匹配 NFE 预算时 F-DDPM 的 FID 一致低于所评估的随机基线；当 NFE 为确定性基线的 1.5 倍时达到与之竞争的质量。确定性方面 F-DPMSolver 在低 NFE 预算下改善样本质量。

Conclusion: 提高近似阶数并不能普遍提升随机采样的收敛阶；forward-value discretization 是一种免训练、单次评估/步且具一阶收敛保证的实用方案，有效缩小了随机与确定性采样的实际效率差距。

Abstract: Stochastic diffusion samplers often require substantially more neural function evaluations (NFEs) than deterministic samplers to achieve comparable sample quality. Although higher-order discretization accelerates deterministic sampling, its benefits for stochastic sampling remains unclear. We establish matching upper and worst-case lower bounds showing that stochastic samplers with higher-order approximations remain first-order convergent, even with exact score functions. Thus, increasing the approximation order does not generally improve the convergence order. Motivated by this limitation, we introduce forward-value discretization, a training-free framework that evaluates the data/noise prediction model at a lookahead estimate constructed from previous predictions. For deterministic sampling, the resulting Forward DPMSolver (F-DPMSolver), improves sample quality at low NFE budgets through its DDIM-based variant, despite remaining first-order convergent. For stochastic sampling, we develop Forward DDPM (F-DDPM), which evaluates the model at a cleaner lookahead point adjusted for subsequent noise injection. This estimate reuses both the previous model prediction and the previously injected noise. Both variants admit first-order convergence guarantees with one model evaluation per iteration. Across CIFAR-10, ImageNet, FFHQ, and LSUN, F-DDPM consistently achieves lower FID than the evaluated stochastic baselines at matched NFE budgets. With 1.5 times as many NFEs as deterministic baselines, it achieves competitive sample quality, narrowing the practical efficiency gap between stochastic and deterministic sampling. Code is available at https://github.com/Na-Li66/F-DDPM.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [221] [Maze Runner: Autonomous Maze Navigation with Line-Based Localization and Control Barrier Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12949)
*Alvaro J. Gaona,Lucas Gómez-Velayos,Peter Pasuy-Quevedo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Maze Runner：面向迷宫导航的开源 C++/ROS 2 自主导航栈，集成 RANSAC+TLS 直线特征感知、EKF 定位与 EKF-SLAM、栅格 A*/Hybrid A* 规划、NMPC+CBF 安全控制，仿真中完成 135 m 路线，定位 RMSE 1.0 cm（已知地图）/24 cm（SLAM），规划 6 ms，NMPC 平均 11.7 ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迷宫场景具有几何稀疏且高度重复、走廊狭窄、地图先验可能不可靠等特点，对定位、规划与安全控制提出耦合性挑战；现有工作多只解决其中单一环节，缺少端到端、可复现的完整自主栈。

Method: 1) 感知：对每帧 2D LiDAR 扫描用 RANSAC 提取墙线，TLS 精化并给出逐线不确定性；2) 定位：同一线特征前端驱动两种估计器——基于迷宫网格构建线地图的 EKF，以及在线建图的 EKF-SLAM 变体；3) 规划：在膨胀占据栅格上在线运行 A* 加视线捷径，或前向式 Hybrid A*；4) 控制：NMPC 跟踪轨迹，并在世界坐标系 LiDAR 点上施加离散时间 CBF 约束，保证与墙体及未建图障碍的安全间距；5) 工程：算法封装为无 ROS 依赖、带单元测试的核心库，整体在单一可复现环境中构建运行（Gazebo 中 Husarion ROSbot XL）。

Result: 仿真中机器人完成 135 m 迷宫路线；对已知地图定位 RMSE 为 1.0 cm，SLAM 模式下为 24 cm；规划器 6 ms 返回路径；NMPC 平均求解 11.7 ms，且从未超出 80 ms 时间预算。

Conclusion: 以"线特征统一前端 + 双估计器 + 栅格规划 + NMPC/CBF 安全层"的模块化设计，可在资源受限条件下实现高精度定位与实时安全导航，验证了端到端开源栈在结构化室内迷宫中的可行性；未来可扩展至真实平台与三维/动态环境。

Abstract: Maze navigation requires localization from sparse, repetitive geometry, planning through narrow corridors, and control that stays safe when the estimate or the map is wrong. We present Maze Runner, an open autonomy stack in C++ and ROS 2 that addresses these needs end to end on a simulated Husarion ROSbot XL with a 2D LiDAR in Gazebo. Perception extracts wall lines from each scan with RANSAC and total least squares, with per-line uncertainty. The same line front end feeds two estimators: an Extended Kalman Filter (EKF) that localizes against a line map built from the maze mesh, and an EKF SLAM variant that builds the line map online. Planning runs online on an inflated occupancy grid with A* plus line-of-sight shortcutting, or with a forward-only Hybrid A*. A nonlinear Model Predictive Controller (NMPC) tracks the plan while discrete-time Control Barrier Functions (CBFs) on world-frame LiDAR points keep the robot clear of walls and unmapped obstacles. The algorithms live in a ROS-free, unit-tested core library, and the whole stack builds and runs from a single reproducible environment. In simulation, the robot drives a 135 m route through the maze with 1.0 cm localization RMSE against the known map and 24 cm in SLAM mode, the planner returns a path in 6 ms, and the NMPC solves in 11.7 ms on average, never exceeding its 80 ms budget.

</details>


### [222] [CAFE: Closed-Loop Autonomous Task Formulation and Execution without Instance-Specific Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12983)
*Mingjia Chen,Xuewen Zhao,Philip Dames,Qi Mao,Fei Xie,Jun Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 CAFE 框架，在预设清洁/安全/收纳三类范畴下实现"场景驱动"的任务自主生成与执行：以在线记忆结合 Trigger-LLM 与 Draft-Complete-Audit 三 LLM 流程自动形成目标并执行，在 12 个 AI2-THOR 场景中取得更高 GCR 得分，并通过消融与真机轨迹验证各模块作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人自主性不仅需要执行任务，还需从观测中自行选择任务；而现有设定多依赖实例级目标或预先给定的动作序列，缺乏在类别约束下由场景自主生成任务并闭环执行、修正的能力。

Method: 构建 CAFE 框架：在线维护物体、功能区域与执行状态的记忆；由规则引导的 Trigger-LLM 依据累积证据判定是否触发任务形成；Draft、Complete、Audit 三个 LLM 分别负责提出目标、补全执行细节、检查依赖关系；执行结果回写记忆以支持纠错与后续发现。在 12 个 AI2-THOR 场景、3 种骨干模型、多个任务复杂度层级上，用覆盖率、约束满足率与执行指标评估，并进行完成/审计/区域/反馈/触发/提示示例等控制实验及 LimoCobot 真机轨迹展示。

Result: Tri-LLM 在三种骨干模型和各任务复杂度层级上 GCR 得分均高于所评测基线；控制实验验证完成、审计、功能区域、反馈、触发机制与提示示例各自的贡献；LimoCobot 轨迹展示了收纳、清理及抓取失败后的恢复过程。

Conclusion: 共享场景状态可作为连接任务发现与任务执行的枢纽，并通过执行结果驱动目标修订，为无实例级目标的场景驱动机器人自主性提供可行范式；未来可进一步扩展到更开放的类别与真实环境。

Abstract: Scene-driven robot autonomy requires selecting tasks from observations as well as executing them. We study scene-driven task formulation and execution under predefined cleaning, safety, and storage/organization categories, without instance-specific goals or action sequences. CAFE maintains online memory of objects, functional zones, and execution states. A rule-guided Trigger-LLM admits formulation from accumulated evidence; Draft, Complete, and Audit propose goals, resolve execution details, and check dependencies. Outcomes update memory for correction and subsequent discovery. Evaluation in 12 AI2-THOR scenes compares three backbones and task-complexity tiers using coverage, constraint, and execution metrics. Tri-LLM records higher GCR scores than the evaluated baselines across three backbones and task-complexity tiers. Controls examine completion, auditing, zones, feedback, triggering, and prompt examples. LimoCobot traces illustrate storage, cleanup, and recovery from a failed grasp. The study examines how shared scene state connects task discovery to execution and outcome-driven revision.

</details>


### [223] [RoboACE: Adversarial Co-Evolution for Robot Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13034)
*Delin Zhao,Zhengrong Yue,Junlin He,Yali Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RoboACE框架，在冻结基础模型外围协同进化"课程harness"与"执行harness"，实现可修订的练习生成与执行能力扩展，在四个LIBERO系列评测上超越被评估的控制器与智能体系统（LIBERO-PRO达74.0%成功率）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人智能体可从执行反馈中改进可复用技能、规划流程与恢复策略，但固定的练习来源使"新弱点搜索"停滞；有效练习取决于智能体当前能力，因此选择练习的机制本身也必须可修订——即课程与执行能力需要协同演化。

Method: RoboACE采用双harness交替进化：课程harness基于累积的能力证据提议有信息量的挑战；执行harness以可复用修订（reusable revisions）作出响应以扩展执行能力。训练时一侧固定、另一侧更新（交替优化），被接纳的挑战形成固定快照，用于公平比较不同执行修订。基础模型保持冻结。

Result: 以LIBERO的小规模练习条件为起点，所得冻结部署在四个LIBERO系列面板上优于所评估的控制器与智能体系统；在LIBERO-PRO上成功率达74.0%，较初始部署提升15.0个百分点，较其VLA控制器的直接执行高13.5个百分点。案例研究展示了证据引导的练习选择、抽屉技能接地（drawer-skill grounding）与控制器干预方面的变化。

Conclusion: 课程选择与执行改进可以围绕冻结基础模型联合演化，且交替更新+固定快照的机制能提供可比性；该方法在分布偏移更强（LIBERO-PRO）的设定下收益尤为显著，但证据主要来自LIBERO系列，泛化性仍需更广泛验证。

Abstract: Robot agents can improve reusable skills, planning procedures, and recovery strategies from execution feedback, yet a fixed practice source leaves the search for new weaknesses unchanged. Useful practice depends on the agent's current capabilities, so the procedure that chooses practice must also be revisable. We present RoboACE (Robot Adversarial Co-Evolution), a framework that jointly evolves curriculum and execution harnesses around frozen foundation models. The curriculum harness uses accumulated capability evidence to propose informative challenges, while the execution harness responds with reusable revisions that expand execution capabilities. Alternating updates hold one side fixed as the other changes, and admitted challenges form fixed snapshots for comparing execution revisions. Starting from a compact set of LIBERO practice conditions, the resulting frozen deployment outperforms the evaluated controllers and agent systems across four LIBERO-family panels. On LIBERO-PRO, it reaches 74.0% success, exceeding the initial deployment by 15.0 percentage points and direct execution by its VLA controller by 13.5 points. Case studies document changes to evidence-guided practice selection, drawer-skill grounding, and controller intervention.

</details>


### [224] [Robo-MLT: Dual-System Robotic Manipulation for Long-Horizon Medical Laboratory Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13039)
*Zequan Zeng,Kai Wang,Delvin Ce Zhang,Huaping Liu,Zhipeng Yuan,Junqin Yang,YunLong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Robo-MLT 是一个"双系统"框架：显式工作流监控 + 学习式动作生成，在血液气体分析与采血管分拣任务上取得82%和70%的任务成功率，较 π0.5 分别高出30和22个百分点，并能在人工移除样本后进行重规划并继续作业。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗实验室自动化要求机器人严格按照协议顺序完成整个流程，并能处理执行中的变化。现有方法下，机器人可能每个动作都执行正确，却因视觉上相似的阶段被混淆、或人工干预后停滞而导致工作流失败，因此需要显式的工作流级监控来提升长时序操作的可靠性与可恢复性。

Method: 提出 Robo-MLT 双系统框架，包含四个组件：1）视觉-语言规划器根据协议与初始场景观测，组合出有序的原子技能序列；2）多帧执行监控器判断当前技能是否完成或已不可行，进而推进流程或触发重规划；3）技能条件化的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略生成操作轨迹；4）异步运行时协调策略与监控器的执行。对比对象为四种策略基线与一个基于 VLM 的监控器基线。

Result: 在血液气体分析与血样试管分拣两个任务上，每种方法50个常规配置、20个样本移除配置。Robo-MLT 任务成功率分别为82%与70%，比 π0.5 分别高30和22个百分点。在操作员移除正在处理的样本后，系统能修改剩余计划并继续处理其他样本。

Conclusion: 在所评估的条件下，显式的工作流监控使长时序实验室操作更加可靠且具备可恢复性，验证了将流程级监控与学习式底层策略解耦的合理性。

Abstract: Reliable medical laboratory automation requires robots to complete entire workflows in protocol order while handling changes during execution. A robot may execute each motion correctly yet fail the workflow by confusing visually similar stages or stalling after human intervention. We present Robo-MLT, a dual-system framework that couples explicit workflow monitoring with learned action generation. A vision--language planner composes an ordered atomic-skill sequence from the protocol and the initial workspace observation. A multiframe execution monitor detects when the active skill has completed or become infeasible, then advances the workflow or triggers replanning. A skill-conditioned vision--language--action policy generates the manipulation trajectories, and an asynchronous runtime coordinates it with the monitor. We evaluate Robo-MLT against four policy baselines and a VLM-based monitor baseline on blood gas analysis and blood sample tube sorting, with 50 nominal configurations per method and 20 sample-removal configurations. Robo-MLT achieves task success rates of 82\% and 70\%, exceeding $π_{0.5}$ by 30 and 22 percentage points, respectively. After an operator removes the sample being handled, the system revises the remaining plan and continues processing the other samples. These results indicate that, under the evaluated conditions, explicit workflow monitoring makes long-horizon laboratory manipulation more reliable and recoverable.

</details>


### [225] [ASPIRE: An Agentic System-Identification Platform for Iterative Reasoning and Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13114)
*Erik Derner,Jiří Kubalík,Robert Babuška*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ASPIRE：由大语言模型智能体自主选择并执行非线性系统辨识方法的框架，可从实测数据构建闭式非线性静态/动态模型，并在三套实测系统30次运行中全部产出有效模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统辨识依赖专家在方法选择、先验物理知识融入与模型验证上的决策，门槛高、难以自动化；作者希望由大语言模型智能体承担这些专家性判断，实现端到端的自动辨识。

Method: ASPIRE 让 LLM 智能体自行选择并执行辨识方法，从测量数据构建闭式非线性静态或动态模型；输入可选地包含系统描述、模型预期用途以及以形式化或自然语言表达的物理要求。每个模型输出包含参数、估计记录、各项的解释，以及在智能体不可见的测试数据上计算的误差；形式化物理要求由独立程序验证。框架支持全自主或交互模式，智能体可提问，用户可要求追加分析与新一轮辨识。

Result: 在移动机器人、含干摩擦的旋转系统、实验室发酵罐三套实测系统上评测，每套分“有描述/无描述”两种条件、每任务5次运行。全部30次运行均产出有效模型，单次成本0.96–3.38美元。作者评估显示：30个推荐模型在150个评估项中，模型解释及结构选择与参数估计的文档记录有132项完全满足、18项部分满足；最常见缺陷（9个模型）为对模型结构选择的说明不准确或缺乏支撑。

Conclusion: ASPIRE 表明 LLM 智能体可低成本、稳定地完成非线性系统辨识全流程并生成可解释、可审计的模型文档，但模型结构选择依据的可靠性仍是主要短板，需进一步约束与验证。

Abstract: System identification requires expert decisions on the identification method, incorporation of prior physical knowledge, and model validation. We propose ASPIRE, a framework for nonlinear system identification in which a large language model agent selects and executes the identification method. ASPIRE constructs closed-form, nonlinear static or dynamic models from measured data, optionally supplemented by a system description, the intended use of the model, and physical requirements expressed formally or in natural language. Each model includes its parameters, an estimation record, an interpretation of its terms, and an error evaluated on test data inaccessible to the agent. Formal physical requirements are verified independently. ASPIRE operates autonomously or interactively, allowing the agent to ask questions and the user to request further analyses and identification rounds. We evaluate ASPIRE on three measured systems: a mobile robot, a rotational system with dry friction, and a laboratory fermenter. Each system is tested with and without a description, in five runs per task. All 30 runs delivered valid models, at a cost of 0.96 to 3.38 US dollars per run. An assessment by the authors found that, for the 30 models recommended by the agent, one per run, the interpretation of the model and the documentation of its structure selection and parameter estimation fully satisfied 132 of 150 assessed items and partly satisfied 18. The most frequent discrepancy, found in nine models, concerned inaccurate or unsupported claims about model structure selection.

</details>


### [226] [DOBB: Dynamic Oriented Bounding Boxes for Multi-Agent Collision Avoidance under State Uncertainty and Heterogeneous Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13142)
*Pedro Antonio Alarcon Granadeno,Jane Cleland-Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出动态有向包围盒（DOBB），将状态估计、不确定性及个体响应特性映射为动态、方向相关的安全裕度，作为在线几何表示供去中心化避碰策略使用，在四个环境中平均总失败率2.6%，分布外条件下碰撞类失败不超过2.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习的去中心化避碰需在状态估计不确定性与异构智能体动力学下保持可靠。现有方法要么依赖训练域随机化，要么将不确定性与能力估计直接输入策略，使策略必须自行推断这些量应如何改变安全交互所需的物理间距，导致避碰行为与训练分布中学到的关系耦合。

Method: 1) 构建在线几何表示DOBB：将当前状态估计、不确定性以及智能体特定响应特性映射为动态、方向相关的安全裕度；2) 推导同时刻画智能体DOBB间相对构型与交互严重程度的几何特征；3) 设计保持上述交互物理含义的奖励函数。

Result: 在四个评估环境中，DOBB取得最低的平均总失败率2.6%；在驱动动力学与状态估计不确定性的分布外偏移下性能保持稳定，所有测试条件下碰撞类失败率均≤2.3%。作者将鲁棒性归因于安全裕度的几何抽象，其与训练中遇到的具体不确定性与驱动工况耦合更弱。

Conclusion: 以几何方式显式编码安全裕度可降低策略对训练分布的依赖，在不确定性与异构动力学条件下实现可靠的去中心化避碰。

Abstract: Learning-based decentralized collision avoidance must remain reliable under state-estimation uncertainty and heterogeneous agent dynamics. Existing methods typically address these factors through training-domain variation or by exposing uncertainty and capability estimates to the policy at runtime. However, both leave the policy to infer how these quantities should alter the physical separation required for safe interaction, which can couple collision-avoidance behavior to relationships learned from the training distribution. We introduce the Dynamic Oriented Bounding Box (DOBB), an online geometric representation that maps the current state estimate, uncertainty, and agent-specific response characteristics into a dynamic, direction-dependent safety margin. We derive geometric features that jointly characterize the relative configuration and severity of interactions between agents' DOBBs, along with a reward function that preserves the physical interpretation of those interactions. Across four evaluation environments, DOBB achieves the lowest mean total failure rate of 2.6%. This performance persists under out-of-distribution shifts in actuation dynamics and state-estimation uncertainty, with collision-based failure remaining at or below 2.3% in every tested condition. We attribute this robustness to the geometric abstraction of the safety margin, which is less coupled to the specific uncertainty and actuation regimes encountered during training.

</details>


### [227] [How Much of Your VLA Is kNN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13160)
*Denis Grachev,Damien LaRocque,Omar Moured*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 无需策略训练，仅靠检索邻近演示并复用其动作块即可在LIBERO上达到91.7%成功率；现有VLA微调在很大程度上只是把视觉-语言骨干变成了更好的检索键，其动作仍接近演示，并提出局部动作一致性（LAC）这一离线指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在LIBERO仿真基准上成功率已超95%，但其性能究竟有多少依赖对训练演示之外的泛化仍不清楚。作者希望厘清高成功率中有多少可由"记忆/检索"而非真正泛化解释。

Method: 1) 构建检索基线：从训练演示中检索邻近观测并直接复用其动作块，不做任何策略训练；2) 在LIBERO及LIBERO-Plus扰动设定下评测九个公开VLA，比较微调前后动作与演示的接近程度；3) 提出离线指标 LAC（Local Action Consistency），衡量表征空间中邻近观测是否对应相似的未来动作，并用其指导检索键的训练。

Result: 检索基线在LIBERO上达到91.7%成功率，无需策略训练；在LIBERO-Plus扰动下，检索方法的性能下降幅度在所有被评测策略中最小。九个公开VLA微调后主要使视觉-语言骨干成为更好的检索键，其输出动作仍与演示高度接近。LAC与检索成功率趋势一致，可用于指导检索键训练。

Conclusion: LIBERO上的高成功率在很大程度上可由演示检索解释，单纯的动作预测准确率不足以证明泛化能力；LAC可作为轻量离线诊断与训练信号，提示后续评测需更关注超越演示分布的能力。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models now exceed 95% success on the LIBERO simulation benchmark, yet it remains unclear how much of this performance requires generalization beyond the training demonstrations. In this work, we show that retrieving nearby demonstrations and reusing their action chunks achieves a success rate of 91.7 % on LIBERO without policy training. Under LIBERO-Plus perturbations, retrieval shows the smallest drop of all evaluated policies. Across nine publicly available VLAs, fine-tuning mostly makes the vision-language backbone a better retrieval key, and the actions of the VLAs remain close to the demonstrations. In addition, we introduce Local Action Consistency (LAC), an offline metric that measures whether nearby observations in representation space are also associated with similar future actions. We show that LAC tracks the success rate of retrieval and can guide the training of a retrieval key. Our evaluation and source code are available at https://github.com/sereact/lerobot_knn.

</details>


### [228] [Ground Reaction Force-Aware Model Predictive Control for Underactuated Upright Physical Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13204)
*Darren Biskup,Jeonghan Yu,Zibo Yu,Sunil K. Agrawal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GRF-MPC框架，将实时地面反作用力与压力中心测量直接纳入人体质心动力学模型，以滚动时域二次规划优化辅助力/力矩，并在仅4根缆索的欠驱动RobUST平台上实现优于8缆索全驱动阻抗基线的辅助效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有康复机器人多采用反应式跟踪控制，执行预设动作而未显式纳入用户瞬时的人-地相互作用信息，导致辅助的物理透明性与有效性受限。

Method: 将实时GRF与CoP测量直接嵌入人体质心动力学模型，构建GRF-MPC；在线求解实时QP，在滚动时域内优化辅助力与力矩；在RobUST上以欠驱动4缆索配置部署，并与全驱动8缆索笛卡尔阻抗基线在6名健康受试者、近300次试验中对比（扰动、弓步轨迹、自主稳定极限任务）。

Result: 扰动下峰值平面CoM误差降低19.6%；轨迹跟踪误差降低24.1%；净机械功减少28.7%；归一化自主偏移面积增大45.9%；QP求解时间小于1 ms，支持100 Hz重规划。

Conclusion: 把实时人-地相互作用纳入预测控制，可在执行器数量减半的条件下获得更强、更透明的物理辅助，验证了GRF-MPC在直立物理辅助中的有效性与实时可行性。

Abstract: Many rehabilitation robots execute prescribed motions using reactive tracking controllers that do not explicitly incorporate the user's instantaneous human-ground interaction. This paper presents Ground Reaction Force-Aware Model Predictive Control (GRF-MPC), a framework for upright physical assistance. By integrating live GRF and center of pressure (CoP) measurements directly into a human centroidal dynamics model, GRF-MPC formulates a real-time quadratic program (QP) that optimizes assistive forces and moments over a receding horizon. We deployed GRF-MPC on the Robotic Upright Stand Trainer (RobUST) in an underactuated 4-cable configuration and evaluated it against a fully actuated 8-cable Cartesian impedance baseline in nearly 300 trials across six healthy participants. Across tasks spanning impulsive perturbations, lunge trajectories, and voluntary limits of stability, GRF-MPC reduced peak planar center-of-mass (CoM) error by 19.6% during perturbations, lowered trajectory tracking error by 24.1%, required 28.7% less net mechanical work, and permitted a 45.9% greater normalized voluntary excursion area. The underlying QP solves in under 1 ms, comfortably enabling 100 Hz replanning and demonstrating that incorporating live human-ground interaction into predictive control yields stronger, more transparent physical assistance with half the actuators.

</details>


### [229] [Latent Barrier Steering: Hierarchical Safety for Generative Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13227)
*Renhao Zhang,Mingzhe Li,Bruno Castro da Silva*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出层次化“语义→路径”框架 Latent Barrier Steering (LBS)：先在生成器的行为潜空间中将采样导向安全裕度更大的行为模式，再对解码路径用 CBF-QP 仅修正残余违规，从而兼顾安全、目标达成率并降低修正负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面向扩散/流匹配规划器的控制障碍函数(CBF)安全滤波只在采样过程中或预测之后对生成样本做局部修正。此类修复对小幅违规有效，但当新约束整体阻断所选行为模式（而非局部扰动）时，路径级修复难以奏效。

Method: LBS 将行为选择与路径认证解耦，采用两级协同干预：(1) 潜空间引导——将生成器的行为潜变量 steer 到安全裕度更大的 rollout 方向；(2) 路径空间修正——解码引导后的潜变量，仅对残余违规求解 path-space CBF 二次规划(QP)。并给出分析：潜空间引导增大了路径级校正器可用的裕度。

Result: 理论上有有限时间恢复保证，且修正负担减小；在导航与机器人末端执行器规划任务中，相较路径级安全滤波器，LBS 在保持安全性的同时提升了目标到达率并降低了修正负担。

Conclusion: 安全滤波应前移到行为语义层而非仅停留在路径层：潜空间引导与路径级 CBF-QP 的层次化组合可有效处理"整模式被阻断"的失效情形，是 flow-matching 生成式规划安全部署的可行方案。

Abstract: We introduce Latent Barrier Steering (LBS), a hierarchical semantic-to-path framework for safe flow-matching-based generative planning. Existing control-barrier-function (CBF) safety filters for diffusion and flow-matching planners act locally on generated samples, either during sampling or after prediction. Such repair is effective for small violations, but can struggle when a new constraint blocks the whole selected behavior mode rather than merely perturbing it locally. LBS decouples behavior selection from path certification through two coordinated interventions: it first steers the generator's behavior latent toward rollouts with larger safety margin, then decodes the steered latent and applies a path-space CBF quadratic program (QP) only for residual violations. We show that latent steering increases the margin available to the path-space corrector, yielding finite-time recovery guarantees and reduced correction burden. Experiments across navigation and robot end-effector planning tasks show that LBS preserves safety while improving goal reaching and reducing correction burden compared with path-level safety filters.

</details>


### [230] [Incorporating World Models in Decision Making for Next Best View Selection in Active Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13229)
*Nikita Sarawgi,Omey M. Manyar,Satyandra K. Gupta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种"部署自适应"的下一最佳视角选择方法：冻结预训练世界模型，仅用少量本地标定数据学习一个传感器回波适配器（sensor-return adapter）来估计预测表面上的测量丢失（dropout），使视角选择偏向于能填补实测重建空洞的视角。真实机器人结构光传感器实验表明，在减少60%采集次数下获得更高实测表面覆盖率，并成功恢复名义方法反复尝试却测不到的表面区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高效机器人三维重建需用尽量少的物理采集选取能带来新表面测量的视角；预训练世界模型可指导选视角，但其预测表面在实际传感器工况下未必能产生有效深度测量，导致名义选择反复瞄准不可测区域。

Method: 部署自适应方法：保持预训练预测器固定，从有限的本地标定数据中学习传感器回波适配器，在预测表面上估计测量丢失概率；该估计被嵌入下一最佳视角选择准则，从而偏好可能填补实测重建空洞的视角。

Result: 真实机器人结构光传感器实验中，自适应方法在减少60%采集次数的情况下取得更高的实测表面覆盖率；并能恢复名义方法反复瞄准却始终测量失败的表面区域。

Conclusion: 机器人可在数据稀缺的部署环境中复用预训练预测模型，仅通过局部工况自适应调整决策；模块化适配（冻结预测器+轻量适配器）是兼顾泛化与部署鲁棒性的可行路径。

Abstract: Efficient robotic 3D reconstruction requires selecting views that contribute new surface measurements with few physical captures. Pretrained world models can guide this choice, but a predicted surface may not produce valid depth measurements under the operating conditions of the deployed sensor. We present a deployment adaptation methodology for next-best-view selection that learns a sensor-return adapter from limited local calibration data while keeping the pretrained predictor fixed. The adapter estimates measurement dropout over each predicted surface, allowing the selection method to favor views likely to fill gaps in the measured reconstruction. In a real-robot experiment with a structured-light sensor, the adapted method achieves higher measured surface coverage with 60% fewer captures than nominal selection. The adapted method recovers a surface region that the nominal method repeatedly targets but fails to measure. These results suggest that robots can reuse pretrained predictive models while adapting their decisions to local operating conditions in data-scarce environments.

</details>


### [231] [Curie's Jarvis: Autonomous Robotic Chemistry Experiments from Printed Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13251)
*Rumi Loghmani,Bozhang Song,Victor Alvarado,Aliyah Redding,Amir Barati Farimani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Curie's Jarvis系统，使机械臂能在为人类设计的普通实验台上按纸质说明书完成化学实验：从说明书照片读取步骤，在无固定位置、无标记的台面上定位容器，通过四视角读取标签区分外观相同的容器，每步前重新定位目标，并依据操作对象自动设定执行速度。在Franka Panda机械臂上，儿童化学套件两个协议分别完成5/5和4/5次全自主运行，160次打印与手写标签定位全部识别正确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类实验者依赖打印说明书、容器标签和逐步目视确认来操作；而现有实验室自动化通常要求脚本化流程、固定容器位置或基准标记（fiducial markers），导致机器人与人类实验环境不兼容，需改造实验台。作者希望机器人直接在"为人布置"的台面上以人的方式做化学实验。

Method: (1) 从打印说明书的照片中解析实验步骤；(2) 在无固定位置、无标记的普通台面上检测容器；(3) 通过四个相机视角读取容器标签，区分外观相同的容器；(4) 每个步骤执行前重新定位目标；(5) 根据所处理对象（容器/内容物）自动确定每步的执行方式，包括运动速度。系统部署于Franka Panda机械臂。

Result: 儿童化学套件中，一个协议5次运行全部成功，另一协议5次中成功4次，全程无人工干预；在160次针对打印标签与手写标签的定位任务中，所有容器均被正确识别。

Conclusion: 该系统展示了机器人在非结构化、为人类设计的实验台上自主完成化学流程的可行性，缩小了机器人操作与人类台面实验方式之间的差距，向"像人一样做台面化学实验"迈进一步。

Abstract: A person running a bench experiment works from a printed instruction sheet, tells identical containers apart by the labels written on them, and looks at the bench again before each step. Lab automation usually works the other way around: the procedure is written as a script, and containers stand at fixed positions or carry fiducial markers. We present Curie's Jarvis, a system that lets a robot arm carry out a chemistry experiment on a bench set up for people. It reads the steps from a photo of the printed sheet, finds containers on an ordinary bench with no fixed positions or markers, tells identical-looking containers apart by reading their labels in four camera views, locates its target again before every step, and sets how each step is carried out, including its speed, from what is being handled. On a Franka Panda arm, Curie's Jarvis completed 5 of 5 runs of one protocol from a children's chemistry kit and 4 of 5 runs of another, with no human intervention, and in 160 locates on printed and handwritten labels it identified every container correctly. This brings robots a step closer to doing bench chemistry the way people do.

</details>


### [232] [Force-Closure Condition for Cooperative Multi-robot Pushing of Rectangular Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13268)
*Duy Anh Nguyen,Trung Dung Ngo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文给出无摩擦四点推动下矩形物体平面力封闭的充要闭式条件，并嵌入轨迹跟踪框架验证其控制价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作操作中推触点的布置决定能否实现力封闭，从而决定智能体能否对物体施加任意力与力矩；需要在接触力分配之前对接触构型进行可行性判定。

Method: 针对长 b、宽 a 的矩形物体与四个无摩擦推触点，解析推导平面力封闭的充要条件：\((l_1+l_3-b)(l_2+l_4-a)<0\)，其中 l1–l4 为四条边上的接触位置；条件与质心无关，不满足时可由两因子符号判别应调整的对置接触对，并将条件与调整规则作为几何可行性层集成到全位姿轨迹跟踪框架中。

Result: 静态力旋量可行性测试验证了该条件；窄通道浮体仿真中，维持力封闭构型将 x、y 与解缠偏航角的 RMS 跟踪误差分别由 12.05 m、18.11 m、535.96° 降至 2.24 m、2.66 m、2.69°。

Conclusion: 力封闭的闭式充要条件简洁、与质心无关，可直接用于接触构型预检与失效时的定向调整，并在控制中显著提升轨迹跟踪精度。

Abstract: In cooperative object manipulation, the placement of pushing contacts determines whether force closure can be achieved and agents can generate arbitrary forces and torques on the object correspondingly. This study derives a necessary and sufficient closed-form condition for the planar force closure of a rectangular object, a common object shape in everyday settings, under four frictionless pushing contacts. For a rectangle of length $b$ and width $a$, force closure holds if and only if $(l_1+l_3-b)(l_2+l_4-a)<0$, where $l_1,l_2,l_3,l_4$ are the four edge contact locations. This condition is independent of the object's center of mass and is used to verify contact configurations before contact force allocation. When the condition is not satisfied, the signs of the two factors indicate which opposing contact pair should be adjusted.
