The condition and adjustment rule are then integrated into a full-pose trajectory-tracking framework as a geometric feasibility layer to demonstrate their use in control. Static wrench feasibility tests validate the proposed condition, while narrow-passage floating-body simulations show that maintaining a force-closure configuration reduces the RMS tracking errors in $x$, $y$, and unwrapped yaw from $12.05$ m, $18.11$ m, and $535.96^\circ$ to $2.24$ m, $2.66$ m, and $2.69^\circ$, respectively.

</details>


### [233] [RaMOD: Moving-Object Detection from Raw mmWave Radar Cubes under Ego Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13271)
*Yuan Fang,Long Zhuang,Lingzi Xing,Xijia Wei,Jingqi Jiang,Fangzhan Shi,Simon Julier,Kevin Chetty*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RaMOD：直接从原始距离-方位-多普勒（RAD）立方体做运动目标检测与测速的实时框架，显式估计自车运动以预测并抑制世界静止结构的多普勒区域，时序编码多尺度特征预测 RAD 空间框及速度（含多普勒解模糊），在 BRIDGE-Liv 上 80.8% cube mAP@0.30、速度 MAE 1.128 m/s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波雷达具有全天候感知与直接多普勒测速优势，但车载感知流水线常把输出压缩为稀疏点云或二维投影，浪费了原始 RAD 立方体中丰富的信息；同时自车运动使世界静止结构产生多普勒响应，多普勒卷绕（wrapping）又会造成径向速度混叠，给运动目标检测与测速带来困难。

Method: 基于单车载单芯片雷达的原始 RAD 立方体：1）逐帧显式估计自车运动，利用估计的雷达平移速度预测世界静止结构的多普勒区域并抑制该区域回波；2）对抑制后的立方体做时序编码，提取多尺度特征，预测带类别标签的 RAD 空间检测框；3）对每个目标在原始多普勒与解卷绕两种假设下预测径向/前向速度，并输出多普勒卷绕概率。

Result: 在自建 BRIDGE-Liv 数据集上，RaMOD 在 RAD-cube、RA、RD 三种检测设置下均优于所有对比的原始雷达方法；IoU 0.30 时 cube mAP 达 80.8%，比最强 RAD-cube 基线高 15.7 个百分点；速度 MAE 为 1.128 m/s，多普勒卷绕判别准确率 99.87%。

Conclusion: 保留原始 RAD 域信息并结合自车运动补偿与多普勒解模糊建模，可显著提升雷达运动目标检测与测速性能，说明原始雷达立方体相较点云/二维投影更具利用价值。

Abstract: Millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar provides weather-robust sensing and direct Doppler measurements, yet automotive perception pipelines often reduce its output to sparse point clouds or two-dimensional projections, leaving raw range-azimuth-Doppler (RAD) cubes underutilized. Ego motion induces Doppler responses from world-static structures, while Doppler wrapping aliases radial velocities, complicating moving-object detection and velocity estimation. We present RaMOD (Radar-based Moving-Object Detection), a real-time framework for moving-object detection and velocity estimation from raw RAD cubes acquired by a vehicle-mounted single-chip radar. RaMOD explicitly estimates frame-wise ego motion and uses the estimated radar translational velocity to predict the Doppler region of world-static structures and suppress returns within it. It temporally encodes the suppressed cubes into multi-scale features to predict RAD-space boxes with class labels. For each detected object, it predicts radial and forward velocities under raw-Doppler and Doppler-unwrapped hypotheses, together with Doppler-wrapping probabilities. On our BRIDGE-Liv dataset, RaMOD outperforms all compared raw-radar methods across RAD-cube, RA, and RD detection settings, achieving 80.8% cube mAP at IoU 0.30, 15.7 percentage points above the strongest RAD-cube baseline. Velocity MAE is 1.128 m/s, with 99.87% Doppler-wrapping accuracy.

</details>


### [234] [Large Language Model Explainability for Planning Lunar Rover Trajectories under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13292)
*Michelle Ho,Maximilian Adang,Zachary Booth,David E. Astle,Margaret Hansen,Edward Balaban,Mykel J. Kochenderfer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于LLM的交互式助手，用ReAct接口调用工具对行星巡视器规划轨迹进行规划后解释与条件重规划，90.9%响应忠实于规划器输出，较零样本提示提升近65%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行星巡视器在概率不确定性下的路径规划是多目标优化问题，目前由科学团队手工迭代求解与验证；任务规划期间时间线与约束频繁变化使人工方式不可行。基于采样的规划器（如MCTS）可加速求解，但输出缺乏对科学目标的溯源可解释性，难以供团队核验。

Method: 构建受NASA VIPER任务启发的仿真器与ReAct可查询接口，接口调用精选的动作工具与检索函数；LLM不介入规划决策循环，而是用于规划后解释（post-planning explanation），以领域词汇解释轨迹并支持在用户指定条件下重跑规划器以比较备选方案。

Result: 在包含方案比较、可行性判断与信息查询三类问题的查询库上评估，助手90.9%的响应忠实于规划器输出，相比同一LLM无工具访问的零样本提示提升近65%。

Conclusion: 将LLM置于规划后解释环节而非决策循环内，可提升可信任度；实验表明基于LLM的系统能为不确定性感知的巡视器路径提供可信的可解释性。

Abstract: Planning planetary rover trajectories under probabilistic uncertainty is a multi-objective optimization problem that science teams currently iteratively solve and verify by hand. This process becomes impractical when timelines and constraints frequently shift throughout the mission planning phase. Sampling-based planners such as Monte Carlo tree search can speed up this process, but their outputs do not preserve traceability to the science objectives that justify each plan, which the science team needs to verify. We propose an interactive assistant based on a large language model (LLM) that can explain planner trajectories using domain vocabulary and re-run the planner under user-specified conditions to compare alternatives. Previous work has used LLMs inside planner decision-making loops, but we explore using them for post-planning explanation, where they are more likely to be trusted since they do not directly influence planner decisions. Our framework pairs a simulator inspired by the NASA Volatiles Investigating Polar Exploration Rover (VIPER) mission with a ReAct queryable interface that calls curated action tools and retrieval functions. We evaluate the assistant on a query bank consisting of plan comparisons, feasibility, and lookup queries. Experiments show that 90.9% of the assistant's responses are faithful to the planner's outputs, a nearly 65% improvement over zero-shot prompting of the same LLM without tool access, demonstrating the potential for trustworthy explainability of uncertainty-aware traverses with an LLM-based system.

</details>


### [235] [Does Haptic Connection to a Healthy Partner Improve Motor Learning in Chronic Stroke? A Pilot Study of Robot-Based Dyadic Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13307)
*Erica L. Waters,Natalia Stroutinsky-Sobotka,Reem M. Azar,Frances S. Shofer,Pamela Z. Cacchione,Rochelle J. Mendonca,Michelle J. Johnson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该初步研究比较慢性脑卒中患者在单独与机器人触觉双人（dyad）条件下学习复杂追踪任务的表现，发现双人训练提升了训练期表现、努力程度与运动复杂度，但两组均未呈现清晰的学习与保持；任务难度与认知负荷过高、样本量小及卒中异质性为主要局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人触觉双人干预被认为可通过促进运动变异性、提供隐式沟通渠道与增强社交互动来提升运动学习，有望用于机器人辅助神经康复；但针对神经疾病人群的研究很少，且缺乏完整的临床评估。

Method: 试点研究：慢性脑卒中患者完成认知、运动与感觉评估后，分别在单独与双人条件下学习复杂追踪任务；分析追踪误差、移动距离、平均绝对功率与样本熵等运动学指标的变化，并将临床评分与基线追踪误差做相关分析。

Result: 基线追踪误差与较低认知评分、较高运动评分强相关（r > 0.6）；仅单独组在评估时追踪误差降低（Δ = -5.33，95%CI [-10.25, -0.40]，p = 0.027），但剔除一名离群被试后该结果不再稳健；两组均无明确保持效果；双人组在训练期表现出显著更高的表现水平、努力程度和运动复杂度。

Conclusion: 双人训练提高了运动复杂度与努力程度，这两者与更好的运动学习相关；病变部位、认知、感觉与病程等临床特征可能影响双人训练的潜在收益。所选任务难度过高（可能源于高认知需求）以及样本量与卒中异质性限制了结论的推广。

Abstract: Purpose: Robot-based haptic dyads, where two users exchange forces through robots, have been proposed for robot-assisted neurorehabilitation. This intervention could increase motor learning by promoting motor variability, providing an implicit communication channel, and facilitating social interaction. Despite the potential benefits, few studies have investigated robot-based haptic dyads for neurological populations, and none have included a comprehensive clinical assessment. Design/Methodology/Approach: We present a pilot study investigation of individuals with chronic stroke learning a complex tracking task in solo and dyad conditions. Prior to training, participants completed a battery of cognitive, motor, and sensory assessments. We assessed changes in key kinematic outcomes including tracking error, distance traveled, mean absolute power, and sample entropy. We also performed a correlation analysis of clinical scores with baseline tracking errors. Findings: Baseline tracking error was strongly correlated (r > 0.6) with lower cognitive scores and higher motor scores. Only the solo group demonstrated lower tracking error at assessment (Δ = -5.33, 95%CI [-10.25, -0.40], p = 0.027), but this result was tempered by the removal of an outlier participant. Neither group demonstrated clear retention. The dyad group showed significantly elevated performance, effort, and movement complexity during training. Research Limitations/Implications: The selected task was too difficult to reveal learning for either group, which may be related to its high cognitive demand. Sample size and stroke heterogeneity were also limitation. Originality/Value: Dyadic training increased movement complexity and effort, which are associated with better motor learning. Clinical characteristics such as lesion location, cognition, sensation, and chronicity may influence potential benefits of dyadic training.

</details>


### [236] [PneuGen: Task-Driven Agentic Co-Design of Soft Pneumatic Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13344)
*Shaohong Zhong,Marco Pontin,Perla Maiolino*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PneuGen 是一个基于大语言模型智能体的软体机器人协同设计框架，通过"设计智能体+评审智能体"在参数化组件库上高效搜索设计空间，配合模块响应模型与自动有限元仿真验证，在抓取与形状匹配任务上显著优于既有计算优化基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人的任务驱动设计需同时考虑几何、形态、材料属性与驱动方式，设计空间庞大复杂，难以探索。现有计算优化方法依赖仿真信号迭代改进设计，但优化与仿真均带来高昂计算负担，效率不足。

Method: 提出 PneuGen 智能体协同设计框架：（1）设计智能体从参数化组件库生成设计程序；（2）独立的评审智能体分析仿真诊断信息并提出针对性修改建议；（3）拟合的模块响应模型作为可复用的物理先验，建立几何与压力到形变的映射；（4）设计程序自动校验并转换为有限元仿真，支持迭代评估。另通过消融实验检验评审智能体与模块响应模型的作用。

Result: 在基础几何体及复杂物体的抓取任务、以及需要多方向平面与三维形变的形状匹配任务中，该框架显著优于先前的计算优化基线；消融实验表明评审智能体与模块响应模型对提升设计效率有实质贡献；并在真实环境中演示了智能体生成的软体机器人设计方案在形状匹配与抓取任务上的表现。

Conclusion: 将 LLM 智能体引入软体机器人协同设计，可在保证仿真验证严谨性的同时显著降低设计搜索成本，模块响应先验与批评反馈机制是关键增益来源，且生成设计具备向真实世界迁移的可行性。

Abstract: Task-driven design of soft robots requires the consideration of factors such as geometry, morphology, material properties, and actuation, resulting in a large and complex design space that is challenging to explore. Computational optimisation methods leverage signals from simulation to iteratively improve designs to achieve task objectives. However, these methods typically demand high computational load for both optimisation and simulation. We present PneuGen, an agentic co-design framework for soft robots that leverages Large Language Model (LLM) agents to search over the soft robot design space efficiently. A designer agent proposes design programs from a parametric component library, and a separate critic agent analyses simulation diagnostics and recommends targeted revisions. Fitted module response models provide reusable physical priors relating geometry and pressure to deformation. Design programs are automatically validated and converted into finite-element simulations, enabling iterative evaluation. Across grasping tasks on primitive shapes and challenging objects, as well as shape-matching tasks that require multi-directional planar and 3D deformation, the proposed framework significantly outperforms prior computational optimisation baselines. Ablation experiments also highlight the utility of the critic agent and the module response model in facilitating efficient design. We additionally demonstrate the agent-generated soft robot designs in real-world shape-matching and grasping tasks.

</details>


### [237] [AHEADance: Anticipatory Humanoid Dance from Streaming Audio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13370)
*Tianhu Peng,Yukun Wang,Lingfan Bao,Yiyang Jia,Chengxu Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AHEADance: 机器人原生的流式舞蹈生成器，通过"预听未来音乐"与"提交强制"实现人形机器人在音乐播放同时实时舞蹈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式音乐下的人形机器人舞蹈必须在听到对应音乐之前就提交每个动作片段，且需从已提交动作自然延续；现有方法难以应对因果性约束下的同步性与动作连贯性。

Method: 1) ListenAhead：以已听音乐加冻结音乐模型对未来音乐的预测作为每个片段的生成条件，预测按目标时间对齐并按可靠性加权；2) Commit Forcing：训练模型从自身已提交片段继续生成，同时更新潜在历史与下一片段的解码边界；3) 全身跟踪器在真机上执行；4) 三个音乐评估器（在真实 G1 舞蹈数据上验证）分别评测同步性、歌曲匹配与风格。

Result: 预测可近乎达到真实未来的节拍时序精度；使用预测后生成器与音乐同步更好，且动作能量更集中于歌曲节拍频率；Commit Forcing 将低活动比例从 83.5% 降至 10.3%（接近真实舞蹈水平）。在 Unitree G1 实机上实现随乐起舞、音乐停止即静止且无摔倒。

Conclusion: 通过前瞻性音乐预测与自回归提交一致性训练，流式舞蹈生成可在物理人形机器人上实现同步、连贯且安全的实时表现，为机器人原生生成式动作提供了可行范式。

Abstract: A humanoid that dances to streamed music must commit each motion chunk before it has heard the music that chunk accompanies, and must continue from motion it has already committed. We present AHEADance, a robot-native streaming dance generator executed on a humanoid robot by a whole-body tracker. ListenAhead conditions each chunk on heard music and a frozen music model's forecasts of the coming music, aligned to their target times and weighted by reliability. The forecasts carry the timing of upcoming beats almost as well as the actual future; with them, the generator is better synchronized with the music and concentrates more motion energy at its song's beat frequency. Commit Forcing trains continuation from the model's own committed chunks, updating both the latent history and the decoded boundary of the next chunk; it reduces low activity from 83.5% to 10.3%, the level of real dance. Three music evaluators, each validated on real G1 dance, test synchronization, song and style. On a physical Unitree G1, it dances while the music plays and comes to rest when it stops, without a fall. Project page: https://ihcr.github.io/AHEADance

</details>


### [238] [Fastener-centric acquisition and AND/OR graph representation of state-dependent disassembly knowledge from CAD-derived virtual assemblies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13379)
*Shiki Iwase*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出以紧固件为中心的虚拟拆卸框架，从CAD装配体中自动获取状态相关的拆卸操作知识，并组织为Transformed AND/OR Graph，用于设计阶段的可拆卸性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计阶段的可拆卸性评估需要产品结构与各装配状态下可用操作的知识，但CAD装配体并不直接给出状态相关的拆卸操作，现有信息缺口需填补。

Method: 将共同释放同一约束的紧固件归并为操作级集合，避免枚举任意单紧固件顺序；通过状态相关的可见性与工具可达性检查及扭矩驱动的虚拟移除试验评估可拆性；紧固件移除后，用图推理识别候选运动单元，并通过初始运动筛选、路径生成与物理仿真验证其分离可行性；将操作、状态、依赖、验证路径及仿真属性组织为Transformed AND/OR Graph。

Result: 受控玩具装配的穷举验证未发现因引入工具可达性与优先级条件而产生的分支违规；54组件OpenManipulator Chain夹爪实验中成功获取状态相关紧固件移除与零件/子装配分离操作；可见性与工具可达性评估剪枝51.4%的紧固件集合候选评估，且移除阻挡组件后使原先不可达的紧固件操作变为可行。

Conclusion: 操作级分组与筛选将详细物理验证限制在机械上合理的候选上；结果证明可在制造之前从CAD派生的虚拟装配体中获取产品专属拆卸知识，支撑后续拆卸推理与面向拆卸的设计评估。

Abstract: Design-stage disassemblability assessment requires knowledge of product structure and the operations available in each assembly state, but CAD assemblies do not directly specify state-dependent disassembly operations. This paper proposes a fastener-centric virtual-disassembly framework that acquires such knowledge from CAD-derived virtual assemblies. Fasteners jointly releasing the same constraint are grouped into operation-level sets, avoiding enumeration of arbitrary individual-fastener orders. Their removability is evaluated through state-dependent visibility and tool-access checks and torque-driven virtual-removal trials. After fastener removal, graph-based reasoning identifies candidate moving units, whose separation is evaluated by initial-motion screening, path generation, and physics-based validation. The acquired operations, states, dependencies, validated paths, and simulation-derived attributes are organized into a Transformed AND/OR Graph for subsequent planning and design evaluation. Exhaustive verification with a controlled toy assembly found no branching violations for introduced tool-accessibility and precedence conditions. Evaluation with a 54-component OpenManipulator Chain gripper demonstrated state-dependent fastener-removal and part- or subassembly-separation operations. Visibility and tool-accessibility evaluations pruned 51.4% of fastener-set candidate evaluations, while removal of obstructing components enabled previously inaccessible fastener operations. Operation-level grouping and screening thereby limited detailed physical validation to mechanically plausible candidates. The results demonstrate the feasibility of acquiring product-specific disassembly knowledge from CAD-derived virtual assemblies before manufacture, supporting subsequent disassembly reasoning and design-for-disassembly evaluation.

</details>


### [239] [Discrete Model-Based Diffusion for Combinatorial Optimization in Multi-Robot Task Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13382)
*Haejoon Lee,Thomas Marshall Vielmetti,Xinyi Wang,Dimitra Panagou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出无需训练的离散扩散采样优化方法 Discrete MBD，直接在离散变量上优化，用于多机器人组合优化问题，性能优于SBO基线且计算成本远低于MIP与RL方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人系统的决策含离散变量，形成需秒级求解的NP-hard组合优化问题。现有去噪扩散方法存在两难：Model-Based Diffusion(MBD)无需训练但仅适用连续空间；基于扩散的组合求解器虽处理离散空间却需离线训练。因此需要一种既能处理离散变量又免训练的采样优化方法。

Method: Discrete MBD：一种免训练的采样优化(SBO)方法，通过蒙特卡洛采样近似分类(categorical)反向转移分布，直接对离散变量进行优化；并从理论上建立找到全局最优解概率的有限步下界。

Result: 理论上证明该概率下界随去噪步数增加收敛到1。在通用整数规划以及异构多机器人联盟形成与调度任务上，Discrete MBD在几乎所有设置下优于SBO基线，解质量与混合整数规划(MIP)和强化学习方法相当，而计算成本仅为其一小部分。

Conclusion: Discrete MBD弥合了MBD连续性与扩散组合求解器需训练之间的鸿沟，实现免训练离散优化，具有良好的解质量-效率权衡，适用于近实时多机器人组合决策。

Abstract: Decision making in multi-robot systems often involves discrete decision variables, leading to NP-hard combinatorial optimization problems that must be solved within seconds for near real-time implementation. Denoising diffusion has emerged as a powerful tool for such problems, but existing approaches face a trade-off: Model-Based Diffusion (MBD) incorporates diffusion in sampling-based optimization (SBO) without training but only over continuous spaces, while existing diffusion-based combinatorial solvers operate over discrete spaces but require offline training. To this end, we propose Discrete Model-Based Diffusion (Discrete MBD), a training-free SBO method that directly optimizes discrete variables by approximating categorical reverse transition distributions through Monte Carlo sampling. We establish a finite-step lower bound on the probability of finding a global minimizer and show that this bound converges to one as the number of denoising steps increases. Extensive experiments on generic integer programs and heterogeneous multi-robot coalition formation and scheduling demonstrate that Discrete MBD outperforms SBO baselines in nearly all settings while achieving solution quality competitive with that of mixed-integer programming and reinforcement learning methods at a fraction of their computational cost.

</details>


### [240] [Rhythmic-Semantic Co-Speech Gestures for Emotional Dialogue in Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13472)
*Christian Arzate Cruz,Edwin C. Montiel-Vázquez,Stefanos Gkikas,Miguel Mejia Tobar,Randy Gomez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出情感感知的节奏-语义协同语音手势系统，实时从文本与机器人内部情感状态生成图标手势与节拍手势，在Haru机器人上部署并经60人用户研究验证优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同语音手势能提升参与度与语音理解，但现有数据驱动机器人系统多只生成单一类型手势：要么是节奏性节拍动作，要么是语义性图标手势，二者难以兼顾。

Method: 将节拍-图标手势生成任务在情感条件下形式化；采用轻量模型预测图标手势的时间与强度，再用情感条件化的节拍手势以合适强度填充剩余时间跨度；在BEAT2数据集上训练，部署于社交机器人Haru。

Result: 60名参与者的在线用户研究表明，该方法在语音-手势对齐性上更强，且相较基线方法更受偏好。

Conclusion: 将图标手势与情感条件化节拍手势统一建模可实时生成更协调的协同语音手势，用户主观评价与对齐度指标均支持其有效性。

Abstract: Co-speech gestures can increase engagement and improve speech understanding. Yet, most data-driven robot systems generate either rhythmic beat-like motion or semantically meaningful iconic gestures, but not both. We present an emotion-aware rhythmic-semantic co-speech gesture system that generates iconic and beat behaviors in real time from text and the robot's internal emotional state. We formalize the task of beat-iconic gesture generation using emotion and propose lightweight models that predict iconic timing and intensity, then fill the remaining spans with emotion-conditioned beats at an appropriate intensity level. We train on the BEAT2 dataset and deploy the system on the social robot Haru. In an online user study with 60 participants, our method shows stronger speech-gesture alignment and is preferred over baseline methods.

</details>


### [241] [ExRL: Sample-Efficient Online Adaptation of Long-Chunk Policies via Execution Length Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13482)
*Xu Luo,Jiaying Yang,Hao Wu,Junlin Xie,Zijin Hong,Ji Zhang,Lianli Gao,Jingkuan Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ExRL，将长动作块策略的在线强化学习自适应从"优化动作"转为"优化每个动作块的执行长度"，从而在低维空间内实现样本高效的自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长动作块（数十个时间步）策略已成为能力更强的机器人策略的关键组成，但用现有 RL 方法对这类策略做在线自适应时，需优化的动作块维度极高，导致所需在线样本量巨大；而真实场景中交互数据采集成本高昂，使此类自适应难以落地。

Method: 核心洞察是把自适应对象从"动作"转移到"每个预测动作块执行多长"。据此提出 ExRL：在基础策略之上附加一个轻量适配器，用于预测每个动作块的执行长度，并用 off-policy RL 算法训练该适配器。这样自适应空间保持低维，而表达能力随块长度增长而扩展。

Result: 在仿真基准与真实机器人任务上，ExRL 使多种长动作块策略均能实现样本高效的自适应，且仅需极少任务相关的超参数调优。

Conclusion: 通过优化执行长度而非高维动作块，ExRL 为长动作块策略提供了一种简单、样本高效且易迁移的在线 RL 自适应方案，具备实际机器人部署价值。

Abstract: Predicting long action chunks, often spanning dozens of timesteps, has become a key ingredient in increasingly capable robot policies. In this paper, we find that adapting these long-chunk policies with existing reinforcement learning (RL) methods can require many online samples due to the high dimensionality of the action chunks being optimized, making such adaptation impractical in real-world scenarios where interaction data are costly to collect. Our key insight is to shift adaptation from optimizing actions to optimizing how long to execute each predicted action chunk. This keeps the adaptation space low-dimensional while its expressivity grows with the chunk size. We thus introduce Execution at the Right Length (ExRL), a simple approach which augments the base policy with a lightweight adapter that predicts the execution length of each chunk and is trained using off-policy RL algorithms. Across simulation benchmarks and real-world robotic tasks, ExRL enables sample-efficient adaptation of a diverse range of long-chunk policies with minimal task-specific hyperparameter tuning.

</details>


### [242] [Route-Time Conflict Maps for Cooperative End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13494)
*Haohua Que,Mingkai Liu,Tianle Zhu,Zhipeng Bao,Yiping Li,Handong Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: When2Go 是一个在 CDA Class B 及以上（意图共享）层面实现的协同端到端驾驶系统，通过共享 LiDAR 实例 token、预测运动与计划轨迹，配合 route-time 冲突图与基于路权规则的 order gate 解决"谁先走"问题，在三个 CARLA 闭环基准上取得 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同端到端驾驶工作多停留在 SAE J3216 的 Class A（状态共享），车辆只交换"感知到什么"而非"打算做什么"，各自独立决策何时通行。这导致协同不足：双方都让行时陷入死锁，双方都通行时发生碰撞。核心问题在于缺乏对通行意图的共享与冲突排序机制。

Method: 1) 每辆车广播其 LiDAR 检测结果及预测运动（编码为 instance tokens），并附带自身规划与意图轨迹；2) 接收方将融合的检测、预测与伙伴计划投射为 route-time 冲突图（沿自身路径在规划时域上的占用图），规划器在其上为候选速度曲线打分；3) 当两条意图轨迹冲突时，由 order gate（从路权规则蒸馏出的优先级网络）裁决先后，双方基于相同消息评估，因而得到一致的通行顺序；4) 采用模仿学习，教师为可访问仿真真值的 V2Xverse 规则专家，并对 2 s 规划时域内无法观测到的停车进行重标注，避免模型学到无因等待；5) 实验设置上，基准 PID 控制器改为跟踪 When2Go 的 waypoints 而非路线，并关闭对长时间等待车辆的强制推进行为。

Result: 单一模型在三个闭环 CARLA 基准上均达 SOTA：InterDrive 无交通流 97.8、有交通流 90.7（已发表最佳 90.1 / 78.9）；公开 V2Xverse 协议 70.0（16 个复跑方法最佳 59.1）；MDrive 88.2（复跑最佳 85.1）。95.0% 的车辆无碰撞（对比 87.7%）。

Conclusion: 将协同从状态共享提升到意图共享（Class B+），并通过一致性 order gate 显式解决通行顺序冲突，是突破协同端到端驾驶死锁与碰撞困境的有效途径；配合针对不可观测停车的重标注，单一模型即可在多个闭环基准上显著超越既有方法。

Abstract: Existing cooperative end-to-end driving works at Class A (status-sharing) of the SAE J3216 classes of cooperative driving automation (CDA): vehicles exchange what they perceive, not what they intend, so each plans alone when to go, which limits cooperation and leads to deadlock when both vehicles yield or to collisions when both go. We present When2Go, a cooperative end-to-end driving system that learns when to go by sharing intent under CDA Class B or higher. Each vehicle broadcasts its LiDAR detections with forecast motion as instance tokens, together with its plan and intended trajectory. The receiver draws the fused detections, forecasts and partner plans into a route-time conflict map, the occupancy along its route over the planning horizon, on which a planner scores candidate speed profiles. Where two intended trajectories conflict, an order gate, a precedence network distilled from a right-of-way rule, decides who goes first; both vehicles evaluate it on the same messages and so reach the same order. When2Go imitates the rule-based expert of V2Xverse, which sees the simulator's ground truth; stops that When2Go cannot observe within its 2 s planning horizon are relabelled, so that it does not learn to wait without an observable cause. For our runs only, the benchmarks' PID controller steers by When2Go's waypoints instead of the route, and its forced push of long-waiting vehicles is off. With one model, When2Go sets the state of the art on three closed-loop CARLA benchmarks: 97.8 and 90.7 driving score on InterDrive without and with traffic (best published 90.1 and 78.9), 70.0 on the public V2Xverse protocol (best of sixteen rerun methods 59.1) and 88.2 on MDrive (best rerun 85.1), with 95.0% of its vehicles collision-free (87.7%).

</details>


### [243] [DeRep: Learning Depth Representations for End-to-End Navigation in Cluttered Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13499)
*Hanyu Jin,Kenji Shimada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DeRep：将局部BEV可达性监督与深度重建结合的表征预训练方法，冻结编码器后在线性探针、流匹配模仿学习与PPO三种下游学习方式下系统比较表征目标，并在Gazebo与真实四旋翼上实现零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端导航表征多与策略联合学习或依赖观测重建，未显式规定表征应保留何种信息；且通常只用单一策略学习器评估，无法判断表征收益是否依赖于策略学习方式。

Method: DeRep将局部鸟瞰图（BEV）可达性监督与深度重建联合作为预训练目标；为进行受控比较，各表征目标使用相同编码器架构预训练并在下游学习中冻结；下游评估采用线性策略探针、流匹配模仿学习和PPO强化学习，并在六个PPO条件下与纯重建基线对比。

Result: DeRep在线性策略探针上预测误差最低；在全部六个PPO条件下均优于纯重建；模仿学习中两种基于可达性的目标平均成功率高于纯重建，仅可达性与联合目标之间成功率相近。

Conclusion: 在深度表征预训练中引入可达性监督，可在不同下游学习方法与评估条件下带来一致收益；冻结的DeRep编码器配合PPO策略无需微调即可在Gazebo与真实四旋翼上零样本运行，具备sim-to-sim与sim-to-real迁移能力。

Abstract: End-to-end navigation in cluttered environments relies on compact visual representations to support collision avoidance and goal-directed motion. Existing methods typically learn these representations jointly with the policy or through observation reconstruction, which does not explicitly specify what information the representation should preserve. Moreover, they are usually evaluated with a single downstream policy learner, although the benefit of a representation may depend on how the policy is learned. It therefore remains unclear how different representation-learning objectives affect downstream navigation performance and robustness. This work proposes DeRep, a navigation-oriented representation-learning method that combines local bird's-eye-view reachability supervision with depth reconstruction. To compare representation objectives under controlled conditions, we pretrain encoders with a common architecture and freeze them during downstream learning. We evaluate the representations using a linear policy probe, flow-matching imitation learning, and reinforcement learning with PPO. DeRep achieves the lowest linear policy probe prediction error and outperforms reconstruction alone across all six tested PPO conditions. In imitation learning, both reachability-based objectives achieve higher mean success rates than reconstruction alone, with similar mean success rates between the reachability-only and joint objectives. These results demonstrate consistent benefits of incorporating reachability supervision into depth representation pretraining across different downstream learning methods and evaluation conditions. We further deploy the PPO policy with the frozen DeRep encoder in Gazebo and on a physical quadrotor without fine-tuning, demonstrating its capability for zero-shot sim-to-sim and sim-to-real transfer.

</details>


### [244] [Differentiable Filtering for Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13517)
*Yaru Gu,Ting Zou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DFIO将误差状态卡尔曼滤波的更新阶段嵌入计算图，以前后位置监督联合优化位移与协方差输出头，在行人数据集上显著降低TLIO的平移误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 惯性里程计因传感器噪声与零偏积分产生无界漂移；TLIO等方法的神经伪观测与下游滤波器独立训练，存在目标不一致的问题。

Method: 提出可微滤波惯性里程计框架：在独立预训练之后，将误差状态卡尔曼滤波的测量更新阶段直接嵌入计算图，通过可微测量更新以事后位置监督联合优化位移输出头与协方差输出头。

Result: 在公开行人运动数据集（含标定与未标定IMU）上验证：相较TLIO基线，平均绝对误差与1秒相对平移误差分别降低13.2%和39.6%；相较其预训练版本分别降低7.7%和17.4%；在未标定智能手机惯性数据的高噪声条件下可有效抑制平移漂移并保持轨迹一致性。

Conclusion: 端到端可微滤波使伪观测学习与滤波更新目标对齐，提升了惯性里程计的精度与鲁棒性；代码将公开。

Abstract: Accurate inertial odometry is critical for autonomous systems but suffers from unbounded drift caused by the integration of sensor noise and biases. In approaches such as TLIO, neural pseudo-measurements are trained independently of their downstream filter. This paper introduces Differentiable Filtering for Inertial Odometry (DFIO), a learning framework that embeds the update stages of an error-state Kalman filter directly into a computational graph. Following standalone pre-training, DFIO jointly optimizes the displacement and covariance output heads using posterior-position supervision through differentiable measurement updates. The proposed system is validated on public pedestrian motion datasets featuring both calibrated and uncalibrated inertial measurements. Experimental results on a pedestrian dataset demonstrate that DFIO reduces mean absolute and one-second relative translation errors by 13.2% and 39.6% relative to the TLIO baseline, and by 7.7% and 17.4% relative to its pre-trained counterpart. Furthermore, evaluations on uncalibrated smartphone inertial data indicate that the proposed method effectively mitigates translational drift and maintains trajectory alignment under highly noisy sensor conditions. Source code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [245] [GEMOR-GS: Global Extrinsic-calibration-refined Mapping and Online Relocalization for 3D Gaussian-Splatting SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13528)
*Yuchen Yang,Ming Ding,Xinyu Wu,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GEMOR-GS 通过“离线外参标定 + 关键帧位姿与高斯参数交替优化 + 提升在线约束后的全局重建 + 基于 XFeat/RANSAC-PnP/高斯 RGB-D warp 的重定位”，构建几何准确、全局一致、可重进入的 3DGS 地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向长时间连续作业的机器人，LiDAR–惯性–视觉 SLAM 需要照片级真实且度量一致的世界表征；3DGS 可提供显式、可渲染、可查询的场景模型，但现有在线高斯地图存在几何精度不足、全局一致性差、跟踪丢失后无法重新进入的问题。

Method: 1) 在专用标定段上用直接法对齐离线估计 LiDAR–相机外参，并复用于所有在线/离线流程；2) 在标定几何下，以门控的可微渲染 + LiDAR 投影联合目标，交替优化关键帧位姿与高斯参数；3) 解除在线约束，按精化后的关键帧模式重建最终全局地图；4) 重定位采用 XFeat bypass 地图检索、RANSAC-PnP 粗定位、高斯 RGB-D warp 精化三步。

Result: 在 1 个自采集 + 5 个公开序列上：离线重建在全部成对关键帧上优于在线地图 14.3–19.9 dB；PSNR 较 Gaussian-LIC2 提升约 9 dB；hold-out 重定位成功率达 86–100%，且与外参质量相关。

Conclusion: GEMOR-GS 提升了 3DGS 地图的几何精度、外观保真度与可重进入性，可作为可靠的机器人世界模型支撑 SLAM 及下游任务。

Abstract: Photorealistic and metrically consistent world representations are a primary requirement for LiDAR--inertial--visual SLAM systems that serve robots over continuous operation. 3D Gaussian splatting makes such representations attainable by supplying an explicit, renderable, and queryable scene model. We present GEMOR-GS (Global Extrinsic-calibration-refined Mapping and Online Relocalization for 3D Gaussian-Splatting SLAM) to make the Gaussian map geometrically accurate, globally refined, and re-enterable, providing a better appearance for robot perception and decision-making. To improve the 3DGS map, the LiDAR--camera extrinsic is first estimated offline by direct-method alignment over dedicated calibration segments and reused by all following online and offline processes. Under the calibrated geometry, keyframe poses and Gaussian parameters are refined alternately under a gated differentiable-rendering and LiDAR--projection objective. The final global reconstruction is then rebuilt from the refined keyframe schema with all online constraints lifted. For the robot SLAM lifecycle, the method recovers the pose from the 3DGS map after tracking loss through an XFeat bypass map, RANSAC-PnP coarse localization, and Gaussian RGB-D warp refinement. Experiments on one self-collected and five public sequences quantify each element: the offline reconstruction wins all paired keyframes against the online map by 14.3--19.9 dB, improves PSNR by approximately 9 dB over Gaussian-LIC2, and achieves 86--100% held-out relocalization success tied to extrinsic quality. The results show that GEMOR-GS improves the geometric accuracy, appearance fidelity, and re-enterability of the 3DGS map, serving as a dependable robot world model for SLAM and downstream tasks.

</details>


### [246] [Few-Shot Residual Adaptation of Pretrained Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13592)
*Jakub Suliga,Pankhuri Vanjani,Nisarga Nilavadi,Wolfram Burgard,Rudolf Lioutikov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种少样本残差自适应方法，在冻结预训练潜在世界模型的前提下，仅用少量交互回合学习自监督残差修正，同时作用于潜在预测器内部与预测的潜在状态，使模型在质心与摩擦等物理动力学变化下恢复高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练潜在世界模型可支撑视觉规划与控制，但其预测依赖训练时的物理动力学；部署时动力学（如质心、摩擦）发生变化会导致预测失效、成功率骤降。作者希望在既不修改预训练权重、也不显式辨识变化物理参数的条件下，仅依靠极少交互实现自适应。

Method: 双位点残差自适应：将预训练世界模型完全冻结，基于潜在预测误差进行自监督学习得到修正量；修正量分别施加于潜在预测器内部（输入端/内部环节）以及直接加在预测的潜在状态上。在仿真与真实机器人上，于质心位置偏移和摩擦系数改变两种条件下评估，并以3个交互回合作为自适应数据，同时进行单/双位点消融与诊断分析。

Result: 质心与摩擦变化使冻结模型成功率降至接近零；使用3个交互回合自适应后，双位点方法在真实机器人上于质心偏移条件下恢复90%成功率、在摩擦改变条件下恢复100%成功率。消融显示双位点修正优于任一位点的单独修正。诊断表明所学修正中很大一部分在补偿一般性的预测失配，而成功控制还依赖于自适应过程中捕获的、与具体变化相关的信息。

Conclusion: 少样本残差自适应可在不微调预训练权重、不显式辨识物理参数的情况下，显著恢复潜在世界模型在动力学变化下的规划与控制性能；且"预测器内部+潜在状态"双位点修正具有互补优势。同时提示修正中包含大量通用失配补偿，真正决定控制成败的是与具体动力学变化相关的信息，这为后续设计更具针对性的自适应机制提供了依据。

Abstract: Pretrained latent world models can support visual planning and control, but their predictions may fail when physical dynamics change at deployment. We study whether such models can adapt from only a few interactions without modifying their pretrained weights or identifying the changed physical parameters. We introduce a few-shot residual adaptation approach that learns self-supervised corrections from latent prediction errors. The corrections are applied both inside the latent predictor and directly to its predicted latent state, while the pretrained world model remains frozen. We evaluate the approach in simulation and on a real robot under center-of-mass and friction shifts. These changes reduce the success of the frozen model to near zero. Using three interaction episodes for adaptation, our dual-site approach recovers 90\% success under the center-of-mass shift and 100\% under the modified friction on the real robot. Ablations show that correcting both locations outperforms single-site adaptation. Our diagnostics further suggest that much of the learned correction reduces a general prediction mismatch, while successful control also depends on shift-specific information captured during adaptation.

</details>


### [247] [From Egocentric Human Video to Robot Manipulation in the Era of Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13610)
*Yi Wang,Jingqiu Wu,Lei Yao,Zhaoshuo Li,Haoyu Zhu,Yirui Zhou,Huaiyuan Xu,Ying Zheng,Chuang Yang,Lap-Pui Chau*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文定义"Ego2Robot学习"——从第一人称人类视频（EHV）中学习或适配机器人操作策略的过程，并归纳为四种范式：显式具身迁移、显式动作与任务监督、第一人称视频表征预训练、隐式动作与动力学建模；同时梳理相关数据集并指出五方面挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EHV记录近距手-物交互（HOI）与日常活动的时间结构，是可扩展的机器人操作经验来源；但缺乏机器动作标签，且存在形态、视角、动作空间差异，亟需厘清"从人类视频中提取什么、机器人策略如何使用、何时需要额外数据"。

Method: 综述性框架构建：按"从EHV派生何种监督"划分四类范式（机器人格式示范、可解释监督、视觉表征、动作与动力学的预测模型），逐一追踪EHV信息提取内容、策略使用方式及额外数据需求；并综述覆盖EHV、机器人示范、合成与仿真资源的基准数据集。

Result: 给出四范式对比框架，将EHV数据的贡献与其保有的任务信息和训练/部署中所用的机器人特定grounding相联结；汇总EHV—机器人—仿真三类数据资源。

Conclusion: EHV未充分利用，未来挑战集中于物理可行性、多智能体协同、上下文内适配、可控评测与预测控制五个方面。

Abstract: Egocentric human video (EHV) records close-range hand-object interaction (HOI) and the temporal structure of everyday activities, offering a potentially scalable source of experience for robot manipulation. Using this experience for robot control requires recovering useful supervision without recorded robot actions and accounting for differences in morphology, viewpoint, and action space. We define Ego2Robot learning as the process of learning or adapting robot manipulation policies from EHV, organized into four learning paradigms: explicit embodiment transfer, explicit action and task supervision, egocentric video representation pretraining, and latent action and dynamics modeling. The four paradigms differ in what they derive from human video: robot-format demonstrations, interpretable supervision, visual representations, or predictive models of actions and dynamics. For each paradigm, we trace what EHV information is extracted, how robot policies use it, and when the additional data is required. Besides, we review the benchmark datasets that cover EHV, robot demonstrations, and synthetic and simulated resources. This paper links EHV data's contribution to the task information preserved and the robot-specific grounding used during training or deployment. Finally, we identify challenges in physical feasibility, agent coordination, in-context adaptation, controlled evaluation, and predictive control.

</details>


### [248] [An IoT Based Approach for Platform Independent Positioning Service](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13634)
*Anh-Van Vu,Dongsoo Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 KAILOS 的扩展架构，利用 IoT 设备替代移动 App 提供室内定位服务，并给出设备软硬件设计，验证 IoT 方案可行且有前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原 KAILOS（2014 年发布的基于 WiFi 的完整室内定位方案）依赖移动 App 提供定位服务，而移动 App 受操作系统诸多限制，形成应用推广的障碍。

Method: 设计 KAILOS 的扩展架构，引入 IoT 设备作为定位服务载体，绕过移动 App 限制；同时给出该 IoT 设备的硬件与软件设计方案。

Result: 通过实现与验证表明，基于 IoT 的方法具有可行性，能消除移动 App 方式的障碍并更便捷地提供室内定位服务。

Conclusion: IoT 驱动方案是室内定位服务的重要特性，可拓宽其应用范围；但摘要未给出具体定位精度与实验数据。

Abstract: KAIST Indoor Localization System (KAILOS) was introduced in 2014 and became one of the world-first complete indoor positioning solutions. It is mainly based on the WiFi signal to estimate location and provide positioning service via mobile application that has many restrictions by the Operating System. This study proposes an extended architecture of KAILOS to make use of the IoT device for removing the barriers of the mobile-app-based approach and conveniently providing indoor positioning service. Design of the IoT device that contains hardware and software is also presented. Via this study, we prove that the IoT-based approach is promising and can be a crucial feature to widen applications of indoor localization services.

</details>


### [249] [NIF-VIO: Visual--Inertial Odometry Across Inertial and Non-Inertial Reference Frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13662)
*Zichao Shu,Lijun Li,Zhuochen Li,Zetao Chen,Jianyu Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出NIF-VIO，一种可在惯性与非惯性参考系下工作的视觉—惯性里程计框架，通过基于量测一致性的自适应融合，提升非惯性条件下的定位精度与可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统VIO假设相机与IMU观测在同一惯性参考系下联合一致，但在加速或旋转载体内部该假设失效：视觉观测反映载体相对运动，IMU测量含载体诱导分量，导致常规融合退化。

Method: 1) 依据量测一致性自适应调整视觉—惯性融合；2) 惯性条件变化时，采用早期约束隔离结合局部重优化，在判定不一致前抑制误差传播并修复受影响的局部几何；3) 非惯性条件下引入时间共享参数的紧凑模型，建模载体运动，同时保留与视觉约束互补的惯性信息。

Result: 在公开基准与自采集数据集上，惯性条件下性能具竞争力，非惯性条件下定位精度与可用性均提升。

Conclusion: NIF-VIO通过一致性驱动的自适应融合与载体运动建模，跨越惯性/非惯性参考系实现相对局部环境的稳健定位。

Abstract: Conventional visual--inertial odometry (VIO) assumes that observations from cameras and an inertial measurement unit (IMU) are jointly consistent in an inertial reference frame. However, this assumption breaks down under non-inertial conditions. Inside accelerating or rotating carriers, visual observations of the interior reflect carrier-relative motion, while IMU measurements contain additional carrier-induced components, which can compromise conventional fusion. We therefore present NIF-VIO for localization relative to the local environment across inertial and non-inertial reference frames. It adapts visual--inertial fusion based on measurement consistency. As inertial conditions change, early constraint isolation is combined with local reoptimization to limit error propagation before inconsistency confirmation and repair the affected local geometry. Under non-inertial conditions, a compact model with temporally shared parameters is introduced to account for carrier motion while preserving inertial information that complements visual constraints. Experiments on public benchmarks and a self-collected dataset demonstrate that NIF-VIO achieves competitive performance under inertial conditions and improved localization accuracy and availability under non-inertial conditions.

</details>


### [250] [FAME-QP: A GPU-Accelerated Quadratic-Programming Solver for Real-Time Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13766)
*Santosh Mohan Rajkumar,Debdipta Goswami,Stephanie Stockar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FAME-QP 是一个面向线性模型预测控制中稠密二次规划的 GPU 加速求解器：它结合约束相关的算子表示、经 CUDA Graph 回放的单/双核融合迭代，并通过设备标定与问题级计时自动选择执行配置；算子在矩阵变化时才惰性更新，初始惩罚由缓存算子库探测确定。基准测试与人形/四旋翼仿真控制实验显示其在保持解精度与控制性能的同时降低了相对竞品 GPU 求解器的延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性 MPC 需要在严格实时约束下反复求解同结构的稠密 QP，单次求解延迟直接决定控制频率上限。现有 GPU QP 求解器在核函数启动开销、算子重建与执行配置选择上存在冗余，难以稳定满足高频控制回路的时延要求。

Method: 1) 采用约束依赖的算子表示（operator representation），使迭代中重复使用的矩阵结构预先构造并复用；2) 提供融合的单核/双核迭代实现，并用 CUDA Graph 回放以消除逐迭代的启动开销；3) 通过设备标定（device calibration）结合具体问题的计时，自动选择最优执行配置（核变体与网格/分块方案）；4) 维护缓存算子库以探测初始惩罚参数；5) 仅在问题矩阵变化时惰性更新算子。

Result: 在数值基准测试中，相较其他 GPU QP 求解器取得更低求解延迟；在仿真人形机器人与四旋翼控制任务中，在保持解精度与控制性能的前提下降低了在线求解时延。

Conclusion: 工程层面的关键收益来自"算子复用+图化融合迭代+自适应配置选择"的组合，而非新的 QP 算法本身；该设计对结构不变的稠密 MPC-QP 场景收益显著，其通用性与稀疏/大规模问题的适用性尚待验证。

Abstract: We present FAME-QP, a GPU-accelerated solver for dense quadratic programs arising in linear model predictive control. FAME-QP combines constraint-dependent operator representations with fused single- and two-kernel iterations replayed through CUDA graphs, automatically selecting the execution configuration through device calibration and problem-specific timing. It probes a cached operator bank to select the initial penalty and updates operators lazily when problem matrices change. Numerical benchmarks and simulated humanoid and quadrotor control experiments demonstrate reduced latency relative to competing GPU solvers while maintaining solution accuracy and control performance.

</details>


### [251] [From Depth to Safe Depth: Uncertainty-Aware Monocular Perception for Mobile Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13769)
*Saeid Firouzi Daghigh,Saeed Ayat*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 离线单目深度基础模型直接用于导航不安全：Depth Anything V2 驱动 Nav2 时63%情节碰撞；提出0.58M参数的不确定性头输出对数深度校正与σ，用单侧置信下界"safe depth"供导航消费，碰撞降至3%、成功率37%→70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用单目RGB相机替代移动机器人的深度传感器具有成本与硬件优势，但现有深度基础模型"开箱即用"时存在系统性近距高估（对近处障碍距离估计偏大），导致导航碰撞。作者要验证并量化这一安全隐患，并提供轻量修复方案。

Method: (1) 在仿真室内导航基准中用标准Nav2栈+Depth Anything V2(metric)复现失效；(2) 在冻结的深度模型上附加0.58 M参数头，逐像素预测对数深度校正μ与不确定性σ，以β加权高斯负对数似然训练；(3) 导航栈使用单侧下界 d_safe = d·exp(μ − kσ)，k由留出世界上的保形校准确定；σ同时用于判定可通行地面像素；(4) 与校准到同等像素覆盖率的常数安全裕度作对照，并做配对情节比较。

Result: AbsRel 0.262→0.134；近距高估率59.8%→12.0%；导航成功率37%→70%，碰撞63%→3%（真值深度基线2%）；常数安全裕度仅30%成功率（机器人冻结），在56个不一致配对情节中不确定性感知方案胜52个；增益在玻璃、无纹理墙面与暗区仍成立。

Conclusion: 单目深度基础模型需经不确定性感知的单侧安全校正才能安全用于导航；该轻量即插即用模块在精度与安全性上均显著提升，并优于无差别的常数安全裕度，具备跨材质/跨场景鲁棒性。

Abstract: Monocular depth foundation models make it tempting to replace depth sensors on mobile robots with a single RGB camera. We show that, used off the shelf, this is unsafe: in a simulated indoor navigation benchmark, a standard Nav2 stack driven by Depth Anything V2 (metric) collides in 63\% of 120 episodes, because the model systematically over-estimates the distance to nearby obstacles. We propose a lightweight plug-in module that turns predicted depth into \emph{safe depth}. A 0.58\,M-parameter head on top of the \emph{frozen} depth model predicts, per pixel, a log-depth correction $μ$ and an uncertainty $σ$, trained with a $β$-weighted Gaussian negative log-likelihood. The navigation stack consumes the one-sided bound $\hat d_{\text{safe}}=d\,e^{μ-kσ}$, where $k$ is set by conformal calibration on a held-out world, and uncertainty also decides which pixels may be treated as floor. The correction halves the depth error (AbsRel $0.262\rightarrow0.134$) and reduces near-range over-estimation from 59.8\% to 12.0\%. In navigation, safe depth raises the success rate from 37\% to 70\% and lowers collisions from 63\% to 3\%, close to the 2\% of ground-truth depth. A constant safety margin calibrated to the same pixel coverage reaches only 30\% success because it freezes the robot; uncertainty-aware safety wins 52 of 56 discordant paired episodes against it. Gains hold on glass, texture-less walls and dark regions.

</details>


### [252] [Softness is something you also need! Why compliance helps control: information metrics of a passive walker with compliant ankle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13779)
*Fabio Bonsignorio,Elisabetta Barletta,Sorin Dragomir,Francesco Esposito,Enrica Zereik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文是一篇技术性综述/研究笔记，主张机器人学应从"笛卡尔式"自上而下控制转向基于形态计算与涌现的生物学范式，并用信息度量与动力学关联的数学结果统一已有工作。作者以倒立摆与被动动态行走模型为对象，计算相空间可行体积W(γ)：从McGeer罗盘步态出发，引入膝-踝柔顺、足摇滚效应及Ruina型分布惯量罗盘步态，推导标准足跟碰撞映射，并用状态依赖蒙特卡洛与再生核希尔伯特空间(RKHS)方法估计W(γ)，指出RKHS方法能提供更深入细致的分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能机器人与自然进化出的动物、植物在适应性、柔顺性与灵巧性上仍存在巨大差距。作者认为差距的根源可能是"范式"层面的：现有刚性与软体机器人多沿用笛卡尔式设计，强调自上而下的控制与清晰的心身分离；而生物系统的组织原则依赖形态计算，以及由松散耦合的具身智能体涌现出适配行为。为在完全或部分柔顺的人工智能体中实现建模与控制，并为理解自然界的"物理智能"提供基础，需要从根本上新的数学建模与控制途径。

Method: 1) 回顾并阐释若干将信息度量与智能体动力学相联系的奠基性数学结果，并将其与本团队及社区近期方法整合；2) 以被动动态行走作为研究载体，构建相空间体积计算框架：从McGeer罗盘步态出发，逐步引入膝-踝柔顺、足摇滚(foot-rocker)效应，以及Ruina式分布腿部惯量罗盘步态；3) 推导标准足跟碰撞(heel-strike)映射；4) 基于该映射，在状态依赖的蒙特卡洛采样与再生核希尔伯特空间(RKHS)方法下估计可行相空间体积W(γ)，并比较两类估计方法的表现。

Result: 给出了倒立摆与被动动态行走模型的相空间体积计算技术笔记；推导了含柔顺与摇滚足效应的罗盘步态的标准足跟碰撞映射，并以之作为可行相空间体积W(γ)估计的基础；状态依赖蒙特卡洛与RKHS两类估计方法均可行，其中基于RKHS的方法能够对W(γ)进行更深入、更细致的分析。

Conclusion: 以形态计算与涌现为核心的生物学范式，配合信息度量与动力学的数学联系，可作为建模与控制完全或部分柔顺人工智能体的具体且高效的途径，并有助于更好理解自然界的物理智能；相空间体积W(γ)及其RKHS估计为该框架提供了可操作的分析工具，作者并给出了当前与近期工作的方向。

Abstract: A great gap in adaptivity, flexibility, and dexterity still exists between intelligent robots, whether they are made of rigid or (partially) compliant components, and their naturally evolved animal and vegetal counterparts. One reason could be of `paradigm' nature. On the one hand, most robots (both rigid and soft) are still designed by following a `Cartesian' approach, with top-down control and a clear mind-body separation. Designing artificial agents on the basis of the principles that we observe in biology is not an easy task. The principles of organization of animals and plants rely on morphological computation and emergence of fit behaviors from loosely coupled networks of embodied agents.
  Modeling and control require radically new approaches. In this paper we briefly recall and explain some seminal mathematical results linking information metrics and dynamics of the agents, already published years ago; we integrate them with some recent methods developed by us and by the community, we highlight some brief remarks about their relevance and we hint to our current and near future work towards finding concrete and efficient ways for the modeling and control of completely or partially compliant artificial agents and for a better understanding of physical intelligence in nature. We present a technical note summarizing phase-space volume calculations for inverted-pendulum and passive-dynamic walking models, following our proposed framework. Starting from the McGeer compass gait, we introduce knee-ankle compliance, foot-rocker effects, and a Ruina-style compass gait with distributed leg inertia. Standard heel-strike maps are derived and used in the estimation of viable phase-space volumes $W(γ)$, based on a state-dependent Monte-Carlo and Reproducing Kernel Hilbert Spaces methods. We show how the methods based on RKHS allow deeper and more subtle analysis.

</details>


### [253] [RiSE-Drive: Risk-Sensitive Long-Horizon Candidate Evaluation for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13790)
*Yu Zhao,Hongyi Duan,Ming Yin,Hongyuan Zhang,Fan Yang,Hongzhi Wang,Xiubo Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RiSE-Drive提出面向端到端自动驾驶的风险敏感长时程候选轨迹评估框架：先做候选条件化的潜在未来rollout，再估计长时程结果分布而非单一标量分数，最后用尾部风险感知且扰动鲁棒的决策准则排序候选。在NAVSIM-v1/v2与闭环HUGSIM上取得SOTA并显著提升候选选择鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶普遍采用"生成多候选轨迹—选一条执行"的范式，此时核心瓶颈已从候选生成转向评估。现有评估多依赖确定性点分数、启发式子分数或单一结果评估，难以刻画延迟失效、尾部风险以及合理未来扰动下的排序不稳定问题。

Method: RiSE-Drive为三阶段框架：(1) 候选条件化的潜在未来rollout（candidate-conditioned latent future rollout）；(2) 估计长时程结果分布以替代单一标量打分；(3) 采用尾部风险感知（tail-risk-aware）且扰动鲁棒（perturbation-robust）的决策准则对候选排序。本质上将候选打分从确定性"oracle式"评估重构为带显式风险控制的分布式结果评估。

Result: 在NAVSIM-v1、NAVSIM-v2及闭环HUGSIM基准上达到SOTA，并显著提升候选选择的鲁棒性；评估器倾向于选择不仅在标称条件下表现强、且在长时程rollout与安全关键偏差下仍可靠的候选。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将候选评估从确定性点估计转为分布化、风险受控的长时程评估，是提升端到端自动驾驶轨迹选择可靠性的有效途径，为"候选生成之后的评估问题"提供了新的建模范式。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving systems increasingly rely on generating multiple trajectory candidates and selecting one for execution. In this setting, a central challenge is no longer only candidate generation, but how to evaluate candidates according to their long-horizon consequences under uncertainty. Existing methods still mainly use deterministic point scores, heuristic subscores, or single-outcome evaluations, which makes it difficult to capture delayed failures, tail risk, and ranking instability under plausible future perturbations. We present RiSE-Drive, a framework for risk-sensitive long-horizon candidate evaluation in end-to-end autonomous driving. RiSE-Drive first performs candidate-conditioned latent future rollout, then estimates long-horizon outcome distributions instead of single scalar scores, and finally ranks candidates using a tail-risk-aware and perturbation-robust decision criterion. This reformulates candidate scoring from deterministic oracle-style evaluation into distributional outcome assessment with explicit risk control. As a result, the evaluator is encouraged to prefer candidates that are not only strong under nominal conditions, but also reliable under long-horizon rollout and safety-critical deviations. Experiments on the NAVSIM-v1 and NAVSIM-v2 benchmarks, as well as the closed-loop HUGSIM benchmark, show that RiSE-Drive achieves state-of-the-art performance and significantly improves the robustness of candidate selection.

</details>


### [254] [Camera equalisation of an 11.5x drop in environmental illuminance: four imitation-learning policies on an SO-101 arm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13810)
*Simon Cowling*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文指出真实机器人实验中"房间照度(lux)"并不能代表策略实际看到的图像光照：在自动曝光/白平衡下，环境光下降11.5倍仅使图像亮度轻微下降，四种模仿学习策略成功率无显著变化。建议报告并控制相机设置与图像统计量，而非仅报告lux。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人视觉运动策略基准常以房间照度作为光照条件的度量并假定其能反映输入图像的光照。作者质疑这一前提，希望验证：在典型低成本平台上，房间光与图像光是否相关，以及lux变化是否真的影响策略表现。

Method: 在SO-101机械臂上进行抓取放置任务，采集同一组50条示教；训练ACT、SmolVLA、Diffusion Policy与pi0.5四种模仿学习策略。每个策略分别在约520 lux与约45 lux（降低11.5倍）下各做10次rollout（共40次/条件）。对比成功率并用交互项检验策略间灵敏度差异；同时测量前后相机图像平均亮度。相机采用自动曝光与自动白平衡，未锁定曝光；另有一次10-rollout区块的意外重复实验。

Result: 正常光照成功率20/40，低光照19/40，变化-2.5个百分点（95% CI -23至+19）；策略间灵敏度无差异证据（交互p=0.86）。图像平均亮度在低光下前相机仅下降3%，腕部相机下降19%，推断被自动曝光/白平衡吸收。意外重复的10次区块成功率与其原区块一致（7/10），但仅6/10的放置位置相同，符合随机水平。

Conclusion: 仅用lux无法描述策略实际观测到的视觉输入。应报告并控制相机曝光/白平衡设置及与训练录像对比的图像统计量；当前结果为单任务单平台、曝光未锁定条件下的证据，需进一步在锁定曝光与更多任务上验证。

Abstract: Real-robot benchmarks of learned visuomotor policies often record room illuminance as the lighting condition. We show that, on a typical low-cost rig, room light and image light can be almost unrelated. Four imitation-learning policies (ACT, SmolVLA, Diffusion Policy and pi0.5) were trained on the same 50 demonstrations of a pick-and-place task on an SO-101 arm. Each was run for ten rollouts at about 520 lux and again at about 45 lux, an 11.5x reduction. Success was 20/40 in normal light and 19/40 in low light: a change of -2.5 percentage points (95% CI -23 to +19), with no evidence that the policies differed in sensitivity (interaction p = 0.86). The recorded video shows why: mean image brightness was only 3% lower in low light on the front camera and 19% lower on the wrist camera. Both cameras were set to automatic exposure and white balance, which most likely absorbed the difference; exposure was not locked in this experiment. An accidental repeat of one 10-rollout block reproduced its success count (7/10) but matched the first block at only 6 of 10 placements, consistent with chance. We conclude that lux alone does not describe what a policy sees. Camera settings, and image statistics compared with the training recordings, are the quantities to report and control. Data and code are public.

</details>


### [255] [WAM is capable in BEHAVIOR-1K: the 2nd-place solution to the 2026 BEHAVIOR Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13818)
*Yelin He,Jiaquan Ye,Xinming Wei,Wenjie Xiao,Gengming Zhang,Guoting Wei,Xianbiao Qi,Rong Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出2026 BEHAVIOR挑战赛第二名方案（Team IF1001），基于世界-动作模型Cosmos 3，通过更丰富视觉历史、任务进度条件化、任务组/技能特定LoRA适配器、进度估计与路由选择、动作候选聚合等改进，在无闭源模型、无RSI/agentic harness条件下实现每端口>2FPS，私有测试集平均Q-score 0.615、全任务成功率41.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BEHAVIOR挑战评估100项家庭任务的移动操作，需要单一系统在长时程、多任务、多技能场景下兼顾成功率与推理实时性（≥1 FPS），且受限于硬件与不允许使用闭源模型、RSI及agentic harness方法。作者旨在构建一个可在受限资源下并发服务100端口的强基线系统。

Method: 以世界动作模型Cosmos 3为骨干，从三方面增强：(1) 输入更丰富的视觉历史；(2) 引入任务进度条件化；(3) 为任务组与具体技能分别训练LoRA适配器。推理阶段使用独立的进度估计器提供任务进度上下文，按任务与进度进行条件化路由以选择任务组或技能特定策略；并辅以动作候选聚合、rolling inpainting，以及任务特定的时间控制与夹爪控制。

Result: 在100个任务、3轮自评测中平均Q-score 0.613、全任务成功率40.9%；官方私有测试集（每任务20个实例）平均Q-score 0.615、全任务成功率41.2%。官方评测中在有限硬件下并发处理100个端口的请求，每端口平均端到端推理速率超过2 FPS，满足1 FPS最低要求。

Conclusion: 将强世界动作模型与进度条件化、分层LoRA专业化及推理期路由/聚合策略结合，可在不开源模型依赖、不使用RSI与agentic harness的前提下，于受限硬件上实现可扩展的高并发实时移动操作，并在BEHAVIOR基准上取得有竞争力的成绩（第二名）。

Abstract: This work presents the 2nd-place solution (Team IF1001) to the 2026 BEHAVIOR Challenge, which evaluates mobile manipulation across 100 household tasks. Our method is built on a powerful world action model (WAM), Cosmos 3. We improve Cosmos 3 by using richer visual history, task-progress conditioning, and task-group and skill-specific LoRA adapters. During inference, we apply a separate progress estimator to supply task-progress context, and use task- and progress-conditioned routing to select a task-group or skill-specific policy, complemented by action-candidate aggregation, rolling inpainting, and task-specific temporal and gripper control. To meet the minimum inference requirement of 1 FPS, we use neither recursive self-improvement (RSI) nor agentic harness methods and rely on no closed-source models; during official private-test evaluation, our system handled concurrent requests across 100 evaluation ports with limited hardware resources, sustaining an average end-to-end inference rate above 2 FPS per port. Our method achieves a mean Q-score of 0.613 and a full-task success rate of 40.9%, averaged over 3 self-evaluation rounds across all 100 tasks. On the official private test set, with 20 instances per task, our system achieves a mean Q-score of 0.615 and a full-task success rate of 41.2%.

</details>


### [256] [Game-Enhanced Embodied Learning with Event and Foresight Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13819)
*Lianlei Shan,Han Jin,Yaunchao Ma,Xinpeng Chen,Linhao Zhou,Xin Bin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 通过人类游戏数据的事件预测与基于冻结视频模型的视觉预测进行预训练，可将LIBERO四套件总成功率从85.90%提升至90.55%（+4.65个百分点），但任务级结果异质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类游戏过程天然提供成对的观测、动作与视觉后果，可作为机器人策略训练的补充数据；作者希望验证"完整游戏预训练阶段"在实际流程中的实用价值。

Method: 面向InternVLA-A1.5提出事件-预见式（event-and-foresight）预训练：联合预测录制的键盘/鼠标事件，并以冻结视频模型提供历史条件化的视觉监督。采用覆盖保持的锚点选择（coverage-preserving anchor selection）构造1秒目标片段，并用注意力掩码阻止teacher-forcing的未来动作泄漏进预见查询。语料含20款游戏、394段录制、时间戳跨度223.36小时、2,072,084个训练锚点；经12,400步游戏预训练后，在统一的套件专用机器人微调协议下与公开基座模型比较（LIBERO四个套件）。

Result: 聚合成功率由1,718/2,000（85.90%）升至1,811/2,000（90.55%），提升4.65个百分点；Spatial、Object、Goal、Long分别提升9.2、6.8、0.2、2.4个百分点。40个任务中24项提升、10项下降、6项持平。

Conclusion: 结果支持完整游戏预训练阶段在该评测设定下的实用价值，同时揭示任务级效果异质；且对比包含额外优化，未能分离游戏数据、两个预训练目标及训练随机性各自的贡献。

Abstract: Human gameplay supplies paired observations, actions, and visual consequences that can supplement robot-policy training. We study event-and-foresight pretraining for InternVLA-A1.5, combining recorded keyboard/mouse event prediction with history-conditioned visual supervision through a frozen video model. Coverage-preserving anchor selection constructs one-second targets, and an attention mask prevents teacher-forced future actions from entering foresight queries. The selected corpus contains 394 recordings across 20 games, with 223.36 hours of recorded timestamp span and 2,072,084 training anchors. After 12,400 game-pretraining updates, we compare the resulting initialization with the public base under the same suite-specific robot fine-tuning protocol. Across four LIBERO suites, aggregate success increases from 1,718/2,000 (85.90%) to 1,811/2,000 (90.55%), a gain of 4.65 percentage points. Suite gains are 9.2, 6.8, 0.2, and 2.4 points on Spatial, Object, Goal, and Long. The 40-task breakdown shows 24 improvements, 10 declines, and six ties. These results support the practical value of the complete game-pretraining stage in this evaluated setting, while revealing heterogeneous task outcomes. The comparison includes additional optimization and does not isolate the effects of game data, the two objectives, or training variability.

</details>


### [257] [RedirectMimic: Local Motion Content Redaction for Humanoid Whole-Body Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14049)
*Zepeng Wang,Xiaochuan Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RedirectMimic 将 actor 观测的参考与训练执行的目标解耦：用规则生成的"编辑参考"引导 PPO 跟踪奖励，而部署时的观测与动作接口保持不变，从而在保留参考输入的同时对指定动作做局部替换（如手臂内容遮蔽）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形动作接口需在保留有用参考行为的同时支持应用特定的运动约束。局部内容遮蔽要求控制器在仍接收原始参考的情况下替换某些动作，但仅去掉这些动作的跟踪激励会使其替代行为不受约束（未指定）。

Method: 提出 RedirectMimic：分离 actor 观察到的参考与用于训练其执行的目标。在 PPO 中用规则生成的编辑参考引导跟踪奖励；部署阶段的观测与动作接口与原始设置一致。在 Unitree G1 仿真中，基于 7,509 条动作片段预训练的 tracker，评估姿态定义与速度定义两类手臂编辑任务，并与奖励置零（reward zeroing）和反向跟踪（anti-tracking）比较。

Result: 举臂任务中，核心窗口内容出现率降至 2.7%（奖励置零 58.9%，反向跟踪 32.8%）；核心窗口联合成功率 94.2%（兼顾内容抑制、无跌倒执行与局部保留）。速度定义任务中，三种局部编辑方法的快速向外手臂运动发生率均同样低。两任务上 RedirectMimic 的替换误差最低，且对未编辑动作的跟踪精度与预训练控制器相当。

Conclusion: 通过参考—目标解耦实现"观察原参考、训练编辑目标"的局部动作编辑，RedirectMimic 在有效抑制指定内容的同时保持未编辑行为与通用跟踪能力，为带约束的人形动作接口提供可用方案。

Abstract: Humanoid motion interfaces should accommodate application-specific movement constraints while retaining useful reference behavior. Local content redaction requires a controller to replace selected movements even when it continues to receive the original reference. Removing the tracking incentive for those movements leaves their replacement unspecified. We introduce RedirectMimic, which separates the reference observed by the actor from the target used to train its execution. A rule-generated edited reference guides tracking rewards during proximal policy optimization, while the deployed observation and action interfaces remain unchanged. We evaluate pose- and speed-defined arm editing in Unitree G1 simulation using a tracker pretrained on 7,509 motion clips. On the overhead-arm task, RedirectMimic reduces core-window content occurrence to 2.7%, compared with 58.9% for reward zeroing and 32.8% for anti-tracking. It achieves 94.2% core-window joint success, combining content suppression, fall-free execution, and local retention. On the speed-defined task, fast outward arm movements occurred at similarly low rates under all three local editing methods. Across both tasks, RedirectMimic achieved the lowest replacement error among these methods and tracked unedited motions with accuracy comparable to that of the pretrained controller. Project page: https://xiaopeng-whu.github.io/RedirectMimic/.

</details>


### [258] [A human-in-the-loop Control Paradigm for Construction Robotics: From VR-Guided Teleoperation to Semi-Autonomous Dual-Arm Operation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14062)
*Jinhua Chen,Xiaoji Xu,Zhongyuan Liao,Huang Zhenmin,Zhang Ye,Changying Xiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种“人在环”控制范式，使VR遥操作、半自主共享控制与规则化自主执行三种模式可在接触密集的双臂建筑任务中结构化切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 危险非结构化环境下的建筑任务既需人的适应性又需机器人精度，但现有方法常将遥操作与自主控制视为彼此独立的控制模式，缺乏统一的人机角色分配与模式衔接机制。

Method: 系统由三部分集成：沉浸式VR引导；人工定义的可复用技能原语库；带显式人机角色分配与指令优先级规则的共享自主框架。由此在VR引导遥操作、半自主共享控制、基于规则的自主执行之间实现结构化转换，操作者保留监督与纠错权限，重复性与精度关键动作交由机器人执行。在双臂平台上以典型幕墙安装任务验证。

Result: 在标定良好的名义条件下，自主执行性能最佳；当环境扰动超出预定义自主流程的纠错能力时，半自主控制表现出更强的韧性；与完全遥操作相比，半自主控制显著降低了操作者主观工作负荷。

Conclusion: 有效的建筑机器人控制应按任务与环境不确定性程度调节人的介入程度：可重复操作采用自主执行，扰动恢复采用共享控制。

Abstract: Construction tasks in hazardous and unstructured environments require both human adaptability and robotic precision, yet existing approaches often treat teleoperation and autonomy as separate control modes. This paper presents a human-in-the-loop control paradigm that enables structured transitions among VR-guided teleoperation, semi-autonomous shared control, and rule-based autonomous execution for contact-rich dual-arm construction tasks. The proposed system combines immersive VR guidance, a manually defined library of reusable skill primitives, and a shared-autonomy framework with explicit human-robot role allocation and command-priority rules. This integration allows operators to retain supervisory and corrective authority while delegating repetitive and precision-critical actions to the robot. The paradigm is validated on a dual-arm platform through representative facade-installation tasks. Results show that autonomous execution provides the best performance under well-calibrated nominal conditions, whereas semi-autonomous control improves resilience when environmental disturbances exceed the predefined autonomous pipeline's correction capability. Semi-autonomous control also substantially reduces subjective operator workload compared with full teleoperation. These findings demonstrate that effective construction-robot control should adapt human involvement to task and environmental uncertainty, using autonomy for repeatable operations and shared control for disturbance recovery.

</details>


### [259] [DexSeed: Expanding Human Hand-Object Interactions into Robot Demonstrations for Dexterous Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14092)
*Huaijin Pi,Zehong Shen,Yong Wang,Kechun Xu,Changpeng Wang,Dingkun Wei,Xueyang Wang,Wenjia Wang,Xing Zhu,Yujun Shen,Taku Komura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexSeed：从人手-物体交互数据低成本生成多样化、物理可执行的灵巧机器人演示数据，用于VLA微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧操作演示依赖昂贵的遥操作；人手数据集虽可复用，但现有迁移方法仅复现给定交互，缺乏对不同物体、操作位置、可执行臂/指动作与视觉观测的覆盖，难以直接作为VLA训练数据。

Method: 按抓取模式并配共享语言描述组织源序列；采用InteractionMesh重定向与手臂轨迹增广，在保持局部手-物关系前提下适配不同物体几何与抓放位置；物理仿真中手指由强化学习控制策略生成可执行rollout，手臂跟踪预计算轨迹；将成功rollout的渲染观测与动作配对抓取描述，用于VLA微调。

Result: 重定向的接触保真度优于评估基线，全臂执行更可靠；仿真评测与真实部署表明，仅用生成演示微调的VLA可执行语言指定的抓取。

Conclusion: 通过"保留抓取关系+轨迹增广+仿真RL执行"的框架，可将人手交互数据规模化为多样、可执行的灵巧机器人VLA训练数据，降低数据采集成本。

Abstract: We study how to generate diverse, physically executable robot demonstrations for dexterous vision-language-action (VLA) learning. Existing demonstration-generation methods typically rely on costly teleoperated examples. Meanwhile, human hand--object datasets provide a reusable source, but existing transfer methods mainly reproduce given interactions. Turning these interactions into VLA training data further requires coverage of different objects and manipulation locations, together with executable arm and finger actions, and visual observations. To achieve this, we present DexSeed, a framework for low-cost generation of dexterous robot data from human hand--object interactions. Our key idea is to expand human sequences while preserving their grasps and realizing the resulting motions under arm-tracking dynamics in physics simulation. Specifically, we organize source sequences by grasp mode with shared language descriptions. InteractionMesh-based retargeting and arm trajectory augmentation adapt these sequences to the robot hand, different object geometries, and pick-and-place locations while preserving local hand--object relationships. An RL finger-control policy produces executable rollouts, while the arm tracks precomputed trajectories. Rendered observations and recorded actions from successful rollouts are paired with grasp descriptions to fine-tune a VLA. Experiments show improved contact fidelity in retargeting and more reliable whole-arm execution than the evaluated baselines. Simulation evaluation and real-world deployment further demonstrate that a VLA fine-tuned solely on the generated demonstrations can execute language-specified grasps. Project page: https://robbyant-research.github.io/DexSeed/.

</details>


### [260] [Model-Free Runtime Measurement of Task-Relevant Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14111)
*Dimitris Panagopoulos,Adolfo Perrusquía,Yang Xing,Weisi Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Acquisition-State Telemetry（AST）：一个模型无关的架构，用域接口把观测映射到任务解析坐标与归一化比较表示，再由固定因果监视器输出"前瞻进度"与"信息摩擦"两路实时信号，用于判断信息采集过程本身是否在正常推进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作能规划信息寻求动作、能解释已获观测，但缺少对"信息采集这一进行中的过程是否运转正常"的监控；导致系统可能持续保持运行状态却在原地打转，无法解决未完成的使命需求。

Method: 模型无关架构：域接口将原始观测映射为任务解析坐标和归一化对比表示；一个固定的因果监视器处理这些表示，输出两路连续实时信号——(i) 针对未满足需求的前瞻性进度；(ii) 反复证据带来可忽略信息增益时产生的信息摩擦。同一套未调参的监视器配置在八个领域（搜救对话、空间网格探索、分层能力获取、连续机器人操作、概率意图估计等）上统一评估；并在带提前终止约束的闭环强化学习实验中，将信息摩擦作为惩罚项。

Result: 跨八个领域，摩擦通道能在任务完成前区分"无产出的停滞"与"有目的的探索"，并在任务完成后的活动中保持低响应；闭环RL中，惩罚信息摩擦的智能体维持高任务成功率，而未加惩罚的策略与运营成本匹配的基线均未能完成所需任务。

Conclusion: 监控信息采集过程本身的状态是可实现且可跨域迁移的，信息摩擦信号可作为通用、可复用的实时诊断与学习信号，用于识别停滞并驱动系统完成使命需求。

Abstract: Autonomous systems increasingly monitor physical kinematics, environmental states and task completion outcomes. While existing work provides methods to plan information seeking actions and interpret acquired observations, current approaches do not monitor whether the live process of information gathering is functioning properly. Consequently, such systems can remain operationally active without resolving outstanding mission requirements. To address this limitation, we introduce Acquisition-State Telemetry (AST), a model free architecture that evaluates ongoing information acquisition from observable evidence relative to declared task requirements. Specifically, a domain interface maps raw observations into task resolution coordinates and normalized comparative representations, which are processed by a fixed causal monitor. This monitor emits two continuous real time signals that quantify (i) prospective progress across unresolved requirements and (ii) informational friction generated when recurrent evidence yields negligible informational gain. We evaluated a single, unadjusted monitor configuration across eight diverse domains including search and rescue dialogue, spatial grid exploration, hierarchical capability acquisition, continuous robotic manipulation, and probabilistic intent estimation. We show that across these domains the friction channel distinguished unproductive stagnation from purposeful exploration prior to task completion, while remaining muted during post completion activity. Furthermore, in closed loop reinforcement learning experiments under early termination constraints, we demonstrate that penalizing informational friction allowed agents to sustain high mission success, whereas unpenalized policies and baselines with matched operational costs failed to complete required tasks.

</details>


### [261] [LEASH: Stay Safe in Agentic Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14115)
*Qinfeng Zhu,Weiguang Zhao,Ke Sun,Xihao Guo,Yunxi Jiang,Rui Zhang,Lei Fan,Kaizhu Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出机器人智能体安全评测基准 RoboRisk（92 个仿真任务 + 13 个真机任务，覆盖五大风险类别，全部闭环执行），并发现六个前沿基础模型普遍存在高危害率；同时提出三层门控安全框架 LEASH，在仿真与真机上有效降低危害，且几乎不损性能、开销小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型（GPT、Claude 等）在空间理解与具身智能上能力显著，使"智能体化机器人"成为通往机器人通用智能的有前景路径；但用基础模型直接控制机器人存在严重安全风险，且缺乏全面的安全评测。现有基准最多只覆盖五类风险中的两类，部分仅评估文本输出，不涉及真实执行。

Method: 1) RoboRisk 基准：92 个仿真任务 + 13 个真机任务，分为五类——任务级拒绝（T）、规划修复（P）、执行期危害（E）、注入攻击（I）、序列记忆（S）；所有任务均在闭环中执行。2) 评测对象：6 个基础模型（GPT-6 Astra、GPT-6.1 Sol、GPT-6 Luna、Claude Opus 5.5、Gemini 4 Argon、Grok 4.7）及两个机器人专用 API（Dream API、PokeBot 的 RoboAPI）。3) 防御方法 LEASH（Lease-Enforced Agentic Safety Harness）：借助决策模型（Jev 或 OpenAI Decisions API）实现任务门、动作门、提交门，分别在视觉上下文中审查任务、每个动作与每个不可逆操作；规划器与三道门共享同一安全策略，可嵌入几乎任意智能体架构。

Result: 六个前沿基础模型在 RoboRisk 上总体呈现较高的危害率，暴露出现有模型在机器人闭环控制中的安全短板；仿真与真机实验表明，LEASH 对全部六个模型均能有效降低危害，同时基本保持任务完成性能，时间与 token 开销增加很少。

Conclusion: 机器人智能体化虽前景广阔，但当前基础模型的安全风险不可忽视，亟需覆盖面完整、闭环执行的评测体系；RoboRisk 填补了五类风险全覆盖的空白，LEASH 作为一种通用、低开销的可插拔安全基础设施，为智能体机器人提供了可落地的防护方案。

Abstract: Foundation models such as GPT and Claude have shown strong capabilities in spatial understanding and embodied intelligence. These advances make agentic robotics a promising path toward artificial general intelligence in robotics. However, we find that using foundation models to control robots poses serious safety risks, and comprehensive safety evaluation is still lacking. We introduce RoboRisk, a comprehensive benchmark for evaluating safety in agentic robotics. Its 92 simulated tasks and 13 physical-robot tasks fall into five categories: Task-level Refusal (T), Plan Repair (P), Execution-time Hazard (E), Injection (I), and Sequential Memory (S). Prior benchmarks cover at most two of these categories, and several evaluate only text outputs. In contrast, RoboRisk covers all five and executes every task in closed loop. We evaluate six state-of-the-art foundation models on RoboRisk: GPT-6 Astra, GPT-6.1 Sol, GPT-6 Luna, Claude Opus 5.5, Gemini 4 Argon, and Grok 4.7. These models generally show high harm rates. We also evaluate two robot-specific APIs: the Dream API and PokeBot's RoboAPI. We further propose the Lease-Enforced Agentic Safety Harness (LEASH), which offers essential safety infrastructure for agentic robotics and can be integrated into nearly any agentic architecture. LEASH leverages a decision model (Jev or OpenAI's Decisions API) to implement task, action, and commit gates, which review the task, each action, and each irreversible operation in their visual context. The planner and all three gates share a common safety policy. Experiments on RoboRisk in simulation and on physical robots show that LEASH effectively reduces harm across all six foundation models. It largely preserves their task completion performance and adds little time and token overhead.

</details>


### [262] [Koopman Model-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14186)
*Wenjian Hao,Paulo C. Heredia,Shaoshuai Mou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于Koopman算子的模型型强化学习算法K-MBRL，用线性Koopman表示近似未知非线性动力学、以critic估计剩余未来代价，实现计算高效且样本高效的实时机器人控制，并在仿真与实物四足机器人上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对未知动力学的非线性机器人系统控制问题：传统无模型RL样本效率低，而模型型RL的长时域rollout会累积预测误差，且模型依赖的策略梯度计算代价高。

Method: 构造近似策略目标：解析即时代价 + 在一步预测状态处评估的未来代价估计（由critic函数给出）。用随机Koopman算子学习方法将未知非线性动力学近似为线性表示；由于Koopman动力学线性，模型依赖的策略梯度退化为常数矩阵乘积，从而高效完成策略改进；采用一步模型前推避免长时域rollout的误差累积，同时由critic保留长期代价信息；学习到的策略以前馈方式在线评估实现实时控制。理论上在标准随机逼近假设下建立了策略学习的收敛性保证。

Result: 在HalfCheetah与四足机器人运动任务仿真中表现出较强的样本效率并优于经典无模型/模型型RL基线；Pendulum摆起任务用于评估策略梯度近似精度并对学习机制与关键超参数做消融；并在实物四足机器人上完成"由后部两个圆弧足支撑起身直立—双足行走保持平衡—安全落回地面"的连续动作验证。

Conclusion: K-MBRL通过Koopman线性化与单步预测+critic的组合，兼顾计算效率、样本效率与预测可靠性，具备收敛性保证并可用于真实机器人实时控制。

Abstract: We propose a Koopman model-based reinforcement learning (K-MBRL) algorithm for controlling nonlinear robotic systems with unknown dynamics. The K-MBRL learns a control policy by minimizing an approximate policy objective that combines the analytical immediate cost with an estimate of the future cost evaluated at the one-step predicted state. To this end, we develop a stochastic Koopman operator-based learning method to approximate the unknown nonlinear dynamics in a linear representation, while a critic function estimates the remaining future cost. This formulation offers two key advantages. First, the linear Koopman dynamics simplify the model-dependent policy gradient computation to a constant matrix product, enabling computationally efficient policy improvement. Second, one-step model propagation mitigates the accumulation of prediction errors associated with long-horizon rollouts while retaining long-term cost information through the critic. The learned policy enables real-time control through feedforward evaluation without online optimization. We establish policy learning convergence guarantees under standard stochastic approximation assumptions. Simulations on HalfCheetah and quadruped four-legged locomotion tasks demonstrate strong sample efficiency and improved performance compared with classical model-free and model-based reinforcement learning baselines. A pendulum swing-up task is used to assess policy gradient approximation accuracy and conduct ablation studies on the learning mechanism and key hyperparameters. The proposed K-MBRL is further validated on a physical quadruped robot performing a challenging sequence of maneuvers: rising to an upright posture supported by two rounded rear feet, maintaining balance during bipedal walking, and safely returning to the ground, as shown in Fig.1.

</details>


### [263] [Elastic-WAM: Adaptive Spatiotemporal Imagination for Efficient Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14257)
*Haitian Li,Tianfu Li,Xuxi Zhao,Zizheng Liu,Yangcheng Yu,Yiwen Hou,Zeyu Xiao,Mutian Xu,Ziqian Yang,Haonan Chen,Lin Shao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Elastic-WAM，用统一时空精炼需求场自适应分配世界模型的精炼算力（何时停止、每步推进多远、刷新哪些区域），在降低推理开销的同时提升控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: World-Action 模型依赖迭代视频去噪预测未来视觉动态，推理开销大；已有"部分未来去噪"方法采用固定精炼预算，但作者观察发现最优精炼深度随任务与状态变化，且未来不同区域对动作生成的相关性不同，固定预算并非最优。

Method: 构建统一的时空精炼需求场，同时决定时间维度（去噪时间上的停止条件与每步推进幅度）和空间维度（需刷新的未来区域）；该场以共享时间基与空间幅度参数化，并通过三阶段、以动作为监督信号的有监督流程学习；推理时用该场引导循环稀疏世界精炼。

Result: 在多种仿真基准与真实世界长程操作任务上取得最优控制性能，同时降低推理延迟。

Conclusion: 自适应、时空联合的精炼预算分配优于固定预算的部分去噪策略，可在效率与成功率之间取得更优权衡；为 World-Action 模型的实际部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: World-Action Models improve robot control by predicting future visual dynamics, but iterative video denoising incurs substantial inference overhead. Recent approaches reduce this cost through partial future denoising, yet typically rely on fixed refinement budgets. Our empirical observations reveal that preferred refinement depths vary across tasks and states, while different future regions exhibit varying relevance to action generation. Motivated by these findings, we propose Elastic-WAM, which adaptively allocates world refinement computation across temporal denoising time and spatial future space. A unified spatiotemporal refinement demand field governs when to stop world refinement, how far to advance each denoising step, and which regions to refresh. We parameterize this field with a shared temporal basis and spatial amplitudes, and learn it through a three-stage action-grounded supervised procedure. At inference, the learned field guides recurrent sparse world refinement to improve task success while reducing inference overhead. Evaluations across diverse simulation benchmarks and real-world long-horizon manipulation demonstrate state-of-the-art control performance with reduced inference latency.

</details>


### [264] [From Prediction to Action: Learning Physical Context for Deformable Linear Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14274)
*Chengxiao Dong,Alessio Caporali,Hongyu Lan,Gianluca Palli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文区分"预测效用"与"决策效用"，提出Geometry-Pointer-Flow (GPF)作为平面单抓取DLO形状控制的决策骨干，比较了真值参数、估计参数与交互历史三种物理上下文表征。结果表明表征的预测精度排序不能推广到控制性能：估计参数预测优于交互历史，但控制上无增益；交互历史在控制与硬件零样本迁移上最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的预测表征未必对动作选择最有用。作者质疑"以预测精度评价物理上下文表征"的惯例，试图在可变形线性物体(DLO)的平面单抓取形状控制中厘清预测效用与决策效用的差异，并回答"何种物理上下文表征真正支撑控制决策"。

Method: 提出GPF（Geometry-Pointer-Flow）几何驱动策略，将抓取点选择与抓取条件下的运动生成解耦，并提供仅几何的变体作为基线；在同一骨干上对比三类物理上下文表征——真值物理参数、估计参数、学习到的交互历史表征，分别评估其前向预测能力与实际执行控制能力，并进行闭环仿真与真机零样本迁移实验（仅仿真训练，无真机微调）。

Result: 几何变体较DLO专用扩散策略一步执行形状误差降低32.8%，参数量少18.7倍，推理快约193倍。预测方面，真值参数比纯几何降低下一步形状误差15.8%，估计参数略优于交互历史。控制方面，该排序不成立：估计参数无显著一步控制增益，而历史条件GPF在470组配对仿真试验中将执行误差较几何基线降低9.9%；闭环仿真中历史表征提升严格阈值达成率，并在硬件上取得最佳零样本性能。

Conclusion: 物理上下文表征应以其所支持的决策质量而非单纯预测精度来评价；预测效用与决策效用可能不一致。交互历史是控制中最有效的物理上下文形式，且纯仿真训练的策略可零样本迁移至真实机器人。

Abstract: A representation that accurately predicts a deformable object's response is not necessarily the one most useful for choosing manipulation actions. We study this distinction between predictive utility and decision utility in planar single-grasp shape control of Deformable Linear Objects (DLOs). As a common decision backbone, we introduce Geometry-Pointer-Flow (GPF), a geometry-grounded policy that separates grasp-location selection from grasp-conditioned motion generation. Its geometry-only variant reduces one-step executed shape error by 32.8% relative to a DLO-specific diffusion policy, while using 18.7 times fewer parameters and achieving approximately 193 times faster inference. On this backbone, we compare physical context represented by ground-truth parameters, estimated parameters, and learned interaction-history representations, for both forward prediction and executed control. For prediction, ground-truth physical parameters reduce next-shape error by 15.8% over geometry alone, and estimated parameters are slightly more predictive than interaction history. This ordering does not carry over to control: estimated parameters provide no detectable one-step control gain, whereas history-conditioned GPF reduces executed error by 9.9% relative to geometry-only GPF across 470 paired simulation trials. In closed loop, interaction history improves strict-threshold attainment in simulation and yields the strongest zero-shot performance on hardware. All policies are trained exclusively in simulation and transferred to the physical robot without real-world training or fine-tuning. These results show that physical context should be evaluated by the decisions it supports rather than by predictive accuracy alone. Code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [265] [ForeseeDrive: Future-Token Conditioning for Anticipatory Generative Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14286)
*Shaokai Wu,Xunchu Zhou,Pinzhi Wang,Bo Dong,Yuting Hu,Yanbiao Ji,Qiuchang Li,Xiao Cui,Qi Kong,Hongtao Lu,Yue Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ForeseeDrive将未来场景演化压缩为"查询索引token"，在候选轨迹生成之前对其几何进行条件化，从而突破下游评分器只能在采样候选内选优、无法恢复缺失机动的瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式驾驶规划器虽能表示多样自车未来，但下游评分器只能从已采样候选中挑选，无法召回未被采样的机动。需要把未来场景演化转化为可在部署时使用的接口，用于塑造候选生成，而非重建稠密未来状态或对固定候选集打分。

Method: 从日志中actor运动、占据演化、路线交互与TTC余量派生查询索引token，并在部署时由当前上下文预测同一token空间；这些token在候选轨迹实体化之前条件化其几何。引入零token门控残差路径与候选保留损失以限制无谓漂移，并用TTC余量保护机制针对易碎的安全—不安全边界穿越。

Result: 在各自基准协议下：NAVSIM navtest 达 89.2 PDMS；nuScenes验证集平均L2 0.41 m、碰撞率0.05%；Bench2Drive驾驶分数76.50。DiffusionDrive/NAVSIM诊断显示：零token时严格实现无操作、在保留损失下对齐token相较打乱token有小的被选PDM优势、oracle-best质量参差、TTC边界失效呈局部化。

Conclusion: 基准结果与有界机制测试共同支持"未来条件化生成"作为面向预判的实用接口。

Abstract: Generative driving planners can represent diverse ego futures, but a downstream scorer can choose only among sampled candidates; it cannot recover an absent maneuver. We present ForeseeDrive, which turns future scene evolution into a deployable interface for shaping candidate generation. Rather than reconstructing dense future states or scoring a fixed menu, ForeseeDrive derives query-indexed tokens from logged actor motion, occupancy evolution, route interactions, and TTC margins, and predicts the same token space from present context at deployment. The tokens condition trajectory geometry before candidates materialize. A zero-token-gated residual path and candidate-preservation loss limit unnecessary drift, while a TTC-margin guard targets brittle safe-to-unsafe boundary crossings. Under their respective benchmark protocols, ForeseeDrive reaches 89.2 PDMS on NAVSIM navtest, 0.41\,m average L2 and 0.05\% collision rate on nuScenes validation, and a 76.50 driving score on Bench2Drive. Controlled DiffusionDrive/NAVSIM diagnostics show an exact zero-token no-op, a small aligned-over-shuffled selected-PDM advantage under preservation, mixed oracle-best quality, and localized TTC-boundary failures. Together, the benchmark results and bounded mechanism tests support future-conditioned generation as a practical interface for anticipation. Code is included in the supplementary materials.

</details>


### [266] [Gaussian Process-Augmented Model Predictive Contouring Control for 6-DoF Trajectory Tracking with a Holonomic AUV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14373)
*David Dörner,Giancarlo Troni,Ignacio Torroba,Ivan Stenius*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向全驱动(holonomic)自主水下机器人(AUV)的全六自由度非线性MPCC控制器，用高斯过程(GP)回归学习动力学残差，并通过零阶鲁棒优化将学习到的残差嵌入最优控制问题，在保持25 Hz实时性的同时提升轨迹跟踪精度，经仿真消融与MOLA AUV实水实验验证优于名义MPCC和PID。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型全驱动AUV需在任意视角下近距离检测水下结构，要求在有状态与输入约束下精确跟踪位置与姿态；MPCC能自然处理这些约束，但依赖精确动力学模型，而水下动力学模型难以获得，模型误差会显著影响控制性能。

Method: 1) 为全驱动AUV构建全6自由度非线性MPCC，直接处理状态与输入约束；2) 用GP回归学习名义模型的残差动力学，以补偿建模不准确；3) 通过零阶鲁棒优化(zero-order robust optimization)将学习残差嵌入最优控制问题，使其对残差不确定性具备鲁棒性，同时保持实时性(25 Hz)；4) 通过仿真消融分析残差选择的必要性；5) 用MOLA AUV开展仿真与水试对比实验（对比名义MPCC与工程验证的PID）。

Result: 仿真消融表明残差项的选择对控制器设计至关重要；仿真与水试结果验证了所提GP增强MPCC在跟踪性能上优于名义MPCC和现场验证的PID控制器，且控制器维持25 Hz的实时运行频率。

Conclusion: 将GP学习的残差动力学以零阶鲁棒优化方式嵌入全六自由度非线性MPCC，是一种在模型不精确条件下实现实时、约束满足且高精度跟踪的有效方案，适用于小型全驱动AUV的近距离水下结构检测任务。
